// ─── Nest Track · Cloudflare Worker ─── // Handles: Anthropic API proxy + OneSignal push + ShowingTime + Email + Webhook + Inbound Leads + Weekly TC Digest + Auto-Send Offer const ALLOWED_ORIGIN = '*'; // ─── Sending domains (per-agent from-fields; reply-to is always the agent's own email) ─── // mail = transactional (status/step updates, offers, appts, billing, alerts) // news = automated nurture (Smart Plans drips + NestTracker home-value digest) // hi = email-builder broadcasts (highest complaint risk — fully isolated) // Nothing ever sends "from NestTracker" — always {AgentName} <{slug}@{domain}>. const MAIL_DOMAIN = 'mail.nesttrack.io'; // ── App download links ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Store listing URLs. Every email that offers the app picks these up automatically — set one and // the buttons, copy, and fallbacks all adjust themselves. Both stores are live as of July 2026. const APP_STORE_URL = 'https://apps.apple.com/us/app/nesttrack-crm/id6782980260'; // listed as "NestTrack CRM" const GOOGLE_PLAY_URL = 'https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=io.nesttrack.app'; const APP_WEB_URL = 'https://app.nesttrack.io'; const NEWS_DOMAIN = 'news.nesttrack.io'; const HI_DOMAIN = 'hi.nesttrack.io'; // Listing alerts get their own sending domain. They are the highest-volume, most automated // mail the platform sends — if they ever draw spam complaints, the damage stays here and // never touches seller reports or transaction email on the other domains. const LISTINGS_DOMAIN = 'listings.nesttrack.io'; // Build a per-agent From header: "Tom Garuccio ". // Mirrors the app's agentFromField() slug logic (first+last, a–z0–9 only) so an agent's // sending identity is identical across mail/news/hi. function _agentFromField(agent, domain){ agent = agent || {}; var first = (agent.firstName || '').trim(); var last = ''; var name = (agent.name || '').trim(); if (!first && name){ var pp = name.split(/\s+/); first = pp[0]||''; last = pp.slice(1).join(' ')||''; } else if (agent._doc){ last = (agent._doc.lastName || '').trim(); } var display = (first + ' ' + last).trim() || name || 'Your Agent'; var slug = (first + last).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,''); if (!slug) slug = display.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,''); if (!slug) slug = 'agent'; return display + ' <' + slug + '@' + (domain || MAIL_DOMAIN) + '>'; } // ─── Per-tenant email send cap (protects the shared Resend quota) ─── // Tune these two anytime. Daily stops a single-day blast; monthly stops slow torching. const EMAIL_DAILY_CAP = 2000; const EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP = 2000; const FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID = 'buyers-880bc'; // Public by design: it identifies the project and grants nothing on its own. Used to ask // Google to verify an ID token rather than checking the signature by hand. const FIREBASE_WEB_API_KEY = 'AIzaSyCy5ZkbPcGtk7I_88dVbNsX40LY-M4R_sY'; const SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL = 'firebase-adminsdk-fbsvc@buyers-880bc.iam.gserviceaccount.com'; const FB_WEB_KEY = 'AIzaSyCy5ZkbPcGtk7I_88dVbNsX40LY-M4R_sY'; // public web key — used only to verify ID tokens server-side // ─── Firestore JWT / Token ─── async function getFirestoreToken(privateKeyPem) { const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000); const header = { alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' }; const payload = { iss: SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL, sub: SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL, aud: 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', iat: now, exp: now + 3600, scope: 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/datastore' }; function b64url(obj) { return btoa(JSON.stringify(obj)).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=/g, ''); } const signingInput = b64url(header) + '.' + b64url(payload); // Import private key const pemContents = privateKeyPem.replace(/-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----|-----END PRIVATE KEY-----|\n/g, ''); const keyData = Uint8Array.from(atob(pemContents), c => c.charCodeAt(0)); const cryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey( 'pkcs8', keyData.buffer, { name: 'RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign'] ); const signature = await crypto.subtle.sign( 'RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5', cryptoKey, new TextEncoder().encode(signingInput) ); const sigBytes = new Uint8Array(signature); let sigStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < sigBytes.length; i++) sigStr += String.fromCharCode(sigBytes[i]); const jwt = signingInput + '.' + btoa(sigStr).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=/g, ''); // Exchange JWT for access token const tokenResp = await fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: `grant_type=urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer&assertion=${jwt}` }); const tokenData = await tokenResp.json(); return tokenData.access_token; } async function firestoreWrite(token, docPath, fields) { const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}`; const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields }) }); return resp; } // ─── Read a single Firestore doc (admin) ─── async function fsGetDoc(token, docPath) { const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}`; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } }); if (!r.ok) return null; const j = await r.json(); return digDoc(j.fields || {}); } function esc(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&').replace(//g, '>').replace(/"/g, '"'); } // ─── AES-GCM encryption for stored 3rd-party credentials ─── // Key comes from env.ENCRYPTION_KEY (base64, 32 bytes). Output: base64(iv[12] + ciphertext). async function _encKey(env){ var raw = Uint8Array.from(atob(env.ENCRYPTION_KEY), c=>c.charCodeAt(0)); return await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', raw, { name:'AES-GCM' }, false, ['encrypt','decrypt']); } async function encryptCred(env, plaintext){ if(!env.ENCRYPTION_KEY || !plaintext) return ''; var key = await _encKey(env); var iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12)); var enc = new TextEncoder().encode(plaintext); var ct = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name:'AES-GCM', iv }, key, enc)); var out = new Uint8Array(iv.length + ct.length); out.set(iv,0); out.set(ct,iv.length); var s=''; for(var i=0;ic.charCodeAt(0)); var iv = bytes.slice(0,12), ct = bytes.slice(12); var dec = await crypto.subtle.decrypt({ name:'AES-GCM', iv }, key, ct); return new TextDecoder().decode(dec); } catch(e){ return ''; } } // ─── List an agent's Slybroadcast audio recordings (for the VM step picker) ─── async function spListSlyAudio(creds){ if(!creds || !creds.slyEmail || !creds.slyPass) return { error:'No Slybroadcast credentials saved.' }; var t = await r.text(); if (/error|invalid|incorrect|denied|fail/i.test(t) && t.length < 120) return { error: t.slice(0,200), raw: t.slice(0,1200) }; var files = []; var isDate = function(s){ return /\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s) || /^\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{2,4}/.test(s); }; var isSystemName = function(s){ return /\.(wav|mp3|m4a|ogg)$/i.test(s) || /^[a-f0-9]{6,}$/i.test(s); }; var clean = function(s){ return String(s||'').replace(/^["'\s]+|["'\s]+$/g,'').trim(); }; // Try JSON first (array of objects, or object wrapping a list) var parsed = false; try { var j = JSON.parse(t); var arr = Array.isArray(j) ? j : (j.audio_files || j.list || j.files || j.data || j.audio || []); if (Array.isArray(arr) && arr.length){ parsed = true; for (var i=0;i]+([^",}\r\n]+)/i); } catch(e){ return { error: e.message }; } } // ─── Native VM drop via Slybroadcast (uses agent's own creds) ─── async function spSendVM(creds, audioName, phone, callerId){ if(!creds || !creds.slyEmail) return { ok:false, reason:'no slybroadcast email saved for this agent' }; } } // ─── Parse a single mailing-address string into thanks.io's components ─── function spParseAddress(s){ var out = { street:'', city:'', state:'', zip:'', country:'US' }; s = String(s||'').replace(/\r/g,'').replace(/\n/g, ', ').trim(); if(!s) return out; var parts = s.split(',').map(function(p){ return p.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); if (parts.length >= 3){ out.street = parts[0]; out.city = parts[1]; var tail = parts.slice(2).join(' '); var m = tail.match(/([A-Za-z]{2})\s*(\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?)/); if (m){ out.state = m[1].toUpperCase(); out.zip = m[2]; } else { out.state = (parts[2]||'').toUpperCase(); out.zip = parts[3]||''; } } else if (parts.length === 2){ out.street = parts[0]; var m2 = parts[1].match(/(.+?)\s+([A-Za-z]{2})\s*(\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?)/); if (m2){ out.city = m2[1].trim(); out.state = m2[2].toUpperCase(); out.zip = m2[3]; } else out.city = parts[1]; } else { out.street = s; } return out; } // ─── List an agent's thanks.io message templates (for the Letter step picker) ─── async function spListThanksioImageTemplates(key){ if(!key) return { error:'No thanks.io API key saved.' }; } } // ─── Text-you-send: queue a task on the agent's user doc + push them ─── function spMergeBody(body, c){ return String(body||'') .replace(/\{first\}/gi, c.firstName||'') .replace(/\{last\}/gi, c.lastName||'') .replace(/\{name\}/gi, ((c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||'')).trim()) .replace(/%FIRST_NAME%/gi, c.firstName||''); } async function spQueueTextTask(c, step, env, creds, note){ var agentUid = creds.uid || ''; if (!agentUid) return { ok:false, reason:'could not resolve the agent to notify' }; if (!c.phone) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no phone number' }; var msg = spMergeBody(step.smsBody||step.content||'', c); if (!msg.trim()) return { ok:false, reason:'this text step has no message' }; var name = ((c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||'')).trim() || 'contact'; var taskId = 'tt_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7); // Write task to users/{agentUid}/textTasks/{taskId} (covered by existing user-subcollection rules) try { var PRIVATE_KEY = env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY; var token = await getFirestoreToken(PRIVATE_KEY); var url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/users/${agentUid}/textTasks/${taskId}`; await fetch(url, { method:'PATCH', headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields: { contactName: { stringValue: name }, contactId: { stringValue: c.uid || '' }, phone: { stringValue: String(c.phone) }, message: { stringValue: msg }, planName: { stringValue: (step.planName||'') }, // Why this is a draft rather than a sent text. Blank when the step was set to draft // on purpose; filled in when Quo was meant to send and did not. quoNote: { stringValue: String(note || '') }, done: { booleanValue: false }, createdAt: { integerValue: String(Date.now()) } }}) }); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'could not save text task: ' + e.message }; } // Push the agent var ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; var pushed = false, pushErr = ''; if (!ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY) pushErr = 'ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY is not set on the worker'; if (ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY) { var _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, agentUid); await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Key ${ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e', include_aliases: { external_id: [agentUid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: ' Text ' + name }, contents: { en: msg.slice(0, 110) }, web_url: 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?texts=1', data: { targetUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?texts=1' } }) }).then(function(pr){ pushed = !!(pr && pr.ok); if (!pushed) pushErr = 'OneSignal ' + (pr && pr.status); }).catch(function(e){ pushErr = 'OneSignal unreachable: ' + (e && e.message); }); } // Report what actually happened. This used to claim 'push sent' even with no OneSignal key // configured, which made a missing push impossible to tell from a delivered one. var why = note ? (note + ' \u2014 drafted instead') : 'reminder queued'; return { ok:true, reason: why + (pushed ? ' + push sent' : ' (no push: ' + (pushErr||'unknown') + ')') }; } // ─── Native letter via thanks.io (uses agent's own API key) ─── // opts: { templateId, message }. Sends a windowless letter. // \u2500\u2500 Quo (formerly OpenPhone) \u2500\u2500 // The agent connects their own workspace, so the carrier registration, the credits and the // compliance are theirs. NestTrack never sends on anyone's behalf. const QUO_BASE = 'https://api.quo.com'; function _quoHeaders(key) { return { 'Authorization': String(key || ''), 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }; } // E.164, which is the only format the API accepts. A ten-digit US number gets a +1. function _quoPhone(raw) { let d = String(raw || '').replace(/[^0-9+]/g, ''); if (d.indexOf('+') === 0) return d; d = d.replace(/\D/g, ''); if (d.length === 10) return '+1' + d; if (d.length === 11 && d.charAt(0) === '1') return '+' + d; return d ? ('+' + d) : ''; } // The agent's numbers, so they can pick which one sends rather than us guessing. async function quoListNumbers(key) { if (!key) return { error: 'No Quo API key saved.' }; try { const r = await fetch(QUO_BASE + '/v1/phone-numbers', { headers: _quoHeaders(key) }); if (r.status === 401 || r.status === 403) return { error: 'Quo rejected that key. Generate a new one under Settings \u2192 API.' }; if (!r.ok) { const t = await r.text(); return { error: 'Quo ' + r.status + ': ' + t.slice(0, 140) }; } const d = await r.json(); const list = (d && d.data) || []; return { ok: true, numbers: list.map(function (n) { return { id: n.id || '', number: n.number || '', name: n.name || '' }; }) }; } catch (e) { return { error: 'Could not reach Quo: ' + (e && e.message) }; } } // One send. Returns a reason rather than throwing, because every caller needs to fall back to // a draft rather than lose the message. async function quoSendText(creds, toPhone, body) { const key = (creds && creds.quoApiKey) || ''; const from = (creds && creds.quoFrom) || ''; if (!key) return { ok: false, reason: 'no Quo API key saved for this agent' }; if (!from) return { ok: false, reason: 'no Quo sending number chosen' }; const to = _quoPhone(toPhone); if (!to) return { ok: false, reason: 'contact has no usable phone number' }; if (!String(body || '').trim()) return { ok: false, reason: 'nothing to send' }; try { const r = await fetch(QUO_BASE + '/v1/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: _quoHeaders(key), body: JSON.stringify({ from: from, to: [to], content: String(body) }) }); if (r.ok) { const d = await r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }); return { ok: true, id: (d && d.data && d.data.id) || '' }; } const t = await r.text(); // Named separately because they need different responses: a key problem is one-time, but // credits or registration affect every message until the agent fixes them. if (r.status === 401 || r.status === 403) { return { ok: false, reason: 'Quo rejected the API key', fatal: 'key' }; } if (/credit/i.test(t)) { return { ok: false, reason: 'Quo account is out of messaging credits', fatal: 'credits' }; } if (/registration|10dlc|a2p/i.test(t)) { return { ok: false, reason: 'Quo number is not registered for US messaging', fatal: 'registration' }; } return { ok: false, reason: 'Quo ' + r.status + ': ' + t.slice(0, 140) }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: 'Could not reach Quo: ' + (e && e.message) }; } } // Nobody sends a client an automated text at 2am, and a number that does gets blocked. Steps // that come due overnight wait for morning rather than sending or being dropped. // Takes the agent's own time zone (an IANA name such as America/Chicago, saved on their // profile by the app) so 8am-8pm means 8 to 8 where they work, daylight saving included. // Eastern is only the fallback for an agent with no zone saved yet or an unreadable one. function _quoWithinSendingHours(tz) { const hourIn = function (zone) { const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: zone, hour: '2-digit', hour12: false }).formatToParts(new Date()); let h = NaN; for (const p of parts) { if (p.type === 'hour') h = parseInt(p.value, 10); } return h === 24 ? 0 : h; }; let h = NaN; try { if (tz && typeof tz === 'string') h = hourIn(tz); } catch (e) { h = NaN; } if (isNaN(h)) { try { h = hourIn('America/New_York'); } catch (e) { h = 12; } } return h >= 8 && h < 20; } async function spSendLetter(key, opts, contact){ if(!key) return { ok:false, reason:'no thanks.io API key saved for this agent' }; opts = opts || {}; var a = spParseAddress(contact.mailingAddress||''); if(!a.street) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no mailing address' }; var recipient = { name: ((contact.firstName||'')+' '+(contact.lastName||'')).trim() || 'Resident', address: a.street, city: a.city, province: a.state, postal_code: a.zip, country: a.country }; var body = { recipients: [recipient] }; // Determine mailer type. 'postcard' needs a FRONT IMAGE (image_template_id or front_image_url) // plus a message/message_template. 'letter' (windowless) needs only a message/message_template. var mailerType = (opts.mailerType||'letter').toLowerCase(); if (mailerType === 'postcard'){ // Front image is REQUIRED for postcards. if (opts.imageTemplateId) body.image_template_id = parseInt(opts.imageTemplateId,10); else if (opts.frontImageUrl) body.front_image_url = opts.frontImageUrl; else return { ok:false, reason:'postcard needs a front image — pick an image template' }; // Back message (template or text). Postcards can technically send image-only, but we require a message. if (opts.messageTemplateId) body.message_template_id = parseInt(opts.messageTemplateId,10); else if (opts.message && opts.message.trim()) body.message = opts.message; else return { ok:false, reason:'postcard needs a message — pick a message template or write one' }; if (opts.size) body.size = opts.size; // 4x6 / 6x9 / 6x11 var pcEndpoint = 'https://api.thanks.io/api/v2/send/postcard'; try { var rp = await fetch(pcEndpoint, { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Authorization':'Bearer '+key, 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Accept':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); if (rp.ok) return { ok:true, reason:'sent (postcard)' }; var tp = await rp.text(); return { ok:false, reason:'thanks.io '+rp.status+': '+String(tp).slice(0,160) }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'send error: '+e.message }; } } // Default: windowless letter (message only) if(!opts.templateId && !(opts.message && opts.message.trim())) return { ok:false, reason:'this letter step has no template and no message' }; } } function fsString(v) { return { stringValue: v || '' }; } function fsInt(v) { return { integerValue: String(v || 0) }; } function fsDouble(v) { return { doubleValue: Number(v) || 0 }; } function fsBool(v) { return { booleanValue: !!v }; } function fsTimestamp() { return { timestampValue: new Date().toISOString() }; } // Agent UID map — agentId short ID → Firebase UID for push notifications const AGENT_UIDS = { 'tom': 'pkVYFB12E4aTJcNlr7VhTJvETnR2', // Add other agents here as they join: 'chrissy': 'their-firebase-uid' }; // Agent email map — agentId short ID → email for lead notifications const AGENT_EMAILS = { 'tom': 'Tom@realtyincle.com', // Add other agents: 'chrissy': 'chrissy@realtyincle.com' }; // ─── Dynamic per-agent push (works for EVERY team, no hardcoded roster) ─── // Resolves any agent's Firebase uid from the publicly-written push_subscriptions/agent_{id} // doc (uid, name, teamId) that each agent's app writes on login. Falls back to the legacy // hardcoded map only as a bootstrap. This is what lets the worker notify any team member. const _OS_APP_ID = 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e'; // ── APP-ICON BADGE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // We keep our OWN badge count in Firestore (badge_counts/{uid}) and send `SetTo: ` on every // push, instead of letting OneSignal track it with `Increase: +1`. // // Why: the only way to reset OneSignal's server-side counter is a SILENT push, and Apple treats // silent pushes as best-effort — they get dropped in Low Power Mode, with Background App Refresh // off, or when the app was swiped away. So the reset almost never landed. The device badge cleared // (that part is local and instant), but OneSignal's counter kept climbing, and the next real push // resumed from the old total. One unread message would show 46. // // With SetTo, there is no counter to drift: every push carries the true number, and opening the app // writes 0 over an ordinary HTTP call that can't be silently dropped. async function _badgeNext(env, uid){ if (!uid) return 1; try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const d = await fsGetDoc(token, 'badge_counts/' + String(uid)); const n = Math.max(0, parseInt((d && d.n) || 0, 10)) + 1; await fsPatchFields(token, 'badge_counts/' + String(uid), { n: fsInt(n), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['n','updatedAt']); return n; } catch(e){ return 1; } // a counter miss should never block the push itself } // Zero the badge. Called when the app opens or returns to the foreground. async function clearBadgeForUid(env, uid){ if (!uid) return false; let wrote = false; // 1) Reset OUR counter — the authoritative one. A plain Firestore write; it always lands. try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); await fsPatchFields(token, 'badge_counts/' + String(uid), { n: fsInt(0), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['n','updatedAt']); wrote = true; } catch(e){} // 2) Best-effort silent push to zero the badge already painted on the device. If iOS drops this // (it often does), it no longer matters — the next real push sends SetTo with the true count. try { const key = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; if (key) { await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Key ${key}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: _OS_APP_ID, include_aliases: { external_id: [String(uid)] }, target_channel: 'push', content_available: true, ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: 0 }) }); } } catch(e){} return wrote; } async function resolveAgentPush(token, agentId) { if (!agentId) return null; try { const sub = await fsGetDoc(token, 'push_subscriptions/agent_' + agentId); if (sub && sub.uid) return { uid: sub.uid, name: sub.name || '', teamId: sub.teamId || '', agentId: agentId }; } catch (e) { /* fall through to legacy bootstrap */ } if (AGENT_UIDS[agentId]) return { uid: AGENT_UIDS[agentId], name: '', teamId: '', agentId: agentId }; return null; } // Push to a single agent by agentId. Returns true if a push was attempted. async function pushToAgentId(token, env, agentId, heading, content, url) { try { const key = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; if (!key) return false; const who = await resolveAgentPush(token, agentId); if (!who || !who.uid) return false; const _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, who.uid); await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Key ${key}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: _OS_APP_ID, include_aliases: { external_id: [who.uid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: heading }, contents: { en: content }, web_url: url || 'https://app.nesttrack.io', data: { targetUrl: url || 'https://app.nesttrack.io' } }) }).catch(() => {}); return true; } catch (e) { console.log('pushToAgentId err:', e && e.message); return false; } } // Push to many agents (blast distribution). Returns count attempted. async function pushToAgentIds(token, env, agentIds, heading, content, url) { let n = 0; for (const aid of (agentIds || [])) { if (await pushToAgentId(token, env, aid, heading, content, url)) n++; } return n; } // ─── LEAD DISTRIBUTION ENGINE (blast / round-robin; powers auto + manual lead sharing) ─── function fsFutureTs(ms) { return { timestampValue: new Date(Date.now() + (ms || 0)).toISOString() }; } function fsStrArr(arr) { return { arrayValue: { values: (arr || []).map(function (s) { return { stringValue: String(s || '') }; }) } }; } // Partial update (does NOT wipe other fields — unlike firestoreWrite's full PATCH). async function fsPatchFields(token, docPath, fields, maskPaths) { const mask = (maskPaths || Object.keys(fields)).map(function (p) { return 'updateMask.fieldPaths=' + encodeURIComponent(p); }).join('&'); const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}?${mask}`; return await fetch(url, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields }) }); } // Read a team's lead-distribution config (written by the leader's settings UI). async function readLeadDistConfig(token, teamId) { if (!teamId) return null; try { return await fsGetDoc(token, 'agent_config/leadDist_' + teamId); } catch (e) { return null; } } // Distribute a lead to a team. Creates a team_leads doc (status 'open') and fires pushes. async function distributeLead(token, env, teamId, lead, cfg) { const rotation = (cfg && Array.isArray(cfg.rotation)) ? cfg.rotation.filter(Boolean) : []; if (!rotation.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'empty rotation' }; const mode = (cfg && cfg.mode === 'roundrobin') ? 'roundrobin' : 'blast'; const leadId = 'tl_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7); const clientName = ((lead.firstName || '') + ' ' + (lead.lastName || '')).trim() || lead.email || 'New Lead'; const fields = { firstName: fsString(lead.firstName), lastName: fsString(lead.lastName), email: fsString(lead.email), phone: fsString(lead.phone), source: fsString(lead.source || 'External'), notes: fsString(lead.notes || ''), address: fsString(lead.address || ''), leadIntent: fsString(lead.intent || ''), teamId: fsString(teamId), mode: fsString(mode), status: fsString('open'), segment: fsString(lead.segment || ''), rotation: fsStrArr(rotation), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), }; let startIdx = 0; if (mode === 'roundrobin') { const last = (cfg && typeof cfg.lastIndex === 'number') ? cfg.lastIndex : -1; startIdx = (last + 1) % rotation.length; fields.currentIndex = fsInt(startIdx); fields.currentAgentId = fsString(rotation[startIdx]); fields.nextAdvanceAt = fsFutureTs(60 * 1000); } await firestoreWrite(token, 'team_leads/' + leadId, fields); const url = 'https://app.nesttrack.io'; if (mode === 'blast') { await pushToAgentIds(token, env, rotation, ' New Lead — first to claim!', clientName + ' just came in via ' + (lead.source || 'a lead source') + '. Tap to claim.', url); } else { await pushToAgentId(token, env, rotation[startIdx], ' New Lead — your turn!', clientName + ' is yours to claim for the next minute. Tap to claim.', url); // advance the team's rotation pointer so the NEXT lead starts at the next agent (fairness) await fsPatchFields(token, 'agent_config/leadDist_' + teamId, { lastIndex: fsInt(startIdx) }, ['lastIndex']); } return { ok: true, leadId, mode, count: rotation.length }; } // Advance round-robin leads whose 1-minute claim window expired. Called from the cron. async function advanceRoundRobinLeads(token, env) { let resp; try { resp = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'team_leads', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'status' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: 'open' } } } }); } catch (e) { return; } if (!Array.isArray(resp)) return; const nowMs = Date.now(); for (const r of resp) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (d.mode !== 'roundrobin') continue; const dueMs = d.nextAdvanceAt ? Date.parse(d.nextAdvanceAt) : 0; if (!dueMs || dueMs > nowMs) continue; // window hasn't lapsed const leadId = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); const rotation = Array.isArray(d.rotation) ? d.rotation : []; const curIdx = (typeof d.currentIndex === 'number') ? d.currentIndex : 0; const nextIdx = curIdx + 1; const clientName = ((d.firstName || '') + ' ' + (d.lastName || '')).trim() || d.email || 'New Lead'; if (nextIdx >= rotation.length) { // Ran the whole rotation unclaimed → hand to the team's leader/owner (teamId == their agentId). await fsPatchFields(token, 'team_leads/' + leadId, { status: fsString('unclaimed'), currentAgentId: fsString('') }, ['status', 'currentAgentId']); await pushToAgentId(token, env, d.teamId, '⚠️ Lead unclaimed — needs assigning', clientName + ' went through the rotation with no claim. Assign it manually.', 'https://app.nesttrack.io'); } else { await fsPatchFields(token, 'team_leads/' + leadId, { currentIndex: fsInt(nextIdx), currentAgentId: fsString(rotation[nextIdx]), nextAdvanceAt: fsFutureTs(60 * 1000) }, ['currentIndex', 'currentAgentId', 'nextAdvanceAt']); await pushToAgentId(token, env, rotation[nextIdx], ' New Lead — your turn!', clientName + ' is yours to claim for the next minute. Tap to claim.', 'https://app.nesttrack.io'); } } } // Zapier webhook URL for Lofty CRM sync const ZAPIER_WEBHOOK_URL = 'https://hooks.zapier.com/hooks/catch/5384562/uvqvyel/'; // ─── ShowingTime ─── async function handleFetchShowings(request, corsHeaders) { try { const body = await request.json(); const icalUrl = body.url; const includeHistory = body.includeHistory === true; if (!icalUrl) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'No URL provided', events: [] }), { status: 400, headers: corsHeaders }); const fetchUrl = icalUrl.replace(/^webcal:\/\//i, 'https://'); const res = await fetch(fetchUrl, { headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 17_0 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 (KHTML, like Gecko) Version/17.0 Mobile/15E148 Safari/604.1', 'Accept': 'text/calendar, application/ics, */*', } }); if (!res.ok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'ShowingTime returned ' + res.status, events: [] }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); const icalText = await res.text(); if (icalText.includes(' 9 ? clean.substr(9,2) : '00'; var mi = clean.length > 11 ? clean.substr(11,2) : '00'; try { return new Date(new Date(y+'-'+mo+'-'+d+'T'+h+':'+mi+':00').getTime() + estOffsetMs).toISOString(); } catch(e) { return null; } } var summary = getVal('SUMMARY'), location = getVal('LOCATION'), dtstart = getVal('DTSTART'), dtend = getVal('DTEND'); var desc = getVal('DESCRIPTION'), uid = getVal('UID'), status = getVal('STATUS'); if (status === 'CANCELLED') return; var startISO = parseDateTime(dtstart), endISO = parseDateTime(dtend); if (startISO) { var start = new Date(startISO); if (!includeHistory && (start < todayEastern || start > cutoff)) return; } events.push({ uid: uid || ('gen-' + Math.random()), summary: summary || location || 'Showing', address: location || summary || 'Address TBD', start: startISO, end: endISO, description: desc, status }); }); events.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.start||'') < (b.start||'') ? -1 : 1; }); return events; } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // WEEKLY TC DIGEST — each agent's Monday-morning rollup of their deals' // open & overdue Transaction-Coordinator tasks. Respects per-agent opt-out // (users/{uid}.tcDigestOff) and per-deal master switch (deal.tcEnabled). // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _digDate(s){ if(!s) return null; function digTaskStatus(deal, t, today){ var st = (deal.tcState && deal.tcState[t.key] && deal.tcState[t.key].status) || ''; if (st==='done' || st==='skip') return st; if (!t.applies(deal)) return 'skip'; var start=t.start(deal), end=t.end(deal); if (!start) return 'upcoming'; if (today < start) return 'upcoming'; if (end && today > end) return 'overdue'; return 'now'; } // Firestore REST value helpers function digVal(f){ if (!f) return undefined; if (f.stringValue !== undefined) return f.stringValue; if (f.integerValue !== undefined) return parseInt(f.integerValue,10); if (f.doubleValue !== undefined) return f.doubleValue; if (f.booleanValue !== undefined) return f.booleanValue; if (f.mapValue !== undefined) { var o={}; var ff=(f.mapValue.fields)||{}; for (var k in ff) o[k]=digVal(ff[k]); return o; } if (f.arrayValue !== undefined) { var arr=(f.arrayValue.values)||[]; return arr.map(digVal); } if (f.timestampValue !== undefined) return f.timestampValue; return undefined; } function digDoc(fields){ var o={}; for (var k in (fields||{})) o[k]=digVal(fields[k]); return o; } // ═══════════════════════ STRIPE BILLING ═══════════════════════ // Price IDs are held server-side so the client can never send (and tamper with) a price. // WARNING: the four subscription ids below are from a PREVIOUS Stripe account and no longer // exist — Stripe answers "No such price". Live pricing is in Firestore at billing_config/tiers // (see handleStripeCheckout). Do not reintroduce these as a fallback. nt_block is still current. const STRIPE_PRICES = { lead_founding: 'price_1TklNiLwO06EVjqoO3768xYU', // Team Lead / Solo — Founding $39.99 member_founding: 'price_1TklO0LwO06EVjqoTMBRSTX2', // Member — Founding $25.99 lead_list: 'price_1TklMmLwO06EVjqocnUC2kCI', // Team Lead / Solo — List $59.99 member_list: 'price_1TklN8LwO06EVjqop2SAhEkb', // Member — List $35.99 nt_block: 'price_1Toll5Q5MTSPhYMTbnDtFDBV' // NestTracker +100 homes — $19/mo (VERIFY same account + Live mode) }; const STRIPE_TRIAL_DAYS = 30; // Stripe wants form-encoded bodies with bracket notation for nested params. function _stripeEncode(params) { const parts = []; function add(key, val) { if (val === undefined || val === null) return; if (Array.isArray(val)) { val.forEach(function(v,i){ add(key+'['+i+']', v); }); } else if (typeof val === 'object') { Object.keys(val).forEach(function(k){ add(key+'['+k+']', val[k]); }); } else { parts.push(encodeURIComponent(key)+'='+encodeURIComponent(val)); } } Object.keys(params||{}).forEach(function(k){ add(k, params[k]); }); return parts.join('&'); } async function stripeAPI(env, method, path, params) { const r = await fetch('https://api.stripe.com/v1/'+path, { method: method, headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: (method === 'GET' || !params) ? undefined : _stripeEncode(params) }); return await r.json(); } // Verify a Stripe webhook signature (HMAC-SHA256 over "t.rawBody"), with 5-min replay protection. async function verifyStripeSig(rawBody, sigHeader, secret) { if (!sigHeader || !secret) return false; secret = String(secret).trim(); // strip any stray whitespace/newline picked up when the secret was pasted into env let t = null; const v1s = []; sigHeader.split(',').forEach(function(kv){ const i = kv.indexOf('='); if (i<0) return; const k = kv.slice(0,i).trim(), v = kv.slice(i+1).trim(); if (k === 't') t = v; else if (k === 'v1') v1s.push(v); }); if (!t || !v1s.length) return false; if (Math.abs(Math.floor(Date.now()/1000) - parseInt(t,10)) > 172800) return false; // 2-day window (was 300s) so dashboard Resends of older events still verify const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', new TextEncoder().encode(secret), { name:'HMAC', hash:'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']); const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, new TextEncoder().encode(t + '.' + rawBody)); const expected = Array.from(new Uint8Array(sig)).map(function(b){ return b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0'); }).join(''); return v1s.some(function(v){ if (v.length !== expected.length) return false; let diff = 0; for (let i=0;i _nowSec + (48*3600 + 600)) params.subscription_data.trial_end = _te; if (memberQty > 0 && memberPrice) params.line_items.push({ price: memberPrice, quantity: memberQty }); if (b.customerId) params.customer = b.customerId; else if (b.email) params.customer_email = b.email; let sess = await stripeAPI(env, 'POST', 'checkout/sessions', params); // If the stored customer was deleted/invalid, retry without it so re-subscribing never dead-ends. if (sess.error && params.customer && /no such customer|missing|resource_missing/i.test(sess.error.message||sess.error.code||'')) { delete params.customer; if (b.email) params.customer_email = b.email; sess = await stripeAPI(env, 'POST', 'checkout/sessions', params); } if (sess.error) return new Response(JSON.stringify({error: sess.error.message}), {status:400, headers:cors}); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ url: sess.url }), { headers: cors }); } async function handleStripePortal(request, env, cors){ const b = await request.json(); if (!b.customerId || !b.returnUrl) return new Response(JSON.stringify({error:'missing fields'}), {status:400, headers:cors}); const sess = await stripeAPI(env, 'POST', 'billing_portal/sessions', { customer: b.customerId, return_url: b.returnUrl }); if (sess.error) return new Response(JSON.stringify({error: sess.error.message}), {status:400, headers:cors}); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ url: sess.url }), { headers: cors }); } async function handleStripeUpdateSeats(request, env, cors){ const b = await request.json(); const subId = b.subscriptionId, memberQty = Math.max(0, parseInt(b.memberQty,10)||0); Browser-only reporting // loses a large share of events to ad blockers and iOS privacy settings, and the people it // loses are indistinguishable from the ones it keeps — so a lookalike audience seeded from // browser events alone is seeded from a biased sample. Both reports carry the same event_id // and Meta de-duplicates them. const META_API_VERSION = 'v21.0'; async function _sha256Hex(s) { const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(String(s))); return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(function (b) { return b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'); }).join(''); } // Credentials live on the agent's own user record, which only they can write and no public key // can read. Deliberately NOT in landing_pages: that collection is served to the browser through // a public API key, so anything in it is readable by anyone who views a landing page. async function _metaPixelCreds(env, agentId, pixelId) { if (!agentId || !pixelId) return null; try { const tk = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const rows = await digRunQuery(tk, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: String(agentId) } } }, limit: 20 }); if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return null; for (const r of rows) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields) || {}; const list = Array.isArray(d.metaPixels) ? d.metaPixels : []; for (const px of list) { if (px && String(px.pixelId || '') === String(pixelId)) { return { token: String(px.token || ''), testCode: String(px.testCode || '') }; } } } } catch (e) { console.log('meta creds lookup:', e && e.message); } return null; } // meta: { eventId, pixelId, qualified, value, fbc, fbp, eventSourceUrl, userAgent, city, state, zip } // Quiz answers never reach Meta — only the event, its value, hashed contact details and click ids. async function sendMetaCapi(env, agentId, meta, contact, request) { try { const pixelId = String((meta && meta.pixelId) || ''); const creds = await _metaPixelCreds(env, agentId, pixelId); if (!creds || !creds.token) { console.log('meta: no token for pixel', pixelId, 'agent', agentId); return; } const digits = function (s) { return String(s || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); }; const letters = function (s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, ''); }; let ph = digits(contact.phone); if (ph.length === 10) ph = '1' + ph; // Meta wants the country code const em = String(contact.email || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const zp = digits(meta.zip).slice(0, 5); const ud = {}; if (em) { ud.em = [await _sha256Hex(em)]; ud.external_id = [await _sha256Hex(em)]; } if (ph) ud.ph = [await _sha256Hex(ph)]; if (letters(contact.firstName)) ud.fn = [await _sha256Hex(letters(contact.firstName))]; if (letters(contact.lastName)) ud.ln = [await _sha256Hex(letters(contact.lastName))]; if (letters(meta.city)) ud.ct = [await _sha256Hex(letters(meta.city))]; // Meta matches on the 2-letter code. The page sends it from address autocomplete; if // anything longer arrives it is dropped rather than sent as an unmatchable string. const _st = letters(meta.state); if (_st.length === 2) ud.st = [await _sha256Hex(_st)]; const srcUrl = String(meta.eventSourceUrl || ''); const base = { event_time: now, action_source: 'website', event_source_url: srcUrl, user_data: ud }; const events = [Object.assign({}, base, { event_name: 'Lead', event_id: String(meta.eventId || ''), custom_data: { lead_source: 'seller_quiz' } })]; if (meta.qualified) { events.push(Object.assign({}, base, { event_name: 'QualifiedLead', event_id: String(meta.eventId || '') + '-q', custom_data: { lead_source: 'seller_quiz', value: Number(meta.value || 0), currency: 'USD' } })); } const payload = { data: events }; if (creds.testCode) payload.test_event_code = creds.testCode; const url = 'https://graph.facebook.com/' + META_API_VERSION + '/' + encodeURIComponent(pixelId) + '/events?access_token=' + encodeURIComponent(creds.token); const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }); // fetch resolves on 4xx, so the status is read rather than assumed. if (!resp.ok) { let t = ''; try { t = (await resp.text()).slice(0, 400); } catch (e2) {} console.log('meta capi rejected', resp.status, t); } else { console.log('meta capi ok:', events.length, 'event(s) for pixel', pixelId); } } catch (e) { // A Meta failure must never cost a lead; this runs detached from the response. console.log('meta capi error:', e && e.message); } } async function handleSelfServeSignup(request, env, cors){ let b; try { b = await request.json(); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({error:'bad json'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); } const idToken = b.idToken || ''; const first = String(b.firstName||'').trim(); const last = String(b.lastName||'').trim(); const phone = String(b.phone||'').trim(); const company = String(b.company||'').trim(); const acct = (b.accountType==='lender') ? Reproduces the app's // offer-summary email; CCs the listing agent; marks the offer Under Review. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function handleAutoSendOffer(request, env, corsHeaders) { const RESEND_API_KEY = env.RESEND_API_KEY; const PRIVATE_KEY = env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY; if (!RESEND_API_KEY || !PRIVATE_KEY) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'missing keys' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); const { listingId, offerId } = await request.json(); if (!listingId || !offerId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'missing ids' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); const token = await getFirestoreToken(PRIVATE_KEY); const listing = await fsGetDoc(token, `offer_listings/${listingId}`); if (!listing || listing.autoSend !== true || !listing.clientUid) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'autoSend off or no client' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); } const o = await fsGetDoc(token, `offer_listings/${listingId}/offers/${offerId}`); const client = await fsGetDoc(token, `users/${listing.clientUid}`); if (!o || !client || !client.email) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ skip: 'missing offer/client/email' }), { status: 200, headers: corsHeaders }); } const clientFirst = client.firstName || 'there'; const rows = [ ['Offer Price', o.offerPrice], ['Financing', o.financingType], ['Down Payment', o.downPayment], ['Inspection', o.inspectionCont === 'Yes' ? Waits are in days; consecutive DOs fire same pass. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function spTodayET(){ return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'America/New_York',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date()); } function spAddDaysStr(str, days){ var d = new Date((str||spTodayET())+'T12:00:00Z'); d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()+(days||0)); return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); } function spWaitDays(s){ var n = parseFloat(s.amount)||1, u=s.unit||'days'; if (u==='days') return n; if (u==='hours') return n/24; if (u==='minutes') return n/1440; return n; } // Minutes-based version of a wait step (for true sub-day precision). function spWaitMinutes(s){ var n = parseFloat(s.amount)||1, u=s.unit||'days'; if (u==='minutes') return n; if (u==='hours') return n*60; if (u==='days') return n*1440; return n*1440; } // Given a total delay in minutes from now, return the next-due value. // For sub-day delays we store a full ISO timestamp (so 10-min / 2-hr waits are exact). // For day+ delays we keep the legacy YYYY-MM-DD date string (noon ET) so day-scale // nurture behaves exactly as before. function spNextDueFromMinutes(mins){ mins = Math.max(0, mins||0); if (mins < 1440){ return new Date(Date.now() + Math.round(mins*60000)).toISOString(); // full timestamp } var days = Math.round(mins/1440); return spAddDaysStr(spTodayET(), Math.max(1, days)); // legacy date string } // Is a stored smartPlanNextDue value due now? Handles BOTH formats: // full ISO timestamp ("2026-07-06T23:10:00.000Z") and legacy date string ("2026-07-06"). function spIsDue(nextDue){ if (!nextDue) return true; var s = String(nextDue); if (s.indexOf('T') !== -1){ // full timestamp → compare to now var t = Date.parse(s); return isNaN(t) ? true : (t <= Date.now()); } return s <= spTodayET(); // legacy date string → date compare } async function spSendEmail(env, step, c, plan){ var RESEND_API_KEY = env.RESEND_API_KEY; if (!RESEND_API_KEY) return { ok:false, reason:'no Resend key' }; var to = (c.email||'').trim(); if (!to) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no email' }; function sub(t){ return spMergeBody(String(t==null?'':t), c); } var subject = sub(step.emailSubject||step.content||'A quick note'); var bodyHtml; if (step.emailHtml && String(step.emailHtml).trim()){ bodyHtml = sub(step.emailHtml); } else { var bodyText = sub(step.emailBody||''); var esc = bodyText.replace(/&/g,'&').replace(//g,'>').replace(/\n/g,'
'); bodyHtml = '

Reuses the exact same // enrollment endpoint as the manual "Add to NestTracker" (so ATTOM valuation, cap check, // anti-spam address gate, and billing stay identical). Auto path = live enroll, no preview. async function spEnrollNestTrackerAuto(c, plan, env, token, step){ step = step || {}; var owner = c.smartPlanOwner || (plan && plan.ownerAgentId) || ''; if (!owner) return { ok:false, reason:'no owner agent — cannot enroll' }; if (!c.uid) return { ok:false, reason:'no contact id' }; var addr = (c.mailingAddress || c.address || '').trim(); if (!addr) return { ok:false, reason:'contact has no mailing address — skipped' }; try { var tok = token || await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); // Dedup: already enrolled? } }; try { var cResp = await apiNestTrackerConfirm(confReq, env, { 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, { agentId: owner }); var cData = {}; try { cData = await cResp.json(); } catch(e){} if (cData && cData.ok) return { ok:true, reason:'enrolled + live in NestTracker' }; } } return { ok:true, reason:'enrolled in NestTracker' }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'NestTracker enroll error: '+e.message }; } } async function spFireStep(c, plan, step, env, creds, token){ var ch = step.channel || 'zapier'; // Native channels — send directly via the agent's own account if (ch === 'email'){ return await spSendEmail(env, step, c, plan); } if (ch === 'vm'){ return await spSendVM(creds||{}, step.audioName||step.content||'', c.phone||'', step.callerId||''); } if (ch === 'letter'){ return await spSendLetter((creds||{}).thanksioKey||'', { mailerType: step.mailerType||'letter', templateId: step.letterTemplateId||'', message: step.letterBody||step.content||'', imageTemplateId: step.imageTemplateId||'', frontImageUrl: step.frontImageUrl||'', messageTemplateId: step.messageTemplateId||step.letterTemplateId||'', size: step.postcardSize||'4x6' }, c); Queue the draft rather than firing at 2am or dropping it. return await spQueueTextTask(c, step, env, creds||{}, 'Quo held: outside sending hours (8am–8pm your time)'); } const msg = spMergeBody(step.smsBody||step.content||'', c); const r = await quoSendText({ quoApiKey: key, quoFrom: from }, c.phone||'', msg); if (r.ok) return { ok:true, reason:'sent via Quo' }; // Credits, registration, a bad key — whatever it was, the agent still gets the draft // so the touch is not simply lost. The reason rides along instead of going only to // console.log, where nobody outside a live tail will ever see it. return await spQueueTextTask(c, step, env, creds||{}, 'Quo did not send: ' + r.reason); } // Auto was chosen but the credentials never reached the runner — say so rather than // drafting silently and looking identical to a step the agent set to draft on purpose. return await spQueueTextTask(c, step, env, creds||{}, 'Quo not attempted: ' + (!key ? 'no API key on the agent record' : 'no sending number')); } return await spQueueTextTask(c, step, env, creds||{}); } // ── State-change action steps (change the contact, don't send a touch) ── if (ch === 'setstatus'){ var newStatus = (step.newStatus||'').trim(); var okStatuses = ['pipeline','active','nurture','cold','past','contact']; if (okStatuses.indexOf(newStatus) < 0) return { ok:false, reason:'invalid status "'+newStatus+'"' }; if (!c.uid) return { ok:false, reason:'no contact id' }; try { var tokS = token || await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); await fsPatchFields(tokS, 'users/'+c.uid, { crmStatus: fsString(newStatus) }, ['crmStatus']); return { ok:true, reason:'status → '+newStatus }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'status change error: '+e.message }; } } if (ch === 'setsegment'){ var newSeg = (step.newSegment||'').trim(); if (!c.uid) return { ok:false, reason:'no contact id' }; try { var tokG = token || await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); await fsPatchFields(tokG, 'users/'+c.uid, { segment: fsString(newSeg) }, ['segment']); return { ok:true, reason:'segment → '+(newSeg||'(cleared)') }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'segment change error: '+e.message }; } } if (ch === 'nesttracker'){ return await spEnrollNestTrackerAuto(c, plan, env, token, step); } // Zapier channels (text / generic) — fire the step's own webhook URL if set, // OTHERWISE log a smart-plan event for the native Zapier polling trigger. var webhookUrl = step.webhookUrl || ''; if (!webhookUrl) { // Native Zapier path: log an event Zapier can poll (replaces pasted webhooks). try { var _tok = token || await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); var _logged = await logSmartPlanEvent(_tok, c, plan, step); return _logged ? { ok:true, reason:'zapier event logged' } : { ok:false, reason:'could not log zapier event' }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'zapier event error: ' + e.message }; } } var payload = { event: 'smartplan_step', channel: ch, label: step.content || '', content: step.content || '', planName: plan.name || '', firstName: c.firstName || '', lastName: c.lastName || '', email: c.email || '', phone: c.phone || '', mailingAddress: c.mailingAddress || '', contactId: c.uid || '', agentId: c.smartPlanOwner || '' }; try { // fetch RESOLVES on 4xx/5xx. A Zapier hook URL dies when its Zap is turned off or rebuilt // and then returns 404/410 — without this check that reported 'webhook fired' and the step // was marked done having sent nothing. var _wh = await fetch(webhookUrl, { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }); if (_wh && !_wh.ok) return { ok:false, reason:'webhook rejected: HTTP ' + _wh.status }; return { ok:true, reason:'webhook fired' }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'webhook error: ' + e.message }; } } async function spWriteProgress(token, uid, c){ var url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/users/${uid}` + `?updateMask.fieldPaths=smartPlanStatus&updateMask.fieldPaths=smartPlanStep&updateMask.fieldPaths=smartPlanNextDue`; await fetch(url, { method:'PATCH', headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type':'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields: { smartPlanStatus: { stringValue: c.smartPlanStatus||'active' }, smartPlanStep: { integerValue: String(c.smartPlanStep||0) }, smartPlanNextDue:{ stringValue: c.smartPlanNextDue||spTodayET() } }}) }).catch(()=>{}); } async function spProcessContact(token, c, plan, today, env, creds, fireLog){ var steps = plan.steps || []; var idx = parseInt(c.smartPlanStep,10) || 0; var fired = 0; // Fire consecutive DO steps (no wait between them) in this pass while (idx < steps.length && steps[idx] && steps[idx].kind === 'do'){ var res = await spFireStep(c, plan, steps[idx], env, creds, token); if (res && res.ok) fired++; if (fireLog) fireLog.push({ contact:(c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||''), channel:steps[idx].channel||'', ok:!!(res&&res.ok), reason:(res&&res.reason)||'unknown' }); idx++; } // Now at a wait or end if (idx >= steps.length){ c.smartPlanStatus = 'completed'; c.smartPlanStep = idx; } else { // consume consecutive waits to compute next due time (real minutes, not day-rounded) var delayMin = 0; while (idx < steps.length && steps[idx] && steps[idx].kind === 'wait'){ delayMin += spWaitMinutes(steps[idx]); idx++; } if (idx >= steps.length){ c.smartPlanStatus = 'completed'; c.smartPlanStep = idx; } else { c.smartPlanNextDue = spNextDueFromMinutes(delayMin); c.smartPlanStep = idx; } } await spWriteProgress(token, c.uid, c); return fired; } async function campaignRunner(token, env){ var today = spTodayET(); var diag = { today: today, activeFound: 0, noPlanId: 0, notDue: 0, planMissing: 0, dueProcessed: 0, sample: [], fires: [] }; // 1. active enrollments var resp = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'users', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'smartPlanStatus'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:'active'} } } }); if (!Array.isArray(resp)) return { processed:0, fired:0, diag: { error:'active-query returned non-array (query failed or index missing)' } }; diag.activeFound = resp.length; // Build per-agent credential map (agentId -> slybroadcast/thanks.io creds) var agentResp = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'users', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'role'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:'agent'} } } }); var credsMap = {}; if (Array.isArray(agentResp)) for (const ar of agentResp){ if (!ar.document || !ar.document.fields) continue; var au = digDoc(ar.document.fields); if (!au.agentId) continue; credsMap[au.agentId] = { uid: ar.document.name.split('/').pop(), slyEmail: au.slyEmail||'', slyCallerId: au.slyCallerId||'', slyPass: au.slyPass || '', thanksioKey: au.thanksioKey || '', // Quo, for text steps set to send automatically. quoApiKey: au.quoApiKey || '', quoFrom: au.quoFrom || '', // The agent's own time zone, for the campaign-text sending window. timeZone: au.timeZone || '' }; } // process due contacts (native VM/letter use agent creds; text/zapier use per-step webhook) var planCache = {}; var processed = 0, fired = 0; for (const r of resp){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; var c = digDoc(r.document.fields); c.uid = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); if (diag.sample.length < 5) diag.sample.push({ name:(c.firstName||'')+' '+(c.lastName||''), plan:c.smartPlanId||'(none)', status:c.smartPlanStatus||'', due:c.smartPlanNextDue||'', owner:c.smartPlanOwner||'', step:c.smartPlanStep }); if (!c.smartPlanId || c.smartPlanStatus !== 'active'){ diag.noPlanId++; continue; } if (!spIsDue(c.smartPlanNextDue)){ diag.notDue++; continue; } // not due yet var owner = c.smartPlanOwner; if (!owner) { continue; } // no owner → skip; never default to a person var key = owner + '/' + c.smartPlanId; var plan = planCache[key]; if (plan === undefined){ plan = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agents/'+owner+'/smartPlans/'+c.smartPlanId); planCache[key] = plan; } if (!plan || !plan.steps){ diag.planMissing++; continue; } // plan deleted → leave enrollment as-is diag.dueProcessed++; fired += await spProcessContact(token, c, plan, today, env, credsMap[owner]||{}, diag.fires); processed++; if (processed >= 40) break; // per-run cap — stay under the Worker subrequest limit; the rest roll to the next cron tick (runs every minute) } return { processed, fired, diag }; } async function handleWeeklyDigest(env, token, diagnostic){ const RESEND_API_KEY = env.RESEND_API_KEY; const _diag = { agents:0, agentsWithEmail:0, deals:0, activeDeals:0, sent:0, sentTo:[], skipped:[], activeList:[] }; if (!RESEND_API_KEY) { if(diagnostic) return Object.assign(_diag,{error:'no RESEND_API_KEY'}); return; } const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0); // 1) All agent users → map agentId -> { email, optOut, name } const agentsResp = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId:'users', allDescendants:false }], where: { fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'role'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:'agent'} } } }); _diag.agents++; if (u.email) _diag.agentsWithEmail++; } // 2) All deals (collection group) → group by agentId const dealsResp = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'deals', allDescendants:true }] }); // onboarding sample data — never a real transaction if (!d.contractDate) continue; // only under-contract deals have tasks // A deal can have a contractDate but have since fallen back to market (step dropped). Require the // CURRENT step to still be in the under-contract range — not just that a contractDate once existed. const _step = (d.step != null) ? d.step : 0; const _isSeller = d.dealType === 'seller'; const underContract = _isSeller ? (_step >= 9 && _step < 13) : (_step >= 5 && _step < 11); if (!underContract) continue; // back on market / pipeline / closed → no tasks const aid = d.agentId; if (!aid) continue; // no owner → do nothing. NEVER fall back to a person. (byAgent[aid] = byAgent[aid] || []).push(d); if (diagnostic) _diag.activeList.push({ addr: d.address || d.clientName || '(no address)', agentId: aid, step: _step, type: d.dealType||'buyer', contractDate: d.contractDate||'', path: (r.document.name||'').split('/documents/').pop(), clientUid: d.clientUid||'' }); _diag.activeDeals++; } // 3) Build + send each agent's digest for (const aid in byAgent){ const agent = agentMap[aid]; if (!agent || !agent.email || agent.optOut) { _diag.skipped.push(aid + (!agent?' (no agent record)':(!agent.email?' (no email)':' (opted out)'))); continue; } const blocks = []; for (const deal of byAgent[aid]){ const overdue=[], now=[], soon=[]; for (const t of DIGEST_TASKS){ const s = digTaskStatus(deal, t, today); if (s==='overdue') overdue.push(t.label); else if (s==='now') now.push(t.label); else if (s==='upcoming'){ const st=t.start(deal); if (st && (st-today)/86400000 <= 7) soon.push(t.label); } } if (!overdue.length && !now.length && !soon.length) continue; const addr = deal.address || (deal.clientName||'Deal'); const li = (arr,color)=> arr.length ? `

` : ''; blocks.push(`
${addr}
${ overdue.length ? `

`+li(now,'#065F46') : '' } ${ soon.length ? `
This week
`+li(soon,'#6B7280') : '' }
`); } if (!blocks.length) { _diag.skipped.push(aid + ' (no actionable tasks)'); continue; } // nothing actionable → don't send a blank digest const html = `

${blocks.join('')} Open Nest Track →

Logged loudly, because a silent failure // here is indistinguishable from working. console.log('obe TRACK FAILED - will resend tomorrow:', due.key, e && e.message); } if(sent >= 60) break; // pace Resend per run } else if (resp) { let why = ''; try { why = (await resp.text()).slice(0, 160); } catch(e) {} console.log('obe send rejected', resp.status, due.key, why); } } console.log('onboarding drip sent:', sent); } function _obeVideoBlock(url){ // Convert a Loom/YouTube share link into a clickable thumbnail (email clients block iframes). let thumb='', link=url; const yt = String(url).match(/(?:v=|youtu\.be\/|embed\/)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{11})/); if(yt){ thumb='https://i.ytimg.com/vi/'+yt[1]+'/hqdefault.jpg'; } return '' + (thumb ? \u2014 Tom"} }; // ── Maintenance reminders ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Once a day: find homes with a task due, push the homeowner one task. Never a digest, never a // daily nag — if they ignore it the task sits overdue in the app and we nudge again a fortnight // later. The push carries the filter size when we know it, because "change your furnace filter · // 16x25x1" is something you can go do, and "change your furnace filter" is something you look up // later and forget. That difference is the whole product. var MAINT_PUSH_CAP = 40; // stay under the Worker subrequest limit; the rest catch tomorrow // OneSignal is keyed on the FIREBASE AUTH uid \u2014 the app calls OneSignal.login(currentUser.uid). // But callers here pass a client DOCUMENT id, and for agent-created contacts those differ: // the doc is contact_1779137702533_gnko5 while the auth uid is stored on the record as // portalUid. Optional // token: without it, or if the lookup fails, behaviour is exactly what it was before. async function _pushResolveUid(token, uid) { if (!token || !uid) return uid; try { const d = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + uid); if (d && d.portalUid && String(d.portalUid).trim()) return String(d.portalUid).trim(); } catch (e) {} return uid; } async function _pushClient(env, uid, heading, content, opts){ const key = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; if (!key || !uid) return false; opts = opts || {}; try { if (opts.token) uid = await _pushResolveUid(opts.token, uid); const url = opts.url || 'https://app.nesttrack.io'; const _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, uid); const r = await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization': `Key ${key}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e', include_aliases: { external_id: [uid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: heading }, contents: { en: content }, web_url: url, data: { targetUrl: url } }) }); return !!(r && r.ok); } catch(e){ return false; } } // Which tasks earn an email. 'none' means email but no pros block — // an anniversary note with "here's who I'd call" attached would read strangely. var MAINT_EMAIL_KEYS = { hvac_service:'HVAC', gutters:'Gutters', water_heater:'Plumbing', insurance:'Insurance', home_anniversary:'none' }; // Read an agent's Preferred Partners for one category (agent_partners/{agentId}, {partners:[...]}). async function _maintPartnersFor(token, agentId, cat){ if (!agentId || !cat) return []; try { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agent_partners/'+agentId); let list = []; try { list = (doc && doc.partners) ? 'Hey '+esc(d.clientFirst)+' \u2014' : 'Hey \u2014'; // A friendly line per task, in Tom's voice — like a text from someone who happens to be your // agent, not a task manager. The renewal date is inferred from the closing date, so this reads // as a heads-up to go check rather than a claim about their actual policy. insurance: 'your home insurance should be coming up for renewal in about a month. Worth a look at the rate before it auto-renews.', home_anniversary: 'hard to believe how fast it goes \u2014 happy anniversary in the house.' }; var line = LINES[d.key] || ('quick reminder \u2014 it\u2019s about time to ' + esc(String(d.title||'').replace(/^Have your /,'').replace(/^Flush your /,'flush your ').toLowerCase()) + '.'); // Agent's own opening line from the editor overrides the default. It already includes their // greeting, so it becomes the whole top line rather than getting a second "Hey" prepended. var opener = d.customBody ? esc(d.customBody) : (hi + ' ' + line); var pros=''; if (d.key === 'home_anniversary'){ pros = ''; // an anniversary note does not come with a list of contractors } else if (d.partners && d.partners.length){ pros = '

Here\u2019s who I\u2019d call \u2014 people I trust and use myself:
'; d.partners.slice(0,3).forEach(function(p,i){ pros += '

'+esc(p.note)+'

'; }); pros += '

Need a good one? Just reply to this \u2014 happy to point you to someone I trust.
'; } // Only pitch the in-app reminders to someone who isn't already using them. An app user gets the // DIY pushes already — telling them to "set up the little stuff" reads like you forgot they have it. var appNote = d.appUser ? '' : ('

'); var unsub = 'turn off these reminders'; // Same reason as the app's wrapper: no declared charset means the client guesses, and every // em dash and curly quote in this email arrives as mojibake. return '' + '' + '

'+opener+'

Once it\u2019s handled, you can ' + 'check it off in the app so it\u2019s on record for you \u2014 or just ignore this, I\u2019ll remind you again down the road.

' + '
'; } async function _maintSendEmail(env, token, home, t, agent){ if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY || !home.email) return false; const cat = MAINT_EMAIL_KEYS[t.key]; const partners = await _maintPartnersFor(token, home.agentId, cat); // Filter size to advertise the in-app reminders, if we captured one. let filterSize = ''; try { const items = home.itemsJson ? } catch(e){ console.log('maintenance query:', e && e.message); return { pushed:0 }; } let pushed = 0, skipped = 0, emailed = 0, realigned = 0; for (const r of (rows||[])){ if (pushed >= MAINT_PUSH_CAP) break; continue; } let tasks = []; try { tasks = JSON.parse(h.tasksJson); } catch(e){ continue; } // Fires ONCE per occurrence, and only if it is actually current. A missed cron run should still send; a missed season should not. let _lateDays = 0; const _dueMs2 = Date.parse(String(t.nextDue) + 'T12:00:00Z'); if (!isNaN(_dueMs2)) _lateDays = Math.floor((Date.parse(today + 'T12:00:00Z') - _dueMs2) / 86400000); const _stale = _lateDays > STALE_AFTER_DAYS; if (!_stale){ const body = (t.spec ? t.spec + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + 'Tap to mark it done.'; // Push and email are independent. A device with no push token must not cost the client // their email, which is what happened when a failed push skipped the rest of the block. // Pass the token so the auth uid can be resolved \u2014 without it these reached nobody // for any client whose document id differs from their auth uid. try { await _pushClient(env, h.contactUid, t.title, body, { token: token }); return { pushed, skipped, emailed, realigned }; } async function handleScheduled(env) { const ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY = env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY; const ONESIGNAL_APP_ID = 'effa5e11-e85f-4b00-bf87-1f6d415ff51e'; } catch(e){ console.log('maintenance reminder error:', e && e.message); } } } const now = new Date(); // Format to YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM for comparison const nowStr = now.getFullYear()+'-' +String(now.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-' +String(now.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')+'T' +String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':' +String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'); // Query all agents for unfired reminders // We use a collection group query via REST const queryUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents:runQuery`;
