for (const result of results) { if (!result.document) continue; const fields = result.document.fields; if (!fields) continue; const reminderTime = fields.reminderTime?.stringValue || ''; const uid = fields.uid?.stringValue || ''; const text = fields.text?.stringValue || 'Reminder'; const docName = result.document.name; // Check if this reminder is due (matches current minute) if (reminderTime.substring(0, 16) !== nowStr) continue; // Fire push const _bdg = await _badgeNext(env, uid); await fetch('https://api.onesignal.com/notifications?c=push', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Key ${ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY}` }, body: JSON.stringify({ app_id: ONESIGNAL_APP_ID, include_aliases: { external_id: [uid] }, target_channel: 'push', ios_badgeType: 'SetTo', ios_badgeCount: _bdg, headings: { en: ' To-Do Reminder' }, contents: { en: text }, web_url: 'https://app.nesttrack.io', data: { targetUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io' } }) }).catch(() => {}); // Mark as fired await fetch(`https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/${docName}?updateMask.fieldPaths=fired`, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ fields: { fired: { booleanValue: true } } }) }).catch(() => {}); } } catch(e) { console.log('Scheduled reminder error:', e.message); } // Clean up team chat messages older than 60 days try { const RTDB_URL = 'https://buyers-880bc-default-rtdb.firebaseio.com'; const cutoff = Date.now() - (60 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); // Get all messages const rtdbResp = await fetch(`${RTDB_URL}/chats/team_chat/messages.json?auth=${env.FIREBASE_RTDB_SECRET||''}`); if (rtdbResp.ok) { const messages = await rtdbResp.json(); if (messages) { const toDelete = Object.entries(messages).filter(([k,v]) => v && v.ts && v.ts < cutoff); for (const [key] of toDelete) { await fetch(`${RTDB_URL}/chats/team_chat/messages/${key}.json?auth=${env.FIREBASE_RTDB_SECRET||''}`, { method: 'DELETE' }).catch(() => {}); } if (toDelete.length > 0) console.log(`Deleted ${toDelete.length} old team chat messages`); } } } catch(e) { console.log('Chat cleanup error:', e.message); } } // ── Free, code-only lead-email parser (no API cost). Pulls name/email/phone/address out of a // forwarded lead email across common formats. Whatever it can't cleanly find is left blank; // the full readable text is kept in notes so the agent never loses context. ── function _decodeEmailBody(raw) { let s = String(raw || ''); // If a base64 body part is present, decode the first one. const b64m = s.match(/Content-Transfer-Encoding:\s*base64[\s\S]*?\r?\n\r?\n([A-Za-z0-9+/=\r\n]{40,})/i); if (b64m) { try { s = (typeof atob === 'function') ? atob(b64m[1].replace(/\s+/g, '')) : s; } catch (e) {} } // Decode quoted-printable (=\n soft breaks and =XX hex). s = s.replace(/=\r?\n/g, '').replace(/=([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})/g, function (_, h) { try { return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)); } catch (e) { return _; } }); // HTML → text: keep line breaks, drop tags, decode a few entities. s = s.replace(/<\s*br\s*\/?>/gi, '\n').replace(/<\/(p|div|tr|li|h[1-6]|table)>/gi, '\n').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' '); s = s.replace(/ /gi, ' ').replace(/&/gi, '&').replace(/</gi, '<').replace(/>/gi, '>').replace(/'/g, "'").replace(/’/gi, "'").replace(/"/gi, '"'); return s; } // ── LIST-EMAIL INGEST (CSV link in the body) ──────────────────────────────── // Some lead sources (Landvoice, etc.) don't put the lead in the email — they send a link to a CSV // holding the whole day's list. Detect that link, fetch it, and import every row instead of trying // to parse one lead out of the body. Auto-detected: no link → normal single-lead path. // Pull the FIRST CSV download link out of an email body. Landvoice ships the same URL twice (one // per MIME part, with slightly different query styles), so we take one and ignore the rest. function _findListCsvUrl(raw){ if (!raw) return ''; const s = String(raw); // Landvoice: /api/{fsbo|expired|preforeclosure|...}/listing/csv?...&tok=... const m = s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]*\/api\/[a-z0-9_-]+\/listing\/csv\?[^\s"'<>]+/i); if (m && m[0]) return m[0].replace(/&/gi, '&').replace(/[.,;)\]]+$/, ''); return ''; } // RFC4180-ish CSV parser: handles quoted fields, embedded commas, doubled quotes, CRLF. function _parseCsv(text){ const rows = []; let row = [], field = '', inQ = false; const s = String(text || ''); for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++){ const c = s[i]; if (inQ){ if (c === '"'){ if (s[i+1] === '"'){ field += '"'; i++; } else inQ = false; } else field += c; } else { if (c === '"') inQ = true; else if (c === ','){ row.push(field); field = ''; } else if (c === '\r'){ /* skip */ } else if (c === '\n'){ row.push(field); rows.push(row); row = []; field = ''; } else field += c; } } if (field !== '' || row.length){ row.push(field); rows.push(row); } return rows.filter(function(r){ return r.length && r.some(function(v){ return String(v).trim() !== ''; }); }); } // Map one CSV row (Landvoice column names) → the lead shape the inbound pipeline already speaks. // Returns null for rows we deliberately skip (already sold). function _mapListRow(get){ const status = (get('Status from MLS') || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (status === 'sold' || status === 'closed') return null; // never solicit someone who already sold const street = (get('Property Street') || '').trim(); const city = (get('Property City') || '').trim(); const st = (get('Property State') || '').trim(); const zip = (get('Property Zip') || '').trim(); const addr = [street, [city, st].filter(Boolean).join(', '), zip].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim(); // Landvoice often has no seller name. Fall back to the street so the list is readable. let firstName = '', lastName = ''; const seller = (get('Seller') || '').trim(); if (seller){ const p = seller.split(/\s+/); firstName = p.shift() || ''; lastName = p.join(' '); } else { firstName = street || 'FSBO Lead'; } const phone = (get('Phone 1') || get('Phone 2') || '').trim(); const price = (get('Price') || '').trim(); const beds = (get('Bedrooms') || '').trim(); const sqft = (get('Square Feet') || '').trim(); const adTxt = (get('Ad Text') || '').trim(); const dnc = (get('Phone 1 Do Not Call') || '').trim(); // Ad Text already states price/beds/sqft. The only thing worth appending is a DNC flag, which is // the one field the agent genuinely needs surfaced before they pick up the phone. const notes = [adTxt.replace(/\$\$+/g, '$'), dnc ? ('⚠️ DO NOT CALL flag from source: ' + dnc) : ''].filter(Boolean).join('\n'); return { externalId: (get('LandvoiceID') || '').trim(), // stable dedupe key across daily sends firstName: firstName, lastName: lastName, email: (get('EMail') || '').trim(), phone: phone, address: addr, notes: notes, leadType: 'seller', // FSBO/expired sellers are seller-side leads dnc: !!dnc }; } function parseLeadEmail(raw, excludeEmail) { if (!raw) return null; const text = _decodeEmailBody(raw); const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/).map(function (l) { return l.replace(/[ \t]+/g, ' ').trim(); }).filter(function (l) { return l.length; }); const flat = lines.join('\n'); const out = { firstName: '', lastName: '', email: '', phone: '', address: '', leadType: '', source: '', notes: '' }; const label = function (re) { const m = flat.match(re); return m ? String(m[1]).trim() : ''; }; // EMAIL — first real-looking address that isn't the platform's own no-reply. const _excl = String(excludeEmail || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const emails = flat.match(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi) || []; const badEmail = /(no-?reply|noreply|notification|do-?not-?reply|mailer|@zillow|@clever|@realtor|@homelight|@trulia|@redfin|@mail\.|resend\.app)/i; out.email = (emails.find(function (e) { return !badEmail.test(e) && e.toLowerCase() !== _excl; }) || '').trim(); // PHONE — labeled first, else first phone-shaped token; validate 10-11 digits. let phone = label(/Phone(?:\s*Number)?\s*[:\-]\s*([+()0-9.\-\s]{7,}\d)/i); if (!phone) { const pm = flat.match(/(\+?\d[\d().\-\s]{8,}\d)/); phone = pm ? pm[1] : ''; } let pdig = phone.replace(/\D/g, ''); if (pdig.length === 11 && pdig[0] === '1') pdig = pdig.slice(1); out.phone = (pdig.length === 10) ? ('(' + pdig.slice(0, 3) + ') ' + pdig.slice(3, 6) + '-' + pdig.slice(6)) : ''; // ADDRESS / LEAD TYPE — labeled. out.address = label(/Address\s*[:\-]\s*(.+)/i); const lt = label(/Lead\s*Type[^:\-]*[:\-]\s*(buyer|seller|both)/i).toLowerCase(); out.leadType = (lt === 'buyer' || lt === 'seller' || lt === 'both') ? lt : ''; // NAME — several strategies, easy → hard. let name = label(/^([A-Z][A-Za-z.'\-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z.'\-]+){0,2})'s\s+Info/im); // "Anthony Iskandar's Info" if (!name) name = label(/\bName\s*[:\-]\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z.'\- ]{1,50})/i); // "Name: ..." if (!name) name = label(/Lead\s*(?:Receipt|for)\s*[:\-]\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z.'\- ]{1,50})/i); // "Lead Receipt: ..." if (!name) { // "new connection from..." → next name-like line for (let i = 0; i < lines.length && !name; i++) { if (/new (connection|lead|contact)/i.test(lines[i]) || /premier agent/i.test(lines[i])) { for (let j = i + 1; j < Math.min(i + 7, lines.length); j++) { const c = lines[j]; if (/^[A-Z][A-Za-z'.\-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z'.\-]+){0,2}$/.test(c) && c.length <= 40 && !/zillow|clever|inquiry|property|follow|connection|premier|agent|message/i.test(c)) { name = c; break; } } } } } name = name.trim(); if (name) { const parts = name.split(/\s+/); out.firstName = parts.shift() || ''; out.lastName = parts.join(' '); } // SOURCE — detect the platform. if (/zillow/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Zillow'; else if (/clever/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Clever'; else if (/realtor\.com/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Realtor.com'; else if (/homelight/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'HomeLight'; else if (/redfin/i.test(flat)) out.source = 'Redfin'; // NOTES — keep the readable text so the agent sees the full inquiry. out.notes = lines.slice(0, 70).join('\n').slice(0, 1800); return out; } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ZAPIER API LAYER (/api/v1/*) // Native Zapier integration endpoints. Auth = an agent's lead_token passed as // `Authorization: Bearer {token}` (same tokens used by /lead/{token}). This lets // Zapier: poll for new contacts (trigger), create contacts (action), and poll // for smart-plan step events (trigger) — replacing per-step pasted webhooks. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function _apiCors() { return { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': ALLOWED_ORIGIN, 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS', 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, Authorization, X-Api-Key' }; } // Pull the bearer token (or X-Api-Key) off the request. function _apiToken(request) { const auth = request.headers.get('Authorization') || ''; if (/^Bearer\s+/i.test(auth)) return auth.replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, '').trim(); const k = request.headers.get('X-Api-Key'); if (k) return k.trim(); // also allow ?api_key= for easy browser testing try { const u = new URL(request.url); const q = u.searchParams.get('api_key'); if (q) return q.trim(); } catch (e) {} return ''; } // Resolve an API token → { agentId, teamId }. Returns null if invalid. async function _apiResolveToken(fbToken, apiKey) { if (!apiKey) return null; try { const tok = await fsGetDoc(fbToken, 'lead_tokens/' + apiKey); if (tok && tok.agentId) { let teamId = tok.teamId || ''; try { const sub = await fsGetDoc(fbToken, 'push_subscriptions/agent_' + tok.agentId); if (sub && sub.teamId) teamId = sub.teamId; } catch (e) {} return { agentId: tok.agentId, teamId: teamId }; } } catch (e) {} return null; } // GET /api/v1/auth-test → verifies the key, returns who it belongs to. async function apiAuthTest(request, env, cors) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, agentId: who.agentId, teamId: who.teamId, label: 'NestTrack (' + who.agentId + ')' }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // GET /api/v1/contacts?since={ISO or ms}&limit= → recent contacts for this agent. // Zapier polls this for the "New Contact" trigger (newest first; Zapier dedupes by id). async function apiListContacts(request, env, cors) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); let limit = 25; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const l = parseInt(u.searchParams.get('limit'), 10); if (l > 0 && l <= 100) limit = l; } catch (e) {} // Query users where agentId == who.agentId AND isContact == true. No orderBy // (avoids composite index) — we sort by createdAt in JS and cap. let rows; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } }, { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'isContact' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { booleanValue: true } } } ] } }, limit: 300 }); } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'query failed', detail: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return new Response(JSON.stringify([]), { status: 200, headers: cors }); const out = []; for (const r of rows) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); const id = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); out.push({ id: id, firstName: d.firstName || '', lastName: d.lastName || '', name: ((d.firstName || '') + ' ' + (d.lastName || '')).trim(), email: d.email || '', phone: d.phone || '', address: d.mailingAddress || '', source: d.source || '', segment: d.segment || '', status: d.crmStatus || '', type: d.journeyType || d.role || '', notes: d.notes || '', createdAt: d.createdAt || '', createdAtMs: d.createdAt ? Date.parse(d.createdAt) || 0 : 0 }); } out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs; }); // Optional ?since filter (ISO date or ms timestamp) let sinceMs = 0; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const s = u.searchParams.get('since'); if (s) sinceMs = /^\d+$/.test(s) ? parseInt(s, 10) : (Date.parse(s) || 0); } catch (e) {} const filtered = sinceMs ? out.filter(function (c) { return c.createdAtMs > sinceMs; }) : out; return new Response(JSON.stringify(filtered.slice(0, limit)), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // GET /api/v1/smartplan-events?since={ms}&limit= → recent smart-plan step events // for this agent. Zapier polls this for the "Smart Plan Step Reached" trigger. async function apiListSmartPlanEvents(request, env, cors) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); let limit = 25; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const l = parseInt(u.searchParams.get('limit'), 10); if (l > 0 && l <= 100) limit = l; } catch (e) {} let rows; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'zapier_events', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } }, // A structured query with no orderBy is ordered by document NAME ascending, and the names // are zev__ — so lexical order is chronological and this cap takes the // OLDEST n events, never the newest. At 200 the window froze mid-batch on 2026-10-26: // every event written after that was unreachable, so Zapier polled, saw nothing new and // left. Steps fired, texts never sent, no error anywhere. // Deliberately NOT fixed with an orderBy: that needs a composite index on // (agentId, createdAtMs), and a missing composite index fails as "Missing or insufficient // permissions" — indistinguishable from a rules denial, and it would read as zero events, // i.e. silently kill every text instead of fixing them. An equality filter on a single // field uses the automatic single-field index, so raising the cap needs no new index at // all. The daily purge (purge-zapier-events) keeps this collection far below the cap. limit: 1000 }); } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'query failed', detail: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return new Response(JSON.stringify([]), { status: 200, headers: cors }); const out = []; for (const r of rows) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); const id = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); out.push({ id: id, planName: d.planName || '', stepLabel: d.stepLabel || '', triggerId: d.triggerId || '', firstName: d.firstName || '', lastName: d.lastName || '', name: ((d.firstName || '') + ' ' + (d.lastName || '')).trim(), email: d.email || '', phone: d.phone || '', address: d.mailingAddress || '', contactId: d.contactId || '', createdAtMs: typeof d.createdAtMs === 'number' ? d.createdAtMs : (d.createdAt ? Date.parse(d.createdAt) || 0 : 0) }); } out.sort(function (a, b) { return b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs; }); let sinceMs = 0; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const s = u.searchParams.get('since'); if (s) sinceMs = /^\d+$/.test(s) ? parseInt(s, 10) : (Date.parse(s) || 0); } catch (e) {} let wantTrig = ''; try { const u = new URL(request.url); wantTrig = (u.searchParams.get('triggerId') || '').trim(); } catch (e) {} let filtered = sinceMs ? out.filter(function (e) { return e.createdAtMs > sinceMs; }) : out; if (wantTrig) filtered = filtered.filter(function (e) { return e.triggerId === wantTrig; }); return new Response(JSON.stringify(filtered.slice(0, limit)), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // GET /api/v1/deals?since={ms}&limit= → recent deals for this agent. // POST /api/v1/homebot-enroll -> the app calls this when an agent taps "Add to Homebot". // Writes a homebot_enrollments doc scoped to THIS agent. Zapier polls it (dedicated trigger). // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // NESTTRACKER — ATTOM AVM home valuation (Phase 1) // Core: given a split address, fetch ATTOM's AVM (value, range, confidence). // Endpoint: /propertyapi/v1.0.0/avm/detail, header `apikey`, params address1/address2. // Confidence: ATTOM returns avm.amount.scr (0–100). Gate below NESTTRACKER_MIN_CONFIDENCE. // ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── const NESTTRACKER_MIN_CONFIDENCE = 88; // tuned from Cleveland testing (good results 91–95; bad one 82) async function attomGetValue(env, parts) { const key = env.ATTOM_API_KEY; if (!key) return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM_API_KEY not configured on the server' }; const street = (parts && parts.street || '').trim(); const cityStateZip = [ (parts && parts.city || '').trim(), [ (parts && parts.state || '').trim(), (parts && parts.zip || '').trim() ].filter(Boolean).join(' ') ].filter(Boolean).join(', '); if (!street || !cityStateZip) return { ok:false, reason:'need a street and city/state/zip to value a home' }; const url = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/propertyapi/v1.0.0/avm/detail' + '?address1=' + encodeURIComponent(street) + '&address2=' + encodeURIComponent(cityStateZip); try { const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'apikey': key, 'accept': 'application/json' } }); if (!r.ok) { const t = await r.text(); // ATTOM 400 "SuccessWithoutResult" = well-formed request, no property matched that address. if (r.status === 400 && /SuccessWithoutResult|"total":0/i.test(t)){ return { ok:false, reason:"We couldn't find that exact address in the property database. Double-check the street number, spelling, and ZIP — or pick it from the address suggestions so it matches.", queried:{ address1: street, address2: cityStateZip } }; } return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM ' + r.status + ': ' + String(t).slice(0,120) }; } const d = await r.json(); const prop = (d.property && d.property[0]) || null; const amt = prop && prop.avm && prop.avm.amount; if (!amt || amt.value == null) { return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM has no valuation for this address', matched: !!prop }; } const confidence = (amt.scr != null) ? Number(amt.scr) : null; const calc = (prop.avm && prop.avm.calculations) || {}; const addr = (prop.address) || {}; return { ok: true, value: Number(amt.value), high: amt.high != null ? Number(amt.high) : null, low: amt.low != null ? Number(amt.low) : null, confidence: confidence, lowConfidence: (confidence != null && confidence < NESTTRACKER_MIN_CONFIDENCE), monthlyChangePct: (calc.monthlyChgPct != null) ? Number(calc.monthlyChgPct) : null, attomId: (prop.identifier && prop.identifier.attomId) || null, matchedAddress: addr.oneLine || null, raw: { value: amt.value, high: amt.high, low: amt.low, scr: amt.scr } }; } catch (e) { return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM request error: ' + (e && e.message || 'network') }; } } // ── Provider dispatcher: swapping AVM providers later is a config flip (env.AVM_PROVIDER) ── async function getHomeValue(env, parts) { const provider = String(env.AVM_PROVIDER || 'attom').toLowerCase(); // Future providers plug in here, each returning the same normalized shape: // if (provider === 'housecanary') return await housecanaryGetValue(env, parts); return await attomGetValue(env, parts); } // ── Free equity estimate: no API cost. Amortizes an assumed loan from purchase price + date. ── // Assumes 20% down, 30-yr fixed, rate by purchase year. Rough by design — labeled "estimated". function _rateForYear(y){ var t = { 2000:8.05,2001:6.97,2002:6.54,2003:5.83,2004:5.84,2005:5.87,2006:6.41,2007:6.34,2008:6.03,2009:5.04,2010:4.69,2011:4.45,2012:3.66,2013:3.98,2014:4.17,2015:3.85,2016:3.65,2017:3.99,2018:4.54,2019:3.94,2020:3.11,2021:2.96,2022:5.34,2023:6.81,2024:6.72,2025:6.80,2026:6.70 }; if (y && t[y]!=null) return t[y]/100; return 0.06; // fallback } // Remaining balance on an amortizing loan after `monthsPaid` of a `termMonths` loan at annual `rate`. // Standard amortization: B = P * [(1+i)^n - (1+i)^p] / [(1+i)^n - 1]. rate as a percent (e.g. 6.5). function _amortBalance(loanAmount, annualRatePct, termMonths, monthsPaid){ loanAmount = Number(loanAmount)||0; termMonths = Number(termMonths)||360; monthsPaid = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(monthsPaid)||0, termMonths)); if (!loanAmount) return 0; var i = (Number(annualRatePct)||0)/100/12; var bal; if (i > 0){ bal = loanAmount * (Math.pow(1+i, termMonths) - Math.pow(1+i, monthsPaid)) / (Math.pow(1+i, termMonths) - 1); } else { bal = loanAmount * (1 - monthsPaid/termMonths); // 0% → straight-line } return Math.max(0, Math.round(bal)); } // Months between a start date (ISO/date string or year) and now. function _monthsSince(startStr){ if (!startStr) return 0; var d = null; if (/^\d{4}$/.test(String(startStr).trim())) d = new Date(Number(startStr), 0, 1); else { d = new Date(startStr); } if (!d || isNaN(d.getTime())) return 0; return Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.now() - d.getTime()) / (30.44*86400000))); } function estimateEquity(currentValue, purchasePrice, purchaseDate, loan){ currentValue = Number(currentValue)||0; loan = loan || {}; // Real loan data path (agent/lender entered loan amount + rate + term): amortize precisely. var loanAmount = Number(loan.loanAmount)||0; if (currentValue && loanAmount){ var term = (String(loan.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; var start = loan.loanStart || loan.purchaseDate || purchaseDate || ''; var monthsPaid = _monthsSince(start); var ratePct; if (loan.ownerRate!=null && String(loan.ownerRate)!==''){ ratePct = Number(loan.ownerRate); // agent entered a percent, e.g. 6.5 } else { var yr = null; if (start){ var sd = /^\d{4}$/.test(String(start).trim()) ? new Date(Number(start),0,1) : new Date(start); if(sd && !isNaN(sd.getTime())) yr = sd.getFullYear(); } ratePct = (_rateForYear(yr)||0.06) * 100; // _rateForYear returns a decimal → percent } var bal = _amortBalance(loanAmount, ratePct, term, monthsPaid); var eq = Math.max(0, Math.round(currentValue - bal)); return { estimated: false, fromLoanData: true, estLoanBalance: bal, equity: eq, equityPct: currentValue ? Math.round((eq/currentValue)*100) : null, loanAmount: loanAmount, loanTerm: (term===180?'15':'30'), ownerRate: (ratePct!=null?Math.round(ratePct*1000)/1000:null), loanStart: start || null }; } // Fallback estimate (no loan data): assume 20% down, 30-yr, rate-by-year — unchanged legacy behavior. purchasePrice = Number(purchasePrice)||0; if (!currentValue || !purchasePrice) return null; var pd = purchaseDate ? new Date(purchaseDate) : null; var monthsElapsed = 0, year = null; if (pd && !isNaN(pd.getTime())) { year = pd.getFullYear(); monthsElapsed = Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.now() - pd.getTime()) / (30.44*86400000))); } var loanF = purchasePrice * 0.80; var balance = _amortBalance(loanF, (_rateForYear(year)||0)*100, 360, monthsElapsed); var equity = Math.max(0, Math.round(currentValue - balance)); return { estimated: true, estLoanBalance: balance, equity: equity, equityPct: currentValue ? Math.round((equity/currentValue)*100) : null, assumedDownPct: 20, basedOn: { purchasePrice: purchasePrice, purchaseYear: year } }; } // ── Equity insight ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Turns the raw equity numbers into the two things a homeowner actually cares about: // 1. growth — how much of this year's gain came from the market vs. paying the loan down // 2. hook — the single most useful thing worth a conversation about these numbers // Pure and synchronous. The emailed quarterly report and the in-app home dashboard both // render from this, so they can never disagree with each other. function _monthlyPayment(principal, annualRatePct, termMonths){ principal = Number(principal)||0; termMonths = Number(termMonths)||0; var i = (Number(annualRatePct)||0)/100/12; if (!principal || termMonths <= 0) return 0; if (i === 0) return principal/termMonths; var f = Math.pow(1+i, termMonths); return principal * i * f / (f - 1); } // Newest stored value at or before N months ago. Returns null when no valuation that old exists, // so the caller can relabel rather than quietly measure from the purchase price and call it a year. function _valueMonthsAgo(history, months){ if (!Array.isArray(history) || !history.length) return null; var cutoff = Date.now() - months*30.44*86400000; var best = null, bestT = 0; for (var i=0;i cutoff) continue; if (t > bestT){ best = h; bestT = t; } } return best ? (Number(best.value)||null) : null; } var PMI_ANNUAL_PCT = 0.005; // ~0.5%/yr of the original loan — typical; always label it "around" var PMI_EQUITY_PCT = 20; // where asking the lender to drop PMI becomes worth doing var PMI_CLOSE_PCT = 15; // close enough that it's worth telling them it's coming var REFI_MIN_DELTA = 0.75; // percentage points of rate improvement before it's worth a call function equityInsight(eq, home, history, currentValue, rate30){ if (!eq || !currentValue) return null; home = home || {}; // These are stored as free text ('$375,000'). Number() on that is NaN, which silently nulls // downPct and makes the PMI hook unreachable. Strip to digits, as the report endpoint does. var _n = function(v){ return Number(String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) || 0; }; var purchasePrice = _n(home.purchasePrice); var loanAmount = _n(home.loanAmount); var termMonths = (String(eq.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; var monthsOwned = _monthsSince(eq.loanStart || home.purchaseDate || ''); // Growth split. We only say "this year" when there's a real valuation from a year ago to // measure against. Otherwise the honest frame is "since you bought". var basis = 'purchase', windowMonths = Math.max(0, monthsOwned), thenValue = purchasePrice; if (monthsOwned >= 12){ var yearAgo = _valueMonthsAgo(history, 12); if (yearAgo){ basis = 'year'; windowMonths = 12; thenValue = yearAgo; } } var appreciation = (thenValue && currentValue) ? Math.round(currentValue - thenValue) : 0; var principalPaid = 0; if (eq.fromLoanData && loanAmount && monthsOwned > 0){ var balThen = _amortBalance(loanAmount, eq.ownerRate, termMonths, Math.max(0, monthsOwned - windowMonths)); principalPaid = Math.max(0, Math.round(balThen - eq.estLoanBalance)); } var growth = { basis:basis, months:windowMonths, appreciation:appreciation, principalPaid:principalPaid, total:appreciation + principalPaid }; // The hook. First one that fires wins — they're ordered by how much money is on the table. // PMI and refi are gated on real loan data; we never make a financial claim off an assumption. var equityPct = Number(eq.equityPct)||0; var downPct = (purchasePrice && loanAmount) ? Math.round(((purchasePrice - loanAmount)/purchasePrice)*100) : null; var real = !!eq.fromLoanData; var hook; if (real && downPct!=null && downPct < 20 && equityPct >= PMI_EQUITY_PCT){ hook = { type:'pmi_ready', monthly: Math.round((loanAmount*PMI_ANNUAL_PCT)/12), downPct:downPct, equityPct:equityPct }; } else if (real && downPct!=null && downPct < 20 && equityPct >= PMI_CLOSE_PCT){ hook = { type:'pmi_close', away: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentValue*(PMI_EQUITY_PCT/100) - eq.equity)), downPct:downPct, equityPct:equityPct }; } else if (real && rate30 && eq.ownerRate && (eq.ownerRate - rate30) >= REFI_MIN_DELTA){ var remaining = Math.max(1, termMonths - monthsOwned); var save = _monthlyPayment(eq.estLoanBalance, eq.ownerRate, remaining) - _monthlyPayment(eq.estLoanBalance, rate30, remaining); hook = { type:'refi', monthly: Math.max(0, Math.round(save)), yourRate: eq.ownerRate, todayRate: rate30 }; } else if (equityPct >= 35){ hook = { type:'equity_strong', equityPct:equityPct, equity:eq.equity }; } else { hook = { type:'baseline', equityPct:equityPct }; } return { growth:growth, hook:hook, monthsOwned:monthsOwned, downPct:downPct, estimated: !real }; } // ── Home maintenance ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Six defaults, not a checklist. Every one is a starting point the homeowner can re-time or // switch off in a single tap, and off means off. Completing a task writes a log entry and // reschedules from the day it was actually done — never from the day the calendar guessed. // The log is the product: eleven taps a year becomes a maintenance record nobody set out to keep. var NT_TASKS = [ { key:'furnace_filter', title:'Change your furnace filter', everyDays:90, seedDays:30, askSpec:true }, { key:'hvac_service', title:'Have your HVAC serviced', everyDays:182, seasonal:true, pro:'HVAC' }, { key:'water_filter', title:'Change your water filter', everyDays:182, seedDays:90, askSpec:true }, { key:'smoke_co', title:'Change your smoke and CO detector batteries', everyDays:182, seedDays:180 }, { key:'gutters', title:'Have your gutters cleaned', everyDays:182, seasonal:true, pro:'Gutters' }, { key:'water_heater', title:'Flush your water heater', everyDays:1095, seedDays:180, pro:'Plumbing' }, // Anniversary-anchored: timed off the closing date, not the calendar. Skipped entirely // when we only have a purchase YEAR, because the renewal day would be a guess. { key:'insurance', title:'Home insurance renewal', everyDays:365, anniversary:true, offsetDays:-30, pro:'Insurance' }, { key:'home_anniversary', title:'Home anniversary', everyDays:365, anniversary:true, offsetDays:0 } ]; function _ymd(ms){ return new Date(ms).toISOString().slice(0,10); } // Seasonal work lands in spring or fall, whichever comes first, never sooner than a month out. // (Spring/fall are national approximations. A climate-zone table would sharpen this later.) function _nextSeasonal(fromMs){ var floor = fromMs + 30*86400000; var y = new Date(floor).getUTCFullYear(); var cands = [Date.UTC(y,3,15), Date.UTC(y,9,15), Date.UTC(y+1,3,15)]; for (var i=0;i= floor) return _ymd(cands[i]); } return _ymd(cands[2]); } // The schedule starts when the schedule starts. Seeding from the purchase date looked right for a // fresh close, but a past client enrolled years later would wake up four years overdue on every // task and get pushed about all of them at once. // Given an anchor month (0-11) and a frequency in days, find the next occurrence of that month at // least 30 days out. For sub-yearly frequencies (6mo) the anchor month plus the interval naturally // gives the pair — Oct + 6mo lands Apr, then Oct again. // Next occurrence of the closing month/day, at least a week out, shifted by offsetDays // (-30 for the insurance heads-up, 0 for the anniversary itself). Returns null when the // stored date is a bare year or unparseable — the caller then skips the task entirely. function _nextAnniversary(dateStr, offsetDays){ var s = String(dateStr||'').trim(); if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return null; var d = new Date(s.slice(0,10) + 'T12:00:00Z'); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return null; var mo = d.getUTCMonth(), day = d.getUTCDate(); var floor = Date.now() + 7*86400000; var y0 = new Date(floor).getUTCFullYear(); for (var i=0; i<3; i++){ var c = Date.UTC(y0+i, mo, day) + (Number(offsetDays)||0)*86400000; if (c >= floor) return _ymd(c); } return null; } // Whole years since closing, for {{YEARS}} in the anniversary copy. function _yearsOwned(dateStr){ var s = String(dateStr||'').trim(); var d = /^\d{4}$/.test(s) ? new Date(Number(s),0,1) : new Date(s.slice(0,10)+'T12:00:00Z'); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return 0; return Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - d.getTime())/(365.25*86400000))); } function _yearsWord(n){ var w = ['zero','one','two','three','four','five','six','seven','eight','nine','ten']; return (n <= 10 ? w[n] : String(n)) + ' year' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's'); } function _nextAnchoredMonth(fromMs, month, everyDays){ var floor = fromMs + 30*86400000; // yearly-or-longer: land on the anchor month; sub-yearly: nearest upcoming anchor, interval handles the rest var y = new Date(floor).getUTCFullYear(); var cands = [Date.UTC(y, month, 15), Date.UTC(y+1, month, 15), Date.UTC(y+2, month, 15)]; for (var i=0;i= floor) return _ymd(cands[i]); } return _ymd(cands[0]); } // settings: optional per-agent map { hvac_service:{months,freqDays,off}, gutters:{...}, water_heater:{...} }. // Absent or malformed → behave exactly as before. This runs at enrollment; a bad settings read must // never produce an empty schedule, so every branch falls back to the global task default. function ntSeedTasks(settings, purchaseDate){ settings = settings || {}; var base = Date.now(); var out = []; NT_TASKS.forEach(function(t){ var s = settings[t.key] || null; // Anniversary tasks: one path, no month or seasonal logic. No usable closing date, // no task — a guessed renewal date in a client's inbox is worse than no email. if (t.anniversary){ if (s && s.off === true) return; var due = _nextAnniversary(purchaseDate, t.offsetDays); if (!due) return; out.push({ key:t.key, title:t.title, everyDays:365, enabled:true, spec:'', pro:t.pro||'', askSpec:false, anniversary:true, offsetDays:(t.offsetDays||0), nextDue:due }); return; } // Only the three big-ticket tasks are agent-configurable; the DIY ones ignore settings entirely. var configurable = !!MAINT_EMAIL_KEYS[t.key]; if (configurable && s){ if (s.off === true) return; // agent turned this reminder off var every = (Number(s.freqDays) > 0) ? Number(s.freqDays) : t.everyDays; var month = (s.month != null && s.month >= 0 && s.month <= 11) ? Number(s.month) : null; out.push({ key:t.key, title:t.title, everyDays:every, enabled:true, spec:'', pro:t.pro||'', askSpec:!!t.askSpec, nextDue: (month != null) ? _nextAnchoredMonth(base, month, every) : (t.seasonal ? _nextSeasonal(base) : _ymd(base + (t.seedDays||30)*86400000)) }); return; } // Default (untouched agent, or non-configurable DIY task) — exactly as before. out.push({ key:t.key, title:t.title, everyDays:t.everyDays, enabled:true, spec:'', pro:t.pro||'', askSpec:!!t.askSpec, nextDue: t.seasonal ? _nextSeasonal(base) : _ymd(base + (t.seedDays||30)*86400000) }); }); return out; } var NT_TASK_BY_KEY = {}; NT_TASKS.forEach(function(t){ NT_TASK_BY_KEY[t.key] = t; }); // Load an agent's per-task maintenance settings (month + frequency + off) from their config doc. // Any failure returns {} → seeding falls back to global defaults. Never throws into the seed path. async function _ntMaintSettings(token, agentId){ if (!agentId) return {}; try { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agents/'+agentId+'/config/maintSchedule'); if (!doc) return {}; var out = {}; ['hvac_service','gutters','water_heater','insurance','home_anniversary'].forEach(function(k){ var v = doc[k]; if (!v) return; out[k] = { off: v.off === true, month: (v.month != null ? Number(v.month) : null), freqDays: (v.freqDays != null ? Number(v.freqDays) : null) }; }); return out; } catch(e){ return {}; } } // The per-home token is the auth, exactly as it is for the report. async function _ntLoadHome(env, tok){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok); if (!home || home.active === false) return { token:token, home:null }; var tasks=[], log=[], items=[]; try { tasks = home.tasksJson ? JSON.parse(home.tasksJson) : []; } catch(e){} try { log = home.logJson ? JSON.parse(home.logJson) : []; } catch(e){} try { items = home.itemsJson ? JSON.parse(home.itemsJson) : []; } catch(e){} return { token:token, home:home, tasks:tasks, log:log, items:items }; } // GET /api/v1/home-tasks?t=TOKEN → PUBLIC. Returns everything the home dashboard needs in one call. async function apiHomeTasks(request, env, cors){ let tok=''; try { tok = (new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('t')||'').trim(); } catch(e){} if (!tok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing token'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const s = await _ntLoadHome(env, tok); if (!s.home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); const on = (s.home.homeDash !== false); // The homeowner is looking at their report right now. Recorded but not awaited — tracking // must never delay or break the thing it is measuring. // Send history, so the agent can see when the last report went and when the next is due. // Both derive from lastPulledAt, which the quarterly sweep already maintains — nothing new // to store, it simply was not being handed back. let _lastSent = null, _nextDue = null; try { const lp = s.home.lastPulledAt; const ms = lp && lp.seconds ? lp.seconds * 1000 : (lp ? Date.parse(lp) : 0); if (ms) { _lastSent = new Date(ms).toISOString(); _nextDue = new Date(ms + 89*24*60*60*1000).toISOString(); } } catch(e) {} return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, homeDash:on, tasks:(on ? s.tasks : []), log:s.log, items:s.items, lastSentAt:_lastSent, nextDueAt:_nextDue, views:(Number(s.home.views)||0) }),{status:200,headers:cors}); } // Deliberately arithmetic, not AI. Every input is a fact the homeowner produced, and the // reasons are shown alongside the score \u2014 an agent should be able to see why someone surfaced // and disagree with it. A black-box number nobody trusts gets ignored within a week. function _ntScore(home) { let score = 0; const why = []; let recent = []; try { recent = JSON.parse(home.recentViewsJson || '[]'); } catch (e) {} const totalViews = Number(home.viewCount) || 0; const today = Math.floor(Date.now() / 86400000); const last7 = recent.filter(function (d) { return d > today - 7; }).length; const last30 = recent.length; // Repeat visits in a short window are the strongest passive signal. if (last7 >= 3) { score += 40; why.push('opened their home value report ' + last7 + ' times this week'); } else if (last7 === 2) { score += 25; why.push('opened their home value report twice this week'); } else if (last7 === 1) { score += 10; why.push('opened their home value report this week'); } else if (last30 >= 3) { score += 15; why.push('opened their home value report ' + last30 + ' times this month'); } else if (last30 >= 1) { score += 5; why.push('opened their home value report ' + (last30 > 1 ? last30 + ' times ' : '') + 'this month'); } // They told you something. Worth more than any inference. if (home.lastIntent) { const i = String(home.lastIntent).toLowerCase(); if (/ready|now|net sheet/.test(i)) { score += 45; why.push(home.lastIntent); } else if (/soon|1.?2|6.?12/.test(i)) { score += 30; why.push(home.lastIntent); } else { score += 10; why.push(home.lastIntent); } } // Equity is what makes a move possible. A large jump is a reason to call today. const eq = Number(home.estEquity) || 0; const eqPrev = Number(home.estEquityPrev) || 0; if (eq >= 150000) { score += 15; why.push('$' + Math.round(eq / 1000) + 'k equity'); } if (eqPrev && eq > eqPrev * 1.1) { score += 20; why.push('equity up $' + Math.round((eq - eqPrev) / 1000) + 'k since last report'); } // Seven years is roughly when people start looking, and the median seller has now been in // place eleven. Weak on its own; useful stacked with anything else. const yrs = Number(home.yearsOwned) || 0; if (yrs >= 7) { score += 10; why.push(yrs + ' years in the home'); } // Never opened it. Not a seller signal \u2014 a re-engagement one, and worth separating. if (!recent.length && totalViews === 0) { return { score: 0, why: ['never opened a report'], cold: true }; } return { score: Math.min(score, 100), why: why, cold: false }; } // GET /api/v1/nt-signals \u2014 an agent's enrolled homeowners, ranked by how likely they look to // be thinking about moving. This is the piece Fello charges for: the reports themselves have // existed here for months, what was missing is the ranked "who to call" list on top. // // Scoring runs server-side so the app fetches a short ordered list rather than every home and // its history. An agent with 400 enrolled homeowners should get eight names, not 400 records. function _srNotFoundHtml(msg) { return '' + '
' + '
\uD83D\uDCC4
' + '

This report is no longer available

' + '

' + (msg || 'Reports are kept while the home is on the market and removed once the sale closes. Ask your agent for the latest one.') + '

' + '
'; } // Banner above the stored report. It is immutable by design \u2014 the numbers were true on the day // it was sent, and quietly refreshing them would undermine the point of a weekly report. function _srDatedHtml(inner, sentDateStr, address) { let when = ''; try { if (sentDateStr) { const d = new Date(sentDateStr + 'T12:00:00'); if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) { when = d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } } } catch (e) {} const banner = '
' + '
' + (when ? 'This update was sent ' + when + '' : 'This is a saved update') + (address ? ' for ' + String(address).replace(/[<>]/g, '') : '') + '.
It shows the numbers as they were that day, not today.' + '
'; return '' + '' + '' + 'Listing Update' + '' + banner + inner + ''; } // \u2500\u2500 MCP OAuth \u2500\u2500 // Claude's connector flow always attempts OAuth Dynamic Client Registration, even against a // server that declares no authentication - there is no "authless" option in its UI. So the // server has to offer the OAuth it expects. // // Done properly rather than rubber-stamped, because the point is other agents connecting their // own Claude to their own NestTrack. A token has to identify one agent and reach only their // data; an approval that handed back a shared token would work today and be a breach the // moment a second person used it. const MCP_ORIGIN = 'https://app.tom-b52.workers.dev'; // The address agents connect to from Claude's directory. The worker answers on both; each // request is told about the address it actually arrived on, so connections made on the // older one keep working. Only these two are ever named - the host is never echoed back. const MCP_PUBLIC_HOST = 'mcp.nesttrack.io'; function _mcpOrigin(request) { try { if (new URL(request.url).hostname.toLowerCase() === MCP_PUBLIC_HOST) return 'https://' + MCP_PUBLIC_HOST; } catch (e) {} return MCP_ORIGIN; } const MCP_APP_ORIGIN = 'https://app.nesttrack.io'; // Only Claude's own callbacks. Taking the redirect from the request would let a stolen client // id send an authorization code somewhere else. const MCP_REDIRECT_URIS = [ 'https://claude.ai/api/mcp/auth_callback', 'https://claude.com/api/mcp/auth_callback' ]; function _oauthJson(obj, status) { return new Response(JSON.stringify(obj), { status: status || 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } }); } // RFC 9728 - which authorization server guards this resource. function _oauthProtectedResource(request) { const MCP_ORIGIN = _mcpOrigin(request); return _oauthJson({ resource: MCP_ORIGIN + '/mcp', authorization_servers: [MCP_ORIGIN], scopes_supported: ['nesttrack'], bearer_methods_supported: ['header'], resource_documentation: MCP_APP_ORIGIN }); } // RFC 8414 - advertises the endpoints below. function _oauthAuthServerMeta(request) { const MCP_ORIGIN = _mcpOrigin(request); return _oauthJson({ issuer: MCP_ORIGIN, authorization_endpoint: MCP_ORIGIN + '/oauth/authorize', token_endpoint: MCP_ORIGIN + '/oauth/token', registration_endpoint: MCP_ORIGIN + '/oauth/register', revocation_endpoint: MCP_ORIGIN + '/oauth/revoke', scopes_supported: ['nesttrack'], response_types_supported: ['code'], grant_types_supported: ['authorization_code', 'refresh_token'], token_endpoint_auth_methods_supported: ['none'], // S256 only. Claude sends PKCE, and requiring it means an intercepted code is useless // without the verifier. code_challenge_methods_supported: ['S256'] }); } function _mcpRandomId(prefix) { const a = 'abcdefghijkmnpqrstuvwxyz0123456789'; let out = ''; const buf = new Uint8Array(32); crypto.getRandomValues(buf); for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) out += a[buf[i] % a.length]; return (prefix || '') + out; } // Tokens are stored hashed - a leaked database read should not hand over working credentials. async function _mcpHash(value) { const data = new TextEncoder().encode(String(value)); const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data); return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(function (b) { return b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'); }).join(''); } // Sends the agent to a consent page in the app rather than showing one here. They are already // signed in at app.nesttrack.io, the page looks like NestTrack, and no NestTrack password ever // touches a workers.dev domain - which is what a phishing page would look like. async function _oauthAuthorize(request, env) { const u = new URL(request.url); const p = u.searchParams; const clientId = p.get('client_id') || ''; const redirectUri = p.get('redirect_uri') || ''; const state = p.get('state') || ''; const challenge = p.get('code_challenge') || ''; const method = p.get('code_challenge_method') || ''; if (MCP_REDIRECT_URIS.indexOf(redirectUri) === -1) { return new Response('Unrecognised redirect_uri.', { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain' } }); } // Without PKCE an intercepted code could be exchanged by anyone who saw it. if (!challenge || method !== 'S256') { return _oauthRedirectError(redirectUri, state, 'invalid_request', 'PKCE with S256 is required.'); } // The pending request is stored server-side so nothing sensitive rides in the URL the agent // is about to be shown. const reqId = _mcpRandomId('mcpr_'); try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); await firestoreWrite(token, 'mcp_requests/' + reqId, { clientId: _fsStr(clientId), redirectUri: _fsStr(redirectUri), state: _fsStr(state), codeChallenge: _fsStr(challenge), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(10 * 60 * 1000) }); } catch (e) { console.log('oauth authorize:', e && e.message); return _oauthRedirectError(redirectUri, state, 'server_error', 'Could not start authorization.'); } return Response.redirect(MCP_APP_ORIGIN + '/connect.html?req=' + encodeURIComponent(reqId), 302); } function _oauthRedirectError(redirectUri, state, code, desc) { const url = redirectUri + '?error=' + encodeURIComponent(code) + '&error_description=' + encodeURIComponent(desc || '') + (state ? '&state=' + encodeURIComponent(state) : ''); return Response.redirect(url, 302); } // The consent page posts the agent's Firebase ID token here once they approve. Verifying it is // what binds the authorization to one real NestTrack account - everything downstream depends // on this being the identity the rest of the platform already trusts. async function _oauthConfirm(request, env) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const reqId = String(body.req || ''); const idToken = String(body.idToken || ''); if (!reqId || !idToken) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_request' }, 400); const uid = await _verifyFirebaseIdToken(idToken); if (!uid) return _oauthJson({ error: 'access_denied', error_description: 'Sign-in could not be verified.' }, 401); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const pending = await fsGetDoc(token, 'mcp_requests/' + reqId).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!pending) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_request', error_description: 'That request expired.' }, 400); const exp = _mcpTsMs(pending.expiresAt); if (exp && Date.now() > exp) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_request', error_description: 'That request expired.' }, 400); // Single-use, ten minutes. Deleted the moment it is exchanged. const code = _mcpRandomId('mcpa_'); await firestoreWrite(token, 'mcp_codes/' + (await _mcpHash(code)), { uid: _fsStr(uid), clientId: _fsStr(pending.clientId || ''), codeChallenge: _fsStr(pending.codeChallenge || ''), redirectUri: _fsStr(pending.redirectUri || ''), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(10 * 60 * 1000) }); // Single use: the pending request is gone the moment it becomes a code. try { await fetch('https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/mcp_requests/' + reqId, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); } catch (e) {} const back = (pending.redirectUri || MCP_REDIRECT_URIS[0]) + '?code=' + encodeURIComponent(code) + (pending.state ? '&state=' + encodeURIComponent(pending.state) : ''); return _oauthJson({ ok: true, redirect: back }); } // Google publishes the certificates for its ID tokens. Verifying the signature here is what // stops anyone posting a made-up uid to the confirm endpoint. // Verified by Google rather than by hand. Parsing X.509 certificates inside a Worker to check // a signature is fragile, and when it goes wrong it fails silently for everyone — this asks the // identity service the token came from, which is the same answer with none of the guesswork. async function _verifyFirebaseIdToken(idToken) { try { const r = await fetch('https://identitytoolkit.googleapis.com/v1/accounts:lookup?key=' + FIREBASE_WEB_API_KEY, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ idToken: String(idToken) }) }); if (!r.ok) return null; const d = await r.json(); const u = d && d.users && d.users[0]; if (!u || !u.localId) return null; return u.localId; } catch (e) { console.log('verify id token:', e && e.message); return null; } } // Exchanges a code for a token, or refreshes one. The PKCE verifier is checked here: without // it a code intercepted in transit would be enough to gain access. async function _oauthToken(request, env) { let form = {}; try { const raw = await request.text(); // Claude posts form-encoded. Accepting JSON too costs nothing and a token endpoint that // only speaks one of them is a documented cause of failed reviews. if (raw.trim().charAt(0) === '{') form = JSON.parse(raw); else new URLSearchParams(raw).forEach(function (v, k) { form[k] = v; }); } catch (e) {} const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const grant = form.grant_type || ''; if (grant === 'authorization_code') { const code = String(form.code || ''); const verifier = String(form.code_verifier || ''); if (!code || !verifier) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_request' }, 400); const key = await _mcpHash(code); const rec = await fsGetDoc(token, 'mcp_codes/' + key).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!rec) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_grant', error_description: 'Code not recognised.' }, 400); const exp = _mcpTsMs(rec.expiresAt); if (exp && Date.now() > exp) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_grant', error_description: 'Code expired.' }, 400); // S256: base64url(sha256(verifier)) must equal the challenge sent at /authorize. const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(verifier)); const computed = btoa(String.fromCharCode.apply(null, Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)))) .replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, ''); if (computed !== (rec.codeChallenge || '')) { return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_grant', error_description: 'PKCE verification failed.' }, 400); } // Codes are single use. Burn it before issuing anything. try { await fetch('https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/mcp_codes/' + key, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); } catch (e) {} return await _oauthIssue(token, rec.uid, rec.clientId || ''); } if (grant === 'refresh_token') { const rt = String(form.refresh_token || ''); if (!rt) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_request' }, 400); const key = await _mcpHash(rt); const rec = await fsGetDoc(token, 'mcp_tokens/' + key).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!rec || rec.kind !== 'refresh') return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_grant' }, 400); const exp = _mcpTsMs(rec.expiresAt); if (exp && Date.now() > exp) return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_grant', error_description: 'Refresh token expired.' }, 400); return await _oauthIssue(token, rec.uid, rec.clientId || ''); } return _oauthJson({ error: 'unsupported_grant_type' }, 400); } // Access tokens last 30 days, refresh 90. Short enough that a leaked token stops working, // long enough that an agent is not reconnecting every week. async function _oauthIssue(token, uid, clientId) { const access = _mcpRandomId('mcpt_'); const refresh = _mcpRandomId('mcpr_'); const ACCESS_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; const REFRESH_MS = 90 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; await firestoreWrite(token, 'mcp_tokens/' + (await _mcpHash(access)), { uid: _fsStr(uid), clientId: _fsStr(clientId), kind: _fsStr('access'), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(ACCESS_MS) }); await firestoreWrite(token, 'mcp_tokens/' + (await _mcpHash(refresh)), { uid: _fsStr(uid), clientId: _fsStr(clientId), kind: _fsStr('refresh'), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(REFRESH_MS) }); return _oauthJson({ access_token: access, token_type: 'Bearer', expires_in: Math.floor(ACCESS_MS / 1000), refresh_token: refresh, scope: 'nesttrack' }); } // A bearer token resolves to one agent. Everything the tools return is filtered by their id, // so this is the single point where a token becomes an identity. async function _mcpWhoFromBearer(request, env) { const auth = request.headers.get('Authorization') || ''; const m = auth.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i); if (!m) return null; const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const rec = await fsGetDoc(token, 'mcp_tokens/' + (await _mcpHash(m[1].trim()))).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!rec || rec.kind !== 'access') return null; // Read as ISO text, which is how Firestore's REST API returns a timestamp. The old check // looked for ".seconds" and so never saw an expiry at all. const exp = _mcpTsMs(rec.expiresAt); if (exp && Date.now() > exp) return null; // The token identifies a NestTrack user; their profile says which tenant they are. const profile = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + rec.uid).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!profile) return null; // Agent and lender accounts only. A client's own record carries their agent's id as well, // so without this a client who signed in could have read that agent's whole client list. const role = String(profile.role || '').toLowerCase(); if (role === 'buyer' || role === 'seller' || role === 'contact' || profile.isContact === true) return null; const agentId = profile.agentId || profile.lenderId || ''; if (!agentId) return null; return { uid: rec.uid, agentId: agentId, fsToken: token, role: role, timeZone: profile.timeZone || '', name: ((profile.firstName || '') + ' ' + (profile.lastName || '')).trim(), email: profile.email || '' }; } // RFC 7591. Claude registers itself before first connection; record what it said and hand back // an id. Public client, no secret, because Claude uses PKCE. async function _oauthRegister(request, env) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const redirects = Array.isArray(body.redirect_uris) ? body.redirect_uris : []; const bad = redirects.filter(function (u) { return MCP_REDIRECT_URIS.indexOf(u) === -1; }); if (redirects.length && bad.length) { return _oauthJson({ error: 'invalid_redirect_uri', error_description: 'Only Claude callback URLs are accepted.' }, 400); } const clientId = _mcpRandomId('mcpc_'); try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); await firestoreWrite(token, 'mcp_clients/' + clientId, { clientId: _fsStr(clientId), clientName: _fsStr(body.client_name || 'Claude'), redirectUris: fsStrArr(redirects.length ? redirects : MCP_REDIRECT_URIS), createdAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch (e) { console.log('oauth register:', e && e.message); return _oauthJson({ error: 'server_error' }, 500); } return _oauthJson({ client_id: clientId, client_id_issued_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000), redirect_uris: redirects.length ? redirects : MCP_REDIRECT_URIS, grant_types: ['authorization_code', 'refresh_token'], response_types: ['code'], token_endpoint_auth_method: 'none', client_name: body.client_name || 'Claude', scope: 'nesttrack' }, 201); } // \u2500\u2500 MCP server \u2500\u2500 // Lets an assistant read the working state of an agent's business \u2014 to-dos, deals, the day's // appointments, follow-ups \u2014 alongside their inbox and calendar, so a morning briefing can say // "you have three deals closing this week and nobody has called the Kowalskis in nine days" // rather than just reading out email. // // Streamable HTTP transport (JSON-RPC over a single POST endpoint), which is what Claude's // custom connectors expect. Anthropic's cloud makes the connection, not the local machine, so // the endpoint is public and the token in the path is what protects it. // // Reads are broad. Writes are limited to the agent's own working records: contacts, notes, // to-dos and appointments. Nothing here sends anything to a client — no email, no text, no // push. An appointment made through the connector is saved with the client email switched // off; the agent sends that from NestTrack if they want it sent. // // Every tool carries a title and says whether it only reads or makes changes, which is what // Claude's directory requires. Descriptions say what the tool does and nothing else. function _mcpAnn(title, kind) { return { title: title, readOnlyHint: kind === 'read', destructiveHint: kind === 'delete', idempotentHint: kind === 'read', openWorldHint: false }; } const MCP_APPT_TYPES = 'Listing Appointment, Buyer Consultation, Showing, Final Walkthrough, Signing, Inspection, Appraisal, Listing Photography, Open House, Call, Meeting'; const MCP_TOOLS = [ { name: 'get_todos', title: 'Get to-dos', annotations: _mcpAnn('Get to-dos', 'read'), description: "Returns the agent's to-do list, newest first, with each item's id, due date, time and linked client.", inputSchema: { type: 'object', properties: { include_done: { type: 'boolean', description: 'Include completed items.
