Omit when the video is not on the plan.' }, part: { type: 'string', description: "Which part to return: 'guide' (the steps; the default), 'engine_1' or 'engine_2' (the editing engine files the guide asks for), or 'scenes' (animated scenes, only when asked for)." } } } } ]; // Firestore needs every value typed. The app writes this array through the client SDK, which // does the encoding for you; from the worker it has to be explicit or the whole list is lost. // // Every field on every item is written back as it was read. An earlier version rebuilt each // item from a fixed list of nine fields, so adding one to-do through the connector stripped // the transaction-coordinator, appointment and reminder fields from every other item. function _fsAny(v) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return { nullValue: null }; if (typeof v === 'boolean') return { booleanValue: v }; if (typeof v === 'number') { if (!isFinite(v)) return { nullValue: null }; return Number.isInteger(v) ? { integerValue: String(v) } : { doubleValue: v }; } if (typeof v === 'string') return { stringValue: v }; if (Array.isArray(v)) return { arrayValue: { values: v.map(_fsAny) } }; if (typeof v === 'object') { const f = {}; for (const k in v) { if (v[k] !== undefined) f[k] = _fsAny(v[k]); } return { mapValue: { fields: f } }; } return { stringValue: String(v) }; } function _mcpTodoArray(items) { return { arrayValue: { values: (items || []).map(function (t) { return _fsAny(t || {}); }) } }; } const MCP_CORS = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*', 'Access-Control-Expose-Headers': 'Mcp-Session-Id, MCP-Protocol-Version' }; function _mcpResult(id, result, sessionId) { const h = Object.assign({}, MCP_CORS); // The 2025-11-25 transport tightened session rules: the client expects an id it can send // back on later requests. This server is stateless, so the value only has to be stable. if (sessionId) h['Mcp-Session-Id'] = sessionId; return new Response(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: id, result: result }), { status: 200, headers: h }); } function _mcpError(id, code, message) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: id, error: { code: code, message: message } }), { status: 200, headers: MCP_CORS }); } // Tool results are text blocks. JSON keeps them unambiguous \u2014 prose would invite the model to // re-derive numbers it was handed. function _mcpText(obj) { // The editing method is long prose and code; it goes out as it is, not wrapped in JSON. if (obj && typeof obj.__text === 'string') return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: obj.__text }] }; return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2) }] }; } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SOCIAL ACCOUNTS — Facebook Pages + Instagram (Meta) // // An agent connects their own Facebook Page, and the Instagram professional account linked // to it, through Meta's own sign-in. NestTrack never sees their Facebook password. // // What comes back from Meta is a set of access keys. Those are the sensitive part: whoever // holds one can post to that Page. So they are encrypted before they are stored, with a key // that only exists in this worker's secrets, and they are never sent to the browser. The app // is only ever told names: which Page, which Instagram account. // // Step 1 (this block): connect, show what is connected, choose a Page, disconnect, and the // data-deletion endpoints Meta requires of any app that uses its sign-in. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const META_APP_ID = '1176443448115567'; // public: it identifies the app and grants nothing const META_SOCIAL_VERSION = 'v26.0'; // the pixel code further up has its own, older, version constant const META_GRAPH = 'https://graph.facebook.com/' + META_SOCIAL_VERSION; const META_DIALOG = 'https://www.facebook.com/' + META_SOCIAL_VERSION + '/dialog/oauth'; // Where Meta sends the agent back to. It has to be listed, character for character, in the // Meta app under Facebook Login for Business > Settings > Valid OAuth Redirect URIs. const SOCIAL_ORIGIN = 'https://mcp.nesttrack.io'; const META_REDIRECT_URI = SOCIAL_ORIGIN + '/social/meta/callback'; const SOCIAL_APP_URL = 'https://app.nesttrack.io/'; // Only what posting needs: see the agent's Pages, and publish to the Page and to Instagram. // business_management is there for one reason: a Page that sits inside a business portfolio, // which is most agents' business Page, is left out of the list Meta returns without it. const META_SCOPES = 'pages_show_list,pages_read_engagement,pages_manage_posts,business_management'; function _socialJson(obj, status, cors) { return new Response(JSON.stringify(obj), { status: status || 200, headers: cors || _apiCors() }); } function _socialEsc(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&', '<': '<', '>': '>', '"': '"', "'": ''' }[c]; }); } function _socialRand(bytes) { const a = new Uint8Array(bytes || 24); crypto.getRandomValues(a); let s = ''; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += ('0' + a[i].toString(16)).slice(-2); return s; } function _socialB64(bytes) { let s = ''; for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]); return btoa(s); } function _socialUnB64(str) { const bin = atob(String(str || '').replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')); const out = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) out[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i); return out; } // The access keys are encrypted with AES-GCM. The key is derived from the Meta app secret, so // there is one secret to look after rather than two, and a copy of the database on its own is // not enough to post to anyone's Page. async function _socialKey(env) { const secret = String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim(); if (!secret) throw new Error('social not configured'); const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode('nesttrack-social-v1|' + secret)); return await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', digest, { name: 'AES-GCM' }, false, ['encrypt', 'decrypt']); } async function _socialEncrypt(env, obj) { const key = await _socialKey(env); const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12)); const ct = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: iv }, key, new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(obj)))); const all = new Uint8Array(iv.length + ct.length); all.set(iv, 0); all.set(ct, iv.length); return 'v1.' + _socialB64(all); } async function _socialDecrypt(env, str) { const s = String(str || ''); if (s.indexOf('v1.') !== 0) return null; try { const all = _socialUnB64(s.slice(3)); const key = await _socialKey(env); const pt = await crypto.subtle.decrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: all.slice(0, 12) }, key, all.slice(12)); return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(pt)); } catch (e) { return null; } } // One call to Meta. Its errors arrive as JSON with a message meant for developers; the message // is kept for the log and a plain one is thrown for the person. The access key is never logged. async function _metaCall(method, path, params) { const qs = new URLSearchParams(params || {}).toString(); // A POST carries its values in the body: a caption can be long, and an access key has no // business sitting in a URL when it does not have to. const isPost = String(method || 'GET').toUpperCase() === 'POST'; const url = (path.indexOf('https://') === 0 ? path : META_GRAPH + path) + (!isPost && qs ? '?' + qs : ''); let r, j = null; try { r = await fetch(url, isPost ? { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: qs } : { method: method || 'GET' }); } catch (e) { throw new Error('Meta could not be reached. Try again in a minute.'); } try { j = await r.json(); } catch (e) {} if (!r.ok || (j && j.error)) { // Instagram's sign-in endpoints report errors in a flatter shape than the rest of Meta. const m = (j && j.error && (j.error.error_user_msg || j.error.message)) || (j && (j.error_message || j.error_description)) || ('HTTP ' + r.status); console.log('meta ' + (method || 'GET') + ' ' + String(path).split('?')[0] + ' failed: ' + m); const err = new Error(String(m)); err.metaCode = j && j.error && j.error.code; throw err; } return j || {}; } // A small standalone page for the moments the agent is outside the app: coming back from // Meta's sign-in, or checking on a deletion request. function _socialPage(title, lines, ok) { const body = (lines || []).map(function (l) { return '

' + l + '

'; }).join(''); const html = '' + '' + _socialEsc(title) + ' — NestTrack' + '' + '
' + (ok ? '✓' : '!') + '

' + _socialEsc(title) + '

' + body + 'Return to NestTrack
'; return new Response(html, { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } }); } // The app-facing endpoints identify the agent the same way the rest of /api/v1 does. async function _socialWho(request, env) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(token, _apiToken(request)); if (!who || !who.agentId || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{1,150}$/.test(String(who.agentId))) return null; return { agentId: String(who.agentId), token: token }; } // What the app is allowed to know about a connection: names, never keys. function _socialPublicMeta(doc) { if (!doc || !doc.metaEnc) return { connected: false }; const pages = (Array.isArray(doc.metaPages) ? doc.metaPages : []).map(function (p) { return { id: String(p.id || ''), name: p.name || '', canPost: p.canPost !== false, instagram: p.igId ? { id: String(p.igId), username: p.igUsername || '' } : null }; }); const expMs = Number(doc.metaTokenExpiresAt || 0); return { connected: true, account: doc.metaUserName || '', pages: pages, selectedPageId: String(doc.metaSelectedPageId || ''), connectedAt: Number(doc.metaConnectedAt || 0) || null, // Meta's sign-in lasts about 60 days. Flag it a week out so the agent is not surprised. reconnectBy: expMs || null, needsReconnect: !!(expMs && Date.now() > expMs - 7 * 86400000), missing: Array.isArray(doc.metaMissingScopes) ? doc.metaMissingScopes : [] }; } // While Meta reviews the app, Social is shown to the account made for its reviewer as well as // to the platform owner. SOCIAL_REVIEW_EMAILS lists that account's sign-in email (commas // between several). It is removed again once the app is approved and Social is public. async function _socialIsReviewer(env, w) { const list = String(env.SOCIAL_REVIEW_EMAILS || '').toLowerCase().split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); if (!list.length) return false; try { const a = await _ntResolveAgent(w.token, w.agentId); return !!(a && a.email && list.indexOf(String(a.email).trim().toLowerCase()) !== -1); } catch (e) { return false; } } // GET /api/v1/social/status async function apiSocialStatus(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); const configured = !!String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim(); let doc = null; try { doc = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId); } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not check your social accounts just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } return _socialJson({ ok: true, // Until Meta approves the app, only people with a role on it can connect. SOCIAL_PUBLIC // is switched on once that approval lands, and the card then shows for every agent. public: String(env.SOCIAL_PUBLIC || '') === '1' || await _socialIsReviewer(env, w), // Every connected account, each one a channel a post can go to. channels: configured ? _socialChannels(doc) : [], facebook: { configured: configured }, instagram: { configured: _igConfigured(env) }, // audited: TikTok has finished approving the app, so posts are no longer private-only. tiktok: { configured: _ttConfigured(env), audited: String(env.TIKTOK_AUDITED || '') === '1' }, youtube: { configured: _ytConfigured(env) }, storage: !!env.MEDIA, schedule: _socialSchedulePublic(doc), meta: Object.assign({ configured: configured }, configured ? _socialPublicMeta(doc) : { connected: false }) }, 200, cors); } // POST /api/v1/social/meta/start -> { url } to send the agent to Meta's sign-in async function apiSocialMetaStart(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim()) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Social posting is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); } // The state value ties the trip to Meta and back to this agent, and is good for one use // within ten minutes. Only its hash is stored. const state = _socialRand(24); await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_oauth_states/' + await _mcpHash(state), { agentId: w.agentId, provider: 'meta' }, { createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(10 * 60 * 1000) }); const p = new URLSearchParams({ client_id: String(env.META_APP_ID || META_APP_ID), redirect_uri: META_REDIRECT_URI, state: state, response_type: 'code' }); // Facebook Login for Business prefers a saved configuration over a list of permissions. // Either works; the configuration id is used when one has been set. const cfg = String(env.META_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID || '').trim(); if (cfg) p.set('config_id', cfg); else p.set('scope', String(env.META_SCOPES || META_SCOPES)); return _socialJson({ ok: true, url: META_DIALOG + '?' + p.toString() }, 200, cors); } // GET /social/meta/callback (Meta sends the agent's browser here) async function socialMetaCallback(request, env) { const q = new URL(request.url).searchParams; const state = String(q.get('state') || ''); if (!/^[a-f0-9]{32,96}$/.test(state)) { return _socialPage('That link is not valid', ['Go back to NestTrack and choose Connect Facebook & Instagram again.'], false); } const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const statePath = 'social_oauth_states/' + await _mcpHash(state); const pending = await fsGetDoc(token, statePath).catch(function () { return null; }); // Single use, whatever happens next. if (pending) { try { await _mcpDelete(token, statePath); } catch (e) {} } const exp = pending ? _mcpTsMs(pending.expiresAt) : 0; if (!pending || !pending.agentId || (exp && Date.now() > exp)) { return _socialPage('That sign-in expired', ['It has to be finished within ten minutes.', 'Go back to NestTrack and choose Connect Facebook & Instagram again.'], false); } if (pending.provider === 'ig') return await _socialIgFinish(env, token, pending, q); if (q.get('error') || !q.get('code')) { return _socialPage('Nothing was connected', ['The Facebook sign-in was cancelled, so NestTrack has no access to your Page or Instagram.', 'You can connect any time from Marketing.'], false); } const agentId = String(pending.agentId); const appId = String(env.META_APP_ID || META_APP_ID); const secret = String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim(); if (!secret) return _socialPage('Not set up yet', ['Social posting is not set up on the server yet.'], false); try { // 1. The code from the redirect becomes a short-lived key, 2. which becomes one that // lasts about 60 days. Both calls carry the app secret, so both happen here. const short = await _metaCall('GET', '/oauth/access_token', { client_id: appId, redirect_uri: META_REDIRECT_URI, client_secret: secret, code: String(q.get('code')) }); const long = await _metaCall('GET', '/oauth/access_token', { grant_type: 'fb_exchange_token', client_id: appId, client_secret: secret, fb_exchange_token: String(short.access_token || '') }); const userToken = String(long.access_token || short.access_token || ''); if (!userToken) throw new Error('Meta did not return an access key.'); const expiresIn = Number(long.expires_in || 0); // 3. Who signed in, what they agreed to, and which Pages they shared. const me = await _metaCall('GET', '/me', { fields: 'id,name', access_token: userToken }); let granted = []; try { const perms = await _metaCall('GET', '/me/permissions', { access_token: userToken }); granted = (perms.data || []).filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'granted'; }).map(function (x) { return x.permission; }); } catch (e) {} const acc = await _metaCall('GET', '/me/accounts', { fields: 'id,name,access_token,tasks,instagram_business_account{id,username}', limit: '100', access_token: userToken }); const rows = (acc.data || []).filter(function (p) { return p && p.id && p.access_token; }); if (!rows.length) { return _socialPage('No Facebook Page was shared', [ 'Facebook signed you in, but no Page was shared with NestTrack, so there is nothing to post to yet.', 'Connect again and, when Facebook asks which Pages to use, tick your business Page. You need to be an admin of that Page.', (granted.indexOf('business_management') === -1 ? 'One permission was missing this time (business access), which Pages inside a business account need. Connecting again will ask for it.' : '') ], false); } const pages = rows.map(function (p) { const tasks = Array.isArray(p.tasks) ? p.tasks : []; const ig = p.instagram_business_account || null; return { id: String(p.id), name: String(p.name || ''), // No task list means Meta did not say; treat that as able to post and let the post itself report otherwise. canPost: !tasks.length || tasks.indexOf('CREATE_CONTENT') !== -1 || tasks.indexOf('MANAGE') !== -1, igId: ig && ig.id ? String(ig.id) : '', igUsername: ig && ig.username ? String(ig.username) : '' }; }); const keys = { userToken: userToken, pages: rows.map(function (p) { return { id: String(p.id), token: String(p.access_token) }; }) }; const wanted = String(env.META_SCOPES || META_SCOPES).split(','); // business_management only matters for finding the Pages. They were found, so its absence // is not something to warn the agent about. const missing = granted.length ? wanted.filter(function (s) { return s !== 'business_management' && granted.indexOf(s) === -1; }) : []; // Keep the Page the agent had picked before, if it is still there; otherwise prefer one // that has Instagram linked, since that is the one most agents mean. const prev = await fsGetDoc(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId).catch(function () { return null; }); const prevSel = prev && prev.metaSelectedPageId ? String(prev.metaSelectedPageId) : ''; const sel = (pages.find(function (p) { return p.id === prevSel; }) || pages.find(function (p) { return p.igId && p.canPost; }) || pages.find(function (p) { return p.canPost; }) || pages[0]).id; await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId, { agentId: agentId, metaUserId: String(me.id || ''), metaUserName: String(me.name || ''), metaPages: pages, metaSelectedPageId: sel, metaScopes: granted, metaMissingScopes: missing, metaConnectedAt: Date.now(), metaTokenExpiresAt: expiresIn ? Date.now() + expiresIn * 1000 : 0, metaEnc: await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) }, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); const chosen = pages.find(function (p) { return p.id === sel; }); const lines = pages.length > 1 ? ['Facebook Pages: ' + pages.map(function (p) { return _socialEsc(p.name); }).join(', ')] : ['Facebook Page: ' + _socialEsc(chosen.name)]; if (missing.length) lines.push('Some permissions were turned off during sign-in, so posting may not work until you connect again and leave them on.'); lines.push('You can close this tab.'); return _socialPage('Connected', lines, true); } catch (e) { console.log('social meta callback:', e && e.message); return _socialPage('That did not connect', [ 'Facebook returned an error, so nothing was connected.', _socialEsc(String((e && e.message) || '').slice(0, 220)), 'Go back to NestTrack and try again.' ], false); } } // POST /api/v1/social/meta/select { pageId } async function apiSocialMetaSelect(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const pageId = String(body.pageId || ''); const doc = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId).catch(function () { return null; }); const pages = (doc && Array.isArray(doc.metaPages)) ? doc.metaPages : []; if (!doc || !doc.metaEnc) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Connect Facebook and Instagram first.' }, 400, cors); if (!pages.some(function (p) { return String(p.id) === pageId; })) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That Page is not one you shared with NestTrack. Connect again to add it.' }, 400, cors); } await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId, { metaSelectedPageId: pageId }, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); doc.metaSelectedPageId = pageId; return _socialJson({ ok: true, meta: Object.assign({ configured: true }, _socialPublicMeta(doc)) }, 200, cors); } // Removes NestTrack's access at Meta, then removes the stored keys. If Meta cannot be reached // the keys are still deleted here, so NestTrack can no longer use them either way. async function _socialForgetMeta(env, token, agentId, doc) { let revoked = false; try { const keys = doc && doc.metaEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.metaEnc) : null; if (keys && keys.userToken && doc.metaUserId) { await _metaCall('DELETE', '/' + encodeURIComponent(String(doc.metaUserId)) + '/permissions', { access_token: keys.userToken }); revoked = true; } } catch (e) { console.log('social meta revoke:', e && e.message); } // Only the Facebook side is cleared: Instagram accounts are connected separately and stay. await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId, { metaPages: [], metaEnc: '', metaUserId: '', metaUserName: '', metaSelectedPageId: '' }, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); return revoked; } // POST /api/v1/social/meta/disconnect async function apiSocialMetaDisconnect(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let doc = null; try { doc = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId); } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not disconnect just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } if (!doc) return _socialJson({ ok: true, revoked: false, meta: { configured: true, connected: false } }, 200, cors); try { const revoked = await _socialForgetMeta(env, w.token, w.agentId, doc); return _socialJson({ ok: true, revoked: revoked, meta: { configured: true, connected: false } }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not disconnect just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } } // Meta signs the requests it sends about a person's data. The signature is checked against // the app secret before anything is deleted, so nobody else can trigger a deletion. async function _metaSignedRequest(env, signed) { const parts = String(signed || '').split('.'); if (parts.length !== 2) return null; const secret = String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim(); if (!secret) return null; try { const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', new TextEncoder().encode(secret), { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['verify']); const okSig = await crypto.subtle.verify('HMAC', key, _socialUnB64(parts[0]), new TextEncoder().encode(parts[1])); if (!okSig) return null; return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(_socialUnB64(parts[1]))); } catch (e) { return null; } } // POST /social/meta/data-deletion (someone asked Meta to have NestTrack delete their data) // POST /social/meta/deauthorize (someone removed NestTrack from their Facebook settings) async function socialMetaDataDeletion(request, env, kind) { let signed = ''; try { const raw = await request.text(); if (raw.trim().charAt(0) === '{') signed = (JSON.parse(raw) || {}).signed_request || ''; else signed = new URLSearchParams(raw).get('signed_request') || ''; } catch (e) {} const data = await _metaSignedRequest(env, signed); if (!data || !data.user_id) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'invalid signed_request' }), { status: 400, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }); } const userId = String(data.user_id); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let removed = 0; try { const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'social_accounts', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'metaUserId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: userId } } }, limit: 50 }); for (const r of (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [])) { if (!r || !r.document || !r.document.name) continue; const id = String(r.document.name).split('/').pop(); // Access was already removed on Meta's side, so there is nothing to revoke: just delete. // Instagram accounts on the same record were connected separately and are left alone. try { await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + id, { metaPages: [], metaEnc: '', metaUserId: '', metaUserName: '', metaSelectedPageId: '' }); removed++; } catch (e) {} } } catch (e) { console.log('social meta deletion:', e && e.message); } // Instagram accounts that were connected by this same person are removed as well. try { const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'social_accounts', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'igGrantIds' }, op: 'ARRAY_CONTAINS', value: { stringValue: userId } } }, limit: 50 }); for (const r of (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [])) { if (!r || !r.document || !r.document.name) continue; const id = String(r.document.name).split('/').pop(); const doc = digDoc(r.document.fields || {}); const all = Array.isArray(doc.igAccounts) ? doc.igAccounts : []; const keep = all.filter(function (a) { return String(a.grantId || '') !== userId; }); const keys = (doc.igEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.igEnc) : null) || { tokens: {} }; all.forEach(function (a) { if (String(a.grantId || '') === userId && keys.tokens) delete keys.tokens[a.id]; }); try { await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + id, { igAccounts: keep, hasIg: keep.length > 0, igGrantIds: keep.map(function (a) { return String(a.grantId || ''); }).filter(function (g, i, arr) { return g && arr.indexOf(g) === i; }), igEnc: keep.length ? await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) : '' }); removed++; } catch (e) {} } } catch (e) { console.log('social ig deletion:', e && e.message); } if (kind === 'deauthorize') return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true }), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }); // Meta wants a code and a page where the person can check on the request. const code = _socialRand(12); try { await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_deletions/' + code, { provider: 'meta', removed: removed, completedAt: Date.now() }, { createdAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch (e) {} return new Response(JSON.stringify({ url: SOCIAL_ORIGIN + '/social/meta/deletion?code=' + code, confirmation_code: code }), { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } }); } // GET /social/meta/deletion?code=... (the status page named in the reply above) // With no code it explains how to have the data removed, which is the page Meta links to. async function socialMetaDeletionStatus(request, env) { const code = String(new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('code') || ''); if (!code) { return _socialPage('Removing your Facebook and Instagram data', [ 'NestTrack stores the access you granted to your Facebook Page and Instagram account so it can post for you. It stores nothing else from Facebook or Instagram.', 'To remove it, open NestTrack, go to Marketing, and choose Disconnect under Social accounts. Your access keys are deleted immediately.', 'You can also remove NestTrack in your Facebook settings under Business Integrations. Facebook then tells NestTrack, and the same data is deleted automatically.', 'Questions: hi@nesttrack.io' ], true); } if (!/^[a-f0-9]{8,64}$/.test(code)) return _socialPage('Request not found', ['That confirmation code is not one NestTrack issued.'], false); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const rec = await fsGetDoc(token, 'social_deletions/' + code).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!rec) return _socialPage('Request not found', ['That confirmation code is not one NestTrack issued.'], false); return _socialPage('Your data has been deleted', [ 'NestTrack has deleted the Facebook and Instagram access it held for your account.', 'Confirmation code: ' + _socialEsc(code) + '' ], true); } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SOCIAL CHANNELS AND POSTING // // Every connected account is its own channel, and a post goes to whichever channels the // agent ticks: // fb_ a Facebook Page, connected through Facebook's sign-in (block above) // ig_ an Instagram professional account, connected through Meta's "unified // login" for the Instagram API. The agent picks the account itself, so it // works whether or not that account is linked to a Facebook Page. // tt_ a TikTok account, connected through TikTok's own sign-in. Video only. // yt_ a YouTube channel, connected through Google's sign-in. Video only. // // Media goes into Cloudflare storage first: Instagram does not take an upload, it fetches // the file from a public address, and the same address is handed to Facebook. // // A post is a small record that the once-a-minute job works through, channel by channel: // pending -> processing (Instagram is preparing the media) -> done | failed // // Nothing here is sent to a client. It publishes to the agent's own accounts. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const SOCIAL_MEDIA_ORIGIN = 'https://media.nesttrack.io'; const SOCIAL_MAX_BYTES = 1024 * 1024 * 1024; // 1 GB per video const SOCIAL_MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024; // Instagram's photo limit const SOCIAL_PART_BYTES = 16 * 1024 * 1024; // upload chunk size const SOCIAL_CAPTION_MAX = 2200; // Instagram's caption limit const SOCIAL_MAX_PHOTOS = 10; // Instagram's carousel limit const SOCIAL_MEDIA_TYPES = { 'video/mp4': 'mp4', 'video/quicktime': 'mov', 'image/jpeg': 'jpg' }; // NestTrack is not file storage. Once a post has gone out the platform has its own copy, so // ours is removed. The hour is there because Facebook can still be copying a video for a // little while after it accepts the post. const SOCIAL_KEEP_MEDIA_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000; // a post that went out everywhere: an hour const SOCIAL_KEEP_FAILED_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // one that failed somewhere: a day const SOCIAL_KEEP_DRAFT_MS = 14 * 86400000; // a draft nobody has touched: two weeks const SOCIAL_IG_WAIT_MS = 20 * 60 * 1000; // how long Instagram gets to prepare media // Instagram sign-in runs on a saved login configuration in the Meta app (Instagram account as // the asset, with instagram_basic and instagram_content_publish). The id is not a secret: it // travels in the sign-in address. const IG_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID = '2199899997595175'; function _igConfigId(env) { return String(env.IG_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID || IG_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID).trim(); } function _igConfigured(env) { return !!(/^[0-9]{5,30}$/.test(_igConfigId(env)) && String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim()); } function _socialMediaOrigin(env) { return String(env.SOCIAL_MEDIA_ORIGIN || SOCIAL_MEDIA_ORIGIN).replace(/\/+$/, ''); } function _socialOwnKey(agentId, key) { const k = String(key || ''); return k.indexOf('social/' + agentId + '/') === 0 && /^social\/[A-Za-z0-9_\-]+\/[a-f0-9]{24,64}\.(mp4|mov|jpg)$/.test(k); } function _socialKind(key) { return /\.jpg$/.test(String(key)) ? 'image' : 'video'; } function _socialNoStorage(cors) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Media storage is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); } // Every channel this agent has, as the app may see it: ids and names, never keys. function _socialChannels(doc) { const out = []; if (!doc) return out; if (doc.metaEnc) { const fbExp = Number(doc.metaTokenExpiresAt || 0); (Array.isArray(doc.metaPages) ? doc.metaPages : []).forEach(function (p) { out.push({ id: 'fb_' + p.id, type: 'facebook', name: p.name || 'Facebook Page', canPost: p.canPost !== false, needsReconnect: !!(fbExp && Date.now() > fbExp - 7 * 86400000) }); }); } (Array.isArray(doc.igAccounts) ? doc.igAccounts : []).forEach(function (a) { const exp = Number(a.expiresAt || 0); out.push({ id: 'ig_' + a.id, type: 'instagram', name: a.username ? '@' + a.username : 'Instagram', canPost: true, needsReconnect: !!(exp && Date.now() > exp) }); }); (Array.isArray(doc.ttAccounts) ? doc.ttAccounts : []).forEach(function (a) { const exp = Number(a.refreshExpiresAt || 0); const sc = Array.isArray(a.scopes) ? a.scopes : []; const modes = []; if (sc.indexOf('video.publish') !== -1) modes.push('post'); if (sc.indexOf('video.upload') !== -1) modes.push('draft'); out.push({ id: 'tt_' + a.id, type: 'tiktok', name: a.username ? '@' + a.username : (a.name || 'TikTok'), canPost: modes.length > 0, modes: modes, needsReconnect: !!(exp && Date.now() > exp) }); }); (Array.isArray(doc.ytAccounts) ? doc.ytAccounts : []).forEach(function (a) { out.push({ id: 'yt_' + a.id, type: 'youtube', name: a.name || a.handle || 'YouTube channel', canPost: true, needsReconnect: false }); }); return out; } // POST /api/v1/social/ig/start -> { url } to send the agent to Instagram's sign-in async function apiSocialIgStart(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!_igConfigured(env)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Instagram sign-in is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); const state = _socialRand(24); await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_oauth_states/' + await _mcpHash(state), { agentId: w.agentId, provider: 'ig' }, { createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(10 * 60 * 1000) }); const p = new URLSearchParams({ client_id: String(env.META_APP_ID || META_APP_ID), redirect_uri: META_REDIRECT_URI, config_id: _igConfigId(env), response_type: 'code', override_default_response_type: 'true', state: state, // These two are in the sign-in address Meta's dashboard gives for the // configuration. With them the person is offered a plain Instagram // login, not only the list of accounts in a business portfolio. display: 'popup', prompt: 'login' }); // The same unversioned address Meta's dashboard shows for this configuration. return _socialJson({ ok: true, url: 'https://www.facebook.com/dialog/oauth?' + p.toString() }, 200, cors); } // The Instagram half of GET /social/meta/callback. Both sign-ins come back to the one address // registered with Meta; the stored state says which of the two this trip was. async function _socialIgFinish(env, token, pending, q) { if (q.get('error') || !q.get('code')) { return _socialPage('Nothing was connected', ['The sign-in was cancelled, so NestTrack has no access to that Instagram account.', 'You can add it any time from Social in Marketing.'], false); } if (!_igConfigured(env)) return _socialPage('Not set up yet', ['Instagram sign-in is not set up on the server yet.'], false); const agentId = String(pending.agentId); try { const got = await _metaCall('GET', '/oauth/access_token', { client_id: String(env.META_APP_ID || META_APP_ID), redirect_uri: META_REDIRECT_URI, client_secret: String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim(), code: String(q.get('code')) }); const tok = String(got.access_token || ''); if (!tok) throw new Error('Meta did not return an access key.'); // This kind of access normally does not run out. Accounts reached through a shared Page are read too, // in case the sign-in was done that way. const found = []; const add = function (x) { const a = x && (x.instagram_business_account || x.instagram_account || x); const id = a && String(a.id || a.ig_id || ''); if (/^[0-9]{3,40}$/.test(id) && !found.some(function (f) { return f.id === id; })) found.push({ id: id, username: String(a.username || ''), name: String(a.name || '') }); }; try { ((await _metaCall('GET', '/me/assigned_instagram_accounts', { fields: 'id,username,name', limit: '50', access_token: tok })).data || []).forEach(add); } catch (e) { console.log('social ig assigned:', e && e.message); } if (!found.length) { try { ((await _metaCall('GET', '/me/accounts', { fields: 'instagram_business_account{id,username,name}', limit: '100', access_token: tok })).data || []) .forEach(function (p) { if (p && p.instagram_business_account) add(p); }); } catch (e) {} } if (!found.length) { return _socialPage('No Instagram account was shared', [ 'The sign-in went through, but no Instagram account was chosen, so there is nothing to post to yet.', 'Add Instagram again and, when asked which accounts to use, tick the account you want.', 'The account has to be a professional one (business or creator).' ], false); } // Names are filled in where the list did not carry them. for (const f of found) { if (f.username) continue; try { const d = await _metaCall('GET', '/' + encodeURIComponent(f.id), { fields: 'username,name', access_token: tok }); f.username = String(d.username || ''); f.name = String(d.name || ''); } catch (e) {} } const doc = (await fsGetDoc(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId).catch(function () { return null; })) || {}; const ids = found.map(function (f) { return f.id; }); const accounts = (Array.isArray(doc.igAccounts) ? doc.igAccounts : []).filter(function (a) { return ids.indexOf(String(a.id)) === -1; }); const keys = (doc.igEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.igEnc) : null) || { tokens: {} }; keys.tokens = keys.tokens || {}; found.forEach(function (f) { accounts.push({ id: f.id, username: f.username, name: f.name, grantId: grantId, connectedAt: Date.now(), expiresAt: expiresAt }); keys.tokens[f.id] = tok; }); await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId, { agentId: agentId, igAccounts: accounts, hasIg: true, igGrantIds: accounts.map(function (a) { return String(a.grantId || ''); }).filter(function (g, i, arr) { return g && arr.indexOf(g) === i; }), igEnc: await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) }, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); return _socialPage('Instagram connected', [ '' + (found.length > 1 ? 'Accounts' : 'Account') + ': ' + found.map(function (f) { return f.username ? '@' + _socialEsc(f.username) : 'connected'; }).join(', '), 'You can close this tab.' ], true); } catch (e) { console.log('social ig callback:', e && e.message); return _socialPage('That did not connect', [ 'Meta returned an error, so nothing was connected.', _socialEsc(String((e && e.message) || '').slice(0, 220)), 'Check the account is a professional account (business or creator), then try again.' ], false); } } // ── TikTok ── // The agent signs in on TikTok's own page and approves NestTrack. What comes back is a // short-lived access key (about a day at most) and a longer-lived one used to renew it. Both // are encrypted before they are stored, the same way the Meta keys are. // // The client key identifies the app and grants nothing by itself; the secret lives in the // worker's secrets as TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET. While TikTok reviews the app this is the Sandbox // key. Once approved, TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY and TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET are set to the live pair. const TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY = 'sbawqgdpz1x03tcbrp'; const TIKTOK_AUTH_URL = 'https://www.tiktok.com/v2/auth/authorize/'; const TIKTOK_API = 'https://open.tiktokapis.com'; // Listed, character for character, in the TikTok app under Login Kit > Redirect URI. const TIKTOK_REDIRECT_URI = SOCIAL_ORIGIN + '/social/tiktok/callback'; const TIKTOK_SCOPES = 'user.info.basic,video.publish,video.upload'; const TIKTOK_PRIVACY = ['PUBLIC_TO_EVERYONE', 'MUTUAL_FOLLOW_FRIENDS', 'FOLLOWER_OF_CREATOR', 'SELF_ONLY']; const SOCIAL_TT_WAIT_MS = 70 * 60 * 1000; // TikTok allows itself an hour to fetch a video function _ttKey(env) { return String(env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY || TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY).trim(); } function _ttConfigured(env) { // The Meta secret is needed too: it is what the stored keys are encrypted with. return !!(/^[A-Za-z0-9]{8,64}$/.test(_ttKey(env)) && String(env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET || '').trim() && String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim()); } const TT_GONE = 'This TikTok account is no longer connected. Add it again in Social, then post again.'; // What TikTok's short error codes mean, in words an agent can act on. const TT_ERRORS = { access_token_invalid: 'TikTok no longer accepts this connection. Remove the account in Social and add it again.', scope_not_authorized: 'This TikTok account did not give NestTrack permission to do that. Remove it in Social and add it again, leaving every permission on.', spam_risk_too_many_posts: 'TikTok says this account has reached its posting limit for today. Try again tomorrow, or post from the TikTok app.', spam_risk_user_banned_from_posting: 'TikTok is not letting this account post right now.', spam_risk_text: 'TikTok turned this down because of the caption. Change the wording and post it again.', spam_risk: 'TikTok turned this post down. Try posting it from the TikTok app.', reached_active_user_cap: 'TikTok has reached its daily limit for accounts posting through NestTrack. Try again tomorrow.', unaudited_client_can_only_post_to_private_accounts: 'TikTok has not finished approving NestTrack yet. Until it does, it only accepts posts to a private TikTok account with "Only me" chosen for who can view.', url_ownership_unverified: 'TikTok has not verified NestTrack\'s media address for this app yet, so it would not fetch the video. Nothing is wrong with your video.', privacy_level_option_mismatch: 'That "who can view" choice is not available on this TikTok account. Open the post and choose again.', rate_limit_exceeded: 'TikTok is busy. Try again in a minute.', file_format_check_failed: 'TikTok could not use this video file. Export it as an MP4 (H.264) and try again.', duration_check_failed: 'This video is longer or shorter than TikTok allows for this account.', frame_rate_check_failed: 'TikTok does not support this video\'s frame rate. Export it at 30 or 60 frames a second.', picture_size_check_failed: 'TikTok does not support this video\'s dimensions. Export it at 1080 by 1920.', video_pull_failed: 'TikTok could not fetch the video. Post it again.', internal: 'TikTok had a problem on its side. Post it again.', internal_error: 'TikTok had a problem on its side. Try again in a minute.', publish_cancelled: 'This post was cancelled.', auth_removed: 'NestTrack\'s access to this TikTok account was removed while this was posting. Add the account again in Social.' }; function _ttErr(code, message) { const e = new Error(TT_ERRORS[code] || String(message || code || 'TikTok did not accept that.').slice(0, 220)); e.ttCode = String(code || ''); return e; } // The sign-in endpoints take a form and answer with the fields at the top level. async function _ttOauth(env, path, params) { const body = new URLSearchParams(Object.assign({ client_key: _ttKey(env), client_secret: String(env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET || '').trim() }, params || {})).toString(); let r, j = null; try { r = await fetch(TIKTOK_API + path, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded', 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' }, body: body }); } catch (e) { throw new Error('TikTok could not be reached. Try again in a minute.'); } try { j = await r.json(); } catch (e) {} if (!r.ok || (j && j.error)) { const m = (j && (j.error_description || (typeof j.error === 'string' ? j.error : ''))) || ('HTTP ' + r.status); console.log('tiktok ' + path + ' failed: ' + m); const err = new Error(String(m)); err.ttAnswered = true; // TikTok said no, as opposed to not being reachable throw err; } return j || {}; } // Everything else takes JSON, carries the access key in a header, and reports how it went in // an "error" object whose code is "ok" when it worked. async function _ttCall(method, path, access, body) { let r, j = null; try { r = await fetch(TIKTOK_API + path, { method: method, headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + access, 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=UTF-8' }, body: method === 'POST' ? JSON.stringify(body || {}) : undefined }); } catch (e) { throw new Error('TikTok could not be reached. Try again in a minute.'); } try { j = await r.json(); } catch (e) {} const code = j && j.error && typeof j.error === 'object' ? String(j.error.code || '') : ''; if (!r.ok || (code && code !== 'ok')) { console.log('tiktok ' + String(path).split('?')[0] + ' failed: ' + (code || ('HTTP ' + r.status)) + ' ' + ((j && j.error && j.error.message) || '')); throw _ttErr(code, (j && j.error && j.error.message) || ('TikTok answered with an error (' + r.status + ').')); } return (j && j.data) || {}; } // A working access key for one TikTok account, renewed first if it is about to run out. // Renewing hands back a new renewal key and retires the old one, so the new pair is saved // before anything else is done with it. async function _ttToken(env, token, agentId, openId) { const read = async function () { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId).catch(function () { return null; }); const keys = doc && doc.ttEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.ttEnc) : null; return { doc: doc, keys: keys, t: keys && keys.tokens ? keys.tokens[openId] : null }; }; const cur = await read(); if (!cur.t || !cur.t.r) { const e = new Error(TT_GONE); e.ttGone = true; throw e; } if (cur.t.a && Number(cur.t.ax || 0) > Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000) return String(cur.t.a); let got; try { got = await _ttOauth(env, '/v2/oauth/token/', { grant_type: 'refresh_token', refresh_token: String(cur.t.r) }); } catch (e) { // Two things renewing in the same moment: the other one won, and its keys are saved. const again = await read(); if (again.t && again.t.a && again.t.r !== cur.t.r && Number(again.t.ax || 0) > Date.now() + 60000) return String(again.t.a); if (!(e && e.ttAnswered)) throw e; // a network hiccup is not a lost connection throw _ttErr('access_token_invalid'); } const access = String(got.access_token || ''); if (!access) throw _ttErr('access_token_invalid'); const now = Date.now(); const next = { a: access, ax: now + Math.max(60, Number(got.expires_in || 0)) * 1000, r: String(got.refresh_token || cur.t.r) }; const fresh = await read(); const keys = fresh.keys || cur.keys || { tokens: {} }; keys.tokens = keys.tokens || {}; keys.tokens[openId] = next; const base = fresh.doc || cur.doc || {}; const accounts = (Array.isArray(base.ttAccounts) ? base.ttAccounts : []).map(function (a) { return String(a.id) === openId && Number(got.refresh_expires_in || 0) > 0 ? Object.assign({}, a, { refreshExpiresAt: now + Number(got.refresh_expires_in) * 1000 }) : a; }); const patch = { ttEnc: await _socialEncrypt(env, keys), ttAccounts: accounts }; try { await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId, patch); } catch (e) { await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId, patch); } return access; } // POST /api/v1/social/tiktok/start -> { url } to send the agent to TikTok's sign-in async function apiSocialTikTokStart(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!_ttConfigured(env)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'TikTok sign-in is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); const state = _socialRand(24); await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_oauth_states/' + await _mcpHash(state), { agentId: w.agentId, provider: 'tt' }, { createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(10 * 60 * 1000) }); const p = new URLSearchParams({ client_key: _ttKey(env), scope: TIKTOK_SCOPES, response_type: 'code', redirect_uri: TIKTOK_REDIRECT_URI, state: state, // Always show TikTok's approval page, so a second account can be // added without TikTok silently reusing the first. disable_auto_auth: '1' }); return _socialJson({ ok: true, url: TIKTOK_AUTH_URL + '?' + p.toString() }, 200, cors); } // GET /social/tiktok/callback (TikTok sends the agent's browser here) async function socialTikTokCallback(request, env) { const q = new URL(request.url).searchParams; const state = String(q.get('state') || ''); if (!/^[a-f0-9]{32,96}$/.test(state)) { return _socialPage('That link is not valid', ['Go back to NestTrack and choose Add TikTok again.'], false); } const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); } catch (e) {} } const exp = pending ? _mcpTsMs(pending.expiresAt) : 0; if (!pending || !pending.agentId || pending.provider !== 'tt' || (exp && Date.now() > exp)) { return _socialPage('That sign-in expired', ['It has to be finished within ten minutes.', 'Go back to NestTrack and choose Add TikTok again.'], false); } if (q.get('error') || !q.get('code')) { return _socialPage('Nothing was connected', ['The TikTok sign-in was cancelled, so NestTrack has no access to that TikTok account.', 'You can add it any time from Social in Marketing.'], false); } if (!_ttConfigured(env)) return _socialPage('Not set up yet', ['TikTok sign-in is not set up on the server yet.'], false); const agentId = String(pending.agentId); try { const got = await _ttOauth(env, '/v2/oauth/token/', { code: String(q.get('code')), grant_type: 'authorization_code', redirect_uri: TIKTOK_REDIRECT_URI }); const access = String(got.access_token || ''), refresh = String(got.refresh_token || ''), openId = String(got.open_id || ''); if (!access || !refresh || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{6,128}$/.test(openId)) throw new Error('TikTok did not return an access key.'); } catch (e) {} return _socialPage('Posting was not allowed', [ 'TikTok signed you in, but the permission to post videos was turned off, so there is nothing NestTrack can do with this account.', 'Add TikTok again and leave the posting permissions on.' ], false); } // Who this is: the display name from the sign-in, and the @name TikTok gives for posting. let name = '', username = ''; try { const u = (await _ttCall('GET', '/v2/user/info/?fields=open_id,display_name,avatar_url', access)).user || {}; name = String(u.display_name || ''); } catch (e) {} if (scopes.indexOf('video.publish') !== -1) { try { const c = await _ttCall('POST', '/v2/post/publish/creator_info/query/', access, {}); username = String(c.creator_username || ''); '@' + _socialEsc(username) : (name ? _socialEsc(name) : 'connected')), 'You can close this tab.' ], true); } catch (e) { console.log('social tiktok callback:', e && e.message); return _socialPage('That did not connect', [ 'TikTok returned an error, so nothing was connected.', _socialEsc(String((e && e.message) || '').slice(0, 220)), 'Go back to NestTrack and try again.' ], false); } } // GET /api/v1/social/tiktok/creator?id=tt_ // What TikTok says about this account right now. TikTok requires the posting screen to show // it: whose account it is, who may be allowed to view a post, and which of comments, duets // and stitches the account has switched off. async function apiSocialTikTokCreator(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!_ttConfigured(env)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'TikTok is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); const id = String(new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('id') || ''); if (id.indexOf('tt_') !== 0) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That account was not found.' }, 400, cors); const openId = id.slice(3); try { const access = await _ttToken(env, w.token, w.agentId, openId); const c = await _ttCall('POST', '/v2/post/publish/creator_info/query/', access, {}); return _socialJson({ ok: true, creator: { nickname: String(c.creator_nickname || ''), username: String(c.creator_username || ''), avatar: String(c.creator_avatar_url || ''), privacy: (Array.isArray(c.privacy_level_options) ?
