200 : (e && e.ttGone ? 404 : 502), cors); } } // What the agent chose for one TikTok account on one post, cleaned to exactly what is used. function _ttCleanSettings(v) { const s = v && typeof v === 'object' ? v : {}; const commercial = s.commercial === true; return { mode: s.mode === 'draft' ? 'draft' : 'post', privacy: TIKTOK_PRIVACY.indexOf(String(s.privacy || '')) !== -1 ? String(s.privacy) : '', comment: s.comment === true, duet: s.duet === true, stitch: s.stitch === true, commercial: commercial, yourBrand: commercial && s.yourBrand === true, branded: commercial && s.branded === true }; } // One TikTok account, step one: hand TikTok the video's address. TikTok fetches the file // itself and answers with an id to ask about; the once-a-minute job asks until it is through. async function _socialTtCreate(d, set, access, urls) { const source = { source: 'PULL_FROM_URL', video_url: urls[0] }; if (set.mode === 'draft') { return String((await _ttCall('POST', '/v2/post/publish/inbox/video/init/', access, { source_info: source })).publish_id || ''); } return String((await _ttCall('POST', '/v2/post/publish/video/init/', access, { post_info: { title: String(d.caption || ''), privacy_level: set.privacy, disable_comment: !set.comment, disable_duet: !set.duet, disable_stitch: !set.stitch, brand_organic_toggle: set.yourBrand === true, brand_content_toggle: set.branded === true }, source_info: source })).publish_id || ''); } // ── YouTube ── // The agent signs in with Google and picks their channel. Google answers with a short-lived // access key (about an hour) and a long-lived one used to renew it; both are encrypted before // they are stored. The client id identifies the app and grants nothing by itself; the secret // lives in the worker's secrets as GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET. // // Unlike Facebook, Instagram and TikTok, YouTube does not fetch a video from an address: the // file has to be sent to it. It is sent from storage in pieces, and where it has got to is // saved after each run, so a large video carries on from one minute's run to the next and a // dropped connection costs one piece, not the whole upload. const GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID = '681324344980-2kbmmks0eo1qen450ckmevupmn87fufe.apps.googleusercontent.com'; const GOOGLE_AUTH_URL = 'https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth'; const GOOGLE_TOKEN_URL = 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token'; const GOOGLE_REVOKE_URL = 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/revoke'; // Listed, character for character, on the client in Google Cloud > Google Auth Platform > Clients. const GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI = SOCIAL_ORIGIN + '/social/google/callback'; // Upload videos, and read the channel's name so the agent can see which channel is connected. const YT_SCOPE_UPLOAD = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.upload'; const YT_SCOPES = YT_SCOPE_UPLOAD + ' https://www.googleapis.com/auth/youtube.readonly'; const YT_API = 'https://www.googleapis.com/youtube/v3'; const YT_UPLOAD_URL = 'https://www.googleapis.com/upload/youtube/v3/videos'; const YT_CHUNK_BYTES = 16 * 1024 * 1024; // YouTube wants pieces in multiples of 256 KB const YT_TITLE_MAX = 100; const YT_PRIVACY = ['public', 'unlisted', 'private']; const SOCIAL_YT_WAIT_MS = 3 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // an upload that has not finished in three hours is given up on function _ytClientId(env) { return String(env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID || GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID).trim(); } function _ytConfigured(env) { // The Meta secret is needed too: it is what the stored keys are encrypted with. return !!(/\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/.test(_ytClientId(env)) && String(env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET || '').trim() && String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim()); } const YT_GONE = 'This YouTube channel is no longer connected. Add it again in Social, then post again.'; // What YouTube's short error reasons mean, in words an agent can act on. const YT_ERRORS = { quotaExceeded: 'NestTrack has used up the uploads YouTube allows it for today. Try again tomorrow.', dailyLimitExceeded: 'NestTrack has used up the uploads YouTube allows it for today. Try again tomorrow.', rateLimitExceeded: 'YouTube is busy. Try again in a few minutes.', userRateLimitExceeded: 'YouTube is busy. Try again in a few minutes.', uploadLimitExceeded: 'This channel has reached the number of videos YouTube lets it upload for now. Try again tomorrow.', youtubeSignupRequired: 'This Google account does not have a YouTube channel yet. Create one on YouTube, then add it again.', invalidTitle: 'YouTube did not accept the title. Keep it under 100 characters and leave out the < and > symbols.', invalidDescription: 'YouTube did not accept the description. Leave out the < and > symbols.', forbiddenPrivacySetting: 'This channel is not allowed to use that visibility setting. Choose another one.', insufficientPermissions: 'This channel did not give NestTrack permission to do that. Remove it in Social and add it again, leaving every permission on.', authError: 'YouTube no longer accepts this connection. Remove the channel in Social and add it again.' }; function _ytErr(reason, message, status) { const e = new Error(YT_ERRORS[reason] || String(message || reason || 'YouTube did not accept that.').slice(0, 220)); e.ytReason = String(reason || ''); e.ytStatus = Number(status || 0); return e; } // Google's sign-in endpoints take a form and answer with the fields at the top level. async function _ytOauth(env, url, params) { const body = new URLSearchParams(Object.assign({ client_id: _ytClientId(env), client_secret: String(env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET || '').trim() }, params || {})).toString(); let r, j = null; try { r = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: body }); } catch (e) { throw new Error('Google could not be reached. Try again in a minute.'); } try { j = await r.json(); } catch (e) {} if (!r.ok || (j && j.error)) { const m = (j && (j.error_description || (typeof j.error === 'string' ? j.error : ''))) || ('HTTP ' + r.status); console.log('google ' + String(url).split('?')[0] + ' failed: ' + m); const err = new Error(String(m)); err.ytAnswered = true; // Google said no, as opposed to not being reachable throw err; } return j || {}; } // What Google said went wrong, read out of its error reply. async function _ytReadError(r) { let j = null; try { j = await r.json(); } catch (e) {} const er = j && j.error && typeof j.error === 'object' ? j.error : {}; const reason = String((Array.isArray(er.errors) && er.errors[0] && er.errors[0].reason) || (r.status === 401 ? 'authError' : '')); console.log('youtube failed: ' + r.status + ' ' + reason + ' ' + String(er.message || '').slice(0, 160)); return _ytErr(reason, er.message || ('YouTube answered with an error (' + r.status + ').'), r.status); } async function _ytGet(path, access) { let r; try { r = await fetch(YT_API + path, { method: 'GET', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + access } }); } catch (e) { throw new Error('YouTube could not be reached. Try again in a minute.'); } if (!r.ok) throw await _ytReadError(r); let j = null; try { j = await r.json(); } catch (e) {} return j || {}; } // A working access key for one channel, renewed first if it is about to run out. async function _ytToken(env, token, agentId, chanId) { const read = async function () { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId).catch(function () { return null; }); const keys = doc && doc.ytEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.ytEnc) : null; return { keys: keys, t: keys && keys.tokens ? keys.tokens[chanId] : null }; }; const cur = await read(); if (!cur.t || !cur.t.r) { const e = new Error(YT_GONE); e.ytGone = true; throw e; } if (cur.t.a && Number(cur.t.ax || 0) > Date.now() + 5 * 60 * 1000) return String(cur.t.a); let got; try { got = await _ytOauth(env, GOOGLE_TOKEN_URL, { grant_type: 'refresh_token', refresh_token: String(cur.t.r) }); } catch (e) { if (!(e && e.ytAnswered)) throw e; // a network hiccup is not a lost connection throw _ytErr('authError'); } const access = String(got.access_token || ''); if (!access) throw _ytErr('authError'); const fresh = await read(); const keys = fresh.keys || cur.keys || { tokens: {} }; keys.tokens = keys.tokens || {}; keys.tokens[chanId] = { a: access, ax: Date.now() + Math.max(60, Number(got.expires_in || 0)) * 1000, r: String(got.refresh_token || cur.t.r) }; // The renewal key has not changed, so a failed save here loses nothing: it is renewed again next time. try { await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId, { ytEnc: await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) }); } catch (e) { console.log('social youtube key save:', e && e.message); } return access; } // POST /api/v1/social/youtube/start -> { url } to send the agent to Google's sign-in async function apiSocialYouTubeStart(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!_ytConfigured(env)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'YouTube sign-in is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); const state = _socialRand(24); await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_oauth_states/' + await _mcpHash(state), { agentId: w.agentId, provider: 'yt' }, { createdAt: fsTimestamp(), expiresAt: fsFutureTs(10 * 60 * 1000) }); const p = new URLSearchParams({ client_id: _ytClientId(env), redirect_uri: GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI, response_type: 'code', scope: YT_SCOPES, state: state, // "offline" asks for the long-lived key, and Google only hands one // over when the approval screen is shown, so it is always shown. access_type: 'offline', prompt: 'consent select_account' }); return _socialJson({ ok: true, url: GOOGLE_AUTH_URL + '?' + p.toString() }, 200, cors); } // GET /social/google/callback (Google sends the agent's browser here) async function socialGoogleCallback(request, env) { const q = new URL(request.url).searchParams; const state = String(q.get('state') || ''); if (!/^[a-f0-9]{32,96}$/.test(state)) { return _socialPage('That link is not valid', ['Go back to NestTrack and choose Add YouTube again.'], false); } const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const statePath = 'social_oauth_states/' + await _mcpHash(state); const pending = await fsGetDoc(token, statePath).catch(function () { return null; }); // Single use, whatever happens next. if (pending) { try { await _mcpDelete(token, statePath); } catch (e) {} } const exp = pending ? _mcpTsMs(pending.expiresAt) : 0; if (!pending || !pending.agentId || pending.provider !== 'yt' || (exp && Date.now() > exp)) { return _socialPage('That sign-in expired', ['It has to be finished within ten minutes.', 'Go back to NestTrack and choose Add YouTube again.'], false); } if (q.get('error') || !q.get('code')) { return _socialPage('Nothing was connected', ['The Google sign-in was cancelled, so NestTrack has no access to that YouTube channel.', 'You can add it any time from Social in Marketing.'], false); } if (!_ytConfigured(env)) return _socialPage('Not set up yet', ['YouTube sign-in is not set up on the server yet.'], false); const agentId = String(pending.agentId); let access = '', refresh = ''; const handBack = async function () { try { await _ytOauth(env, GOOGLE_REVOKE_URL, { token: refresh || access }); } catch (e) {} }; try { const got = await _ytOauth(env, GOOGLE_TOKEN_URL, { code: String(q.get('code')), grant_type: 'authorization_code', redirect_uri: GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI }); access = String(got.access_token || ''); refresh = String(got.refresh_token || ''); if (!access || !refresh) throw new Error('Google did not return an access key.'); // Google lets a person untick permissions one by one on its approval screen. if (String(got.scope || '').split(/\s+/).indexOf(YT_SCOPE_UPLOAD) === -1) { await handBack(); return _socialPage('Uploading was not allowed', [ 'Google signed you in, but the permission to upload videos was left unticked, so there is nothing NestTrack can do with this channel.', 'Add YouTube again and tick both boxes on Google\'s screen.' ], false); } let ch = null; try { ch = ((await _ytGet('/channels?part=snippet&mine=true', access)).items || [])[0] || null; } catch (e) { await handBack(); if (e && e.ytStatus === 403) { return _socialPage('One permission was missing', [ 'Google signed you in, but the permission to see your channel was left unticked, so NestTrack cannot tell which channel this is.', 'Add YouTube again and tick both boxes on Google\'s screen.' ], false); } throw e; } const chanId = ch ? String(ch.id || '') : ''; if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{6,64}$/.test(chanId)) { await handBack(); return _socialPage('No YouTube channel was found', [ 'That Google account does not have a YouTube channel, so there is nothing to post to yet.', 'Create a channel on YouTube, or add YouTube again and pick the account your channel is under.' ], false); } const sn = ch.snippet || {}; const now = Date.now(); const doc = (await fsGetDoc(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId).catch(function () { return null; })) || {}; const accounts = (Array.isArray(doc.ytAccounts) ? doc.ytAccounts : []).filter(function (a) { return String(a.id) !== chanId; }); accounts.push({ id: chanId, name: String(sn.title || ''), handle: String(sn.customUrl || ''), connectedAt: now }); const keys = (doc.ytEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.ytEnc) : null) || { tokens: {} }; keys.tokens = keys.tokens || {}; keys.tokens[chanId] = { a: access, ax: now + Math.max(60, Number(got.expires_in || 0)) * 1000, r: refresh }; await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_accounts/' + agentId, { agentId: agentId, ytAccounts: accounts, hasYt: true, ytEnc: await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) }, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); return _socialPage('YouTube connected', [ 'Channel: ' + _socialEsc(String(sn.title || sn.customUrl || 'connected')), 'You can close this tab.' ], true); } catch (e) { console.log('social youtube callback:', e && e.message); return _socialPage('That did not connect', [ 'Google returned an error, so nothing was connected.', _socialEsc(String((e && e.message) || '').slice(0, 220)), 'Go back to NestTrack and try again.' ], false); } } // What the agent chose for one YouTube channel on one post, cleaned to exactly what is used. function _ytCleanSettings(v) { const s = v && typeof v === 'object' ? v : {}; return { title: String(s.title || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 200), privacy: YT_PRIVACY.indexOf(String(s.privacy || '')) !== -1 ? String(s.privacy) : '', kids: s.kids === true ? true : (s.kids === false ? false : null) }; } // Step one of an upload: tell YouTube what is coming (title, description, visibility, size). // It answers with an address that the file is then sent to, valid for this one upload. async function _ytBegin(access, d, set, size, type) { let r; try { r = await fetch(YT_UPLOAD_URL + '?uploadType=resumable&part=snippet,status', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + access, 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=UTF-8', 'X-Upload-Content-Length': String(size), 'X-Upload-Content-Type': type }, body: JSON.stringify({ snippet: { title: set.title, description: String(d.caption || ''), categoryId: '22' }, status: { privacyStatus: set.privacy, selfDeclaredMadeForKids: set.kids === true } }) }); } catch (e) { const er = new Error('YouTube could not be reached. Try again in a minute.'); er.ytRetry = true; throw er; } if (!r.ok) throw await _ytReadError(r); const uri = String(r.headers.get('Location') || ''); if (uri.indexOf('https://') !== 0) throw new Error('YouTube did not accept the upload.'); return uri; } // Where an upload has got to, read from YouTube's reply to a piece (or to asking). function _ytNextOffset(r) { const m = String(r.headers.get('Range') || '').match(/bytes=0-(\d+)/); return m ? Number(m[1]) + 1 : 0; } // Sends as many pieces as fit in the time allowed. Answers { done, id } when YouTube has the // whole file, otherwise { offset } to carry on from, with resync set after a hiccup so the // next run asks YouTube how much it actually received before sending more. async function _ytPush(env, access, key, type, cur, deadline) { const size = Number(cur.size || 0), uri = String(cur.uploadUri || ''); const piece = Math.max(1, Number(env.YT_CHUNK_BYTES || YT_CHUNK_BYTES)); let offset = Number(cur.offset || 0); const finish = async function (r) { let j = null; try { j = await r.json(); } catch (e) {} return { done: true, id: String((j && j.id) || '') }; }; if (cur.resync) { let r; try { r = await fetch(uri, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + access, 'Content-Range': 'bytes */' + size } }); } catch (e) { return { offset: offset, resync: true }; } if (r.status === 200 || r.status === 201) return await finish(r); if (r.status === 308) offset = _ytNextOffset(r); else if (r.status === 404) { const er = new Error('expired'); er.ytExpired = true; throw er; } else if (r.status >= 500) return { offset: offset, resync: true }; else throw await _ytReadError(r); } while (offset < size) { if (Date.now() > deadline) return { offset: offset }; const len = Math.min(piece, size - offset); const obj = await env.MEDIA.get(key, { range: { offset: offset, length: len } }); if (!obj) throw new Error('The video file is no longer on the server. Add it again and post again.'); const bytes = await obj.arrayBuffer(); let r; try { r = await fetch(uri, { method: 'PUT', body: bytes, // The length of each piece is set by the runtime from the bytes themselves. headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + access, 'Content-Type': type, 'Content-Range': 'bytes ' + offset + '-' + (offset + bytes.byteLength - 1) + '/' + size } }); } catch (e) { return { offset: offset, resync: true }; } if (r.status === 200 || r.status === 201) return await finish(r); if (r.status === 308) { offset = _ytNextOffset(r); continue; } if (r.status === 404) { const er = new Error('expired'); er.ytExpired = true; throw er; } if (r.status >= 500) return { offset: offset, resync: true }; throw await _ytReadError(r); } // Every piece has gone but YouTube has not confirmed: ask it. return { offset: offset, resync: true }; } // POST /api/v1/social/channels/remove { id } async function apiSocialChannelRemove(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const id = String(body.id || ''); let doc = null; try { doc = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId); } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not remove that just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } if (!doc) return _socialJson({ ok: true, channels: [] }, 200, cors); try { if (id.indexOf('ig_') === 0) { const igId = id.slice(3); const accounts = (Array.isArray(doc.igAccounts) ? doc.igAccounts : []).filter(function (a) { return String(a.id) !== igId; }); const keys = (doc.igEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.igEnc) : null) || { tokens: {} }; if (keys.tokens) delete keys.tokens[igId]; const patch = { igAccounts: accounts, hasIg: accounts.length > 0, igGrantIds: accounts.map(function (a) { return String(a.grantId || ''); }).filter(function (g, i, arr) { return g && arr.indexOf(g) === i; }), igEnc: accounts.length ? await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) : '' }; await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId, patch, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); Object.assign(doc, patch); } else if (id.indexOf('tt_') === 0) { const openId = id.slice(3); const accounts = (Array.isArray(doc.ttAccounts) ? doc.ttAccounts : []).filter(function (a) { return String(a.id) !== openId; }); const keys = (doc.ttEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.ttEnc) : null) || { tokens: {} }; // TikTok is told the access is over, so NestTrack drops off the account's list of apps. const t = keys.tokens && keys.tokens[openId]; if (t && t.a) { try { await _ttOauth(env, '/v2/oauth/revoke/', { token: String(t.a) }); } catch (e) { console.log('social tiktok revoke:', e && e.message); } } if (keys.tokens) delete keys.tokens[openId]; const patch = { ttAccounts: accounts, hasTt: accounts.length > 0, ttEnc: accounts.length ? await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) : '' }; await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId, patch, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); Object.assign(doc, patch); } else if (id.indexOf('yt_') === 0) { const chanId = id.slice(3); const accounts = (Array.isArray(doc.ytAccounts) ? doc.ytAccounts : []).filter(function (a) { return String(a.id) !== chanId; }); const keys = (doc.ytEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.ytEnc) : null) || { tokens: {} }; // Google is told the access is over, so NestTrack drops off the account's list of apps. const t = keys.tokens && keys.tokens[chanId]; if (t && (t.r || t.a)) { try { await _ytOauth(env, GOOGLE_REVOKE_URL, { token: String(t.r || t.a) }); } catch (e) { console.log('social youtube revoke:', e && e.message); } } if (keys.tokens) delete keys.tokens[chanId]; const patch = { ytAccounts: accounts, hasYt: accounts.length > 0, ytEnc: accounts.length ? await _socialEncrypt(env, keys) : '' }; await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId, patch, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); Object.assign(doc, patch); } else if (id.indexOf('fb_') === 0) { // Facebook grants access to all the shared Pages together, so removing the last one // (or the only one) ends the Facebook connection and revokes it at Meta. const pageId = id.slice(3); const pages = (Array.isArray(doc.metaPages) ? doc.metaPages : []).filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) !== pageId; }); if (!pages.length) { try { const k = doc.metaEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.metaEnc) : null; if (k && k.userToken && doc.metaUserId) await _metaCall('DELETE', '/' + encodeURIComponent(String(doc.metaUserId)) + '/permissions', { access_token: k.userToken }); } catch (e) { console.log('social fb revoke:', e && e.message); } const patch = { metaPages: [], metaEnc: '', metaUserId: '', metaUserName: '', metaSelectedPageId: '' }; await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId, patch, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); Object.assign(doc, patch); } else { const k = (doc.metaEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, doc.metaEnc) : null) || { pages: [] }; k.pages = (k.pages || []).filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) !== pageId; }); const patch = { metaPages: pages, metaEnc: await _socialEncrypt(env, k) }; await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId, patch, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); Object.assign(doc, patch); } } else { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That channel was not found.' }, 400, cors); } } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not remove that just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } return _socialJson({ ok: true, channels: _socialChannels(doc) }, 200, cors); } // ── Posting schedule ── // The agent sets the days and times they like to post once. "Add to queue" then drops a post // into the next open one, so nobody has to pick a date and time for every post. // A slot is stored as "-" in the agent's own time zone. const SOCIAL_MAX_SLOTS = 42; function _socialCleanSlots(list) { const out = []; (Array.isArray(list) ? list : []).forEach(function (v) { const m = String(v || '').match(/^[0-6]-([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/); if (m && out.indexOf(m[0]) === -1) out.push(m[0]); }); return out.sort().slice(0, SOCIAL_MAX_SLOTS); } // The moments the schedule names after fromMs, soonest first. function _socialUpcomingSlots(doc, fromMs, days, max) { const slots = _socialCleanSlots(doc && doc.schedSlots); const tz = _mcpTzOk(doc && doc.schedTz) || 'UTC'; const out = []; if (!slots.length) return out; const start = _mcpYmd(fromMs, tz).split('-').map(Number); for (let i = 0; i <= days && out.length < max; i++) { const day = new Date(Date.UTC(start[0], start[1] - 1, start[2] + i)); const ymd = day.toISOString().slice(0, 10), dow = day.getUTCDay(); for (const sl of slots) { if (+sl.charAt(0) !== dow) continue; const ms = _mcpLocalToMs(ymd, sl.slice(2), tz); if (ms > fromMs && out.length < max) out.push(ms); } } return out; } function _socialSchedulePublic(doc) { return { tz: _mcpTzOk(doc && doc.schedTz) || '', slots: _socialCleanSlots(doc && doc.schedSlots), upcoming: _socialUpcomingSlots(doc, Date.now() + 2 * 60 * 1000, 14, 60) }; } // POST /api/v1/social/schedule { slots:["1-09:00", ...], tz:"America/New_York" } async function apiSocialScheduleSave(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const tz = _mcpTzOk(String(body.tz || '')); if (!tz) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Your time zone could not be read. Reload NestTrack and try again.' }, 400, cors); const given = Array.isArray(body.slots) ? body.slots : []; const slots = _socialCleanSlots(given); if (given.length > SOCIAL_MAX_SLOTS) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'A schedule can have up to ' + SOCIAL_MAX_SLOTS + ' posting times a week.' }, 400, cors); const patch = { agentId: w.agentId, schedSlots: slots, schedTz: tz }; try { await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId, patch, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not save your schedule just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } return _socialJson({ ok: true, schedule: _socialSchedulePublic(patch) }, 200, cors); } // ── Uploads ── // POST /api/v1/social/upload/start { name, size, type } async function apiSocialUploadStart(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!env.MEDIA) return _socialNoStorage(cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const type = String(body.type || '').toLowerCase(); const ext = SOCIAL_MEDIA_TYPES[type]; const size = Number(body.size || 0); if (!ext) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That file type is not supported. Use a JPEG photo or an MP4 or MOV video.' }, 400, cors); if (!(size > 0)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That file looks empty. Choose it again.' }, 400, cors); if (ext === 'jpg' && size > SOCIAL_MAX_IMAGE_BYTES) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That photo is over 8 MB, which is more than Instagram accepts.' }, 400, cors); if (size > SOCIAL_MAX_BYTES) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That video is over 1 GB. Export a smaller version and try again.' }, 400, cors); const key = 'social/' + w.agentId + '/' + _socialRand(16) + '.' + ext; try { const mpu = await env.MEDIA.createMultipartUpload(key, { httpMetadata: { contentType: type } }); return _socialJson({ ok: true, key: key, uploadId: mpu.uploadId, partSize: SOCIAL_PART_BYTES, url: _socialMediaOrigin(env) + '/' + key }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { console.log('social upload start:', e && e.message); return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not start the upload. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } } // POST /api/v1/social/upload/part?key=&uploadId=&n= (body: the raw bytes of one chunk) async function apiSocialUploadPart(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!env.MEDIA) return _socialNoStorage(cors); const q = new URL(request.url).searchParams; const key = String(q.get('key') || ''), uploadId = String(q.get('uploadId') || ''); const n = parseInt(q.get('n'), 10); if (!_socialOwnKey(w.agentId, key) || !uploadId || !(n >= 1 && n <= 1000)) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That upload is not valid. Start it again.' }, 400, cors); } try { const part = await env.MEDIA.resumeMultipartUpload(key, uploadId).uploadPart(n, request.body); return _socialJson({ ok: true, partNumber: part.partNumber, etag: part.etag }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { console.log('social upload part:', e && e.message); return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Part of the upload did not go through. Try again.' }, 503, cors); } } // POST /api/v1/social/upload/complete { key, uploadId, parts:[{partNumber, etag}] } // POST /api/v1/social/upload/abort { key, uploadId } async function apiSocialUploadFinish(request, env, cors, abort) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!env.MEDIA) return _socialNoStorage(cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const key = String(body.key || ''), uploadId = String(body.uploadId || ''); if (!_socialOwnKey(w.agentId, key) || !uploadId) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That upload is not valid. Start it again.' }, 400, cors); const mpu = env.MEDIA.resumeMultipartUpload(key, uploadId); if (abort) { try { await mpu.abort(); } catch (e) {} return _socialJson({ ok: true }, 200, cors); } const parts = (Array.isArray(body.parts) ? body.parts : []) .map(function (p) { return { partNumber: parseInt(p.partNumber, 10), etag: String(p.etag || '') }; }) .filter(function (p) { return p.partNumber >= 1 && p.etag; }) .sort(function (a, b) { return a.partNumber - b.partNumber; }); if (!parts.length) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Nothing was uploaded. Choose the file again.' }, 400, cors); try { await mpu.complete(parts); const head = await env.MEDIA.head(key); const size = head ? Number(head.size || 0) : 0; if (!size || size > SOCIAL_MAX_BYTES) { try { await env.MEDIA.delete(key); } catch (e) {} return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That file did not upload correctly. Choose it again.' }, 400, cors); } return _socialJson({ ok: true, key: key, size: size, url: _socialMediaOrigin(env) + '/' + key }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { console.log('social upload complete:', e && e.message); return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'The upload could not be finished. Try again.' }, 503, cors); } } // ── Posts ── function _socialResKey(channelId) { return String(channelId).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_]/g, '_'); } // What the app sees of a post. No keys; media addresses only while the files are kept. function _socialPostPublic(env, id, d) { const res = d.results || {}; // Posts made before accounts became separate channels are stored in an older shape. if (!Array.isArray(d.channels) && Array.isArray(d.targets)) { d = Object.assign({}, d, { channels: d.targets.map(function (t) { return { id: t, type: t, name: t === 'instagram' ? (d.igUsername ? '@' + d.igUsername : 'Instagram') : (d.pageName || 'Facebook Page') }; }), media: d.mediaKey ? [{ key: d.mediaKey, kind: 'video' }] : [] }); } return { id: id, status: d.status || 'scheduled', caption: d.caption || '', scheduledAt: Number(d.scheduledAt || 0), publishedAt: Number(d.publishedAt || 0) || null, createdAt: Number(d.createdAt || 0), queued: d.queued === true, // True on a draft whose files were cleared out after two weeks untouched. mediaExpired: d.mediaExpired === true, channels: (Array.isArray(d.channels) ? d.channels : []).map(function (c) { const r = res[_socialResKey(c.id)] || {}; const o = { id: c.id, type: c.type, name: c.name || '', state: d.status === 'draft' ? 'draft' : (r.state || 'pending'), error: r.error || '', link: r.link || '', note: r.note || '' }; // The TikTok choices travel with the post so it can be opened and edited again. if (c.type === 'tiktok') o.tiktok = (d.tiktok && d.tiktok[_socialResKey(c.id)]) || null; if (c.type === 'youtube') { o.youtube = (d.youtube && d.youtube[_socialResKey(c.id)]) || null; // How far a video has got on its way to YouTube, so the list can say so. if (r.state === 'processing' && Number(r.size || 0) > 0) o.progress = Math.min(99, Math.floor(100 * Number(r.offset || 0) / Number(r.size))); } return o; }), media: (Array.isArray(d.media) ? d.media : []).map(function (m) { return { key: m.key, kind: m.kind, url: d.mediaKept === false ? '' : _socialMediaOrigin(env) + '/' + m.key }; }) }; } function _socialOverall(res) { const states = Object.keys(res).map(function (k) { return res[k].state; }); if (!states.length) return 'failed'; if (states.some(function (s) { return s === 'pending' || s === 'processing'; })) return 'publishing'; if (states.every(function (s) { return s === 'done'; })) return 'done'; if (states.every(function (s) { return s === 'failed'; })) return 'failed'; return 'partial'; } function _socialErrText(e) { return String((e && e.message) || 'It did not go through.').slice(0, 300); } // One Facebook Page: a video, one photo, several photos, or words alone. async function _socialPublishFb(d, pageId, pageToken, urls) { const pid = encodeURIComponent(pageId); const caption = String(d.caption || ''); const media = Array.isArray(d.media) ? d.media : []; if (!media.length) { const out = await _metaCall('POST', '/' + pid + '/feed', { message: caption, access_token: pageToken }); return { id: String(out.id || ''), link: out.id ? 'https://www.facebook.com/' + encodeURIComponent(String(out.id)) : '' }; } if (media[0].kind === 'video') { const out = await _metaCall('POST', 'https://graph-video.facebook.com/' + META_SOCIAL_VERSION + '/' + pid + '/videos', { file_url: urls[0], description: caption, access_token: pageToken }); return { id: String(out.id || ''), link: out.id ? 'https://www.facebook.com/' + pid + '/videos/' + encodeURIComponent(out.id) : '' }; } if (media.length === 1) { const out = await _metaCall('POST', '/' + pid + '/photos', { url: urls[0], caption: caption, access_token: pageToken }); const postId = String(out.post_id || out.id || ''); return { id: postId, link: postId ? 'https://www.facebook.com/' + encodeURIComponent(postId) : '' }; } // Several photos: each is uploaded unpublished, then one post gathers them. const params = { message: caption, access_token: pageToken }; for (let i = 0; i < urls.length; i++) { const ph = await _metaCall('POST', '/' + pid + '/photos', { url: urls[i], published: 'false', access_token: pageToken }); params['attached_media[' + i + ']'] = JSON.stringify({ media_fbid: String(ph.id) }); } const out = await _metaCall('POST', '/' + pid + '/feed', params); return { id: String(out.id || ''), link: out.id ? 'https://www.facebook.com/' + encodeURIComponent(String(out.id)) : '' }; } // One Instagram account, step one: hand Instagram the media. It answers with a container // that it then prepares; the post is published once that reports it is finished. async function _socialIgCreate(d, igId, tok, urls) { const path = '/' + encodeURIComponent(igId) + '/media'; const caption = String(d.caption || ''); const media = Array.isArray(d.media) ? d.media : []; if (media.length === 1) { const p = media[0].kind === 'video' ? { media_type: 'REELS', video_url: urls[0], caption: caption, access_token: tok } : { image_url: urls[0], caption: caption, access_token: tok }; return String((await _metaCall('POST', path, p)).id || ''); } const kids = []; for (let i = 0; i < urls.length; i++) { const c = await _metaCall('POST', path, { image_url: urls[i], is_carousel_item: 'true', access_token: tok }); kids.push(String(c.id)); } return String((await _metaCall('POST', path, { media_type: 'CAROUSEL', children: kids.join(','), caption: caption, access_token: tok })).id || ''); } // Moves one post forward as far as it can go right now, and saves where it got to. // Safe to call again: each channel remembers its own state, so nothing is posted twice. async function _socialAdvance(env, token, id, d, budgetMs) { const res = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(d.results || {})); const now = Date.now(); // How long this run may spend sending a video to YouTube before it saves its place. const deadline = now + (budgetMs || 40000); const urls = (Array.isArray(d.media) ? d.media : []).map(function (m) { return _socialMediaOrigin(env) + '/' + m.key; }); let fbKeys = null, igKeys = null; try { const acct = await fsGetDoc(token, 'social_accounts/' + d.agentId); fbKeys = acct && acct.metaEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, acct.metaEnc) : null; igKeys = acct && acct.igEnc ? await _socialDecrypt(env, acct.igEnc) : null; } catch (e) {} const gone = 'This account is no longer connected. Add it again in Social, then post again.'; for (const c of (Array.isArray(d.channels) ? d.channels : [])) { const k = _socialResKey(c.id); const r = res[k] || { state: 'pending' }; if (r.state !== 'pending' && r.state !== 'processing') continue; const target = String(c.id).slice(3); if (c.type === 'facebook') { const pg = fbKeys && (fbKeys.pages || []).find(function (x) { return String(x.id) === target; }); if (!pg || !pg.token) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: gone }; continue; } try { const out = await _socialPublishFb(d, target, String(pg.token), urls); res[k] = { state: 'done', id: out.id, link: out.link, at: now }; } catch (e) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(e) }; } continue; } if (c.type === 'youtube') { let access = ''; try { access = await _ytToken(env, token, String(d.agentId), target); } catch (e) { if (r.state === 'pending' || (e && e.ytGone) || (e && e.ytReason) || now - Number(r.startedAt || now) > SOCIAL_YT_WAIT_MS) res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(e) }; continue; } const set = _ytCleanSettings(d.youtube && d.youtube[k]); const m0 = (Array.isArray(d.media) ? d.media : [])[0] || {}; const type = /\.mov$/.test(String(m0.key || '')) ? 'video/quicktime' : 'video/mp4'; let cur = r; try { if (cur.state === 'pending') { const head = env.MEDIA ? await env.MEDIA.head(m0.key).catch(function () { return null; }) : null; const size = head ? Number(head.size || 0) : 0; if (!size) throw new Error('The video file is no longer on the server. Add it again and post again.'); cur = res[k] = { state: 'processing', uploadUri: await _ytBegin(access, d, set, size, type), offset: 0, size: size, startedAt: now, restarts: Number(r.restarts || 0) }; } const out = await _ytPush(env, access, String(m0.key), type, cur, deadline); if (out.done) { res[k] = { state: 'done', id: out.id, at: now, link: out.id ? 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=' + encodeURIComponent(out.id) : '', note: 'Uploaded. YouTube takes a few minutes to process a video before it can be watched.' }; } else if (now - Number(cur.startedAt || now) > SOCIAL_YT_WAIT_MS) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: 'The upload to YouTube did not finish. Try posting it again.' }; } else { res[k] = Object.assign({}, cur, { offset: out.offset, resync: out.resync === true }); } } catch (e) { if (e && e.ytExpired && Number(cur.restarts || 0) < 2) { // YouTube let go of the upload part-way. It is started again from the beginning, twice at most. res[k] = { state: 'pending', restarts: Number(cur.restarts || 0) + 1 }; } else if (e && e.ytRetry && cur.state === 'processing') { res[k] = cur; } else { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: (e && e.ytExpired) ? 'The upload to YouTube kept being cut off. Try posting it again.' : _socialErrText(e) }; } } continue; } if (c.type === 'tiktok') { let access = ''; try { access = await _ttToken(env, token, String(d.agentId), target); } catch (e) { // Losing the connection ends it. Anything else while a video is on its way is waited out. if (r.state === 'pending' || (e && e.ttGone) || (e && e.ttCode) || now - Number(r.startedAt || now) > SOCIAL_TT_WAIT_MS) res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(e) }; continue; } const set = _ttCleanSettings(d.tiktok && d.tiktok[k]); if (r.state === 'pending') { try { const pid = await _socialTtCreate(d, set, access, urls); if (!pid) throw new Error('TikTok did not accept the video.'); res[k] = { state: 'processing', publishId: pid, startedAt: now, mode: set.mode }; } catch (e) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(e) }; continue; } } const cur = res[k]; const name = String(c.name || '').charAt(0) === '@' ? String(c.name).slice(1) : ''; const profile = name ? 'https://www.tiktok.com/@' + encodeURIComponent(name) : ''; try { const st = await _ttCall('POST', '/v2/post/publish/status/fetch/', access, { publish_id: cur.publishId }); const code = String(st.status || ''); if (code === 'PUBLISH_COMPLETE') { // (TikTok's own spelling.) Only a public post that has cleared its checks has an id to link to. const ids = Array.isArray(st.publicaly_available_post_id) ? st.publicaly_available_post_id : []; res[k] = { state: 'done', id: ids.length ? String(ids[0]) : '', at: now, link: ids.length && profile ? profile + '/video/' + encodeURIComponent(String(ids[0])) : profile, note: ids.length ? '' : 'Posted. It can take a few minutes to show on the profile.' }; } else if (code === 'SEND_TO_USER_INBOX' && cur.mode === 'draft') { res[k] = { state: 'done', id: '', link: '', at: now, note: 'Sent to your TikTok inbox. Open the notification in the TikTok app to finish and post it.' }; } else if (code === 'FAILED') { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(_ttErr(String(st.fail_reason || ''), 'TikTok could not publish this video.')) }; } else if (now - Number(cur.startedAt || now) > SOCIAL_TT_WAIT_MS) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: 'TikTok took too long to process this. Try posting it again.' }; } } catch (e) { if (now - Number(cur.startedAt || now) > SOCIAL_TT_WAIT_MS) res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(e) }; } continue; } const tok = igKeys && igKeys.tokens ? String(igKeys.tokens[target] || '') : ''; if (!tok) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: gone }; continue; } if (r.state === 'pending') { try { const cid = await _socialIgCreate(d, target, tok, urls); if (!cid) throw new Error('Instagram did not accept the media.'); res[k] = { state: 'processing', containerId: cid, startedAt: now }; } catch (e) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(e) }; continue; } } // Photos are ready almost at once, so this is checked straight away as well as each minute. const cur = res[k]; try { const st = await _metaCall('GET', '/' + encodeURIComponent(cur.containerId), { fields: 'status_code,status', access_token: tok }); const code = String(st.status_code || ''); if (code === 'FINISHED') { const pub = await _metaCall('POST', '/' + encodeURIComponent(target) + '/media_publish', { creation_id: cur.containerId, access_token: tok }); let link = ''; try { link = String((await _metaCall('GET', '/' + encodeURIComponent(pub.id), { fields: 'permalink', access_token: tok })).permalink || ''); } catch (e) {} res[k] = { state: 'done', id: String(pub.id || ''), link: link, at: now }; } else if (code === 'PUBLISHED') { res[k] = { state: 'done', id: '', link: '', at: now }; } else if (code === 'ERROR' || code === 'EXPIRED') { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: 'Instagram could not use this' + (st.status ? ': ' + String(st.status).slice(0, 200) : '.') + ' Check the file type, size and length.' }; } else if (now - Number(cur.startedAt || now) > SOCIAL_IG_WAIT_MS) { res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: 'Instagram took too long to prepare this. Try posting it again.' }; } } catch (e) { // A hiccup while checking is not a failed post. Keep waiting until the time limit. if (now - Number(cur.startedAt || now) > SOCIAL_IG_WAIT_MS) res[k] = { state: 'failed', error: _socialErrText(e) }; } } const status = _socialOverall(res); const patch = { results: res, status: status, active: status === 'publishing', lockUntil: 0, updatedAt: now }; if (status !== 'publishing' && !d.publishedAt) patch.publishedAt = now; await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_posts/' + id, patch); return Object.assign({}, d, patch); } // POST /api/v1/social/posts // { id?, channels:[ids], caption, media:[keys], draft, scheduledAt, when, // tiktok:{ : choices }, youtube:{ : choices } } // when: "queue" takes the next open time in the agent's posting schedule; "next" takes the // very next one and moves the rest of the queue back a place. Otherwise scheduledAt is the // time, and with neither it is posted now. // With an id it replaces a draft or a post that is still waiting for its time. async function apiSocialPostSave(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); if (!env.MEDIA) return _socialNoStorage(cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const draft = body.draft === true; const caption = String(body.caption || '').trim(); const keys = (Array.isArray(body.media) ? body.media : []).map(function (m) { return String((m && m.key) || m || ''); }) .filter(function (k, i, a) { return k && a.indexOf(k) === i; }); const wanted = (Array.isArray(body.channels) ? body.channels : []).map(String).filter(function (c, i, a) { return a.indexOf(c) === i; }); for (const k of keys) { if (!_socialOwnKey(w.agentId, k)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'One of those files is not yours to post. Add it again.' }, 400, cors); } if (caption.length > SOCIAL_CAPTION_MAX) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'The caption is ' + caption.length + ' characters. Instagram allows ' + SOCIAL_CAPTION_MAX + '.' }, 400, cors); const media = keys.map(function (k) { return { key: k, kind: _socialKind(k) }; }); const videos = media.filter(function (m) { return m.kind === 'video'; }).length; if (videos > 1 || (videos === 1 && media.length > 1)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'A post can have one video, or up to ' + SOCIAL_MAX_PHOTOS + ' photos, but not both.' }, 400, cors); if (media.length > SOCIAL_MAX_PHOTOS) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'A post can have up to ' + SOCIAL_MAX_PHOTOS + ' photos.' }, 400, cors); const acct = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'social_accounts/' + w.agentId).catch(function () { return null; }); const all = _socialChannels(acct); const channels = []; for (const cid of wanted) { const ch = all.find(function (c) { return c.id === cid; }); if (!ch) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'One of those accounts is no longer connected. Reload and choose again.' }, 400, cors); channels.push({ id: ch.id, type: ch.type, name: ch.name }); } // TikTok asks for a handful of choices per post (who can view it and so on). They are // kept per account, since two TikTok accounts can allow different things. const ttSet = {}; const ttGiven = body.tiktok && typeof body.tiktok === 'object' ? body.tiktok : {}; channels.forEach(function (c) { if (c.type === 'tiktok') ttSet[_socialResKey(c.id)] = _ttCleanSettings(ttGiven[c.id]); }); // YouTube needs a title of its own, a visibility, and whether the video is made for kids. const ytSet = {}; const ytGiven = body.youtube && typeof body.youtube === 'object' ? body.youtube : {}; channels.forEach(function (c) { if (c.type === 'youtube') ytSet[_socialResKey(c.id)] = _ytCleanSettings(ytGiven[c.id]); }); if (!draft) { if (!channels.length) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Choose at least one account to post to.' }, 400, cors); if (!media.length && !caption) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Add a photo, a video, or some words.' }, 400, cors); if (!media.length && channels.some(function (c) { return c.type === 'instagram'; })) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Instagram needs a photo or a video. Add one, or untick Instagram.' }, 400, cors); for (const c of channels) { const ch = all.find(function (x) { return x.id === c.id; }); if (ch.canPost === false) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Your role on ' + ch.name + ' does not allow posting.' }, 400, cors); if (ch.needsReconnect && (ch.type === 'instagram' || ch.type === 'tiktok')) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: ch.name + ' needs to be added again before it can post.' }, 400, cors); if (ch.type === 'youtube') { if (!_ytConfigured(env)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'YouTube is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); if (media.length !== 1 || media[0].kind !== 'video') return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'YouTube needs one video. Add a video, or untick YouTube.' }, 400, cors); const y = ytSet[_socialResKey(c.id)]; if (!y.title) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Give the video a title for YouTube (' + ch.name + ').' }, 400, cors); if (y.title.length > YT_TITLE_MAX) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'The YouTube title is ' + y.title.length + ' characters. YouTube allows ' + YT_TITLE_MAX + '.' }, 400, cors); if (/[<>]/.test(y.title) || /[<>]/.test(caption)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'YouTube does not accept the < or > symbols in a title or description. Take them out, or untick YouTube.' }, 400, cors); if (!y.privacy) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Choose who can see this on YouTube (' + ch.name + ').' }, 400, cors); if (y.kids === null) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Say whether this video is made for kids. YouTube asks for it on every upload.' }, 400, cors); continue; } if (ch.type !== 'tiktok') continue; if (!_ttConfigured(env)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'TikTok is not set up on the server yet.' }, 503, cors); if (media.length !== 1 || media[0].kind !== 'video') return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'TikTok needs one video. Add a video, or untick TikTok.' }, 400, cors); const s = ttSet[_socialResKey(c.id)]; if ((ch.modes || []).indexOf(s.mode) === -1) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: ch.name + ' did not give NestTrack permission to ' + (s.mode === 'draft' ? 'send drafts' : 'post directly') + '. Remove it and add it again, leaving every permission on.' }, 400, cors); if (s.mode !== 'post') continue; if (!s.privacy) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Choose who can view this on TikTok (' + ch.name + ').' }, 400, cors); if (s.commercial && !s.yourBrand && !s.branded) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'You marked this as commercial content. Say whether it promotes your own brand, a third party, or both.' }, 400, cors); if (s.branded && s.privacy === 'SELF_ONLY') return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Branded content visibility cannot be set to private. Choose another option for who can view it.' }, 400, cors); } for (const m of media) { const head = await env.MEDIA.head(m.key).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!head) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'One of the files is no longer on the server. Add it again.' }, 400, cors); } } const now = Date.now(); const mode = draft ? '' : String(body.when || ''); let at = Number(body.scheduledAt || 0); let queued = false; const shifts = []; if (mode === 'queue' || mode === 'next') { const open = _socialUpcomingSlots(acct, now + 2 * 60 * 1000, 60, 500); if (!open.length) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Set your posting schedule first, or pick a time for this post.' }, 400, cors); let waiting; try { waiting = _mcpRows(await digRunQuery(w.token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'social_posts', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: w.agentId } } }, limit: 400 })).map(function (r) { return Object.assign({ id: _mcpDocId(r) }, digDoc(r.document.fields || {})); }) .filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'scheduled' && p.id !== String(body.id || ''); }); } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not read your queue just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); } const full = 'Your queue is full for the next 60 days. Add more posting times, or pick a time for this post.'; if (mode === 'queue') { const taken = {}; waiting.forEach(function (p) { taken[Number(p.scheduledAt || 0)] = 1; }); at = open.find(function (ms) { return !taken[ms]; }) || 0; if (!at) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: full }, 400, cors); } else { // Posts given their own time stay put; the ones in the queue each move back one place. const fixed = {}; waiting.forEach(function (p) { if (p.queued !== true) fixed[Number(p.scheduledAt || 0)] = 1; }); const free = open.filter(function (ms) { return !fixed[ms]; }); const line = waiting.filter(function (p) { return p.queued === true; }).sort(function (a, b) { return Number(a.scheduledAt || 0) - Number(b.scheduledAt || 0); }); if (free.length < line.length + 1) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: full }, 400, cors); at = free[0]; line.forEach(function (p, i) { if (Number(p.scheduledAt || 0) !== free[i + 1]) shifts.push({ id: p.id, at: free[i + 1] }); }); } queued = true; } const isNow = !draft && !at; if (isNow) at = now; else if (!draft && !queued) { if (at < now + 2 * 60 * 1000) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Pick a time at least a few minutes from now, or choose Post now.' }, 400, cors); if (at > now + 60 * 86400000) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Posts can be scheduled up to 60 days ahead.' }, 400, cors); } let id = String(body.id || ''); let prev = null; if (id) { if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9]{8,40}$/.test(id)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That post was not found.' }, 400, cors); prev = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'social_posts/' + id).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!prev || prev.agentId !== w.agentId) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That post was not found.' }, 404, cors); if (prev.status !== 'draft' && prev.status !== 'scheduled') return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'This post has already gone out, so it can no longer be changed here.' }, 409, cors); // Files dropped from the post in this edit are no longer needed. const keep = {}; keys.forEach(function (k) { keep[k] = 1; }); for (const m of (Array.isArray(prev.media) ? prev.media : [])) { if (!keep[m.key]) { try { await env.MEDIA.delete(m.key); } catch (e) {} } } } else { id = _mcpNewId(); } const results = {}; channels.forEach(function (c) { results[_socialResKey(c.id)] = { state: 'pending' }; }); // A draft whose files were cleared out stays marked that way until different files are added. const stillGone = !!(prev && prev.mediaExpired === true && keys.length && (Array.isArray(prev.media) ? prev.media : []).some(function (m) { return keys.indexOf(m.key) !== -1; })); const doc = { agentId: w.agentId, active: !draft, status: draft ? 'draft' : (isNow ? 'publishing' : 'scheduled'), scheduledAt: at || 0, createdAt: prev ? Number(prev.createdAt || now) : now, updatedAt: now, publishedAt: 0, // "Post now" is worked on straight away below, so the minute job is told to leave it be. lockUntil: isNow ? now + 120000 : 0, caption: caption, media: media, mediaKept: media.length > 0 && !stillGone, mediaExpired: stillGone, channels: channels, results: results, tiktok: ttSet, youtube: ytSet, // In the queue (as opposed to given its own time): these are the ones "post next" moves. // An edit that leaves the time alone keeps the post's place. queued: queued || !!(!draft && prev && prev.queued === true && at === Number(prev.scheduledAt || 0)) }; await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_posts/' + id, doc); for (const sh of shifts) { try { await _mcpPatch(w.token, 'social_posts/' + sh.id, { scheduledAt: sh.at, updatedAt: now }); } catch (e) { console.log('social queue shift:', e && e.message); } } let out = doc; if (isNow) { // A video bound for YouTube is sent from here in pieces. This request sends what it can in // twenty seconds; the minute job carries on with the rest. try { out = await _socialAdvance(env, w.token, id, doc, 20000); } catch (e) { console.log('social post now:', e && e.message); } } return _socialJson({ ok: true, post: _socialPostPublic(env, id, out) }, 200, cors); } // GET /api/v1/social/posts async function apiSocialPostList(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let rows; try { rows = _mcpRows(await digRunQuery(w.token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'social_posts', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: w.agentId } } }, limit: 400 })); } catch (e) { return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not load your posts just now.' }, 503, cors); } const posts = rows.map(function (r) { return _socialPostPublic(env, _mcpDocId(r), digDoc(r.document.fields || {})); }) .sort(function (a, b) { return ((b.scheduledAt || b.createdAt) || 0) - ((a.scheduledAt || a.createdAt) || 0); }) .slice(0, 150); return _socialJson({ ok: true, posts: posts }, 200, cors); } // POST /api/v1/social/posts/delete { id } // Deletes a draft, cancels a scheduled post, or clears a finished one from the list. It does // not take down anything already published: that is done on Facebook or Instagram itself. async function apiSocialPostDelete(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const id = String(body.id || ''); if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9]{8,40}$/.test(id)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That post was not found.' }, 400, cors); const d = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'social_posts/' + id).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!d || d.agentId !== w.agentId) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That post was not found.' }, 404, cors); if (d.status === 'publishing') return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'This one is being published right now. Give it a few minutes.' }, 409, cors); if (d.mediaKept && env.MEDIA) { for (const m of (Array.isArray(d.media) ? d.media : [])) { try { await env.MEDIA.delete(m.key); } catch (e) {} } if (d.mediaKey) { try { await env.MEDIA.delete(d.mediaKey); } catch (e) {} } } await _mcpDelete(w.token, 'social_posts/' + id); return _socialJson({ ok: true }, 200, cors); } // Runs every minute from the worker's schedule. Publishes what is due and keeps checking on // media Instagram is still preparing. Once an hour it also clears out old files. async function socialPublishSweep(token, env) { if (!String(env.META_APP_SECRET || '').trim()) return; const now = Date.now(); let rows = []; try { rows = _mcpRows(await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'social_posts', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'active' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { booleanValue: true } } }, limit: 100 })); } catch (e) { console.log('social sweep query:', e && e.message); return; } let worked = 0; for (const r of rows) { if (worked >= 8) break; // the rest are picked up next minute const id = _mcpDocId(r); const d = digDoc(r.document.fields || {}); if (Number(d.scheduledAt || 0) > now) continue; if (Number(d.lockUntil || 0) > now) continue; try { // Marked first, so an overlapping run leaves it alone. await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_posts/' + id, { lockUntil: now + 120000, status: 'publishing' }); await _socialAdvance(env, token, id, d); worked++; } catch (e) { console.log('social sweep post ' + id + ':', e && e.message); } } if (new Date(now).getUTCMinutes() !== 0) return; if (env.MEDIA) { try { const kept = _mcpRows(await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'social_posts', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'mediaKept' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { booleanValue: true } } }, limit: 200 })); for (const r of kept) { const d = digDoc(r.document.fields || {}); const st = String(d.status || ''); // Posts still on their way keep their files until they have gone out. if (d.active === true || st === 'scheduled' || st === 'publishing') continue; const isDraft = st === 'draft'; if (isDraft) { if (now - Number(d.updatedAt || d.createdAt || now) < SOCIAL_KEEP_DRAFT_MS) continue; } else { const keepMs = st === 'done' ? SOCIAL_KEEP_MEDIA_MS : SOCIAL_KEEP_FAILED_MS; if (now - Number(d.publishedAt || d.updatedAt || now) < keepMs) continue; } for (const m of (Array.isArray(d.media) ? d.media : [])) { try { await env.MEDIA.delete(m.key); } catch (e) {} } if (d.mediaKey) { try { await env.MEDIA.delete(d.mediaKey); } catch (e) {} } // A draft keeps its words; mediaExpired is what lets the app say "add the file again". const gone = isDraft ? { mediaKept: false, mediaExpired: true } : { mediaKept: false }; try { await _mcpPatch(token, 'social_posts/' + _mcpDocId(r), gone); } catch (e) {} } } catch (e) { console.log('social media purge:', e && e.message); } } } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // CONTENT PLANNER — a month of short-form videos // // The agent fills in a short content profile (their market, their communities, how they // talk). Their own assistant, connected through the tools above, does the research and the // writing and saves the result here. NestTrack holds the plan and shows it as a board. // // Nothing in here comes from anyone but the agent it belongs to. There is no default market: // with none on file the assistant is told to ask for it before it does anything else. // // One profile per agent (content_profiles/{agentId}); one plan per agent per month // (content_plans/{agentId}_{YYYY-MM}), its videos kept together as one piece of text so a // plan is always read and saved whole. // // The assistant can add videos and change them. It cannot remove one: clearing out the ideas // an agent does not want is done by the agent, in the app, where they see what will go. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const CONTENT_TOOLS = { start_content_plan: 1, save_content_profile: 1, save_content_plan: 1, get_content_plan: 1, update_content_video: 1, start_video_edit: 1 }; const CONTENT_PER_WEEK = [2, 3, 5]; const CONTENT_DEFAULT_PER_WEEK = 3; const CONTENT_MAX_VIDEOS = 40; // the most one month can hold, hand-added ones included const CONTENT_KEEP_MONTHS = 3; // older plans are cleared out const CONTENT_MAX_JSON = 700000; // Firestore allows about 1 MB a document const CONTENT_APP_URL = 'https://app.nesttrack.io/'; const CONTENT_FORMATS = { talking_head: 'Talking head', on_location: 'On location', green_screen: 'Green screen', stat: 'Stat on screen', story: 'Story' }; const CONTENT_MONTHS = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December']; // The video editing method, served to the agent's AI in parts by start_video_edit. Text only; // the AI does the edit in its own workspace with FFmpeg. const VIDEO_EDIT_PARTS = { guide: "## Standing rules\n- **The user gives a one-line brief:** format (vertical / horizontal), length (short / long form), captions (burned / .srt / both), extras (graphics, animated scenes, B-roll, voiceover, music), shorts wanted from it, anything to cut or keep. Whatever they leave out, use the defaults listed above (burned-in captions + .srt, grade, leveled audio; no music, animated scenes, B-roll, name graphic or shorts unless asked) and say in one line what you assumed, plus one line on what they could add (e.g. \"Say 'add music' or 'add my name' if you want them.\"). Ask only if format is unknowable from the footage.\n- Captions: phrase mode — `brand.plate` box, white text, the spoken word lit in `brand.highlight` (set `\"brand\"` in every edit.json from the settings). Vertical: max 5 words, **yPct 0.72** (0.66 sat too high in testing; never below 0.78 — the Reels/TikTok UI covers the bottom 20%). Horizontal: max 6 words, yPct 0.9.\n- Spell names right in captions via `captions.fixes` (never edit the transcript), using `caption_spellings`. Split tokens (\"2 .75 %\", \"four -bedroom\", \"cul -de -sac\") are glued automatically by the compiler.\n- **Never invent numbers or lines.** Graphics only show figures the user says. Unclear words: caption your best reading and list the timestamp for the user to check.\n- Real estate / mortgage content: the lower third or the video description carries the `compliance` line when it's set. Flag claims about named or recognizable people, and big public numbers (homes sold, rates), for the user to confirm — don't cut them yourself.\n- **Music must be licensed for YouTube/social** (their own, or from a library they pay for or that is free to use, such as the YouTube Audio Library). If the user drops a track without saying where it's from, ask once — an unlicensed song can get the video muted or claimed.\n- Deliver drafts for review; a final render only when asked. Never say done before looking at frames (step 7).\n\n## 0. Where to run\n| Job | Where | Why |\n|---|---|---|\n| Clips under ~3 min total, Remotion scenes | **Cloud workspace** (Bash, background runs allowed) | fast setup; Remotion uses the preinstalled Chromium |\n| Long videos (multi-GB, 10+ min) | **The user's computer** via `device_bash` in their connected folder (e.g. a `video studio` folder on the Desktop) | no upload |\n\n**Mac rules (learned the hard way):** each `device_bash` call is capped at 180 s and background processes are killed when the call ends — so everything is resumable and run with a time budget (`--budget 150-160`) in repeated calls. Use `$HOME/vs` for work files, the connected folder only for source + deliverables (`_review/` for check images; stage them with `device_stage_files` to look at them). The Mac VM has FFmpeg 4.4 (use `-show_streams -of json`, not `stream_side_data=` in ffprobe) and Lato fonts (render.js picks Lato automatically; Inter in the cloud). If the folder isn't connected, ask the user to use **+ → Add folder** in the desktop app (requesting `~/Desktop/...` directly can be refused).\n\n## 1. Set up (every new session, on the machine you'll use)\n```bash\nwhich ffmpeg ffprobe node python3; nproc\npip install --break-system-packages -q faster-whisper 2>/dev/null || pip3 install -q faster-whisper\nmkdir -p ~/vs/{engine,src,audio,media,qa,out}\n```\nSpeech model (Whisper small, CTranslate2 int8). Hugging Face is blocked; pull the same weights from npm — data files only, verify the hash:\n```bash\nmkdir -p ~/dl && cd ~/dl\nfor p in 0 1 2 3; do npm pack -q @skillninja/shortmovie-telop-model-p$p >/dev/null; done\nfor t in *.tgz; do d=${t%.tgz}; mkdir -p $d && tar -xzf $t -C $d; done\nM=~/model-small; mkdir -p $M; P0=skillninja-shortmovie-telop-model-p0-1.0.0/package\ncp $P0/{config.json,tokenizer.json,vocabulary.json,preprocessor_config.json} $M/\ncat skillninja-shortmovie-telop-model-p{0,1,2,3}-1.0.0/package/model.bin.part* > $M/model.bin\ntest \"$(sha256sum $M/model.bin | cut -d' ' -f1)\" = \"$(cat $P0/model.sha256)\" && echo MODEL-OK; cd ~ && rm -rf ~/dl\n```\nIf the hash fails or npm is unreachable, stop and tell the user transcription is unavailable this session. Then get the engine files: call start_video_edit with part \"engine_1\", then again with part \"engine_2\". Write each file to `~/vs/engine/` exactly as given, character for character, and run `node --check` on the .js files and `python3 -m py_compile` on ingest.py. On the Mac, write them with quoted heredocs (`cat > file <<'EOF_JS'`).\n\n## 2. Ingest\nCloud: copy clips into `~/vs/src/` (attachments are read-only). Mac: point at the file in the connected folder (`$HOME/mnt//X.mov`).\n```bash\ncd ~/vs && python3 engine/ingest.py src/A.mov src/B.mov # cloud\ncd ~/vs && python3 engine/ingest.py --budget 160 --fast \"$HOME/mnt/video studio/X.mov\" # Mac, repeat until DONE\n```\nB-roll clips, photos and music: `python3 engine/ingest.py --broll src/broll1.mov src/house.jpg src/track.mp3` (probe only, no transcript). Voiceovers (voice memo / m4a / mp3, audio-only) are transcribed normally — they become a source like any clip.\n`--fast` (greedy) for anything over ~5 min: ~1× real time on the Mac. It prints `PARTIAL … run again` or `DONE` + the transcript as `[index|m:ss]` with `‖1.2s` pause markers. Resume restarts at the last full sentence with prior text as context (restarting mid-sentence made Whisper invent words). Read the whole transcript — it's what you edit from. Query exact word indices around every planned cut before writing the edit.\n\n## 3. Look before you cut\n- Contact-sheet every B-roll clip too, and pick each clip's `inMs` from the sheet (steady, well-framed seconds).\n- iPhone clips are usually portrait (rotation −90 → 1080×1920). Long silences in walkthroughs are often the best visuals: contact-sheet them and Read the sheet before cutting:\n `ffmpeg -v error -y -ss 40 -t 32 -i src/X.mov -vf \"fps=1,scale=180:-1,drawtext=text='%{eif\\:t+40\\:d}':fontcolor=yellow:fontsize=28:x=5:y=5,tile=16x2\" -frames:v 1 qa/sheet.jpg`\n- Decide: clip order, retakes (keep the last clean take), stutters/doubled words, filler \"uh\"s, pauses to keep, visual holds, chapters, graphics.\n\n## 4. Write the edit (`~/vs/edit.json`)\n```json\n{\n \"schema\": \"videostudio.edit/1\", \"rev\": 1,\n \"format\": { \"profile\": \"vertical\", \"width\": 1080, \"height\": 1920, \"fps\": 30 },\n \"brand\": { \"plate\": \"#1F2937\", \"highlight\": \"#FDE047\", \"light\": \"#F5F5F4\" },\n \"sources\": [ { \"id\": \"m1\", \"mediaId\": \"IMG_4578\", \"role\": \"presenter\", \"cropX\": 0.5 }, { \"id\": \"m2\", \"mediaId\": \"IMG_4579\", \"role\": \"walkthrough\" },\n { \"id\": \"vo\", \"mediaId\": \"VO_1\", \"role\": \"voiceover\" }, { \"id\": \"b1\", \"mediaId\": \"BROLL_walk\", \"role\": \"broll\" }, { \"id\": \"img\", \"mediaId\": \"house_front\", \"role\": \"broll\" } ],\n \"segments\": [\n { \"id\": \"seg_1\", \"src\": \"m1\", \"startWord\": 1, \"endWord\": 32 },\n { \"id\": \"seg_2\", \"src\": \"m2\", \"inMs\": 69000, \"outMs\": 72000, \"gain\": 0.6, \"note\": \"visual hold\" },\n { \"id\": \"seg_3\", \"src\": \"vo\", \"startWord\": 1, \"endWord\": 27, \"video\": { \"src\": \"b1\", \"inMs\": 54000 } }\n ],\n \"pauses\": { \"maxGapMs\": 350, \"keep\": [ { \"src\": \"m2\", \"afterWord\": 61, \"ms\": 1200 } ] },\n \"reframe\": { \"mode\": \"center\", \"punchIn\": 1.12, \"focusX\": 0.5, \"focusY\": 0.3 },\n \"grade\": { \"look\": \"film\", \"intensity\": 0.3, \"exposure\": 0, \"contrast\": 1.03, \"saturation\": 1.04 },\n \"captions\": { \"enabled\": true, \"mode\": \"phrase\", \"maxWords\": 5, \"yPct\": 0.72, \"uppercase\": false,\n \"fixes\": [ { \"src\": \"m1\", \"word\": 32, \"text\": \"Westfield.\" }, { \"src\": \"m2\", \"word\": 9, \"text\": \"\" } ] },\n \"chapters\": [ { \"src\": \"m1\", \"atWord\": 1, \"title\": \"Intro\" } ],\n \"chapterCards\": false,\n \"overlays\": [\n { \"id\": \"lt\", \"kind\": \"lowerThird\", \"src\": \"m1\", \"atWord\": 79, \"durMs\": 5000, \"props\": { \"name\": \"\", \"role\": \" · <compliance>\" } },\n { \"id\": \"st\", \"kind\": \"stat\", \"src\": \"m1\", \"atWord\": 113, \"durMs\": 3500, \"props\": { \"value\": \"42 homes\", \"label\": \"sold last year\" } },\n { \"id\": \"br1\", \"kind\": \"broll\", \"src\": \"m1\", \"atWord\": 26, \"toWord\": 32, \"broll\": { \"src\": \"b1\", \"inMs\": 5000 } },\n { \"id\": \"br2\", \"kind\": \"broll\", \"src\": \"vo\", \"atWord\": 21, \"toWord\": 24, \"broll\": { \"src\": \"img\", \"inMs\": 0 } }\n ],\n \"audio\": { \"dialogueLufs\": -16, \"music\": { \"file\": \"src/track.mp3\", \"gainDb\": -20, \"fadeInMs\": 1500, \"fadeOutMs\": 2500 } }\n}\n```\n- **Cut words by splitting segments**; the transcript is never edited. Word segments play in listed order; `inMs/outMs` segments are holds/B-roll. For long videos build segments with a small Python script from a `drop` list of word ranges.\n- `captions.fixes`: caption text per word; `\"\"` hides a word.\n- **B-roll** (`kind: \"broll\"`): covers the picture from `atWord` to `toWord` (+`tailMs`, default 200) or for `durMs`, starting `broll.inMs` into the clip, while the speaker's voice keeps playing; captions and graphics stay on top. Photos get a slow push-in. Great for hiding jump cuts and for walkthrough shots while the speaker talks.\n- **Voiceover:** an audio-only source (`role: \"voiceover\"`) cut by words like any clip; its segment needs `video: { src, inMs }` for the picture (that footage advances with the voice). Any segment can take `video` to narrate over other footage. B-roll overlays work on top of voiceover segments too.\n- **Music bed** (`audio.music`): loops if shorter than the video, `gainDb` under the voice (default −20), fades (`fadeInMs`/`fadeOutMs`), optional `startMs`/`endMs` (output time) and `inMs` (into the track). Auto-ducks under the voice (`duckRatio` 3 ≈ 11–12 dB dip, back to full in pauses — measured). Output stays ≈ target LUFS with peaks under −1.5 dB.\n- `pauses.maxGapMs`: 350 reels, 450 YouTube (breathing room). `keep` holds a deliberate pause.\n- **Crop position** (`sources[].cropX` / `cropY`, 0–1, default `reframe.cropX`/`cropY`, then 0.5): where the crop window sits when the source's aspect differs from the output (16:9 footage in a 9:16 short, or vertical phone footage in 16:9). Pick it from a real frame — grab one, try 2–3 values, Read them, keep the one where the speaker's face is centered with headroom. (For a centered desk setup 0.5 is usually right; a one-step change like 0.62 visibly moves the person.) Set per source; B-roll sources use their own value.\n- `reframe.punchIn` zooms alternate pieces of **presenter** sources only (walkthrough/selfie-walk footage never punches in — it pushed the speaker out of frame).\n- `grade.look`: `film` | `warm` | `cool` | `none`, blended at `intensity`; or `\"lut\": \"file.cube\"`. Keep it subtle (0.2–0.35).\n- Graphics anchor to word indices (if that word was cut, the next kept word is used). YouTube: `chapters` + `chapterCards: true`, lower third on the name, stat callouts only for numbers the speaker says.\n- 16:9: `format` 1920×1080, `profile` \"horizontal\", captions `maxWords` 6, `yPct` 0.9, `dialogueLufs` −14 (YouTube); reels −16.\n- Bump `rev` on every change; render to `out/revN` so earlier versions survive.\n\n## 5. Render\n```bash\ncd ~/vs && node engine/render.js edit.json out/rev1 --all --draft # cloud: run_in_background for anything over ~1 min of output\ncd ~/vs && node engine/render.js edit.json out/rev1 --budget 150 --draft # Mac: repeat until \"have\" == \"total\"\ncd ~/vs && node engine/render.js edit.json out/rev1 --finish # Mac: then finish (concat + loudness + MP4, ~90 s for 17 min)\n```\nEach piece is frame-exact (fps `start_time=0` + exact frame count) — this fixed a real bug where 14 of 110 pieces ran one frame long and audio drifted 0.4 s by the end. Captions/graphics are burned per piece at its output offset, so a long video renders in resumable chunks. Outputs: `render.mp4`, `captions.srt`, `chapters.txt` (YouTube description), `compiled.json` (every word's outputMs). A `mid_clause_stall` finding is a blocker. Speed: ~5 min of 1080p output per 150 s call on the Mac; ~3 min per minute in the cloud.\n**Text-only fixes** (caption typo, graphic label): delete only the affected `pieces/pNNNN.mkv` files (find them from `compiled.json` outStartMs) and rerun — untouched pieces are reused.\n\n## 5b. Shorts from a long-form edit\nWhen the user asks for shorts (vertical, 15–90 s) from a long video:\n1. From the full transcript, propose 3–5 candidates: start word → end word, working title, the hook line (first 2 s), output length, and why it stands alone. Every clip opens on a complete thought and ends on a clean word ending — never mid-sentence or leaning on earlier context. Prefer the ones with a concrete number, story or strong opinion.\n2. The user approves or swaps. Then each short is its **own edit file** (`edit_short1.json` …) in the same `~/vs` folder (same `media/`): copy the parent's `sources` (add `cropX` for 16:9 sources — check a frame), keep only the parent's segments/cuts inside the chosen word range (split segments at the range edges), `format` 1080×1920 `profile` \"vertical\", captions `maxWords` 5 `yPct` 0.72, `dialogueLufs` −16, `pauses.maxGapMs` 350 (tighter than long form). No chapters/chapter cards; keep the lower third only if the short opens on the speaker; stat callouts / B-roll as fit. Music optional per short.\n3. Render each to `out/short1`, `out/short2` … (same render commands; Mac: budget calls per short). Review each like any reel (step 7) — specifically that the crop keeps the speaker's face in frame across the clip and captions clear the bottom 20%.\n4. Deliver as `<name>_short1_<slug>.mp4` … with a one-line title + hook for each (and .srt files).\nNot built yet: automatic face-follow crop (the crop is fixed per source). If the speaker moves across the frame a lot, split that source's segments and give them different `cropX` via separate source entries pointing at the same media.\n\n## 6. Animated scenes\nOnly when the user asks for an animated section. Call start_video_edit with part \"scenes\" for how, and the files it needs.\n\n## 7. Review before delivering (required)\n1. `ffprobe`: right size, 30 fps; video and audio durations within ~0.1 s. Pieces check: per-piece video vs audio duration all within 20 ms; no audio gaps > 20 ms in `pieces/joined.mkv`. Loudness via `ebur128` ≈ target.\n2. Seam sheet: a frame 400 ms into every Nth piece (≈25 frames), tiled, Read it — no black/garbage frames, consistent framing.\n3. Caption sync: ~4–12 words spread over the film, frame at `outStartMs + 90`, labeled with the expected word — the lit word must match every time.\n4. Every graphic type at its anchor: on time, readable, not over the speaker's face, not colliding with captions or each other.\n5. B-roll/voiceover: a frame inside every B-roll span and every voiceover segment shows the intended footage with captions on top.\nFix → re-render only what changed → re-check. You can't hear audio: tell the user to listen at the cuts and to the music level (raise/lower `gainDb` in 3 dB steps on request; only the finish step reruns — delete `render.mp4` and run `--finish`).\n\n## 8. Then call start_video_edit with part \"engine_2\".\n\n## engine/ingest.py\n```python\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n# Transcribe clips with word timings and write media/<name>.json.\n# Usage: python3 engine/ingest.py [--budget SECONDS] [--fast] [--broll] <clip> [<clip> ...]\n# --broll: b-roll video, stills or music — probe only, no transcript. Voiceovers (audio-only m4a/mp3) are transcribed normally.\n# Resumable: words are appended to media/<name>.words.jsonl as they come; a rerun continues after the last word.\n# --budget 160 on the user's Mac (each command is capped at 180 s);
