i += 1 # b-roll / stills / music: probe only, no transcript\n else: clips.append(args[i]); i += 1\nT0 = time.time()\nMODEL = os.path.expanduser(\"~/model-small\")\n\ndef probe(path):\n out = subprocess.run([\"ffprobe\", \"-v\", \"error\", \"-show_streams\", \"-show_format\", \"-of\", \"json\", path],\n capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout\n j = json.loads(out)\n v = next((s for s in j[\"streams\"] if s[\"codec_type\"] == \"video\" and s.get(\"disposition\", {}).get(\"attached_pic\", 0) == 0), None)\n if v is None: # audio-only (voice memo, mp3 voiceover, music)\n return float(j[\"format\"][\"duration\"]), None, None\n w, h = v[\"width\"], v[\"height\"]\n rot = 0\n for sd in v.get(\"side_data_list\", []):\n if \"rotation\" in sd: rot = int(sd[\"rotation\"] or 0)\n rot = rot or int(v.get(\"tags\", {}).get(\"rotate\", 0) or 0)\n if abs(rot) == 90: w, h = h, w\n return float(j[\"format\"][\"duration\"]), w, h\n\ndef show(words):\n out, prev = [], None\n for x in words:\n gap = (x[\"startMs\"] - prev[\"endMs\"]) if prev else 0\n if prev is None or gap > 700 or prev[\"text\"][-1:] in \".?!\":\n s = x[\"startMs\"] // 1000\n out.append((\"\\n\" if (gap > 700 or prev is None) else \" \") + (f\"‖{gap/1000:.1f}s \" if gap > 700 else \"\") + f\"[{x['i']}|{s//60}:{s%60:02d}]\")\n out.append(\" \" + x[\"text\"]); prev = x\n return \"\".join(out)\n\nfrom faster_whisper import WhisperModel\nmodel = None\nos.makedirs(\"audio\", exist_ok=True); os.makedirs(\"media\", exist_ok=True)\nfor p in clips:\n name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(p))[0]\n if os.path.exists(f\"media/{name}.json\"):\n print(f\"DONE {name} (already ingested)\"); continue\n if nowords:\n dur, w, h = probe(p) if not p.lower().endswith(('.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp', '.heic')) else (3600.0, None, None)\n json.dump({\"mediaId\": name, \"path\": p, \"durationMs\": round(dur * 1000), \"width\": w, \"height\": h, \"hasVideo\": w is not None or dur == 3600.0, \"words\": []},\n open(f\"media/{name}.json\", \"w\"))\n print(f\"DONE {name} (b-roll/music, no transcript) {dur:.1f}s\"); continue\n wav = f\"audio/{name}.wav\"\n if not os.path.exists(wav):\n subprocess.run([\"ffmpeg\", \"-v\", \"error\", \"-y\", \"-i\", p, \"-vn\", \"-ac\", \"1\", \"-ar\", \"16000\", wav], check=True)\n jl = f\"media/{name}.words.jsonl\"\n words = [json.loads(l) for l in open(jl)] if os.path.exists(jl) else []\n # Resume at a clean boundary: drop words after the last sentence end, so Whisper restarts on a fresh sentence\n # (restarting mid-sentence or in a pause makes it invent words).\n # Only cut back if that still moves past where the previous run started (otherwise a long first sentence loops forever).\n rf = f\"media/{name}.resume\"\n prev_start = float(open(rf).read()) if os.path.exists(rf) else -1.0\n if words:\n cut = max((k for k, x in enumerate(words) if x[\"text\"][-1:] in \".?!\"), default=-1)\n if cut >= 0 and words[cut][\"endMs\"] / 1000 > prev_start + 1.0 and cut < len(words) - 1:\n words = words[:cut + 1]\n with open(jl, \"w\") as f:\n for x in words: f.write(json.dumps(x) + \"\\n\")\n start = words[-1][\"endMs\"] / 1000 + 0.05 if words else 0.0\n prompt = \"Um, uh, so, like, you know, I mean. \" + \" \".join(x[\"text\"] for x in words[-40:])\n open(rf, \"w\").write(f\"{start:.3f}\")\n wf = wave.open(wav); audio = np.frombuffer(wf.readframes(wf.getnframes()), np.int16).astype(np.float32) / 32768\n total = len(audio) / 16000\n finished = True\n if start < total - 0.5:\n if model is None:\n model = WhisperModel(MODEL, device=\"cpu\", compute_type=\"int8\", cpu_threads=os.cpu_count() or 2)\n segs, _ = model.transcribe(audio[int(start * 16000):], word_timestamps=True, vad_filter=False,\n beam_size=1 if fast else 5, language=\"en\",\n initial_prompt=prompt)\n with open(jl, \"a\") as f:\n for s in segs:\n for w in s.words:\n x = {\"i\": len(words) + 1, \"text\": w.word.strip(), \"startMs\": round((w.start + start) * 1000),\n \"endMs\": round((w.end + start) * 1000), \"conf\": round(w.probability, 2)}\n words.append(x); break\n dur, w, h = probe(p)\n json.dump({\"mediaId\": name, \"path\": p, \"durationMs\": round(dur * 1000), \"width\": w, \"height\": h, \"hasVideo\": w is not None, \"words\": words},\n open(f\"media/{name}.json\", \"w\"))\n print(f\"DONE {name} {dur:.1f}s {f'{w}x{h}' if w else 'audio-only'} {len(words)} words\")\n print(show(words))\n```\n\n## engine/compiler.js\n```javascript\n'use strict';\n// Video Studio — compiler (phase 1).\n// Turns a videostudio.edit/1 document + per-source word timings into exact\n// source in/out pieces, an output timeline, caption pages, and outputMs for\n// every kept word. Pure function: no I/O, so it can move into the worker as-is.\n\nconst DEFAULTS = { maxGapMs: 350, padMs: 120, minRemoveMs: 120 };\n\nfunction snap(ms, fps, dir) {\n const f = 1000 / fps;\n const n = ms / f;\n return (dir === 'down' ? Math.floor(n + 1e-6) : dir === 'up' ? Math.ceil(n - 1e-6) : Math.round(n)) * f;\n}\n\nfunction compile(edit, mediaById) {\n const fps = edit.format.fps;\n const P = Object.assign({}, DEFAULTS, edit.pauses || {});\n const keep = (P.keep || []);\n const srcMap = {};\n for (const s of edit.sources) {\n const m = mediaById[s.mediaId];\n if (!m) throw new Error('missing media for source ' + s.id);\n srcMap[s.id] = m;\n }\n const findings = [];\n const pieces = [];\n\n for (const seg of edit.segments) {\n const m = srcMap[seg.src];\n if (!m) throw new Error('segment ' + seg.id + ' has unknown src ' + seg.src);\n\n // Visual hold / B-roll segment: explicit ms range, no words.\n if (seg.inMs != null) {\n pieces.push({ seg: seg.id, src: seg.src, inMs: seg.inMs, outMs: seg.outMs, gain: seg.gain != null ? seg.gain : 1, words: [] });\n continue;\n }\n\n const words = m.words.filter(w => w.i >= seg.startWord && w.i <= seg.endWord);\n if (!words.length) throw new Error('segment ' + seg.id + ' has no words');\n const before = m.words.find(w => w.i === seg.startWord - 1);\n const after = m.words.find(w => w.i === seg.endWord + 1);\n\n let curIn = words[0].startMs - P.padMs + (seg.trimHeadMs || 0);\n if (before) curIn = Math.max(curIn, before.endMs + 20);\n curIn = Math.max(0, curIn);\n let curWords = [words[0]];\n\n for (let k = 0; k < words.length - 1; k++) {\n const a = words[k], b = words[k + 1];\n const gap = b.startMs - a.endMs;\n const kp = keep.find(x => x.src === seg.src && x.afterWord === a.i);\n const allowed = kp ? kp.ms : P.maxGapMs;\n const air = kp ? kp.ms : 2 * P.padMs;\n if (gap > allowed && gap - air >= P.minRemoveMs) {\n const outEdge = a.endMs + (kp ? kp.ms / 2 : P.padMs);\n const inEdge = b.startMs - (kp ? kp.ms / 2 : P.padMs);\n pieces.push({ seg: seg.id, src: seg.src, inMs: curIn, outMs: outEdge, gain: 1, words: curWords });\n findings.push({ type: 'silence_removed', src: seg.src, afterWord: a.i, gapMs: gap, removedMs: Math.round(inEdge - outEdge) });\n curIn = inEdge; curWords = [];\n } else if (gap > 900 && !/[.?!,]$/.test(a.text)) {\n findings.push({ type: 'mid_clause_stall', src: seg.src, afterWord: a.i, gapMs: gap, blocker: true });\n }\n curWords.push(b);\n }\n const last = words[words.length - 1];\n let curOut = last.endMs + P.padMs + (seg.trimTailMs || 0);\n if (after) curOut = Math.min(curOut, after.startMs - 20);\n curOut = Math.min(curOut, m.durationMs);\n pieces.push({ seg: seg.id, src: seg.src, inMs: curIn, outMs: curOut, gain: 1, words: curWords });\n }\n\n // Frame-snap and lay out the output timeline.\n let t = 0;\n for (const p of pieces) {\n p.inMs = snap(p.inMs, fps, 'down');\n p.outMs = snap(p.outMs, fps, 'up');\n if (p.outMs - p.inMs < 1000 / fps) throw new Error('piece shorter than one frame in ' + p.seg);\n p.outStartMs = t;\n t += p.outMs - p.inMs;\n }\n const durationMs = t;\n\n // Picture source per piece: seg.video = { src, inMs } plays that source's video (advancing with output time)\n // under this segment's audio — used for voiceovers (audio-only source) or narrating over other footage.\n const segFirst = {};\n for (const p of pieces) if (segFirst[p.seg] == null) segFirst[p.seg] = p.outStartMs;\n for (const p of pieces) {\n const seg = edit.segments.find(s => s.id === p.seg);\n if (seg && seg.video) p.video = { src: seg.video.src, inMs: Math.round(seg.video.inMs + (p.outStartMs - segFirst[p.seg])) };\n else if (srcMap[p.src].hasVideo === false) throw new Error('segment ' + p.seg + ' is audio-only: give it video: { src, inMs }');\n }\n\n // Word fixes (caption text only; transcript stays untouched).\n const fixes = {};\n for (const f of (edit.captions && edit.captions.fixes) || []) fixes[f.src + ':' + f.word] = f.text;\n\n // outputMs for every kept word.\n const outWords = [];\n for (const p of pieces) {\n for (const w of p.words) {\n const s = Math.max(w.startMs, p.inMs), e = Math.min(w.endMs, p.outMs);\n const key = p.src + ':' + w.i;\n const text = key in fixes ? fixes[key] : w.text;\n outWords.push({ src: p.src, i: w.i, text, outStartMs: Math.round(p.outStartMs + s - p.inMs), outEndMs: Math.round(p.outStartMs + e - p.inMs) });\n }\n }\n\n // Caption pages (phrase mode).\n // Glue transcriber-split tokens (\"four\" \"-bedroom\", \"2\" \".75\" \"%\") onto the previous kept word for captions.\n for (let k = 1; k < outWords.length; k++) {\n const w = outWords[k];\n if (!/^[-.%]/.test(w.text) || w.text === '.') continue;\n let j = k - 1;\n while (j >= 0 && outWords[j].text === '') j--;\n if (j < 0 || outWords[j].src !== w.src || w.i - outWords[j].i > 3) continue;\n outWords[j].text += w.text; w.text = '';\n }\n\n const C = edit.captions || {};\n const pages = [];\n if (C.enabled || C.srtOnly) {\n const maxWords = C.maxWords || 5;\n const vis = outWords.filter(w => w.text !== '');\n // 1) split into clauses at punctuation, source changes, and long gaps.\n const clauses = [];\n let cl = [];\n for (const w of vis) {\n const prev = cl[cl.length - 1];\n if (prev && (w.outStartMs - prev.outEndMs > 600 || w.src !== prev.src)) { clauses.push(cl); cl = []; }\n cl.push(w);\n if (/[.?!,;:]$/.test(w.text)) { clauses.push(cl); cl = []; }\n }\n if (cl.length) clauses.push(cl);\n // 2) merge a 1-2 word clause into its neighbour when the result still fits one page.\n for (let k = 0; k < clauses.length; k++) {\n const c = clauses[k], nx = clauses[k + 1];\n if (!nx) continue;\n const joinable = nx[0].src === c[c.length - 1].src && nx[0].outStartMs - c[c.length - 1].outEndMs <= 600;\n const fits = Math.min(c.length, nx.length) === 1 || (Math.min(c.length, nx.length) <= 2 && c.length + nx.length <= maxWords);\n if (joinable && fits && !/[.?!]$/.test(c[c.length - 1].text)) {\n clauses.splice(k, 2, c.concat(nx)); k--;\n }\n }\n // 3) chunk each clause into balanced pages of at most maxWords.\n for (const c of clauses) {\n const n = Math.ceil(c.length / maxWords), size = Math.ceil(c.length / n);\n for (let k = 0; k < c.length; k += size) pages.push(c.slice(k, k + size));\n }\n }\n const capPages = pages.map((pg, k) => {\n const next = pages[k + 1];\n let end = pg[pg.length - 1].outEndMs + 250;\n if (next) end = Math.min(end, next[0].outStartMs);\n return { startMs: pg[0].outStartMs, endMs: Math.max(end, pg[pg.length - 1].outEndMs), words: pg };\n });\n\n const wordOut = {}, wordOutEnd = {};\n for (const w of outWords) { wordOut[w.src + ':' + w.i] = w.outStartMs; wordOutEnd[w.src + ':' + w.i] = w.outEndMs; }\n // Anchor resolution: if the anchor word was cut, use the next kept word from the same source.\n const anchor = (src, i) => {\n for (let k = i; k < i + 50; k++) if (wordOut[src + ':' + k] != null) return wordOut[src + ':' + k];\n return null;\n };\n const chapters = (edit.chapters || []).map(c => ({ title: c.title, atWord: c.atWord, src: c.src, outMs: anchor(c.src, c.atWord) }));\n const anchorEnd = (src, i) => {\n for (let k = i; k > i - 50; k--) if (wordOutEnd[src + ':' + k] != null) return wordOutEnd[src + ':' + k];\n return null;\n };\n const overlays = (edit.overlays || []).map(o => {\n const outMs = anchor(o.src, o.atWord);\n const endMs = o.toWord != null ? anchorEnd(o.src, o.toWord) + (o.tailMs != null ? o.tailMs : 200) : null;\n const dur = endMs != null && outMs != null ? Math.max(500, endMs - outMs) : (o.durMs || 3000);\n return { id: o.id, kind: o.kind, props: o.props || {}, durMs: Math.min(dur, durationMs - (outMs || 0)), outMs, broll: o.broll || null };\n }).filter(o => o.outMs != null);\n\n return {\n schema: 'videostudio.compiled/1', rev: edit.rev, format: edit.format, durationMs,\n pieces: pieces.map(p => ({ seg: p.seg, src: p.src, inMs: p.inMs, outMs: p.outMs, outStartMs: p.outStartMs, gain: p.gain, video: p.video || null, firstWord: p.words[0] ? p.words[0].i : null, lastWord: p.words.length ? p.words[p.words.length - 1].i : null })),\n words: outWords, captions: capPages, chapters, overlays, findings,\n };\n}\n\nmodule.exports = { compile };\n```\n", engine_2: "# NestTrack video editor: engine files, part 2 of 2\nWrite each file below to `~/vs/engine/` under the name in its heading, exactly as given. Then run `node --check` on the .js files and `python3 -m py_compile` on ingest.py, and carry on with the method.\n\n## engine/render.js\n```javascript\n'use strict';\n// Video Studio — FFmpeg renderer.\n// Usage: node render.js <edit.json> <outDir> [--draft] [--budget SECONDS] [--all]\n// Pieces are rendered one at a time and skipped if they already exist, so the job can be resumed.\n// --budget N : stop starting new pieces after N seconds (use 150 on the user's Mac, where each command is capped at 180 s)\n// --all : no budget, and finish in the same run (use in the cloud workspace, run in the background)\n// Finishing (concat + loudness + MP4) happens once every piece exists, or when --finish is passed.\n// --clean : no burned captions or graphics (footage source for Remotion scenes)\n// Each piece: frame-exact seek, fill-crop, grade, burned captions/graphics at its output offset, de-clicked audio.\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst path = require('path');\nconst { spawnSync } = require('child_process');\nconst { compile } = require('./compiler');\n\n// ---------- fonts: Inter in the cloud workspace, Lato on the user's Mac VM ----------\nconst fc = (() => { try { return spawnSync('fc-list', [':', 'family'], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } })();\nconst HAS_INTER = /Inter ExtraBold/.test(fc) || /\\bInter\\b/.test(fc);\nconst FONT = HAS_INTER ? { heavy: 'Inter ExtraBold', black: 'Inter Black' } : { heavy: 'Lato Heavy', black: 'Lato Black' };\n\n// ---------- helpers ----------\nfunction hexToAss(hex) { const h = hex.replace('#', ''); return '&H00' + h.slice(4, 6) + h.slice(2, 4) + h.slice(0, 2); }\nfunction assTime(ms) { const cs = Math.round(ms / 10); const h = Math.floor(cs / 360000), m = Math.floor(cs / 6000) % 60, s = Math.floor(cs / 100) % 60, c = cs % 100; return `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(c).padStart(2, '0')}`; }\nfunction srtTime(ms) { const h = Math.floor(ms / 3600000), m = Math.floor(ms / 60000) % 60, s = Math.floor(ms / 1000) % 60, x = Math.round(ms % 1000); return `${String(h).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')},${String(x).padStart(3, '0')}`; }\nfunction ytTime(ms) { const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000), h = Math.floor(s / 3600), m = Math.floor(s / 60) % 60, x = s % 60; return (h ? h + ':' + String(m).padStart(2, '0') : String(m).padStart(2, '0')) + ':' + String(x).padStart(2, '0'); }\nfunction esc(t) { return String(t).replace(/\\\\/g, '\\\\\\\\').replace(/{/g, '(').replace(/}/g, ')'); }\n\n// ---------- graphics + captions (ASS) ----------\nconst WHITE = '&H00FFFFFF';\n// Brand colors come from edit.brand { plate, highlight, light } (hex); defaults are neutral.\nfunction buildAss(compiled, edit) {\n const C = edit.captions || {}, F = edit.format;\n const W = F.width, H = F.height, horiz = W > H;\n const u = Math.min(W, H) / 1080; // 1.0 for 1080p landscape and 1080x1920 portrait\n const capSize = Math.round((horiz ? 52 : 76) * u);\n const BR = edit.brand || {};\n const PINE = hexToAss(BR.plate || '#1F2937'), MINT = hexToAss(BR.highlight || '#FDE047'), IVORY = hexToAss(BR.light || '#F5F5F4');\n const hi = hexToAss(C.highlight || BR.highlight || '#FDE047');\n const capY = Math.round(H * (C.yPct != null ? C.yPct : (horiz ? 0.9 : 0.72)));\n const side = Math.round(W * 0.09);\n const S = (name, font, size, fill, back, bw) => `Style: ${name},${font},${Math.round(size)},${fill},${fill},${back},&H80000000,0,0,0,0,100,100,0,0,3,${Math.round(bw)},0,7,${side},${side},0,1`;\n let out = `[Script Info]\\nScriptType: v4.00+\\nPlayResX: ${W}\\nPlayResY: ${H}\\nWrapStyle: 0\\nScaledBorderAndShadow: yes\\n\\n[V4+ Styles]\\nFormat: Name, Fontname, Fontsize, PrimaryColour, SecondaryColour, OutlineColour, BackColour, Bold, Italic, Underline, StrikeOut, ScaleX, ScaleY, Spacing, Angle, BorderStyle, Outline, Shadow, Alignment, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Encoding\\n`;\n out += S('Cap', FONT.heavy, capSize, WHITE, PINE.replace('&H00', '&H20'), capSize * 0.21) + '\\n';\n out += S('LTName', FONT.black, 58 * u, WHITE, PINE, 16 * u) + '\\n';\n out += S('LTRole', FONT.heavy, 34 * u, PINE, IVORY, 12 * u) + '\\n';\n out += S('ChNum', FONT.heavy, 26 * u, MINT, PINE, 10 * u) + '\\n';\n out += S('ChTitle', FONT.black, 46 * u, WHITE, PINE, 14 * u) + '\\n';\n out += S('StatVal', FONT.black, 112 * u, MINT, PINE, 18 * u) + '\\n';\n out += S('StatLabel', FONT.heavy, 34 * u, WHITE, PINE, 12 * u) + '\\n';\n out += `\\n[Events]\\nFormat: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\\n`;\n const ev = (layer, s, e, style, text) => { out += `Dialogue: ${layer},${assTime(s)},${assTime(e)},${style},,0,0,0,,${text}\\n`; };\n const X = Math.round((horiz ? 96 : 80) * u), off = -Math.round(900 * u);\n const slide = (yy, delay) => `{\\\\an7\\\\move(${off},${yy},${X},${yy},${delay},${delay + 340})\\\\fad(0,280)}`;\n // Layout per orientation (portrait keeps clear of the bottom 20% that Reels/TikTok cover).\n const L = horiz\n ? { ltName: H - 360 * u, ltRole: H - 270 * u, chNum: 56 * u, chTitle: 104 * u, statVal: 330 * u, statLabel: 490 * u }\n : { ltName: H * 0.56, ltRole: H * 0.56 + 90 * u, chNum: H * 0.08, chTitle: H * 0.08 + 48 * u, statVal: H * 0.22, statLabel: H * 0.22 + 160 * u };\n\n if (C.enabled) for (const pg of compiled.captions) {\n const ws = pg.words;\n for (let k = 0; k < ws.length; k++) {\n const s = k === 0 ? pg.startMs : ws[k].outStartMs;\n const e = k === ws.length - 1 ? pg.endMs : ws[k + 1].outStartMs;\n if (e <= s) continue;\n const pop = k === 0 ? '\\\\fscx90\\\\fscy90\\\\t(0,90,\\\\fscx100\\\\fscy100)' : '';\n const text = ws.map((w, j) => { const t = esc(C.uppercase ? w.text.toUpperCase() : w.text); return j === k ? `{\\\\c${hi}}${t}{\\\\c${WHITE}}` : t; }).join(' ');\n ev(5, s, e, 'Cap', `{\\\\an5\\\\pos(${W / 2},${capY})${pop}}${text}`);\n }\n }\n if (edit.chapterCards) compiled.chapters.forEach((c, k) => {\n if (k === 0 || c.outMs == null) return;\n const s = c.outMs + 150, e = s + 3200;\n ev(3, s, e, 'ChNum', slide(Math.round(L.chNum), 0) + `CHAPTER ${String(k + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`);\n ev(3, s, e, 'ChTitle', slide(Math.round(L.chTitle), 90) + esc(c.title));\n });\n for (const o of compiled.overlays) {\n if (o.kind === 'broll') continue;\n const s = o.outMs, e = o.outMs + o.durMs;\n if (o.kind === 'lowerThird') {\n ev(4, s, e, 'LTName', slide(Math.round(L.ltName), 0) + esc(o.props.name));\n ev(4, s, e, 'LTRole', slide(Math.round(L.ltRole), 130) + esc(o.props.role));\n } else if (o.kind === 'stat') {\n const pop = `\\\\fscx70\\\\fscy70\\\\t(0,170,\\\\fscx106\\\\fscy106)\\\\t(170,280,\\\\fscx100\\\\fscy100)\\\\fad(100,260)`;\n ev(4, s, e, 'StatVal', `{\\\\an7\\\\pos(${X + Math.round(24 * u)},${Math.round(L.statVal)})${pop}}` + esc(o.props.value));\n ev(4, s + 120, e, 'StatLabel', `{\\\\an7\\\\pos(${X + Math.round(24 * u)},${Math.round(L.statLabel)})\\\\fad(160,260)}` + esc(o.props.label));\n }\n }\n return out;\n}\nfunction buildSrt(compiled) { return compiled.captions.map((pg, k) => `${k + 1}\\n${srtTime(pg.startMs)} --> ${srtTime(pg.endMs)}\\n${pg.words.map(w => w.text).join(' ')}\\n`).join('\\n'); }\n\n// ---------- color grade ----------\n// grade: { look: 'none'|'film'|'warm'|'cool', intensity: 0..1, exposure: -0.1..0.1, contrast: 1, saturation: 1, lut: 'file.cube' }\nconst LOOKS = {\n film: 'curves=preset=medium_contrast,colorbalance=rs=0.03:bs=-0.03:rh=0.02:bh=-0.02,eq=saturation=0.92',\n warm: 'colorbalance=rs=0.05:gs=0.01:bs=-0.05:rm=0.03:bm=-0.03',\n cool: 'colorbalance=rs=-0.04:bs=0.05:rm=-0.02:bm=0.03',\n};\nfunction gradeFilter(g, root) {\n if (!g) return '';\n const parts = [];\n const ex = g.exposure || 0, ct = g.contrast || 1, sat = g.saturation || 1;\n if (ex || ct !== 1 || sat !== 1) parts.push(`eq=brightness=${ex}:contrast=${ct}:saturation=${sat}`);\n let look = g.lut ? `lut3d=file='${path.resolve(root, g.lut).replace(/:/g, '\\\\:')}'` : (LOOKS[g.look] || '');\n if (look) {\n const i = g.intensity != null ? g.intensity : 0.3;\n parts.push(i >= 0.999 ? look : `split[ga][gb];[gb]${look}[gc];[ga][gc]blend=all_mode=normal:all_opacity=${i}`);\n }\n return parts.length ? ',' + parts.join(',') : '';\n}\n\nfunction main() {\n const argv = process.argv.slice(2);\n const [editPath, outDir] = argv;\n const draft = argv.includes('--draft'), all = argv.includes('--all');\n const opt = (k, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf(k); return i >= 0 ? Number(argv[i + 1]) : d; };\n const root = path.dirname(path.resolve(editPath));\n const edit = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(editPath, 'utf8'));\n const media = {};\n for (const s of edit.sources) media[s.mediaId] = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'media', s.mediaId + '.json'), 'utf8'));\n const compiled = compile(edit, media);\n fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });\n fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, 'compiled.json'), JSON.stringify(compiled, null, 1));\n const assPath = path.resolve(outDir, 'captions.ass');\n // --clean: no burned captions/graphics (use as the footage source for a Remotion scene).\n const burn = !argv.includes('--clean') && !!((edit.captions && edit.captions.enabled) || compiled.overlays.some(o => o.kind !== 'broll') || edit.chapterCards);\n if (burn) fs.writeFileSync(assPath, buildAss(compiled, edit));\n if (compiled.captions.length) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, 'captions.srt'), buildSrt(compiled));\n if (compiled.chapters.length) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, 'chapters.txt'), compiled.chapters.map(c => `${ytTime(c.outMs)} ${c.title}`).join('\\n') + '\\n');\n const assEsc = assPath.replace(/\\\\/g, '/').replace(/:/g, '\\\\:');\n\n const F = edit.format, fps = F.fps;\n const run = (args) => {\n const r = spawnSync('ffmpeg', ['-hide_banner', '-nostats', '-y', ...args], { stdio: ['ignore', 'ignore', 'pipe'], maxBuffer: 1 << 26 });\n const err = r.stderr ? r.stderr.toString() : '';\n if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error('ffmpeg failed (' + (r.status ?? r.signal) + '): ' + err.slice(-1500));\n return err;\n };\n const work = path.resolve(outDir, 'pieces');\n fs.mkdirSync(work, { recursive: true });\n const pf = k => path.join(work, `p${String(k).padStart(4, '0')}.mkv`);\n const budgetMs = all ? Infinity : opt('--budget', 150) * 1000, t0 = Date.now();\n const grade = gradeFilter(edit.grade, root);\n\n // Step 1: pieces (resumable; stops starting new ones when the time budget is spent).\n for (let k = 0; k < compiled.pieces.length; k++) {\n if (fs.existsSync(pf(k))) continue;\n if (Date.now() - t0 > budgetMs) break;\n const p = compiled.pieces[k];\n const srcOf = id => { const so = edit.sources.find(s => s.id === id); if (!so) throw new Error('unknown source ' + id); return { so, m: media[so.mediaId] }; };\n const { so, m } = srcOf(p.src);\n const d = (p.outMs - p.inMs) / 1000, dMs = p.outMs - p.inMs;\n const nFrames = Math.round(d * fps);\n const tmp = pf(k) + '.tmp.mkv';\n // Fill the frame; when the aspect differs (e.g. 16:9 footage in a 9:16 short) the crop window sits at the\n // source's cropX/cropY (0..1, where the subject is; default reframe.cropX/cropY, then centre).\n const RF = edit.reframe || {};\n const fillFor = sObj => {\n const cx = sObj.cropX != null ? sObj.cropX : (RF.cropX != null ? RF.cropX : 0.5);\n const cy = sObj.cropY != null ? sObj.cropY : (RF.cropY != null ? RF.cropY : 0.5);\n return `fps=fps=${fps}:start_time=0,scale=${F.width}:${F.height}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=${F.width}:${F.height}:(iw-${F.width})*${cx}:(ih-${F.height})*${cy}`;\n };\n // Inputs: 0 = this piece's audio source; picture comes from it, or from p.video (voiceover / narrate over other footage).\n const inputs = ['-ss', (p.inMs / 1000).toFixed(4), '-t', d.toFixed(4), '-i', path.resolve(root, m.path)];\n let vIn = '0:v', vRole = so.role, vSo = so;\n if (p.video) {\n const v = srcOf(p.video.src);\n inputs.push('-ss', (p.video.inMs / 1000).toFixed(4), '-t', d.toFixed(4), '-i', path.resolve(root, v.m.path));\n vIn = '1:v'; vRole = v.so.role; vSo = v.so;\n }\n // fps start_time=0 + exact frame count keeps every piece's video exactly as long as its audio (no cumulative drift).\n // Punch-in on alternate pieces hides jump cuts: reframe.punchIn (e.g. 1.12), focus point reframe.focusX/focusY (0..1).\n const R = edit.reframe || {}, P = R.punchIn || 0;\n const punch = P > 1 && k % 2 === 1 && vRole === 'presenter' // walkthrough / selfie-walk / b-roll footage never punches in\n ? `,scale=${Math.round(F.width * P / 2) * 2}:${Math.round(F.height * P / 2) * 2},crop=${F.width}:${F.height}:(iw-${F.width})*${R.focusX != null ? R.focusX : 0.5}:(ih-${F.height})*${R.focusY != null ? R.focusY : 0.35}`\n : '';\n let g = `[${vIn}]${fillFor(vSo)}${punch},setsar=1[base0];`, cur = 'base0';\n // B-roll overlays that intersect this piece cover the picture; the voice keeps playing underneath.\n const pS = p.outStartMs, pE = p.outStartMs + dMs;\n compiled.overlays.filter(o => o.kind === 'broll' && o.outMs < pE && o.outMs + o.durMs > pS).forEach((o, j) => {\n const b = srcOf(o.broll.src);\n const from = Math.max(pS, o.outMs), to = Math.min(pE, o.outMs + o.durMs);\n const off = (from - pS) / 1000, len = (to - from) / 1000;\n const idx = inputs.filter(x => x === '-i').length;\n const isImg = /\\.(jpe?g|png|webp|heic)$/i.test(b.m.path);\n if (isImg) inputs.push('-loop', '1', '-t', len.toFixed(4), '-i', path.resolve(root, b.m.path));\n else inputs.push('-ss', ((o.broll.inMs + (from - o.outMs)) / 1000).toFixed(4), '-t', len.toFixed(4), '-i', path.resolve(root, b.m.path));\n // Stills get a slow push-in (Ken Burns), continuous across pieces.\n const kb = isImg ? `,scale=iw*1.08:ih*1.08,zoompan=z='1+0.06*(${((from - o.outMs) / 1000).toFixed(3)}+on/${fps})/${(o.durMs / 1000).toFixed(3)}':x='iw/2-(iw/zoom/2)':y='ih/2-(ih/zoom/2)':d=1:s=${F.width}x${F.height}:fps=${fps}` : '';\n g += `[${idx}:v]${fillFor(b.so)}${kb},setsar=1,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS+${off.toFixed(4)}/TB[br${j}];`;\n g += `[${cur}][br${j}]overlay=enable='between(t,${off.toFixed(4)},${(off + len).toFixed(4)})':eof_action=pass[ov${j}];`;\n cur = `ov${j}`;\n });\n g += `[${cur}]null${grade}` + (burn ? `,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS+${(p.outStartMs / 1000).toFixed(4)}/TB,ass='${assEsc}',setpts=PTS-STARTPTS` : '') + ',format=yuv420p[vout];';\n g += `[0:a]aformat=sample_rates=48000:channel_layouts=stereo,volume=${p.gain},afade=t=in:d=0.012,afade=t=out:st=${Math.max(0, d - 0.015).toFixed(4)}:d=0.015,apad,atrim=end=${d.toFixed(4)}[aout]`;\n run([...inputs, '-filter_complex', g, '-map', '[vout]', '-map', '[aout]', '-frames:v', String(nFrames),\n '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'veryfast', '-crf', draft ? '21' : '18', '-maxrate', '12M', '-bufsize', '24M', '-g', String(fps * 2), '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', tmp]);\n fs.renameSync(tmp, pf(k));\n }\n const total = compiled.pieces.length;\n const have = compiled.pieces.filter((_, k) => fs.existsSync(pf(k))).length;\n const finish = all || argv.includes('--finish');\n if (have < total || !finish) {\n console.log(JSON.stringify({ stage: 'pieces', have, total, durationMs: compiled.durationMs, findings: compiled.findings.filter(f => f.type !== 'silence_removed') }));\n return;\n }\n\n // Step 2: concat (stream copy) + loudness measure.\n const listFile = path.join(work, 'list.txt');\n fs.writeFileSync(listFile, compiled.pieces.map((_, k) => `file '${pf(k)}'`).join('\\n') + '\\n');\n const joined = path.join(work, 'joined.mkv');\n fs.rmSync(joined, { force: true });\n run(['-f', 'concat', '-safe', '0', '-i', listFile, '-c', 'copy', joined]);\n const lufs = (edit.audio && edit.audio.dialogueLufs) || -16;\n const meas = run(['-i', joined, '-vn', '-af', `highpass=f=80,loudnorm=I=${lufs}:TP=-1.5:LRA=11:print_format=json`, '-f', 'null', '-']);\n const js = JSON.parse(meas.slice(meas.lastIndexOf('{'), meas.lastIndexOf('}') + 1));\n\n // Step 3: finish — video stream copied (pieces are already delivery quality), audio gap-filled and leveled.\n const ln = `loudnorm=I=${lufs}:TP=-1.5:LRA=11:measured_I=${js.input_i}:measured_TP=${js.input_tp}:measured_LRA=${js.input_lra}:measured_thresh=${js.input_thresh}:offset=${js.target_offset}:linear=true`;\n const outFile = path.join(outDir, 'render.mp4');\n const voice = `aresample=async=1:first_pts=0,highpass=f=80,${ln},aresample=48000,aformat=sample_rates=48000:channel_layouts=stereo`;\n const M = edit.audio && edit.audio.music;\n if (M && M.file) {\n // Music bed: looped to length, gainDb under the voice (default -20), fades, and ducked by the voice (sidechain).\n const D = compiled.durationMs / 1000, st = (M.startMs || 0) / 1000, fi = (M.fadeInMs != null ? M.fadeInMs : 1500) / 1000, fo = (M.fadeOutMs != null ? M.fadeOutMs : 2500) / 1000;\n const end = Math.min(D, M.endMs != null ? M.endMs / 1000 : D), len = Math.max(0.1, end - st);\n const g = `[0:a]${voice},asplit=2[voice][sc];`\n + `[1:a]aresample=48000,aformat=sample_rates=48000:channel_layouts=stereo,atrim=start=${((M.inMs || 0) / 1000).toFixed(3)},asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS,atrim=end=${len.toFixed(3)},`\n + `volume=${M.gainDb != null ? M.gainDb : -20}dB,afade=t=in:d=${fi}:curve=tri,afade=t=out:st=${Math.max(0, len - fo).toFixed(3)}:d=${fo},adelay=${Math.round(st * 1000)}|${Math.round(st * 1000)},apad[mus];`\n + `[mus][sc]sidechaincompress=threshold=${M.duckThreshold || 0.02}:ratio=${M.duckRatio || 3}:attack=30:release=450:makeup=1[duck];`\n + `[voice][duck]amix=inputs=2:duration=first:dropout_transition=0,volume=2,alimiter=limit=0.79:level=false[a]`;\n run(['-i', joined, '-stream_loop', '-1', '-i', path.resolve(root, M.file), '-filter_complex', g, '-map', '0:v:0', '-map', '[a]', '-c:v', 'copy', '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-movflags', '+faststart', '-t', D.toFixed(3), outFile]);\n } else {\n run(['-i', joined, '-map', '0:v:0', '-map', '0:a:0', '-c:v', 'copy', '-af', voice, '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-movflags', '+faststart', outFile]);\n }\n console.log(JSON.stringify({ stage: 'finished', out: outFile, durationMs: compiled.durationMs, pieces: total, captionPages: compiled.captions.length, inputLufs: js.input_i, findings: compiled.findings.filter(f => f.type !== 'silence_removed') }));\n}\nmain();\n```\n\n## engine/section.js\n```javascript\n'use strict';\n// Cut a section (whole thought, word to word) out of a CLEAN render and export its word timings for a Remotion scene.\n// Usage: node section.js <outDir-with-compiled.json-and-clean-render.mp4> <fromWord> <toWord> <destDir> [--src m1]\n// Writes <destDir>/public/src.mp4 (the footage) and <destDir>/src/data.json ({ words:[{i,text,s,e}], cuts:[ms], startMs, endMs }).\n// Times in data.json are relative to the section start. startMs/endMs are the section's place in the full render (for splice.sh).\nconst fs = require('fs'), path = require('path'), { spawnSync } = require('child_process');\nconst [dir, a, b, dest] = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst src = process.argv.includes('--src') ? process.argv[process.argv.indexOf('--src') + 1] : null;\nconst c = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, 'compiled.json'), 'utf8'));\nconst fps = c.format.fps, fr = 1000 / fps;\nconst inRange = w => w.i >= +a && w.i <= +b && w.text !== '' && (!src || w.src === src);\nconst ws = c.words.filter(inRange);\nif (!ws.length) throw new Error('no words in range');\nconst startMs = Math.floor(Math.max(0, ws[0].outStartMs - 120) / fr) * fr;\nconst endMs = Math.min(c.durationMs, Math.ceil((ws[ws.length - 1].outEndMs + 250) / fr) * fr);\nfs.mkdirSync(path.join(dest, 'public'), { recursive: true }); fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dest, 'src'), { recursive: true });\nconst r = spawnSync('ffmpeg', ['-v', 'error', '-y', '-ss', (startMs / 1000).toFixed(4), '-i', path.join(dir, 'render.mp4'), '-t', ((endMs - startMs) / 1000).toFixed(4),\n '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'veryfast', '-crf', '16', '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-movflags', '+faststart', path.join(dest, 'public', 'src.mp4')], { stdio: 'inherit' });\nif (r.status !== 0) process.exit(1);\nconst words = ws.map(w => ({ i: w.i, text: w.text, s: w.outStartMs - startMs, e: w.outEndMs - startMs }));\nconst cuts = c.pieces.filter(p => p.outStartMs + (p.outMs - p.inMs) > startMs && p.outStartMs < endMs).map(p => Math.max(0, p.outStartMs - startMs));\nfs.writeFileSync(path.join(dest, 'src', 'data.json'), JSON.stringify({ words, cuts, startMs, endMs, width: c.format.width, height: c.format.height, fps }));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({ startMs, endMs, durationS: (endMs - startMs) / 1000, words: words.length, cuts: cuts.length }));\n```\n\n## engine/splice.sh\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\n# Replace startMs..endMs of a full render with a finished scene clip (same length), keeping audio from the scene clip.\n# Usage: splice.sh <full.mp4> <scene.mp4> <startMs> <endMs> <out.mp4>\n# Re-encodes the whole video: fine in the cloud workspace (run in background); for long videos on the user's Mac,\n# render scenes as standalone clips instead (each Mac command is capped at 3 minutes).\nset -e\nFULL=$1; SCENE=$2; A=$(python3 -c \"print($3/1000)\"); B=$(python3 -c \"print($4/1000)\"); OUT=$5\nread W H FPS < <(ffprobe -v error -select_streams v:0 -show_entries stream=width,height,r_frame_rate -of csv=p=0 \"$FULL\" | awk -F, '{split($3,f,\"/\"); print $1, $2, f[1]/f[2]}')\nffmpeg -v error -y -i \"$FULL\" -i \"$SCENE\" -filter_complex \"\\\n[0:v]trim=end=$A,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[v0];[0:a]atrim=end=$A,asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS[a0];\\\n[1:v]scale=$W:$H:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=$W:$H,fps=$FPS,setsar=1,trim=end=$(python3 -c \"print($B-$A)\"),setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[v1];\\\n[1:a]aresample=48000,atrim=end=$(python3 -c \"print($B-$A)\"),asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS[a1];\\\n[0:v]trim=start=$B,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[v2];[0:a]atrim=start=$B,asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS[a2];\\\n[v0][a0][v1][a1][v2][a2]concat=n=3:v=1:a=1[v][a]\" -map \"[v]\" -map \"[a]\" \\\n -c:v libx264 -preset veryfast -crf 18 -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:a aac -b:a 192k -movflags +faststart \"$OUT\"\necho \"SPLICED $OUT\"\n```\n", scenes: "# NestTrack video editor: animated scenes\n\n## 6. Animated scenes (Remotion)\nUse for produced YouTube sections: custom 2D scenes (`scene_style` colors: dark background + one bright accent), the speaker in an accent-bordered picture-in-picture top-right, accent-colored word captions, counters, sound effects. Runs in the **cloud workspace**. ~9 s of render per 1 s of scene on 2 CPUs, so do sections (20–60 s), not whole films. Remotion is free for individuals, for-profit companies with up to 3 employees, and non-profits; larger companies need a Remotion Company License (remotion.dev/license). If the user hasn't said which applies, mention it once the first time they ask for an animated scene.\n1. Render the edit with `--clean` (no burned text) → footage source: `node engine/render.js edit.json out/clean --all --clean` (cloud; on the Mac use `--budget 150` calls then `--finish`).\n2. Write the remo-kit files (below) to `~/remo-kit/`, run `bash ~/remo-kit/setup.sh` once (creates ~/remo, installs Remotion 4.0.539, finds Chromium, makes pop/tick/whoosh/chime SFX), and copy `render.mjs`, `stills.mjs`, `mix.sh` into ~/remo.\n3. Cut the section: `node ~/vs/engine/section.js ~/vs/out/clean <fromWord> <toWord> ~/remo [--src m1]` → `public/src.mp4` + `src/data.json` (words relative to the section, piece cut times, startMs/endMs). **Start and end on full sentences** so the scene doesn't split a caption page. Long video on the Mac: run section.js there with the destination inside the connected folder (`\"$HOME/mnt/<folder>/_review/scene\"` — files outside mnt/ can't be staged), then `device_stage_files` the two files up and copy them into ~/remo.\n4. Write `~/remo/src/index.jsx` for this section, adapting `scene-example.jsx` (below): anchor every beat to a spoken word with `at(wordIndex)`, footage → PiP spring at the scene start, punch-ins on alternate cuts while full-frame, captions component, design only from what the speaker says; swap the example's colors `C.bg`/`C.lime` for `scene_style.background`/`accent`. Keep designed text ≥ 20 px, clear of the PiP (top-right 576×324 at 64 px margins) and the caption band (y ≈ 930).\n5. Check stills at every beat first: `node stills.mjs <Comp> <frames…>`, tile them, Read. Fix overlaps (the example's counter originally touched the PiP and the focus bucket overlapped the title — both caught this way). Then `node render.mjs <Comp> out/scene.mp4` (background) and `./mix.sh out/scene.mp4 out/scene_final.mp4 -14 \"whoosh 10.30 0.30\" \"pop 10.80 0.35\" …` with SFX on the same word times.\n6. Deliver as a standalone clip, or splice into a cloud render: `~/vs/engine/splice.sh full.mp4 scene_final.mp4 <startMs> <endMs> out.mp4` (re-encodes; cloud/background only).\nMusic for scenes: mix the scene with `mix.sh`, or splice it into a render that already has the music bed.\n\n\n## remo-kit/setup.sh\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\n# One-time per session: Remotion project in ~/remo (cloud workspace; uses the preinstalled Playwright Chromium).\nset -e\nmkdir -p ~/remo/src ~/remo/public ~/remo/out ~/remo/sfx && cd ~/remo\n[ -f package.json ] || npm init -y >/dev/null\nnpm install --no-audit --no-fund remotion@4.0.539 @remotion/cli@4.0.539 @remotion/renderer@4.0.539 @remotion/bundler@4.0.539 react@18 react-dom@18 >/dev/null\nCH=$(ls -d /opt/pw-browsers/chromium-*/chrome-linux64/chrome 2>/dev/null | head -1)\n[ -n \"$CH\" ] || CH=$(node -e \"console.log(require('playwright').chromium.executablePath())\" 2>/dev/null)\necho \"$CH\" > chrome_path.txt\n# Sound effects (synthesized, royalty-free).\nffmpeg -v error -y -f lavfi -i \"aevalsrc='0.6*sin(2*PI*(500+900*t)*t)*exp(-t*38)':s=48000:d=0.12\" -ac 2 sfx/pop.wav\nffmpeg -v error -y -f lavfi -i \"aevalsrc='0.5*sin(2*PI*2600*t)*exp(-t*120)':s=48000:d=0.05\" -ac 2 sfx/tick.wav\nffmpeg -v error -y -f lavfi -i \"anoisesrc=d=0.45:c=pink:a=0.5:r=48000\" -af \"highpass=f=400,lowpass=f=5000,afade=t=in:d=0.18:curve=exp,afade=t=out:st=0.2:d=0.25\" -ac 2 sfx/whoosh.wav\nffmpeg -v error -y -f lavfi -i \"aevalsrc='0.35*(sin(2*PI*1318*t)+0.6*sin(2*PI*1975*t))*exp(-t*9)':s=48000:d=0.5\" -ac 2 sfx/chime.wav\necho \"REMOTION $(node -e \"console.log(require('remotion/package.json').version)\") chrome=$CH\"\n```\n\n## remo-kit/render.mjs\n```javascript\n// Usage: node render.mjs <CompositionId> <out.mp4> [entry=src/index.jsx]\nimport { bundle } from '@remotion/bundler';\nimport { renderMedia, selectComposition } from '@remotion/renderer';\nimport fs from 'fs';\nconst [id, out, entry = 'src/index.jsx'] = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst browserExecutable = fs.readFileSync(new URL('./chrome_path.txt', import.meta.url), 'utf8').trim() || undefined;\nconst t = Date.now();\nconst serveUrl = await bundle({ entryPoint: new URL('./' + entry, import.meta.url).pathname, publicDir: new URL('./public', import.meta.url).pathname });\nconst composition = await selectComposition({ serveUrl, id, browserExecutable });\nawait renderMedia({ composition, serveUrl, codec: 'h264', crf: 18, concurrency: 2, outputLocation: out, browserExecutable });\nconsole.log('RENDERED', out, ((Date.now() - t) / 1000).toFixed(1) + 's');\n```\n\n## remo-kit/stills.mjs\n```javascript\n// Usage: node stills.mjs <CompositionId> <frame> [<frame> ...] -> out/still_<frame>.png at half size\nimport { bundle } from '@remotion/bundler';\nimport { renderStill, selectComposition } from '@remotion/renderer';\nimport fs from 'fs';\nconst [id, ...frames] = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst browserExecutable = fs.readFileSync(new URL('./chrome_path.txt', import.meta.url), 'utf8').trim() || undefined;\nconst serveUrl = await bundle({ entryPoint: new URL('./src/index.jsx', import.meta.url).pathname, publicDir: new URL('./public', import.meta.url).pathname });\nconst composition = await selectComposition({ serveUrl, id, browserExecutable });\nfor (const fr of frames.map(Number)) await renderStill({ composition, serveUrl, frame: fr, output: `out/still_${String(fr).padStart(4, '0')}.png`, browserExecutable, scale: 0.5 });\nconsole.log('STILLS', frames.join(' '));\n```\n\n## remo-kit/mix.sh\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\n# Mix sound effects under a rendered scene and level it.\n# Usage: mix.sh <in.mp4> <out.mp4> <lufs> \"name seconds gain\" [\"name seconds gain\" ...]\n# names: pop tick whoosh chime (from ~/remo/sfx). Example: mix.sh out/scene.mp4 out/final.mp4 -14 \"whoosh 10.30 0.30\" \"tick 22.51 0.40\"\nset -e\nIN=$1; OUT=$2; LUFS=$3; shift 3\nARGS=(-i \"$IN\"); F=\"\"; L=\"[0:a]\"; n=0\nfor ev in \"$@\"; do set -- $ev; n=$((n+1)); ms=$(python3 -c \"print(int($2*1000))\")\n ARGS+=(-i ~/remo/sfx/$1.wav); F+=\"[$n:a]adelay=${ms}|${ms},volume=$3[s$n];\"; L+=\"[s$n]\"; done\nF+=\"${L}amix=inputs=$((n+1)):normalize=0:duration=first,loudnorm=I=$LUFS:TP=-1.5:LRA=11[a]\"\nffmpeg -v error -y \"${ARGS[@]}\" -filter_complex \"$F\" -map 0:v -map \"[a]\" -c:v copy -c:a aac -b:a 192k -movflags +faststart \"$OUT\"\necho \"MIXED $OUT\"\n```\n\n## remo-kit/scene-example.jsx (the 11:20 commission-buckets scene — adapt per section)\n```javascript\nimport React from 'react';\nimport {\n registerRoot, Composition, AbsoluteFill, OffthreadVideo, staticFile,\n useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, interpolate, spring, Easing,\n} from 'remotion';\nimport data from './data.json';\n\n// ---- Style tokens (Loop \"iman\"-style look: charcoal + electric lime + white) ----\nconst C = { bg: '#12151B', panel: '#1A1D24', lime: '#CCFF00', white: '#FFFFFF', dim: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)', glass: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.07)' };\nconst FONT = \"'Inter', 'Inter Display', sans-serif\";\nconst FPS = 30;\nconst W = data.words;\nconst at = (i) => { const w = W.find((x) => x.i >= i); return Math.round((w.s / 1000) * FPS); }; // frame where word i starts\nconst clamp = { extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp' };\nconst ease = Easing.bezier(0.33, 0, 0.2, 1);\n\n// ---- Key beats, anchored to spoken words ----\nconst B = {\n sceneIn: at(2631), // \"at my company we call them buckets\"\n buckets: at(2636), // \"buckets,\"\n three: at(2658), // \"three different levels\"\n top: at(2667), // \"2.75%\"\n bps: at(2671), // \"275 basis points\"\n focus: at(2696), // \"It's 2.75.\"\n split: at(2703), // \"the company takes it down to 2.20\"\n splitVal: at(2708), // \"2.20.\"\n team: at(2728), // \"On my team they take some too\"\n less: at(2745), // \"less than 2%.\"\n full: at(2747), // \"2% at a full commission.\"\n};\n\n// ---- Footage: full frame -> PiP top-right, with punch-ins on alternate cuts ----\nconst Footage = () => {\n const f = useCurrentFrame();\n const { fps } = useVideoConfig();\n const p = spring({ frame: f - B.sceneIn, fps, config: { damping: 18, stiffness: 120 } });\n const cutIdx = data.cuts.filter((c) => (c / 1000) * fps <= f).length - 1;\n const punch = cutIdx % 2 === 1 ? 1.12 : 1.0;\n const zoom = interpolate(p, [0, 1], [punch, 1.0]); // drop the punch-in once we're in PiP\n const pip = { x: 1920 - 64 - 576, y: 64, w: 576, h: 324 };\n const box = {\n left: interpolate(p, [0, 1], [0, pip.x]), top: interpolate(p, [0, 1], [0, pip.y]),\n width: interpolate(p, [0, 1], [1920, pip.w]), height: interpolate(p, [0, 1], [1080, pip.h]),\n radius: interpolate(p, [0, 1], [0, 22]), border: interpolate(p, [0, 1], [0, 3]),\n };\n return (\n <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: box.left, top: box.top, width: box.width, height: box.height, borderRadius: box.radius,\n overflow: 'hidden', boxShadow: p > 0.01 ? `0 0 0 ${box.border}px ${C.lime}, 0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.55), 0 0 40px rgba(204,255,0,${0.18 * p})` : 'none', zIndex: 5 }}>\n <OffthreadVideo src={staticFile('src.mp4')} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover',\n transform: `scale(${zoom})`, transformOrigin: '62% 35%', filter: 'contrast(1.06) saturate(1.05)' }} />\n </div>\n );\n};\n\n// ---- Scene background ----\nconst SceneBG = () => {\n const f = useCurrentFrame();\n const o = interpolate(f, [B.sceneIn - 2, B.sceneIn + 10], [0, 1], clamp);\n return (\n <AbsoluteFill style={{ opacity: o, background: `radial-gradient(1200px 700px at 30% 55%, #1E2430 0%, ${C.bg} 70%)` }}>\n <svg width=\"1920\" height=\"1080\" style={{ position: 'absolute', opacity: 0.35 }}>\n {Array.from({ length: 24 }).map((_, x) => Array.from({ length: 14 }).map((__, y) => (\n <circle key={`${x}-${y}`} cx={40 + x * 80} cy={40 + y * 80} r={1.6} fill=\"rgba(255,255,255,0.18)\" />\n )))}\n </svg>\n <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 120, top: 92, fontFamily: FONT, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 30, letterSpacing: 4, color: C.lime }}>WHAT YOU TAKE HOME</div>\n <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 120, top: 136, fontFamily: FONT, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 64, color: C.white }}>Commission buckets</div>\n </AbsoluteFill>\n );\n};\n\n// ---- A glass bucket with animated lime liquid ----\nconst Bucket = ({ x, y, w, h, level, glow, label, sub, appear }) => {\n const f = useCurrentFrame();\n const { fps } = useVideoConfig();\n const s = spring({ frame: f - appear, fps, config: { damping: 13, stiffness: 140 } });\n if (f < appear) return null;\n const r = w * 0.16;\n const path = `M ${x} ${y} L ${x + w} ${y} L ${x + w - w * 0.08} ${y + h - r} Q ${x + w - w * 0.1} ${y + h} ${x + w - w * 0.08 - r} ${y + h} L ${x + w * 0.08 + r} ${y + h} Q ${x + w * 0.1} ${y + h} ${x + w * 0.08} ${y + h - r} Z`;\n const ly = y + h - h * level;\n const wave = (k) => Math.sin(f / 9 + k) * 6;\n const liquid = `M ${x - 10} ${ly + wave(0)} C ${x + w * 0.3} ${ly + wave(1.5)}, ${x + w * 0.7} ${ly + wave(3)}, ${x + w + 10} ${ly + wave(4.5)} L ${x + w + 10} ${y + h + 10} L ${x - 10} ${y + h + 10} Z`;\n const id = `clip-${x}`;\n return (\n <g style={{ transform: `translateY(${(1 - s) * 60}px) scale(${0.85 + 0.15 * s})`, transformOrigin: `${x + w / 2}px ${y + h}px`, opacity: s }}>\n <defs>\n <clipPath id={id}><path d={path} /></clipPath>\n <linearGradient id={`lg-${x}`} x1=\"0\" y1=\"0\" x2=\"0\" y2=\"1\">\n <stop offset=\"0\" stopColor={C.lime} stopOpacity=\"0.95\" /><stop offset=\"1\" stopColor=\"#7FA300\" stopOpacity=\"0.9\" />\n </linearGradient>\n </defs>\n <path d={path} fill={C.glass} />\n <g clipPath={`url(#${id})`}><path d={liquid} fill={`url(#lg-${x})`} /></g>\n <path d={path} fill=\"none\" stroke={glow ? C.lime : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'} strokeWidth={glow ? 4 : 2.5}\n style={{ filter: glow ? `drop-shadow(0 0 18px ${C.lime})` : 'none' }} />\n <path d={`M ${x + w * 0.16} ${y + 24} L ${x + w * 0.2} ${y + h - 40}`} stroke=\"rgba(255,255,255,0.18)\" strokeWidth={6} strokeLinecap=\"round\" />\n <text x={x + w / 2} y={y + h + 58} textAnchor=\"middle\" fontFamily={FONT} fontWeight={800} fontSize={34} fill={glow ? C.lime : C.white}>{label}</text>\n {sub ? <text x={x + w / 2} y={y + h + 96} textAnchor=\"middle\" fontFamily={FONT} fontWeight={600} fontSize={24} fill={C.dim}>{sub}</text> : null}\n </g>\n );\n};\n\n// ---- Animated counter ----\nconst fmt = (v) => v.toFixed(2) + '%';\nconst Counter = () => {\n const f = useCurrentFrame();\n let v = interpolate(f, [B.top, B.top + 20], [0, 2.75], { ...clamp, easing: ease });\n v = f >= B.splitVal - 6 ? interpolate(f, [B.splitVal - 6, B.splitVal + 14], [2.75, 2.20], { ...clamp, easing: ease }) : v;\n const isLess = f >= B.less - 4;\n const o = interpolate(f, [B.top - 4, B.top + 6], [0, 1], clamp);\n if (f < B.top - 4) return null;\n const big = f >= B.focus;\n return (\n <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: big ? 860 : 1180, top: big ? 430 : 720, opacity: o, fontFamily: FONT }}>\n <div style={{ fontWeight: 900, fontSize: big ? 150 : 96, color: C.lime, lineHeight: 1, textShadow: '0 0 30px rgba(204,255,0,0.35)', fontVariantNumeric: 'tabular-nums' }}>\n {isLess ? '< 2%' : fmt(v)}\n </div>\n {!big ? <div style={{ marginTop: 10, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 30, color: C.white, opacity: interpolate(f, [B.bps, B.bps + 8], [0, 1], clamp) }}>= 275 basis points</div> : null}\n </div>\n );\n};\n\n// ---- Ledger of cuts (appears in focus phase) ----\nconst Ledger = () => {\n const f = useCurrentFrame();\n if (f < B.focus) return null;\n const rows = [\n { at: B.focus, label: 'Top bucket', val: '2.75%', color: C.white },\n { at: B.split, label: 'Company split', val: '− 0.55%', color: '#FF6B6B' },\n { at: B.team, label: 'Team split', val: '− team share', color: '#FF6B6B' },\n { at: B.less, label: 'You keep (full commission)', val: '< 2%', color: C.lime },\n ];\n return (\n <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 868, top: 630, width: 760, fontFamily: FONT }}>\n {rows.map((r, k) => {\n const o = interpolate(f, [r.at, r.at + 8], [0, 1], clamp);\n const x = interpolate(f, [r.at, r.at + 10], [-30, 0], { ...clamp, easing: ease });\n return (\n <div key={k} style={{ opacity: o, transform: `translateX(${x}px)`, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', padding: '14px 0',\n borderTop: k === 3 ? `2px solid ${C.lime}` : '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.14)', fontSize: k === 3 ? 38 : 32, fontWeight: k === 3 ? 900 : 700 }}>\n <span style={{ color: k === 3 ? C.lime : C.white }}>{r.label}</span><span style={{ color: r.color }}>{r.val}</span>\n </div>\n );\n })}\n </div>\n );\n};\n\n// ---- Bucket layout: three buckets, then focus on one that drains ----\nconst Buckets = () => {\n const f = useCurrentFrame();\n if (f < B.sceneIn + 8) return null;\n const focus = interpolate(f, [B.focus, B.focus + 18], [0, 1], { ...clamp, easing: ease });\n const level3 = interpolate(f, [B.top, B.top + 24], [0.35, 0.92], { ...clamp, easing: ease });\n let lv = level3;\n lv = f >= B.splitVal - 6 ? interpolate(f, [B.splitVal - 6, B.splitVal + 14], [0.92, 0.74], { ...clamp, easing: ease }) : lv;\n lv = f >= B.team + 20 ? interpolate(f, [B.team + 20, B.team + 40], [0.74, 0.64], { ...clamp, easing: ease }) : lv;\n const glow3 = f >= B.top;\n const threePulse = f >= B.three && f < B.three + 30;\n const others = 1 - focus;\n return (\n <svg width=\"1920\" height=\"1080\" style={{ position: 'absolute' }}>\n <g style={{ opacity: others }}>\n <Bucket x={150} y={430} w={200} h={300} level={0.28} label=\"Level 1\" appear={B.sceneIn + 10} glow={threePulse} />\n <Bucket x={410} y={390} w={220} h={340} level={0.5} label=\"Level 2\" appear={B.sceneIn + 15} glow={threePulse} />\n </g>\n <g style={{ transform: `translate(${interpolate(focus, [0, 1], [0, -420])}px, ${interpolate(focus, [0, 1], [0, 30])}px) scale(${interpolate(focus, [0, 1], [1, 1.25])})`, transformOrigin: '800px 730px' }}>\n <Bucket x={690} y={350} w={240} h={380} level={lv} label=\"Top level\" sub={f >= B.top ? '2.75% · 275 bps' : null} appear={B.sceneIn + 20} glow={glow3} />\n </g>\n </svg>\n );\n};\n\n// ---- Captions: phrase pages, white, active word lime ----\nconst pages = (() => {\n const out = []; let cur = [];\n for (const w of W) {\n const prev = cur[cur.length - 1];\n if (prev && w.s - prev.e > 600) { out.push(cur); cur = []; }\n cur.push(w);\n if (cur.length >= 5 || /[.?!,]$/.test(w.text)) { out.push(cur); cur = []; }\n }\n if (cur.length) out.push(cur);\n for (let k = out.length - 1; k > 0; k--) if (out[k].length === 1 && out[k - 1].length < 5 && !/[.?!]$/.test(out[k - 1].slice(-1)[0].text)) { out[k - 1].push(...out[k]); out.splice(k, 1); }\n return out;\n})();\nconst Captions = () => {\n const f = useCurrentFrame();\n const ms = (f / FPS) * 1000;\n const k = pages.findIndex((p, j) => ms >= p[0].s && ms < (pages[j + 1] ? Math.min(pages[j + 1][0].s, p[p.length - 1].e + 400) : p[p.length - 1].e + 400));\n if (k < 0) return null;\n const pg = pages[k];\n const pop = interpolate(ms - pg[0].s, [0, 90], [0.92, 1], clamp);\n return (\n <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: 0, right: 0, top: 930, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', zIndex: 20 }}>\n <div style={{ transform: `scale(${pop})`, fontFamily: FONT, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 58, color: C.white, letterSpacing: -0.5,\n textShadow: '0 3px 0 rgba(0,0,0,0.55), 0 0 18px rgba(0,0,0,0.45)', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>\n {pg.map((w, j) => {\n const active = ms >= w.s && (j === pg.length - 1 || ms < pg[j + 1].s);\n return <span key={w.i} style={{ color: active ? C.lime : C.white }}>{w.text}{j < pg.length - 1 ? ' ' : ''}</span>;\n })}\n </div>\n </div>\n );\n};\n\nconst Commission = () => (\n <AbsoluteFill style={{ background: C.bg }}>\n <SceneBG />\n <Buckets />\n <Counter />\n <Ledger />\n <Footage />\n <Captions />\n </AbsoluteFill>\n);\n\nregisterRoot(() => (\n <Composition id=\"Commission\" component={Commission} durationInFrames={Math.floor(((data.endMs - data.startMs) || 50700) / 1000 * FPS)} fps={FPS} width={1920} height={1080} />\n));\n```\n" }; // ── Video editor ── // The steps come back as text with this agent's own details filled in. Nothing is assumed about // who they are: a missing detail is left blank and asked for only when the edit needs it. function _videoEditSlug(t) { return String(t || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '').slice(0, 50) || 'video'; } function _videoEditYaml(v) { return JSON.stringify(String(v == null ? '' : v)); } function _videoEditGuide(agent, profile, video, month) { const who = agent.name || 'this agent'; const spell = []; [agent.name, agent.brokerage, profile.market].concat(profile.communities || []).forEach(function (x) { String(x || '').split(',').forEach(function (p) { p = p.trim(); if (p.length > 2 && spell.indexOf(p) === -1) spell.push(p); }); }); const out = [ '# NestTrack video editor', 'You are editing a video for ' + who + ', a real estate agent, through NestTrack. Do not post anything for them.', '', '# The method', 'Tool names differ between Claude apps; use the ones you have for running commands and for handing files over.', '', VIDEO_EDIT_PARTS.guide); return out.join('\n'); } function _contentLine(v, max) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, max); } function _contentBlock(v, max) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n').replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim().slice(0, max); } function _contentLines(v, maxItems, maxLen) { const arr = Array.isArray(v) ? v : String(v == null ? '' : v).split(/\n+/); return arr.map(function (x) { return _contentLine((x && typeof x === 'object') ? (x.text || '') : x, maxLen); }) .filter(Boolean).slice(0, maxItems); } function _contentCommunities(v) { const arr = Array.isArray(v) ? v : String(v == null ? '' : v).split(/[,;\n]+/); const out = [], seen = {}; arr.forEach(function (x) { const s = _contentLine(x, 60), k = s.toLowerCase(); if (s && !seen[k]) { seen[k] = 1; out.push(s); } }); return out.slice(0, 5); } function _contentFormat(v) { const s = String(v == null ? '' : v).toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[\s\-\/]+/g, '_'); if (CONTENT_FORMATS[s]) return s; if (/green/.test(s)) return 'green_screen'; if (/stat|text|number|graphic/.test(s)) return 'stat'; if (/story|client/.test(s)) return 'story'; if (/drive|location|walk|tour/.test(s)) return 'on_location'; if (/talk|head|camera/.test(s)) return 'talking_head'; return ''; } function _contentDemand(v) { const s = String(v == null ? '' : v).toUpperCase(); return /HIGH/.test(s) ? 'HIGH' : (/MED/.test(s) ? 'MEDIUM' : (/LOW|EMERG/.test(s) ? 'LOW' : '')); } // ── Months ── function _contentMonthOk(m) { const s = String(m == null ? '' : m).trim(); return /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.test(s) ? s : ''; } function _contentMonthAdd(m, n) { const d = new Date(Date.UTC(+m.slice(0, 4), +m.slice(5, 7) - 1 + n, 1)); return d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'); } function _contentMonthName(m) { return CONTENT_MONTHS[+m.slice(5, 7) - 1] + ' ' + m.slice(0, 4); } function _contentThisMonth(tz) { return _mcpYmd(Date.now(), _mcpTzOk(tz) || 'UTC').slice(0, 7); } // The month a tool call is about. Plans are made for this month or the next one. function _contentPickMonth(given, tz, anyMonth) { const cur = _contentThisMonth(tz); if (given === undefined || given === null || String(given).trim() === '') return cur; const m = _contentMonthOk(given); if (!m) throw new Error('month must look like ' + cur + ' (YYYY-MM).'); if (!anyMonth && m !== cur && m !== _contentMonthAdd(cur, 1)) { throw new Error('A plan can be made for ' + _contentMonthName(cur) + ' (' + cur + ') or ' + _contentMonthName(_contentMonthAdd(cur, 1)) + ' (' + _contentMonthAdd(cur, 1) + ').'); } return m; } // ── Profile ── function _contentProfile(d) { d = d || {}; const pw = Number(d.perWeek); return { market: _contentLine(d.market, 120), communities: _contentCommunities(d.communities), focus: _contentLine(d.focus, 160), voice: _contentLine(d.voice, 160), cta: _contentLine(d.cta, 200), perWeek: CONTENT_PER_WEEK.indexOf(pw) !== -1 ? pw : CONTENT_DEFAULT_PER_WEEK }; } // Only what was given is changed. function _contentProfileMerge(prevDoc, given) { const p = _contentProfile(prevDoc); given = given || {}; if (given.market !== undefined) p.market = _contentLine(given.market, 120); if (given.communities !== undefined) p.communities = _contentCommunities(given.communities); if (given.focus !== undefined) p.focus = _contentLine(given.focus, 160); if (given.voice !== undefined) p.voice = _contentLine(given.voice, 160); if (given.cta !== undefined) p.cta = _contentLine(given.cta, 200); if (given.perWeek !== undefined) { const n = Number(given.perWeek); if (CONTENT_PER_WEEK.indexOf(n) === -1) throw new Error('Videos per week must be 2, 3 or 5.'); p.perWeek = n; } return p; } async function _contentProfileWrite(token, agentId, p) { await _mcpPatch(token, 'content_profiles/' + agentId, { agentId: agentId, market: p.market, communities: p.communities, focus: p.focus, voice: p.voice, cta: p.cta, perWeek: p.perWeek, updatedAt: Date.now() }); } // ── Videos ── function _contentStatus(v) { return v.posted ? 'posted' : (v.filmed ? 'filmed' : (v.script ? 'scripted' : 'idea')); } function _contentBlank() { const now = Date.now(); return { id: 'v' + _mcpNewId().slice(0, 10), n: 0, week: 0, title: '', search: '', demand: '', evidence: '', gap: false, format: '', angle: '', hooks: [], best: 0, script: '', bullets: [], shots: [], captions: { instagram: '', tiktok: '', youtube: '' }, keep: false, own: false, filmed: false, edited: false, editFile: '', posted: false, status: 'idea', postId: '', createdAt: now, updatedAt: now }; } // A stored video, with anything missing filled in so the rest of the code can rely on its shape. function _contentNormalize(v) { const b = _contentBlank(); v = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {}; const o = Object.assign(b, v); o.captions = Object.assign({ instagram: '', tiktok: '', youtube: '' }, (v.captions && typeof v.captions === 'object') ? v.captions : {}); o.hooks = Array.isArray(o.hooks) ? o.hooks : []; o.bullets = Array.isArray(o.bullets) ? o.bullets : []; o.shots = Array.isArray(o.shots) ? o.shots : []; o.keep = o.keep === true; o.own = o.own === true; o.filmed = o.filmed === true; o.posted = o.posted === true; o.gap = o.gap === true; o.edited = o.edited === true; o.editFile = (o.edited && typeof o.editFile === 'string') ? o.editFile : ''; o.week = (o.week >= 1 && o.week <= 4) ? Math.round(o.week) : 1; o.status = _contentStatus(o); return o; } // Applies the given fields to a video. Field names are the ones the tools use. function _contentApply(v, a) { a = a || {}; if (a.title !== undefined) { const t = _contentLine(a.title, 160); if (!t) throw new Error('A video needs a title.'); v.title = t; } if (a.search !== undefined) v.search = _contentLine(a.search, 200); if (a.demand !== undefined) v.demand = _contentDemand(a.demand); if (a.evidence !== undefined) v.evidence = _contentLine(a.evidence, 300); if (a.gap !== undefined) v.gap = a.gap === true; if (a.format !== undefined) v.format = _contentFormat(a.format); if (a.angle !== undefined) v.angle = _contentLine(a.angle, 300); if (a.week !== undefined) { const w = Math.round(Number(a.week)); if (!(w >= 1 && w <= 4)) throw new Error('week must be 1, 2, 3 or 4.'); v.week = w; } if (a.hooks !== undefined) v.hooks = _contentLines(a.hooks, 3, 200); if (a.best_hook !== undefined) { const b = Math.round(Number(a.best_hook)); v.best = (b >= 0 && b <= 2) ? b : 0; } if (a.script !== undefined) v.script = _contentBlock(a.script, 3000); if (a.bullets !== undefined) v.bullets = _contentLines(a.bullets, 8, 240); if (a.shots !== undefined) v.shots = _contentLines(a.shots, 10, 240); if (a.captions && typeof a.captions === 'object') { if (a.captions.instagram !== undefined) v.captions.instagram = _contentBlock(a.captions.instagram, 2200); if (a.captions.tiktok !== undefined) v.captions.tiktok = _contentBlock(a.captions.tiktok, 300); if (a.captions.youtube !== undefined) v.captions.youtube = _contentBlock(a.captions.youtube, 600); } if (a.keep !== undefined) v.keep = a.keep === true; if (a.filmed !== undefined) { v.filmed = a.filmed === true; if (!v.filmed) { v.posted = false; v.edited = false; } } // Edited is its own tick between filmed and posted. A video that is edited has been filmed. if (a.edited !== undefined) { v.edited = a.edited === true; if (v.edited) v.filmed = true; } // The name of the finished file, so the card can say which one to drop on it. A name only, never a path. if (a.edit_file !== undefined) v.editFile = _contentLine(String(a.edit_file == null ? '' : a.edit_file).split(/[\\/]/).pop(), 120); if (!v.edited) v.editFile = ''; if (a.posted !== undefined) { v.posted = a.posted === true; if (v.posted) v.filmed = true; } // The post made from this card in the scheduler, so the card can follow it out the door. if (a.post_id !== undefined) { const pid = String(a.post_id == null ? '' : a.post_id); v.postId = /^[A-Za-z0-9]{8,40}$/.test(pid) ? pid : ''; } if (v.best >= v.hooks.length) v.best = 0; v.status = _contentStatus(v); v.updatedAt = Date.now(); return v; } function _contentVideoPublic(v) { return { id: v.id, n: v.n, week: v.week, title: v.title, search: v.search, demand: v.demand, evidence: v.evidence, gap: v.gap, format: v.format, format_label: CONTENT_FORMATS[v.format] || '', angle: v.angle, status: v.status, keep: v.keep, own: v.own, filmed: v.filmed, edited: v.edited === true, edit_file: v.editFile || '', posted: v.posted, post_id: v.postId || '', hooks: v.hooks, best_hook: v.best, script: v.script, script_words: v.script ? v.script.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length : 0, bullets: v.bullets, shots: v.shots, captions: v.captions }; } // Week order, then the order they were added. The number on a card is its place in that order. function _contentNumber(videos) { videos.forEach(function (v, i) { v._i = i; }); videos.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.week - b.week) || (a._i - b._i); }); videos.forEach(function (v, i) { v.n = i + 1; delete v._i; }); return videos; } // A video that survives a re-plan: one the agent kept, wrote themselves, or has already filmed. function _contentStays(v) { return v.keep === true || v.own === true || v.filmed === true || v.posted === true; } function _contentEmptiestWeek(videos) { const c = [0, 0, 0, 0]; videos.forEach(function (v) { if (v.week >= 1 && v.week <= 4) c[v.week - 1]++; }); let best = 0; for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) { if (c[i] < c[best]) best = i; } return best + 1; } // ── Plans ── async function _contentLoadPlan(token, agentId, month) { const d = await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'content_plans/' + agentId + '_' + month); if (!d || d.agentId !== agentId) return { month: month, headline: '', videos: [], exists: false, updatedAt: 0 }; let list = []; try { list = JSON.parse(d.videosJson || '[]'); } catch (e) { list = []; } return { month: month, headline: _contentLine(d.headline, 300), exists: true, updatedAt: Number(d.updatedAt || 0), videos: _contentNumber((Array.isArray(list) ? list : []).map(_contentNormalize)) }; } async function _contentSavePlan(token, agentId, plan) { _contentNumber(plan.videos); const json = JSON.stringify(plan.videos); if (json.length > CONTENT_MAX_JSON) throw new Error('This month\'s plan is too long to save. Shorten a script or remove a video.'); await _mcpPatch(token, 'content_plans/' + agentId + '_' + plan.month, { agentId: agentId, month: plan.month, headline: plan.headline || '', videosJson: json, count: plan.videos.length, updatedAt: Date.now() }, plan.exists ? {} : { createdAt: fsTimestamp() }); plan.exists = true; plan.updatedAt = Date.now(); } function _contentFind(plan, id) { const want = String(id == null ? '' : id).trim(); if (!want) throw new Error('id is required. Get it from get_content_plan.'); const v = plan.videos.find(function (x) { return x.id === want; }); if (!v) throw new Error('No video with id "' + want.slice(0, 40) + '" on the ' + _contentMonthName(plan.month) + ' plan. Get the id from get_content_plan.'); return v; } function _contentTarget(profile) { return profile.perWeek * 4; } // ── What the assistant is handed ── // The steps travel with the agent's own details already in them, so the assistant plans for // this agent's market and nobody else's. They are returned as data by start_content_plan. function _contentRules(profile) { return [ 'RULES FOR EVERYTHING YOU WRITE', '- Fair housing: lifestyle, cost, commute, amenities and the process only. Never demographics, never "good" or "bad" areas, never who belongs somewhere, never schools used as a stand-in for any of those.', '- A number appears only with the source you found it in ("per [source], as of [month]"). Never invent a statistic, and never state how many times something is searched.', '- No price predictions, no urgency that is not real, no legal, tax or lending advice, no promises about income.', '- Voice: ' + (profile.voice ? profile.voice + '.' : 'warm and plain, the way a person talks.') + ' Plain 8th-grade English. Never use: unlock, supercharge, game-changer, revolutionary, secret weapon, leverage (as a verb).', '- You write and save. You never post, send or schedule anything.' ].join('\n'); } function _contentScriptRules(profile, agent) { const cta = profile.cta ? 'One call to action per video, and it is this one: "' + profile.cta + '".' : 'One call to action per video: a single plain ask to message the agent.'; return [ 'HOW TO SCRIPT A VIDEO (vertical, 30-60 seconds, one person, filmed on a phone)', '- hooks: three opening lines, one a question, one contrarian, one a number or what is at stake. Each 8-12 words. The first word is the hook: no greetings, no "in today\'s video". Set best_hook to the strongest (0, 1 or 2).', '- script: word for word, 90-130 words. The hook, the turn, two or three beats of real substance, then the call to action. Every line can be said in one breath.', '- Local anchor: name a street, a community, a price or a place people there know. If the script could be posted in any city by swapping the name, rewrite it.', '- ' + cta + (agent.booking_link ? ' Their booking link is ' + agent.booking_link + '.' : '') + ' Never stack several asks.', '- Leave bullets and shots out. The agent works from the script; a shot list is not wanted.', '- captions: written separately for each platform. instagram: the hook line, line breaks, 5-8 local hashtags at the end. tiktok: under 150 characters, the point first. youtube: a searchable title, then a two-line description. Never the same caption three times.' ].join('\n'); } function _contentPlanSteps(ctx) { const p = ctx.profile, n = ctx.open; const places = [p.market].concat(p.communities).filter(Boolean).join(', '); const gapMin = n >= 12 ? 3 : (n >= 6 ? 2 : 1); const lines = [ 'You are planning ' + n + ' short-form video' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' (Reels, TikTok, Shorts) for ' + (ctx.agent.name || 'this agent') + ' for ' + _contentMonthName(ctx.month) + '. Their market is ' + p.market + '. Plan for that market only.', '', '1. RESEARCH FIRST, with web search. Do not answer from memory. Find what buyers, sellers and people moving to the area are asking right now about: ' + places + '. Look at search suggestions for "moving to", "living in", "buying a house in", "selling my house in" and "vs"; the "people also ask" questions on those searches; Reddit and local forums; YouTube titles and the questions in their comments; local news and the latest local market report. If web search is not available, tell the agent to turn it on and stop. Do not guess a plan.', '2. GRADE BY EVIDENCE. HIGH: it shows in search suggestions and is asked again and again in forums or comments. MEDIUM: seen in one place, or asked a few times. LOW: asked once, or brand new this month. Keep only questions one person can answer honestly in under a minute; turn a bigger one into a two- or three-part series. Note the questions people keep asking that nobody local answers well.', '3. BUILD ' + n + ' VIDEO' + (n === 1 ? '' : 'S') + ', spread across weeks 1 to 4, highest demand in week 1. At least ' + gapMin + ' from the questions nobody is answering (gap: true). For each: title (written the way a person would type the search, not the way an agent talks), search, demand, evidence (one line: where you saw it), format and angle. Rotate the formats: talking_head, on_location, green_screen, stat, story.' + (p.focus ? ' About one video a week speaks directly to ' + p.focus + '; the rest are for anyone moving to or living in the market.' : '') + ' Do not repeat anything in already_on_plan or titles_used_recently.', '4. SCRIPT WEEK 1 ONLY, following the scripting notes below. The other weeks stay as ideas until the agent asks for them.', '5. SAVE with one call to save_content_plan: the month, a one-sentence headline on what the market is making people ask this month, and all ' + n + ' video' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ', the week 1 ones carrying their hooks, script and captions.', '6. TELL THE AGENT, in a few lines: the headline, which three to film first and why, which can be filmed in one sitting, and that the whole plan is on the Planner tab in NestTrack (' + CONTENT_APP_URL + '). Do not paste the plan into the chat.' ]; if (ctx.missing.length) { lines.push('', 'Their content profile has nothing yet for: ' + ctx.missing.join(', ') + '. Carry on without it, and at the end mention that filling it in on the Planner tab will sharpen the next plan.'); } lines.push('', _contentScriptRules(p, ctx.agent), '', _contentRules(p)); return lines.join('\n'); } // ── The tools ── async function _contentMcp(name, args, token, who) { const agentId = String(who.agentId || ''); if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{1,150}$/.test(agentId)) throw new Error('Your NestTrack account could not be read. Reconnect NestTrack and try again.'); args = args || {}; if (name === 'save_content_profile') { const given = {}; if (args.market !== undefined) given.market = args.market; if (args.communities !== undefined) given.communities = args.communities; if (args.focus !== undefined) given.focus = args.focus; if (args.voice !== undefined) given.voice = args.voice; if (args.call_to_action !== undefined) given.cta = args.call_to_action; if (args.videos_per_week !== undefined) given.perWeek = args.videos_per_week; if (!Object.keys(given).length) throw new Error('Nothing to save. Give at least one of: market, communities, focus, voice, call_to_action, videos_per_week.'); const prev = await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'content_profiles/' + agentId); const p = _contentProfileMerge(prev, given); if (!p.market) throw new Error('market is required: the city or area the agent works in.'); await _contentProfileWrite(token, agentId, p); return { saved: true, profile: { market: p.market, communities: p.communities, focus: p.focus, voice: p.voice, call_to_action: p.cta, videos_per_week: p.perWeek } }; } if (name === 'get_content_plan') { const month = _contentPickMonth(args.month, who.timeZone, true); const profile = _contentProfile(await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'content_profiles/' + agentId)); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(token, agentId, month); return { month: month, month_name: _contentMonthName(month), headline: plan.headline, videos_planned_for: _contentTarget(profile), count: plan.videos.length, videos: plan.videos.map(_contentVideoPublic) }; } if (name === 'update_content_video') { const month = _contentPickMonth(args.month, who.timeZone, true); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(token, agentId, month); const v = _contentFind(plan, args.id); const fields = Object.assign({}, args); delete fields.id; delete fields.month; // What a video is about is set when it is planned; these are not changed from here. delete fields.search; delete fields.demand; delete fields.evidence; delete fields.gap; delete fields.post_id; if (!Object.keys(fields).length) throw new Error('Nothing to change. Give at least one field besides id.'); _contentApply(v, fields); await _contentSavePlan(token, agentId, plan); return { updated: true, month: month, video: _contentVideoPublic(v) }; } if (name === 'save_content_plan') { const month = _contentPickMonth(args.month, who.timeZone, false); const incoming = Array.isArray(args.videos) ? args.videos : []; if (!incoming.length) throw new Error('videos is required: the list of videos to add.'); const profile = _contentProfile(await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'content_profiles/' + agentId)); if (!profile.market) throw new Error('This agent has no market saved yet. Ask them for it, save it with save_content_profile, then call start_content_plan.'); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(token, agentId, month); const target = _contentTarget(profile); const open = Math.min(target, CONTENT_MAX_VIDEOS) - plan.videos.length; if (open <= 0) { throw new Error('The ' + _contentMonthName(month) + ' plan is already full (' + plan.videos.length + ' videos), so nothing was added. To swap in new ideas the agent uses Re-plan on the Planner tab in NestTrack, which clears the ones they have not kept.'); } const have = {}; plan.videos.forEach(function (v) { have[v.title.toLowerCase()] = 1; }); let added = 0, repeats = 0, over = 0; for (const raw of incoming) { const a = Object.assign({}, (raw && typeof raw === 'object') ? raw : {}); const title = _contentLine(a.title, 160); if (!title) continue; if (have[title.toLowerCase()]) { repeats++; continue; } if (added >= open) { over++; continue; } // The assistant never decides these. delete a.keep; delete a.filmed; delete a.edited; delete a.edit_file; delete a.posted; delete a.post_id; const wk = Math.round(Number(a.week)); if (!(wk >= 1 && wk <= 4)) a.week = _contentEmptiestWeek(plan.videos); const v = _contentApply(_contentBlank(), a); plan.videos.push(v); have[title.toLowerCase()] = 1; added++; } if (!added) throw new Error('None of those could be added: each one needs a title that is not already on the plan.'); if (args.headline !== undefined && _contentLine(args.headline, 300)) plan.headline = _contentLine(args.headline, 300); await _contentSavePlan(token, agentId, plan); const out = { saved: true, month: month, added: added, on_plan: plan.videos.length, videos_planned_for: target, note: 'Saved to the Planner tab in NestTrack: ' + CONTENT_APP_URL, videos: plan.videos.map(function (v) { return { id: v.id, n: v.n, week: v.week, title: v.title, status: v.status }; }) }; if (repeats) out.left_out_as_repeats = repeats; if (over) out.left_out_as_over_the_plan = over; return out; } if (name === 'start_video_edit') { const part = String(args.part || 'guide').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, ''); if (!VIDEO_EDIT_PARTS[part]) throw new Error("part must be 'guide', 'engine_1', 'engine_2' or 'scenes'."); if (part !== 'guide') return { __text: VIDEO_EDIT_PARTS[part] }; const profile = _contentProfile(await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'content_profiles/' + agentId)); let u = null; try { u = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + who.uid); } catch (e) {} const agent = { name: _contentLine(who.name, 120), brokerage: _contentLine(u && (u.brokerage || u.company), 120) }; let video = null, month = ''; const wantId = String(args.id == null ? '' : args.id).trim(); if (wantId) { month = _contentPickMonth(args.month, who.timeZone, true); video = _contentFind(await _contentLoadPlan(token, agentId, month), wantId); } return { __text: _videoEditGuide(agent, profile, video, month) }; } if (name === 'start_content_plan') { const month = _contentPickMonth(args.month, who.timeZone, false); const task = String(args.task || 'plan').toLowerCase() === 'scripts' ? 'scripts' : 'plan'; const profile = _contentProfile(await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'content_profiles/' + agentId)); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(token, agentId, month); let u = null; try { u = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + who.uid); } catch (e) {} const agent = { name: _contentLine(who.name, 120), brokerage: _contentLine(u && (u.brokerage || u.company), 120), booking_link: _contentLine(u && u.calendly, 300) }; const shown = { market: profile.market, communities: profile.communities, focus: profile.focus, voice: profile.voice, call_to_action: profile.cta, videos_per_week: profile.perWeek }; const base = { task: task, month: month, month_name: _contentMonthName(month), agent: agent, profile: shown }; // No market, no plan. It is asked for; it is never assumed. if (!profile.market) { return Object.assign(base, { ready: false, steps: [ 'This agent has not set up their content profile yet, so there is no market to plan for. Do not assume one.', 'Ask them, in ONE short message, for:', '1. Their market: the city or area they work in.', '2. The three to five neighborhoods or communities where most of their business is.', '3. Who they focus on most, if anyone (first-time buyers, relocation, luxury...). Optional.', '4. How they talk: straight shooter, warm and patient, high-energy, calm numbers person, or their own words.', '5. What they want a viewer to do at the end of a video (book a call, send a message, get a guide).', 'Tell them short, messy answers are fine. Then save the answers with save_content_profile and call start_content_plan again.' ].join('\n') }); } if (task === 'scripts') { const wk = args.week === undefined || args.week === null || args.week === '' ? 0 : Math.round(Number(args.week)); if (wk && !(wk >= 1 && wk <= 4)) throw new Error('week must be 1, 2, 3 or 4.'); const todo = plan.videos.filter(function (v) { return !v.posted && !v.script && (!wk || v.week === wk); }); if (!plan.videos.length) throw new Error('There is no plan for ' + _contentMonthName(month) + ' yet. Call start_content_plan with task "plan" first.'); if (!todo.length) return Object.assign(base, { ready: false, videos: [], steps: 'Every video' + (wk ? ' in week ' + wk : '') + ' on the ' + _contentMonthName(month) + ' plan already has a script. Tell the agent so; there is nothing to write.' }); return Object.assign(base, { ready: true, videos: todo.map(function (v) { return { id: v.id, n: v.n, week: v.week, title: v.title, search: v.search, demand: v.demand, evidence: v.evidence, format: v.format, angle: v.angle }; }), steps: [ 'Write a script for each of the ' + todo.length + ' video' + (todo.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' listed, for ' + (agent.name || 'this agent') + ' in ' + profile.market + '. Each one answers the search it lists.', 'Use web search for any fact or number you put in a script, and carry its source.', 'Save each one with update_content_video (its id, hooks, best_hook, script, captions).', 'Then tell the agent, in a few lines, that the scripts are on the Planner tab in NestTrack (' + CONTENT_APP_URL + ') and which of them can be filmed in one sitting.
