Do not paste the scripts into the chat.', '', _contentScriptRules(profile, agent), '', _contentRules(profile) ].join('\n') }); } const target = _contentTarget(profile); const open = Math.max(0, Math.min(target, CONTENT_MAX_VIDEOS) - plan.videos.length); const missing = []; if (!profile.communities.length) missing.push('communities'); if (!profile.voice) missing.push('how they talk'); if (!profile.cta) missing.push('call to action'); // What they were given in the last two months, so the same video is not handed back. const used = []; for (const back of [1, 2]) { try { const old = await _contentLoadPlan(token, agentId, _contentMonthAdd(month, -back)); old.videos.forEach(function (v) { if (used.length < 80) used.push(v.title); }); } catch (e) {} } const out = Object.assign(base, { videos_planned_for: target, videos_to_plan: open, already_on_plan: plan.videos.map(function (v) { return { id: v.id, week: v.week, title: v.title, status: v.status }; }), titles_used_recently: used }); if (!open) { out.ready = false; out.steps = 'The ' + _contentMonthName(month) + ' plan is already full: ' + plan.videos.length + ' videos, and this agent plans ' + target + ' a month. Do not plan more. Tell them that to swap in fresh ideas they use Re-plan on the Planner tab in NestTrack (' + CONTENT_APP_URL + '), which keeps the ones they marked Keep and clears the rest; or you can write scripts for the ones already there.'; return out; } out.ready = true; out.steps = _contentPlanSteps({ profile: profile, agent: agent, month: month, open: open, missing: missing }); return out; } throw new Error('Unknown tool: ' + name); } // ── What the app calls ── // A month the app may ask for: this one, the one before (a time zone can be a day behind // the server's), or the next. function _contentAppMonth(v) { const m = _contentMonthOk(v); if (!m) return ''; const d = new Date(Date.now()); const cur = d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'); return (m === cur || m === _contentMonthAdd(cur, -1) || m === _contentMonthAdd(cur, 1)) ? m : ''; } function _contentAppFail(e, cors) { const msg = String((e && e.message) || ''); // A failed read or save is the server's trouble; anything else is something to fix and resend. if (!msg || /could not/i.test(msg)) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Could not reach your planner just now. Try again in a minute.' }, 503, cors); return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: msg }, 400, cors); } function _contentAppPlan(plan, profile) { return { month: plan.month, monthName: _contentMonthName(plan.month), headline: plan.headline, target: _contentTarget(profile), updatedAt: plan.updatedAt || 0, videos: plan.videos.map(_contentVideoPublic) }; } // GET /api/v1/content?month=YYYY-MM async function apiContentGet(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let asked = ''; try { asked = new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('month') || ''; } catch (e) {} const today = new Date(Date.now()); const month = _contentAppMonth(asked || (today.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + String(today.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'))); if (!month) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That month cannot be shown.' }, 400, cors); try { const pdoc = await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'content_profiles/' + w.agentId); const profile = _contentProfile(pdoc); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(w.token, w.agentId, month); return _socialJson({ ok: true, profile: profile, hasProfile: !!profile.market, perWeekChoices: CONTENT_PER_WEEK, formats: CONTENT_FORMATS, plan: _contentAppPlan(plan, profile) }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { return _contentAppFail(e, cors); } } // POST /api/v1/content/profile { market, communities, focus, voice, cta, perWeek } async function apiContentProfileSave(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} try { const given = {}; ['market', 'communities', 'focus', 'voice', 'cta', 'perWeek'].forEach(function (k) { if (body[k] !== undefined) given[k] = body[k]; }); const p = _contentProfileMerge(await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'content_profiles/' + w.agentId), given); if (!p.market) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Add your market: the city or area you work in.' }, 400, cors); await _contentProfileWrite(w.token, w.agentId, p); return _socialJson({ ok: true, profile: p, hasProfile: true }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { return _contentAppFail(e, cors); } } // POST /api/v1/content/video { month, id?, video:{ ...fields } } // Without an id it adds a video the agent wrote themselves. async function apiContentVideoSave(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const month = _contentAppMonth(body.month); if (!month) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That month cannot be changed.' }, 400, cors); const fields = (body.video && typeof body.video === 'object') ? body.video : {}; try { const profile = _contentProfile(await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'content_profiles/' + w.agentId)); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(w.token, w.agentId, month); let v; if (body.id) { v = plan.videos.find(function (x) { return x.id === String(body.id); }); if (!v) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That video is no longer on the plan. Reload and try again.' }, 404, cors); _contentApply(v, fields); } else { if (plan.videos.length >= CONTENT_MAX_VIDEOS) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'A month can hold up to ' + CONTENT_MAX_VIDEOS + ' videos. Remove one first.' }, 400, cors); const a = Object.assign({}, fields); const wk = Math.round(Number(a.week)); if (!(wk >= 1 && wk <= 4)) a.week = _contentEmptiestWeek(plan.videos); v = _contentApply(_contentBlank(), a); if (!v.title) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Give the video a title.' }, 400, cors); v.own = true; plan.videos.push(v); } await _contentSavePlan(w.token, w.agentId, plan); return _socialJson({ ok: true, id: v.id, plan: _contentAppPlan(plan, profile) }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { return _contentAppFail(e, cors); } } // POST /api/v1/content/video/delete { month, id } async function apiContentVideoDelete(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const month = _contentAppMonth(body.month); if (!month) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That month cannot be changed.' }, 400, cors); try { const profile = _contentProfile(await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'content_profiles/' + w.agentId)); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(w.token, w.agentId, month); const before = plan.videos.length; plan.videos = plan.videos.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== String(body.id || ''); }); if (plan.videos.length === before) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That video is no longer on the plan. Reload and try again.' }, 404, cors); await _contentSavePlan(w.token, w.agentId, plan); return _socialJson({ ok: true, plan: _contentAppPlan(plan, profile) }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { return _contentAppFail(e, cors); } } // POST /api/v1/content/clear { month } // Re-plan: clears the ideas the agent has not kept, so fresh ones can take their place. // What they kept, wrote themselves or have already filmed stays. async function apiContentClear(request, env, cors) { const w = await _socialWho(request, env); if (!w) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'Sign in to NestTrack again, then retry.' }, 401, cors); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const month = _contentAppMonth(body.month); if (!month) return _socialJson({ ok: false, error: 'That month cannot be changed.' }, 400, cors); try { const profile = _contentProfile(await _mcpGetStrict(w.token, 'content_profiles/' + w.agentId)); const plan = await _contentLoadPlan(w.token, w.agentId, month); const before = plan.videos.length; plan.videos = plan.videos.filter(_contentStays); const removed = before - plan.videos.length; if (removed) await _contentSavePlan(w.token, w.agentId, plan); return _socialJson({ ok: true, removed: removed, kept: plan.videos.length, plan: _contentAppPlan(plan, profile) }, 200, cors); } catch (e) { return _contentAppFail(e, cors); } } // Once a day: plans more than three months old are cleared out. They are only kept that long // so the assistant can see what was planned recently and not hand the same videos back. async function contentPlanSweep(token) { const d = new Date(Date.now()); if (d.getUTCHours() !== 9 || d.getUTCMinutes() !== 0) return; const cur = d.getUTCFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'); const cutoff = _contentMonthAdd(cur, -CONTENT_KEEP_MONTHS); const rows = _mcpRows(await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'content_plans', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'month' }, op: 'LESS_THAN', value: { stringValue: cutoff } } }, limit: 200 })); for (const r of rows) { const m = _contentMonthOk(digDoc(r.document.fields || {}).month); // Checked again here, so nothing recent is removed whatever the query returned. if (!m || !(m < cutoff)) continue; try { await _mcpDelete(token, 'content_plans/' + _mcpDocId(r)); } catch (e) { console.log('content plan sweep:', e && e.message); } } } function _mcpNeedAuth(request, id) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: id, error: { code: -32001, message: 'Authorization required' } }), { status: 401, headers: Object.assign({}, MCP_CORS, { 'WWW-Authenticate': 'Bearer resource_metadata="' + _mcpOrigin(request) + '/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource"' }) }); } async function handleMcp(request, env, pathToken) { // GET means "open an SSE stream for server-initiated messages". This server has none to // send, and 405 is the spec's way of saying so — answering 200 with plain JSON looks like a // malformed stream and the client abandons the connection. if (request.method !== 'POST') { // Asked without a sign-in, the answer is still "sign in first", so a client checking how // this server is protected gets the same answer whichever way it asks. if (!(await _mcpWhoFromBearer(request, env).catch(function () { return null; }))) return _mcpNeedAuth(request, null); return new Response('Method not allowed', { status: 405, headers: { 'Allow': 'POST', 'Content-Type': 'text/plain', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } }); } let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { return _mcpError(null, -32700, 'Parse error'); } const id = (body && body.id !== undefined) ? body.id : null; const method = body && body.method; // Everything needs a token, the opening handshake included. Answering the handshake // without one made Claude conclude this server needs no sign-in at all. The 401 carries the // metadata URL, which is how a client discovers where to send the user to authorize. const pre = await _mcpWhoFromBearer(request, env).catch(function () { return null; }); if (!pre) return _mcpNeedAuth(request, id); if (method === 'initialize') { const asked = (body.params && body.params.protocolVersion) || '2025-06-18'; return _mcpResult(id, { protocolVersion: asked, capabilities: { tools: { listChanged: false } }, serverInfo: { name: 'nesttrack', title: 'NestTrack', version: '1.3.0' } }, 'nesttrack-' + (pathToken || 'anon').slice(-8)); } // Notifications carry no id and expect no result. if (method === 'notifications/initialized') return new Response('', { status: 202 }); if (method === 'tools/list') return _mcpResult(id, { tools: MCP_TOOLS }); if (method !== 'tools/call') return _mcpError(id, -32601, 'Method not found: ' + method); const who = pre; const token = who.fsToken; const name = body.params && body.params.name; const args = (body.params && body.params.arguments) || {}; try { const out = await _mcpCall(name, args, token, env, who); return _mcpResult(id, _mcpText(out)); } catch (e) { console.log('mcp ' + name + ':', e && e.message); // The message is written for the person reading it: what went wrong and what to do next. return _mcpResult(id, { content: [{ type: 'text', text: String((e && e.message) || 'NestTrack could not do that just now. Try again in a minute.') }], isError: true }); } } const MCP_CLIENT_ROLES = { buyer: 1, seller: 1, contact: 1 }; const MCP_STATUSES = ['pipeline', 'active', 'nurture', 'cold', 'past', 'contact']; const MCP_CLIENT_FIELDS = ['firstName', 'lastName', 'email', 'phone', 'role', 'journeyType', 'crmStatus', 'currentStep', 'currentSellerStep', 'agentNotes', 'notes', 'segment', 'coAgentId', 'followUp', 'followUpCadence', 'followUpCustomDays', 'followUpLastTouch', 'followUpNextDue']; const MCP_SAVE_FAILED = 'NestTrack could not save that just now, so nothing was changed. Try again in a minute.'; function _mcpDocId(row) { try { return String(row.document.name).split('/').pop(); } catch (e) { return ''; } } // Ids go into document paths, so anything that is not a plain id is refused outright. function _mcpCleanId(v, label, from) { const s = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); if (!s) throw new Error(label + ' is required. Get it from ' + from + '.'); if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{1,150}$/.test(s)) throw new Error(label + ' "' + s.slice(0, 40) + '" is not a NestTrack id. Get the id from ' + from + '.'); return s; } function _mcpRows(rows) { if (!Array.isArray(rows)) throw new Error('NestTrack could not be read just now. Try again in a minute.'); if (rows[0] && rows[0].error) throw new Error('NestTrack could not be read just now. Try again in a minute.'); return rows.filter(function (r) { return r && r.document && r.document.name; }); } // A read that tells "not there" apart from "could not read". fsGetDoc returns null for both, // and saving a list back after a failed read would replace it with an empty one. async function _mcpGetStrict(token, docPath) { const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}`; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` } }); if (r.status === 404) return null; if (!r.ok) throw new Error('NestTrack could not be read just now, so nothing was changed. Try again in a minute.'); const j = await r.json(); return digDoc(j.fields || {}); } // Writes only the named fields, and throws when Firestore refuses rather than reporting a // save that did not happen. async function _mcpPatch(token, docPath, plain, typed) { const fields = {}; for (const k in (plain || {})) { if (plain[k] !== undefined) fields[k] = _fsAny(plain[k]); } for (const k2 in (typed || {})) fields[k2] = typed[k2]; const r = await fsPatchFields(token, docPath, fields, Object.keys(fields)); if (!r || !r.ok) throw new Error(MCP_SAVE_FAILED); } async function _mcpDelete(token, docPath) { const r = await fetch(`https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/(default)/documents/${docPath}`, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); if (!r || !r.ok) throw new Error(MCP_SAVE_FAILED); } // Timestamps come back from Firestore's REST API as ISO text. Older code here looked for a // ".seconds" property, which only exists in the browser SDK, so every date read as zero. function _mcpTsMs(v) { if (!v) return 0; if (typeof v === 'number') return v; if (typeof v === 'string') { const t = Date.parse(v); return isNaN(t) ? 0 : t; } if (v.seconds) return v.seconds * 1000; return 0; } function _mcpNewId() { const a = new Uint8Array(15); crypto.getRandomValues(a); const abc = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789'; let s = ''; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) s += abc[a[i] % abc.length]; return s + Date.now().toString(36).slice(-5); } // ── Dates in the agent's own time zone ── // The worker runs in UTC. "Tuesday at 2" means 2 where the agent is, so every date the // connector reads or writes goes through the IANA zone saved on their profile at login. function _mcpTzOk(tz) { if (!tz) return ''; try { new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: String(tz) }); return String(tz); } catch (e) { return ''; } } function _mcpWall(ms, tz) { const p = {}; new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: tz || 'UTC', hourCycle: 'h23', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' }) .formatToParts(new Date(ms)).forEach(function (x) { p[x.type] = x.value; }); if (p.hour === '24') p.hour = '00'; return p; } function _mcpYmd(ms, tz) { const p = _mcpWall(ms, tz); return p.year + '-' + p.month + '-' + p.day; } function _mcpHm(ms, tz) { const p = _mcpWall(ms, tz); return p.hour + ':' + p.minute; } function _mcpCheckYmd(v) { const s = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); const m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/); const ok = m && (function () { const d = new Date(Date.UTC(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3])); return d.getUTCFullYear() === +m[1] && d.getUTCMonth() === +m[2] - 1 && d.getUTCDate() === +m[3]; })(); if (!ok) throw new Error('"' + s.slice(0, 30) + '" is not a date NestTrack can use. Send it as YYYY-MM-DD, for example 2026-03-09.'); return s; } function _mcpCheckHm(v) { const s = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim(); const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/); if (!m || +m[1] > 23 || +m[2] > 59) throw new Error('"' + s.slice(0, 30) + '" is not a time NestTrack can use. Send it as HH:MM on a 24-hour clock, for example 14:30.'); return ('0' + m[1]).slice(-2) + ':' + m[2]; } // A wall-clock date and time in a zone, as the instant it names. function _mcpLocalToMs(ymd, hm, tz) { const a = ymd.split('-').map(Number), b = hm.split(':').map(Number); const wall = Date.UTC(a[0], a[1] - 1, a[2], b[0], b[1], 0); let ms = wall; for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const p = _mcpWall(ms, tz); const shown = Date.UTC(+p.year, +p.month - 1, +p.day, +p.hour, +p.minute, +p.second); if (shown === wall) break; ms += (wall - shown); } return ms; } function _mcpDateStr(ms, tz) { return new Date(ms).toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: tz, weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } function _mcpTimeStr(ms, tz) { return new Date(ms).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { timeZone: tz, hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); } function _mcpNeedTz(who) { const tz = _mcpTzOk(who.timeZone); if (!tz) throw new Error('Your time zone is not saved in NestTrack yet, so the appointment time cannot be set correctly. Open NestTrack once on your phone or computer, then try again.'); return tz; } // ── Clients ── function _mcpStatus(c) { const s = String(c.crmStatus || '').toLowerCase(); if (!s || s === 'auto') return 'pipeline'; if (s === 'closed') return 'past'; return s; } function _mcpName(c) { return ((c.firstName || '') + ' ' + (c.lastName || '')).trim(); } function _mcpPhoneKey(p) { return String(p || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10); } // Everyone assigned to this agent: buyers, sellers and contact-only records. One query on // agentId; the agent's own account and teammates share that field, so role sorts them out. async function _mcpLoadClients(token, agentId) { const rows = _mcpRows(await digRunQuery(token, { select: { fields: MCP_CLIENT_FIELDS.map(function (f) { return { fieldPath: f }; }) }, from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: agentId } } }, limit: 6000 })); const out = []; for (const r of rows) { const c = digDoc(r.document.fields || {}); if (!MCP_CLIENT_ROLES[String(c.role || '')]) continue; c._id = _mcpDocId(r); out.push(c); } return out; } // One client, and only if they belong to this agent. async function _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, rawId) { const id = _mcpCleanId(rawId, 'client_id', 'get_clients'); const c = await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'users/' + id); if (!c || !MCP_CLIENT_ROLES[String(c.role || '')] || (c.agentId !== agentId && c.coAgentId !== agentId)) { throw new Error('No client with id "' + id + '" was found in your NestTrack. Use get_clients to look up the right id.'); } c._id = id; return c; } // ── To-dos ── async function _mcpTodoState(token, who) { const doc = await _mcpGetStrict(token, 'agent_todos/' + who.uid); return { items: (doc && Array.isArray(doc.items)) ? doc.items.filter(function (t) { return t && typeof t === 'object'; }) : [], dismissed: (doc && Array.isArray(doc.dismissedAppt)) ? doc.dismissedAppt : [] }; } async function _mcpTodoSave(token, who, items, dismissed) { const fields = { items: _mcpTodoArray(items), updatedAt: { integerValue: String(Date.now()) } }; const mask = ['items', 'updatedAt']; if (dismissed) { fields.dismissedAppt = fsStrArr(dismissed); mask.push('dismissedAppt'); } const r = await fsPatchFields(token, 'agent_todos/' + who.uid, fields, mask); if (!r || !r.ok) throw new Error(MCP_SAVE_FAILED); } function _mcpTodoRow(t) { return { id: t.id, text: t.text, due: t.date || null, time: t.time || null, client: t.clientName || null, client_id: t.clientUid || null, done: !!t.done }; } function _mcpFindTodo(items, rawId) { const id = String(rawId == null ? '' : rawId).trim(); if (!id) throw new Error('id is required. Get it from get_todos.'); const t = items.find(function (x) { return String(x.id) === id; }); if (!t) throw new Error('No to-do with id "' + id.slice(0, 60) + '" is on the list. Use get_todos to see the current ids; it may already have been removed.'); return t; } // ── Appointments ── async function _mcpLoadAppts(token, agentId) { const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'appointments', allDescendants: false }], limit: 500 }, 'agents/' + agentId); return _mcpRows(rows).map(function (r) { const a = digDoc(r.document.fields || {}); a._id = _mcpDocId(r); return a; }); } function _mcpApptRow(a) { // The app stores the start under "date". "start" is read too for anything saved by an // older build; reading only that one is why this tool used to come back empty. return { id: a._id, when: a.date || a.start || '', day: a.dateStr || '', time: a.timeStr || '', kind: a.type || 'appointment', address: a.address || a.title || '', client: a.clientName || '', client_id: a.clientUid || null, notes: a.notes || '' }; } function _mcpApptTodoText(a) { return (a.type || 'Appointment') + (a.clientName ? ' — ' + a.clientName : '') + (a.address ? ' @ ' + a.address : '') + ' ' + (a.timeStr || ''); } async function _mcpLoadDeals(token, agentId) { const rows = _mcpRows(await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'deals', allDescendants: true }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'memberIds' }, op: 'ARRAY_CONTAINS', value: { stringValue: agentId } } }, limit: 200 })); return rows.filter(function (r) { return r.document.fields; }).map(function (r) { const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); return { id: _mcpDocId(r), address: d.address || '', client: d.clientName || '', client_id: d.clientUid || null, type: d.dealType || 'buyer', status: d.status || '', stage: d.step != null ? d.step : null, closing: d.closingDate || null, price: d.price || d.salePrice || null }; }); } async function _mcpCall(name, args, token, env, who) { const agentId = who.agentId; args = args || {}; // ── To-dos ── if (name === 'get_todos') { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agent_todos/' + who.uid).catch(function(){ return null; }); let items = []; try { items = (doc && doc.items) || []; } catch (e) {} if (!args.include_done) items = items.filter(function(t){ return !t.done; }); return { count: items.length, todos: items.map(_mcpTodoRow) }; } if (name === 'add_todo') { if (!args.text || !String(args.text).trim()) throw new Error('text is required: say what needs doing.'); const date = args.date ? _mcpCheckYmd(args.date) : ''; const time = args.time ? _mcpCheckHm(args.time) : ''; let cl = null; if (args.client_id) cl = await _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, args.client_id); const st = await _mcpTodoState(token, who); const item = { id: Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6), text: String(args.text).trim(), date: date, time: time, done: false, createdAt: Date.now(), clientUid: cl ? cl._id : null, clientName: cl ? (_mcpName(cl) || cl.email || 'Client') : null, // Marked so the agent can see at a glance what came from the assistant rather than // wondering whether they wrote it themselves. source: 'assistant' }; st.items.unshift(item); await _mcpTodoSave(token, who, st.items); return { added: true, todo: _mcpTodoRow(item) }; } if (name === 'update_todo') { const st = await _mcpTodoState(token, who); const t = _mcpFindTodo(st.items, args.id); const changed = []; if (args.text !== undefined) { if (!String(args.text).trim()) throw new Error('text cannot be empty. To remove the to-do, use delete_todo.'); t.text = String(args.text).trim(); changed.push('text'); } if (args.date !== undefined) { t.date = String(args.date).trim() ? _mcpCheckYmd(args.date) : ''; changed.push('date'); } if (args.time !== undefined) { t.time = String(args.time).trim() ? _mcpCheckHm(args.time) : ''; changed.push('time'); } if (args.client_id !== undefined) { if (String(args.client_id || '').trim()) { const cl = await _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, args.client_id); t.clientUid = cl._id; t.clientName = _mcpName(cl) || cl.email || 'Client'; } else { t.clientUid = null; t.clientName = null; } changed.push('client'); } if (args.done !== undefined) { const want = (args.done === true || args.done === 'true'); // A transaction-coordinator task is checked off on the deal, which updates the deal's // checklist and digest too. Ticking only the to-do would leave the deal showing it open. if (t.isTC && want && !t.done) throw new Error('"' + t.text + '" is a transaction task tied to a deal. Mark it done on the deal in NestTrack so the deal checklist updates too.'); if (want !== !!t.done) { t.done = want; if (want) t.doneAt = Date.now(); st.items.sort(function (a, b) { return !!a.done === !!b.done ? 0 : (a.done ? 1 : -1); }); } changed.push('done'); } if (!changed.length) throw new Error('Nothing to change was given. Send at least one of text, date, time, done or client_id.'); await _mcpTodoSave(token, who, st.items); return { updated: true, changed: changed, todo: _mcpTodoRow(t) }; } if (name === 'delete_todo') { const st = await _mcpTodoState(token, who); const t = _mcpFindTodo(st.items, args.id); if (t.isTC && !t.done) throw new Error('"' + t.text + '" is a transaction task tied to a deal, and NestTrack would put it straight back. Mark it done or turn it off on the deal in NestTrack.'); const tid = String(t.id); let dismissed = null; // The app keeps a list of appointment to-dos the agent has dismissed, so re-saving the // appointment does not bring one back. Same rule here. if (tid.indexOf('appt-') === 0 && st.dismissed.indexOf(tid) === -1) { dismissed = st.dismissed.concat([tid]); } const left = st.items.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== tid; }); await _mcpTodoSave(token, who, left, dismissed); return { deleted: true, todo: _mcpTodoRow(t) }; } // ── Deals ── if (name === 'get_deals') { let deals = await _mcpLoadDeals(token, agentId); if (args.status) { const want = String(args.status).toLowerCase(); deals = deals.filter(function(d){ return String(d.status).toLowerCase() === want; }); } else { deals = deals.filter(function(d){ return String(d.status).toLowerCase() !== 'closed'; }); } return { count: deals.length, deals: deals }; } // ── Appointments ── if (name === 'get_appointments') { const days = Math.max(1, Number(args.days) || 1); const now = Date.now(); const until = now + days * 86400000; const all = await _mcpLoadAppts(token, agentId); const appts = all.map(function(a){ const row = _mcpApptRow(a); row._ms = row.when ? Date.parse(row.when) : 0; return row; }).filter(function(a){ return a._ms >= now - 3600000 && a._ms <= until; }) .sort(function(x, y){ return x._ms - y._ms; }) .map(function(a){ delete a._ms; return a; }); return { window_days: days, time_zone: _mcpTzOk(who.timeZone) || null, count: appts.length, appointments: appts }; } if (name === 'create_appointment') { const tz = _mcpNeedTz(who); const ymd = _mcpCheckYmd(args.date); const hm = _mcpCheckHm(args.time); const ms = _mcpLocalToMs(ymd, hm, tz); let cl = null; if (args.client_id) cl = await _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, args.client_id); const appt = { clientUid: cl ? cl._id : '', clientName: cl ? (_mcpName(cl) || cl.email || '') : '', clientEmail: cl ? (cl.email || '') : '', type: String(args.type || '').trim() || 'Meeting', date: new Date(ms).toISOString(), dateStr: _mcpDateStr(ms, tz), timeStr: _mcpTimeStr(ms, tz), address: String(args.address || '').trim(), notes: String(args.notes || '').trim(), agentName: who.name || '', agentEmail: who.email || '', // Always off. The app's own form has an "email the client" box; from here the agent // decides that in NestTrack, never the assistant. emailSent: false, source: 'assistant' }; const id = _mcpNewId(); await _mcpPatch(token, 'agents/' + agentId + '/appointments/' + id, appt, { createdAt: fsTimestamp() }); // The same two side effects the app has: a line on the client's history and a to-do. // Neither is allowed to fail the appointment, which is already saved. if (cl) { try { await _mcpPatch(token, 'users/' + cl._id + '/emailLog/' + _mcpNewId(), { subject: appt.type + ' — ' + appt.dateStr + ' at ' + appt.timeStr, type: 'appointment', agentId: agentId }, { sentAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch (e) { console.log('mcp appt log:', e && e.message); } } let todoAdded = false; try { const st = await _mcpTodoState(token, who); st.items.unshift({ id: 'appt-' + id, text: _mcpApptTodoText(appt), date: ymd, time: appt.timeStr, done: false, createdAt: Date.now(), apptId: id }); await _mcpTodoSave(token, who, st.items); todoAdded = true; } catch (e) { console.log('mcp appt todo:', e && e.message); } appt._id = id; return { created: true, appointment: _mcpApptRow(appt), time_zone: tz, added_to_todos: todoAdded, client_notified: false }; } if (name === 'update_appointment' || name === 'cancel_appointment') { const id = _mcpCleanId(args.id, 'id', 'get_appointments'); const path = 'agents/' + agentId + '/appointments/' + id; const a = await _mcpGetStrict(token, path); if (!a) throw new Error('No appointment with id "' + id + '" was found. Use get_appointments to see the current ones; it may already have been removed.'); a._id = id; if (name === 'cancel_appointment') { await _mcpDelete(token, path); try { const st = await _mcpTodoState(token, who); const left = st.items.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== 'appt-' + id; }); if (left.length !== st.items.length) await _mcpTodoSave(token, who, left); } catch (e) { console.log('mcp appt cancel todo:', e && e.message); } return { cancelled: true, appointment: _mcpApptRow(a), client_notified: false }; } const patch = {}; const changed = []; if (args.date !== undefined || args.time !== undefined) { const tz = _mcpNeedTz(who); const oldMs = Date.parse(a.date || a.start || ''); if (isNaN(oldMs) && (args.date === undefined || args.time === undefined)) { throw new Error('This appointment has no usable start time saved, so send both date and time.'); } const ymd = args.date !== undefined ? _mcpCheckYmd(args.date) : _mcpYmd(oldMs, tz); const hm = args.time !== undefined ? _mcpCheckHm(args.time) : _mcpHm(oldMs, tz); const ms = _mcpLocalToMs(ymd, hm, tz); patch.date = new Date(ms).toISOString(); patch.dateStr = _mcpDateStr(ms, tz); patch.timeStr = _mcpTimeStr(ms, tz); patch._ymd = ymd; changed.push('when'); } if (args.type !== undefined) { if (!String(args.type).trim()) throw new Error('type cannot be empty.'); patch.type = String(args.type).trim(); changed.push('type'); } if (args.address !== undefined) { patch.address = String(args.address || '').trim(); changed.push('address'); } if (args.notes !== undefined) { patch.notes = String(args.notes || '').trim(); changed.push('notes'); } if (args.client_id !== undefined) { if (String(args.client_id || '').trim()) { const cl = await _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, args.client_id); patch.clientUid = cl._id; patch.clientName = _mcpName(cl) || cl.email || ''; patch.clientEmail = cl.email || ''; } else { patch.clientUid = ''; patch.clientName = ''; patch.clientEmail = ''; } changed.push('client'); } if (!changed.length) throw new Error('Nothing to change was given. Send at least one of date, time, type, address, notes or client_id.'); const newYmd = patch._ymd; delete patch._ymd; await _mcpPatch(token, path, patch, { updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); Object.assign(a, patch); // Keep the linked to-do in step, when there is one. try { const st = await _mcpTodoState(token, who); const t = st.items.find(function (x) { return String(x.id) === 'appt-' + id; }); if (t) { t.text = _mcpApptTodoText(a); if (newYmd) t.date = newYmd; t.time = a.timeStr || t.time || ''; await _mcpTodoSave(token, who, st.items); } } catch (e) { console.log('mcp appt update todo:', e && e.message); } return { updated: true, changed: changed, appointment: _mcpApptRow(a), client_notified: false }; } // ── Clients ── if (name === 'get_clients') { const all = await _mcpLoadClients(token, agentId); let out = all.map(function (c) { return { id: c._id, name: _mcpName(c), email: c.email || '', phone: c.phone || '', type: c.journeyType || c.role || '', status: _mcpStatus(c), journey: c.journeyType || '', buyer_step: c.currentStep || 0, seller_step: c.currentSellerStep || 0, notes: c.agentNotes || c.notes || '' }; }); if (args.status) { const w = String(args.status).toLowerCase(); out = out.filter(function(c){ return c.status === w; }); } if (args.search) { const q = String(args.search).toLowerCase().trim(); const qd = q.replace(/\D/g, ''); out = out.filter(function(c){ if ((c.name + ' ' + c.email).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) !== -1) return true; return qd.length >= 4 && String(c.phone).replace(/\D/g, '').indexOf(qd) !== -1; }); } const total = out.length; const cap = Math.min(2000, Math.max(1, Number(args.limit) || 500)); out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.name.toLowerCase() < b.name.toLowerCase() ? -1 : 1; }); const res = { count: Math.min(total, cap), total: total, clients: out.slice(0, cap) }; if (total > cap) res.truncated = 'Showing ' + cap + ' of ' + total + '. Narrow it with search or status.'; return res; } if (name === 'get_client') { const c = await _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, args.client_id); const id = c._id; const out = { id: id, name: _mcpName(c), first_name: c.firstName || '', last_name: c.lastName || '', email: c.email || '', second_email: c.email2 || '', phone: c.phone || '', address: c.mailingAddress || '', type: c.journeyType || c.role || '', status: _mcpStatus(c), segment: c.segment || '', buyer_step: c.currentStep || 0, seller_step: c.currentSellerStep || 0, portal_access: c.portalAccess === true, added: c.createdAt ? String(c.createdAt).slice(0, 10) : null, notes: c.agentNotes || c.notes || '', follow_up: c.followUp === true ? { tracked: true, cadence: c.followUpCadence || 'warm', last_touch: c.followUpLastTouch || null, next_due: c.followUpNextDue || null } : { tracked: false } }; // The rest is useful context but not worth failing the whole lookup over. try { out.deals = (await _mcpLoadDeals(token, agentId)).filter(function (d) { return d.client_id === id; }); } catch (e) { out.deals = []; } try { const now = Date.now(); out.upcoming_appointments = (await _mcpLoadAppts(token, agentId)) .filter(function (a) { return a.clientUid === id && Date.parse(a.date || a.start || '') >= now - 3600000; }) .sort(function (x, y) { return Date.parse(x.date || x.start) - Date.parse(y.date || y.start); }) .map(_mcpApptRow); } catch (e) { out.upcoming_appointments = []; } try { const doc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'agent_todos/' + who.uid); out.open_todos = ((doc && doc.items) || []).filter(function (t) { return t && !t.done && t.clientUid === id; }).map(_mcpTodoRow); } catch (e) { out.open_todos = []; } return out; } if (name === 'add_client') { if (who.role === 'lender') throw new Error('Adding contacts through Claude is only available on agent accounts. Add this contact in NestTrack.'); const first = String(args.first_name || '').trim(); const last = String(args.last_name || '').trim(); if (!first) throw new Error('first_name is required.'); const email = String(args.email || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error('"' + email.slice(0, 60) + '" does not look like an email address. Fix it or leave email out.'); const phone = String(args.phone || '').trim(); const jt = String(args.type || 'contact').toLowerCase().trim(); if (['buyer', 'seller', 'both', 'contact'].indexOf(jt) === -1) throw new Error('type must be buyer, seller, both or contact.'); let status = String(args.status || '').toLowerCase().trim(); if (status && MCP_STATUSES.indexOf(status) === -1) throw new Error('status must be one of: ' + MCP_STATUSES.join(', ') + '.'); if (!status) status = (jt === 'contact') ? 'contact' : 'pipeline'; // Same person twice is worse than not adding them. Email first, then the last ten digits // of the phone, because the two systems an agent copies from never agree on formatting. const pk = _mcpPhoneKey(phone); const all = await _mcpLoadClients(token, agentId); const dupe = all.find(function (c) { if (email && String(c.email || '').toLowerCase() === email) return true; return pk.length === 10 && _mcpPhoneKey(c.phone) === pk; }); if (dupe) { return { added: false, reason: 'Someone with that ' + ((email && String(dupe.email || '').toLowerCase() === email) ? 'email' : 'phone number') + ' is already in NestTrack, so nothing was added.', existing: { id: dupe._id, name: _mcpName(dupe), email: dupe.email || '', phone: dupe.phone || '', status: _mcpStatus(dupe) } }; } const notes = String(args.notes || '').trim(); const addr = String(args.address || '').trim(); const id = 'contact_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7); await _mcpPatch(token, 'users/' + id, { firstName: first, lastName: last, email: email, phone: phone, agentId: agentId, lenderId: 'none', role: jt === 'contact' ? 'contact' : (jt === 'seller' ? 'seller' : 'buyer'), journeyType: jt, crmStatus: status, segment: '', agentNotes: notes || null, notesUpdatedAt: notes ? Date.now() : null, currentStep: 0, currentSellerStep: 0, portalAccess: false, isContact: true, mailingAddress: addr || null, addedVia: 'assistant' }, { createdAt: fsTimestamp(), lastActive: fsTimestamp() }); return { added: true, client: { id: id, name: (first + ' ' + last).trim(), email: email, phone: phone, type: jt, status: status }, client_notified: false }; } if (name === 'update_client') { const c = await _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, args.client_id); const patch = {}; const changed = []; if (args.first_name !== undefined) { if (!String(args.first_name).trim()) throw new Error('first_name cannot be empty.'); patch.firstName = String(args.first_name).trim(); changed.push('first_name'); } if (args.last_name !== undefined) { patch.lastName = String(args.last_name || '').trim(); changed.push('last_name'); } if (args.phone !== undefined) { patch.phone = String(args.phone || '').trim(); changed.push('phone'); } if (args.address !== undefined) { patch.mailingAddress = String(args.address || '').trim() || null; changed.push('address'); } if (args.email !== undefined) { const email = String(args.email || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (email && !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error('"' + email.slice(0, 60) + '" does not look like an email address.'); if (email !== String(c.email || '').toLowerCase()) { // Their login is tied to the email they signed up with. Changing it here would leave // the card showing one address and the login using another. if (c.portalAccess === true) throw new Error((_mcpName(c) || 'This client') + ' has a client portal login under their current email, so it cannot be changed from here. Change it on their card in NestTrack.'); patch.email = email; changed.push('email'); } } if (args.status !== undefined) { const s = String(args.status || '').toLowerCase().trim(); if (MCP_STATUSES.indexOf(s) === -1) throw new Error('status must be one of: ' + MCP_STATUSES.join(', ') + '.'); patch.crmStatus = s; changed.push('status'); } if (!changed.length) throw new Error('Nothing to change was given. Send at least one of first_name, last_name, phone, email, address or status.'); await _mcpPatch(token, 'users/' + c._id, patch); Object.assign(c, patch); return { updated: true, changed: changed, client: { id: c._id, name: _mcpName(c), email: c.email || '', phone: c.phone || '', address: c.mailingAddress || '', status: _mcpStatus(c) }, client_notified: false }; } if (name === 'add_client_note') { const note = String(args.note || '').trim(); if (!note) throw new Error('note is required: say what to write on the card.'); if (note.length > 4000) throw new Error('That note is ' + note.length + ' characters. Keep a single note under 4,000.'); const c = await _mcpOwnClient(token, agentId, args.client_id); const tz = _mcpTzOk(who.timeZone) || 'UTC'; const stamp = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US', { timeZone: tz, month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); const before = String(c.agentNotes || c.notes || ''); const after = (before ? before.replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n\n' : '') + '[' + stamp + '] ' + note; await _mcpPatch(token, 'users/' + c._id, { agentNotes: after, notesUpdatedAt: Date.now() }); return { added: true, client: { id: c._id, name: _mcpName(c) }, notes: after }; } if (name === 'get_follow_ups') { const tz = _mcpTzOk(who.timeZone) || 'UTC'; const ahead = Math.min(365, Math.max(0, Number(args.days_ahead) || 0)); const today = _mcpYmd(Date.now(), tz); const until = _mcpYmd(Date.now() + ahead * 86400000, tz); const todayMs = Date.parse(today + 'T00:00:00Z'); const all = await _mcpLoadClients(token, agentId); const due = all.filter(function (c) { return c.followUp === true && c.followUpNextDue && String(c.followUpNextDue) <= until; }) .map(function (c) { const dueMs = Date.parse(String(c.followUpNextDue) + 'T00:00:00Z'); const lastMs = c.followUpLastTouch ? Date.parse(String(c.followUpLastTouch) + 'T00:00:00Z') : NaN; const cad = c.followUpCadence || 'warm'; return { id: c._id, name: _mcpName(c), phone: c.phone || '', email: c.email || '', status: _mcpStatus(c), cadence: cad === 'custom' ? ('every ' + Math.max(1, parseInt(c.followUpCustomDays, 10) || 7) + ' days') : cad, due: c.followUpNextDue, days_overdue: isNaN(dueMs) ? null : Math.max(0, Math.round((todayMs - dueMs) / 86400000)), last_touch: c.followUpLastTouch || null, days_since_last_touch: isNaN(lastMs) ? null : Math.round((todayMs - lastMs) / 86400000) }; }) .sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.due) < String(b.due) ? -1 : 1; }); return { today: today, days_ahead: ahead, count: due.length, follow_ups: due }; } // ── Content planner ── if (CONTENT_TOOLS[name] === 1) return await _contentMcp(name, args, token, who); // ── Briefing ── if (name === 'save_briefing') { if (!args.one_thing || !args.body) throw new Error('one_thing and body are both required.'); const kind = (String(args.kind || 'morning').toLowerCase() === 'afternoon') ? 'afternoon' : 'morning'; // The agent's day, not the server's. Workers run in UTC, so a brief written in the // evening was already tomorrow and landed on the next morning's dashboard looking fresh. // The zone is the one saved on the agent's profile at login. const day = _mcpYmd(Date.now(), _mcpTzOk(who.timeZone) || 'UTC'); // One document per agent per day per kind, so re-running a briefing replaces it rather // than stacking duplicates on the dashboard. const id = who.uid + '_' + day + '_' + kind; const wr = await firestoreWrite(token, 'agent_briefings/' + id, { uid: _fsStr(who.uid), agentId: _fsStr(agentId), kind: _fsStr(kind), day: _fsStr(day), oneThing: _fsStr(String(args.one_thing).trim()), oneThingWhy: _fsStr(String(args.one_thing_why || '').trim()), body: _fsStr(String(args.body).trim()), createdAt: fsTimestamp() }); if (!wr || !wr.ok) throw new Error(MCP_SAVE_FAILED); return { saved: true, kind: kind, day: day, note: 'Saved to the NestTrack dashboard.' }; } // ── Client activity ── if (name === 'get_signals') { const days = Math.max(1, Number(args.days) || 7); const cutoff = Date.now() - days * 86400000; const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'listings', allDescendants: true }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: agentId } } }, limit: 400 }); const acts = []; for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const l = digDoc(r.document.fields); const ms = _mcpTsMs(l.lastActivityAt); if (!ms || ms < cutoff) continue; // The app records a showing request as a time (showingRequestedAt), not a yes/no. const asked = !!(l.showingRequested || l.showingRequestedAt); if (!l.rating && !asked) continue; acts.push({ client: l.clientName || '', address: l.street || l.address || '', did: asked ? 'asked to see it' : (l.rating === 'love' ? 'loved it' : (l.rating === 'maybe' ? 'maybe' : 'passed')), days_ago: Math.floor((Date.now() - ms) / 86400000) }); } acts.sort(function(a, b){ return a.days_ago - b.days_ago; }); return { window_days: days, count: acts.length, activity: acts }; } throw new Error('"' + String(name).slice(0, 60) + '" is not a NestTrack tool.'); } // \u2500\u2500 Follow Up Boss import \u2500\u2500 // One direction, on demand: an agent's FUB contacts become NestTrack clients. Not a sync - // nothing is written back to FUB, and running it again tops up rather than duplicating. // // Their API needs both a system header identifying NestTrack and the agent's own API key. The // key carries the same privileges as that user's login, so it is stored on their user doc and // only ever read here, never returned to the browser. const FUB_SYSTEM = 'Nesttrack'; // Their limit maxes at 100 and they are emphatic that `next` should be preferred over `offset` // - deep result sets refuse offset entirely. const FUB_PAGE = 100; // 2,500 contacts on a single run. Enough for almost any agent's book, and a ceiling means a // runaway import cannot hang the worker. Running again picks up the rest. const FUB_MAX_PAGES = 25; function _fubAuth(apiKey, env) { return { 'Authorization': 'Basic ' + btoa(String(apiKey) + ':'), 'X-System': FUB_SYSTEM, 'X-System-Key': env.FUB_SYSTEM_KEY || '', 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }; } // Digits only. FUB and NestTrack will not agree on formatting, and a phone-only contact would // otherwise import again on every run. function _fubPhoneKey(p) { return String(p || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10); } // Their person object nests names, emails and phones. Written against a real response rather // than assumed - see the probe route below. function _fubMapPerson(p) { const emails = Array.isArray(p.emails) ? p.emails : []; const phones = Array.isArray(p.phones) ? p.phones : []; const email = String((emails[0] && emails[0].value) || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const phone = String((phones[0] && phones[0].value) || '').trim(); let first = String(p.firstName || '').trim(); let last = String(p.lastName || '').trim(); // Some records only carry a display name. if (!first && p.name) { const parts = String(p.name).trim().split(/\s+/); first = parts[0] || ''; last = parts.slice(1).join(' '); } return { first: first, last: last, email: email, phone: phone, stage: String(p.stage || '').trim(), source: String(p.source || '').trim(), fubId: p.id != null ? String(p.id) : '' }; } // Every agent with a saved Follow Up Boss key, topped up with whatever appeared there since // the last run. Uses createdAfter so the nightly cost is proportional to new contacts, not to // how large their database is - the difference between a few reads and a few thousand. // Clears stored step updates past their thirty days. An update nobody opened in three weeks // will not be opened, and letting them accumulate turns a convenience into an archive of every // email ever sent to every client. async function stepUpdateSweep(token, env) { let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'step_updates', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'expiresAt' }, op: 'LESS_THAN', value: { integerValue: String(Date.now()) } } }, limit: 300 }); } catch (e) { console.log('step sweep scan:', e && e.message); return; } let gone = 0; for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (!r.document || !r.document.name) continue; try { await fetch('https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/' + r.document.name, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); gone++; } catch (e) {} } if (gone) console.log('step updates expired: ' + gone); } // Sends the neighbourhood report to every client with a sold feed, once a month. Runs on the // 1st so the month just ended is complete. // Clears sold listings past 13 months. Thirteen, not twelve: a report comparing with the same // month last year needs that month still present when it runs. async function soldPurgeSweep(token, env) { const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - 5 * 3600000); cutoff.setMonth(cutoff.getMonth() - 13); const cut = cutoff.toISOString().slice(0, 7); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'soldListings', allDescendants: true }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'month' }, op: 'LESS_THAN', value: { stringValue: cut } } }, limit: 300 }); } catch (e) { console.log('sold purge scan:', e && e.message); return; } let gone = 0; for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (!r.document || !r.document.name) continue; try { await fetch('https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/' + r.document.name, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); gone++; } catch (e) {} } if (gone) console.log('sold listings purged:', gone); } // Age out smart-plan Zapier events. This collection is append-only — every zapier/text step // writes one row for Zapier's polling trigger to collect — and nothing ever removed them. Left // alone it grows forever, and because the poll reader caps how many rows it fetches, an // unbounded collection eventually pushes NEW events outside the fetched window. That is exactly // what happened on 2026-10-26: the cap was 200, the collection crossed it mid-campaign, and // every event written afterwards became unreachable — steps fired, texts never sent, no error. // Zapier only ever needs the last few days, so 30 is generous. // opts: // keepDays — delete anything older than this many days (default 30) // cutoffMs — one-off override: delete everything written before this moment instead // cap — max rows scanned/deleted in one pass (subrequest budget); call again if more:true // agentId — scope to ONE agent's events. The cron sweep omits it and ages out the whole // collection; the HTTP endpoint always sets it to the authenticated caller, so a // request can only ever clear its own events, never another agent's. async function purgeZapierEvents(token, opts) { const o = opts || {}; const limit = o.cap || 300; const cut = (typeof o.cutoffMs === 'number' && o.cutoffMs > 0) ? o.cutoffMs : (Date.now() - (typeof o.keepDays === 'number' ? o.keepDays : 30) * 86400000); const q = { from: [{ collectionId: 'zapier_events', allDescendants: false }], limit: limit }; // Equality on a single field uses the automatic single-field index, so neither shape of this // query needs a composite index. Dates are filtered in the worker rather than in the query: a // createdAtMs inequality filter would EXCLUDE any early row written without that field // (Firestore omits documents missing a filtered/ordered field), stranding them here forever. // With no orderBy, Firestore returns documents by name ascending — oldest first, which is // exactly the order a purge wants. if (o.agentId) { q.where = { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: o.agentId } } }; } let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, q); } catch (e) { console.log('zapier events purge scan:', e && e.message); return { deleted: 0, scanned: 0, kept: 0, more: false, error: e && e.message }; } if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return { deleted: 0, scanned: 0, kept: 0, more: false, error: 'query returned non-array' }; let gone = 0, kept = 0, seen = 0; for (const r of rows) { if (!r.document || !r.document.name) continue; seen++; const id = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); const d = (r.document.fields ? digDoc(r.document.fields) : {}) || {}; // Three ways to date a row, most reliable first. The document id always carries the epoch // ms (zev_<ms>_<rand>), so a row missing both timestamp fields is still datable. let ms = (typeof d.createdAtMs === 'number') ? d.createdAtMs : 0; if (!ms && d.createdAt) ms = Date.parse(d.createdAt) || 0; if (!ms) { const m = /^zev_(\d{10,})_/.exec(id); if (m) ms = parseInt(m[1], 10) || 0; } if (!ms) { kept++; continue; } // undatable → leave it rather than guess if (ms >= cut) { kept++; continue; } // still inside the keep window try { await fetch('https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/' + r.document.name, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); gone++; } catch (e) {} } if (gone) console.log('zapier events purged:', gone); return { deleted: gone, scanned: seen, kept: kept, more: seen >= limit }; } // POST /api/v1/sold-report-test - builds the report from whatever has accumulated for one // client and emails it to the agent. Same code path as the monthly send, so what arrives is // exactly what the client would get. async function apiSoldReportTest(request, env, cors) { const fbToken = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(fbToken, _apiToken(request)); if (!who || !who.agentId) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); } let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const uid = String(body.uid || '').trim(); const to = String(body.to || '').trim(); if (!uid || !to) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Missing client or address' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); }) .map(function (x) { return digDoc(x.document.fields); }); } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Could not read their sales: ' + (e && e.message) }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } if (!rows.length) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'No sales collected yet \u2014 nothing to report on.' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); } // Everything collected, not just last month: a test should show what is actually there. const st = _soldStats(rows); const area = (rows[0] && rows[0].cityState) || 'your area'; const html = _soldReportHtml({ stats: st, prev: null, rows: rows, area: area, monthLabel: 'Sample', onNestTracker: false, agent: { name: body.agentName || '', phone: body.agentPhone || '' } }); try { const r2 = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + env.RESEND_API_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ from: _agentFromField({ name: body.agentName || '', firstName: '' }, LISTINGS_DOMAIN), to: [to], subject: '[Sample] ' + st.count + ' home' + (st.count === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' sold near you', html: html }) }); // fetch resolves for a 4xx too, so without this a rejection reported success and the // agent waited for an email Resend had already refused. if (!r2.ok) { const why = await r2.text().catch(function () { return ''; }); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Resend refused it (' + r2.status + '): ' + why.slice(0, 160) }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); } } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Send failed: ' + (e && e.message) }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, count: st.count }), { headers: cors }); } // POST /api/v1/claim-contact \u2014 called during signup, before the old contact record is deleted. // // Firebase Auth assigns a new uid when someone creates an account, so the contact record the // agent built and the account the client just created are two different documents. Everything // keyed to the old uid \u2014 their deals, their listing feed, their journeys \u2014 is stranded the // moment the contact record goes. // // The client cannot fix this themselves: they have no permission to read deals. So the worker // does it with a service key, during signup, before their first screen renders. // // Proof of ownership is the invite code. Only the person holding the invite should be able to // claim that contact, and the code is checked against the invite record rather than trusted. const CLAIM_SUBCOLLECTIONS = [ // Everything the client sees. emailLog is deliberately absent \u2014 that history is read from // the agent's own sent list, so leaving it behind costs nothing. 'listings', 'mortgageJourneys', 'sellerReports', 'tours', 'properties', 'textTasks' ]; async function apiClaimContact(request, env, cors) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const oldUid = String(body.oldUid || '').trim(); const newUid = String(body.newUid || '').trim(); const code = String(body.code || '').trim(); if (!oldUid || !newUid) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, reason: 'missing uid' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); if (oldUid === newUid) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, moved: 0, note: 'same uid' }), { headers: cors }); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); // Verify the caller actually holds an invite for this contact. Without this the route would // move any client's records to any uid on request. let ok = false; if (code) { try { const inv = await fsGetDoc(token, 'invites/' + code); if (inv && (inv.contactUid === oldUid || inv.uid === oldUid)) ok = true; } catch (e) {} } if (!ok) { // Fall back to proving the new account already carries the old uid, which signup stamps. try { const me = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + newUid); if (me && me.previousUid === oldUid) ok = true; } catch (e) {} } if (!ok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, reason: 'not authorised for this contact' }), { status: 403, headers: cors }); let movedDocs = 0, repointed = 0; const detail = {}; // \u2500\u2500 Move the subcollections \u2500\u2500 // Copy the raw typed fields across and delete the original. Copying `fields` verbatim keeps // the move lossless \u2014 no conversion, no dropped types. for (const coll of CLAIM_SUBCOLLECTIONS) { let docs = []; try { const url = 'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/users/' + oldUid + '/' + coll + '?pageSize=300'; let n = 0; for (const d of docs) { const id = String(d.name || '').split('/').pop(); if (!id || !d.fields) continue; try { const w = await firestoreWrite(token, 'users/' + newUid + '/' + coll + '/' + id, d.fields); n++; movedDocs++; } catch (e) {} } if (n) detail[coll] = n; } // \u2500\u2500 Repoint the collections that store the uid as a field \u2500\u2500 for (const spec of [ { from: [{ collectionId: 'deals', allDescendants: true }], label: 'deals' }, { from: [{ collectionId: 'offer_listings', allDescendants: false }], label: 'offer_listings' } ]) { try { const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: spec.from, where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'clientUid' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: oldUid } } }, limit: 200 }); for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (!r.document || !r.document.name) continue; const path = String(r.document.name).split('/documents/')[1]; try { const w = await fsPatchFields(token, path, { clientUid: fsString(newUid) }, ['clientUid']); repointed++; } catch (e) {} } if (repointed) detail[spec.label] = repointed; } catch (e) { console.log('claim repoint ' + spec.label + ':', e && e.message); } } console.log('claim-contact:', oldUid, '\u2192', newUid, '| moved', movedDocs, '| repointed', repointed, JSON.stringify(detail)); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, moved: movedDocs, repointed: repointed, detail: detail }), { headers: cors }); } async function soldReportSweep(token, env) { const RESEND_API_KEY = env.RESEND_API_KEY; if (!RESEND_API_KEY) return; let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'soldFeedToken' }, op: 'NOT_EQUAL', value: { stringValue: '' } } }, limit: 500 }); } catch (e) { console.log('sold report scan:', e && e.message); return; } // Last full month, and the one before it for the comparison line. const now = new Date(Date.now() - 5 * 3600000); const thisM = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1); const prevM = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 2, 1); const key = function (d) { return d.toISOString().slice(0, 7); }; const monthLabel = thisM.toLocaleString('en-US', { month: 'long' }); body.to : []; const text = String(body.body || ''); if (!to.length || !text.trim()) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Nothing to send' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); } // Same profile lookup the other integrations use. let me = null; for (const role of ['agent', 'lender']) { if (me) break; try { const rows = await digRunQuery(fbToken, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'role' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: role } } }, { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } } ] } }, limit: 1 }); if (Array.isArray(rows) && rows[0] && rows[0].document && rows[0].document.fields) { me = digDoc(rows[0].document.fields); } } catch (e) {} } if (!me || !me.quoApiKey || !me.quoFrom) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Quo is not connected.' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); } // No sending-hours block here on purpose. Unattended campaign texts are still held to // 8am-8pm in the agent's time zone, in spProcessContact. const creds = { quoApiKey: me.quoApiKey, quoFrom: me.quoFrom }; let sent = 0, firstErr = ''; for (const num of to) { const r = await quoSendText(creds, num, text); if (r.ok) { sent++; } else if (!firstErr) { firstErr = r.reason || 'send failed'; } // A fatal problem affects every remaining recipient, so stop rather than burning through // the list generating identical failures. if (r.fatal) break; } if (!sent) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: firstErr || 'Nothing sent' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, sent: sent, note: firstErr || '' }), { headers: cors }); } async function apiQuoNumbers(request, env, cors) { const fbToken = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(fbToken, _apiToken(request)); if (!who || !who.agentId) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); } // Same lookup the Follow Up Boss import uses, and the Smart Plan credential loader before it. let me = null; for (const role of ['agent', 'lender']) { if (me) break; try { const rows = await digRunQuery(fbToken, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'role' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: role } } }, { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } } ] } }, limit: 1 }); if (Array.isArray(rows) && rows[0] && rows[0].document && rows[0].document.fields) { me = digDoc(rows[0].document.fields); } } catch (e) { console.log('quo lookup:', e && e.message); } } if (!me) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Could not find your NestTrack profile.' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); if (!me.quoApiKey) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'No Quo API key saved. Null means a full import (first run, or the agent pressing the // button); a value means the nightly top-up, which asks FUB only for what is new. async function _fubImportFor(request, env, cors, sinceIso) { const fbToken = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const who = await _apiResolveToken(fbToken, _apiToken(request)); if (!who || !who.agentId) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Invalid API key' }), { status: 401, headers: cors }); } if (!env.FUB_SYSTEM_KEY) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Follow Up Boss is not configured on this server.' }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } let _lookupErr = ''; // Same lookup the Smart Plan steps use to find an agent's thanks.io and Slybroadcast keys. // Two equality filters, which Firestore serves without a composite index. let me = null, meUid = ''; try { const aResp = await digRunQuery(fbToken, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'role' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: 'agent' } } }, { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } } ] } }, limit: 1 }); _lookupErr = e && e.message; } // A lender is a platform user too, and role would be 'lender' rather than 'agent'. if (!me) { try { const lResp = await digRunQuery(fbToken, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'role' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: 'lender' } } }, { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } } ] } }, limit: 1 }); Save the key again, then retry.' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); } const out = await _fubPull(fbToken, env, who.agentId, meUid, fubKey, sinceIso); if (out.fatal) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: out.fatal }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); return new Response(JSON.stringify(out), { headers: cors }); } // The cron's entry point. Same work, no Request and no CORS. async function _fubSweepOne(fbToken, env, uid, profile, sinceIso) { return await _fubPull(fbToken, env, profile.agentId || '', uid, profile.fubApiKey, sinceIso); } // The import itself. Shared so the button and the nightly sweep can never drift apart. async function _fubPull(fbToken, env, agentId, uid, fubKey, sinceIso) { const who = { agentId: agentId, uid: uid }; // Existing clients, so a run tops up rather than duplicating. Skipped entirely on a nightly // top-up: with createdAfter there are only a few candidates, and checking those individually // costs far less than reading every client the agent has, every night. const seenEmail = {}, seenPhone = {}; if (!sinceIso) try { const rows = await digRunQuery(fbToken, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } }, limit: 2000 }); for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const c = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (c.email) seenEmail[String(c.email).toLowerCase()] = true; const pk = _fubPhoneKey(c.phone); if (pk) seenPhone[pk] = true; } } catch (e) { console.log('fub existing scan:', e && e.message); } // createdAfter is documented as ISO-8601 in UTC. With it, a nightly run reads the few // contacts added that day rather than the whole database. let url = 'https://api.followupboss.com/v1/people?limit=' + FUB_PAGE + (sinceIso ? ('&createdAfter=' + encodeURIComponent(sinceIso)) : ''); let imported = 0, phoneOnly = 0, dupes = 0, unusable = 0, pages = 0, total = null; let sample = null, stopped = ''; while (url && pages < FUB_MAX_PAGES) { pages++; let res; try { res = await fetch(url, { headers: _fubAuth(fubKey, env) }); } catch (e) { stopped = 'Could not reach Follow Up Boss.'; break; } if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) { return { fatal: 'Follow Up Boss rejected that key. Generate a new one in FUB under Admin \u2192 API.' }; } if (res.status === 429) { // One backoff, then stop and report honestly rather than hammering them. await new Promise(function (r) { setTimeout(r, 11000); }); try { res = await fetch(url, { headers: _fubAuth(fubKey, env) }); } catch (e) {} if (!res || res.status === 429) { stopped = 'Follow Up Boss rate limit reached. Run it again in a minute to continue.'; break; } } if (!res.ok) { stopped = 'Follow Up Boss returned an error (' + res.status + ').'; break; } let data = {}; try { data = await res.json(); } catch (e) { stopped = 'Unreadable response.'; break; } const people = Array.isArray(data.people) ? data.people : []; const meta = data._metadata || {}; if (total === null && meta.total != null) total = meta.total; if (!sample && people[0]) sample = people[0]; for (const p of people) { const m = _fubMapPerson(p); // A name and any way to reach them. Plenty of an agent's sphere is a name and a mobile // number, and refusing those drops the people they have known longest. if (!m.first && !m.last) { unusable++; continue; } if (!m.email && !m.phone) { unusable++; continue; } const pk = _fubPhoneKey(m.phone); let isDupe = (m.email && seenEmail[m.email]) || (pk && seenPhone[pk]); if (!isDupe && sinceIso && m.email) { try { const hit = await digRunQuery(fbToken, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op: 'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: who.agentId } } }, { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'email' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: m.email } } } ] } }, limit: 1 }); if (hit && hit[0] && hit[0].document) isDupe = true; } catch (e) {} } if (isDupe) { dupes++; continue; } const id = 'fub_' + (m.fubId || Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)); const noteBits = ['Imported from Follow Up Boss on ' + new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]]; if (m.stage) noteBits.push('FUB stage: ' + m.stage); if (m.source) noteBits.push('Source: ' + m.source); const fields = { firstName: _fsStr(m.first), lastName: _fsStr(m.last), email: _fsStr(m.email), phone: _fsStr(m.phone), agentId: _fsStr(who.agentId), lenderId: _fsStr('none'), role: _fsStr('contact'), journeyType: _fsStr('contact'), // Imported, not worked. Landing them in nurture keeps a 400-contact import out of the // active pipeline the agent looks at every morning. crmStatus: _fsStr('nurture'), agentNotes: _fsStr(noteBits.join(' \u00b7 ')), notesUpdatedAt: _fsInt(Date.now()), currentStep: _fsInt(0), currentSellerStep: _fsInt(0), portalAccess: { booleanValue: false }, isContact: { booleanValue: true }, importedFrom: _fsStr('followupboss'), fubId: _fsStr(m.fubId), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), lastActive: fsTimestamp() }; try { await firestoreWrite(fbToken, 'users/' + id, fields); imported++; if (!m.email) phoneOnly++; if (m.email) seenEmail[m.email] = true; if (pk) seenPhone[pk] = true; } catch (e) { console.log('fub write:', e && e.message); } } url = meta.nextLink || ''; if (!people.length) break; } if (!stopped && pages >= FUB_MAX_PAGES && url) { stopped = 'Stopped at ' + (FUB_MAX_PAGES * FUB_PAGE) + ' contacts. Run it again to continue.'; } return { ok: true, imported: imported, phoneOnly: phoneOnly, duplicates: dupes, skipped: unusable, totalInFub: total, note: stopped || '', // The first run reports the field names the API actually returned, so the mapping can be // checked against reality rather than against what it was assumed to be. sampleFields: sample ? Object.keys(sample) : [] }; } async function apiNtSignals(request, env, cors, who) { const agentId = (who && who.agentId) || ''; if (!agentId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no agent' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants: false }], where: { compositeFilter: { op:'AND', filters: [ { fieldFilter: { field:{fieldPath:'agentId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue: agentId} } }, { fieldFilter: { field:{fieldPath:'active'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{booleanValue: true} } } ]}}, limit: 1000 }); } catch (e) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'query: ' + e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } const hot = [], cold = []; for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const h = digDoc(r.document.fields); const sc = _ntScore(h); const item = { token: r.document.name.split('/').pop(), contactUid: h.contactUid || '', name: ((h.firstName || '') + ' ' + (h.lastName || '')).trim() || 'A homeowner', address: h.street || h.mailingAddress || '', score: sc.score, why: sc.why, equity: Number(h.estEquity) || 0, value: Number(h.homeValue) || 0, lastViewAt: h.lastViewAt || null }; if (sc.cold) cold.push(item); else if (sc.score > 0) hot.push(item); } hot.sort(function (a, b) { return b.score - a.score; }); // Never-opened homeowners are a separate problem \u2014 the report is not landing, which is a // re-engagement job, not a listing lead. Counted, not mixed into the ranking. return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, signals: hot.slice(0, 25), totalActive: hot.length + cold.length, neverOpened: cold.length }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST X-Action: nesttracker-home-dash body { t, on } // Turn a home's dashboard on or off after the fact. Agents enroll before they close and close // before they remember, so this can never be a decision you only get to make once. async function apiHomeDashToggle(request, env, cors){ let b={}; try{ b = await request.json(); }catch(e){} const tok = String(b.t||'').trim(); if (!tok || b.on == null) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing token or state'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const on = !!b.on; const s = await _ntLoadHome(env, tok); if (!s.home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); var patch = { homeDash: fsBool(on) }, mask = ['homeDash']; var seeded = false; if (on && !s.home.tasksJson){ const _ms = await _ntMaintSettings(s.token, s.home.agentId); patch.tasksJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(ntSeedTasks(_ms, s.home.purchaseDate))); mask.push('tasksJson'); seeded = true; } try { await fsPatchFields(s.token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok, patch, mask); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'write failed'}),{status:500,headers:cors}); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, homeDash:on, seeded:seeded }),{status:200,headers:cors}); } // POST X-Action: home-file-save body { t, item } // Saving a filter size onto the furnace writes it straight into the furnace reminder. That link // is the entire reason the home file exists — a reminder that knows the size is a task you can do. async function apiHomeFileSave(request, env, cors){ let b={}; try{ b = await request.json(); }catch(e){} const tok = String(b.t||'').trim(); const item = b.item || {}; if (!tok || !item.name) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing token or name'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const s = await _ntLoadHome(env, tok); if (!s.home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); ['name','brand','model','serial','spec','notes','key','taskKey'].forEach(function(k){ if (item[k] != null) item[k] = String(item[k]).trim().slice(0,80); }); var items = s.items || []; item.id = String(item.id || ('f_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9))); var i = -1; for (var n=0;n<items.length;n++){ if (items[n].id === item.id){ i = n; break; } } if (i >= 0) items[i] = Object.assign(items[i], item); else { item.ts = new Date().toISOString(); items.push(item); } if (items.length > 60) items = items.slice(0,60); var patch = { itemsJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(items)) }, mask = ['itemsJson']; if (item.taskKey && item.spec){ var t = s.tasks.filter(function(x){ return x.key === item.taskKey; })[0]; if (t){ t.spec = item.spec; patch.tasksJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(s.tasks)); mask.push('tasksJson'); } } try { await fsPatchFields(s.token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok, patch, mask); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'write failed'}),{status:500,headers:cors}); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, items:items, tasks:s.tasks }),{status:200,headers:cors}); } // POST X-Action: home-issue-log body { t, label, note? } // Something broke. "Plumbing issue" alone is thin; the note is // what makes it worth reading three years from now. async function apiHomeIssueLog(request, env, cors){ let b={}; try{ b = await request.json(); }catch(e){} const tok = String(b.t||'').trim(), label = String(b.label||'').trim().slice(0,60); const note = String(b.note||'').trim().slice(0,200); if (!tok || !label) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing token or label'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const s = await _ntLoadHome(env, tok); if (!s.home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); var log = s.log; var entry = { ts:new Date().toISOString(), key:'issue', title: label + ' issue', open:true }; if (note) entry.note = note; log.unshift(entry); if (log.length > 400) log = log.slice(0,400); try { await fsPatchFields(s.token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok, { logJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(log)) }, ['logJson']); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'write failed'}),{status:500,headers:cors}); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, log:log }),{status:200,headers:cors}); } // POST X-Action: home-log-note body { t, ts, note?, by?
