Optional + non-blocking: if it fails, // the report still returns value + equity. NOTE: verify endpoint/params against your ATTOM // plan via /api/v1/nesttracker-test?comps=1 before relying on it in the digest. ── async function attomGetComps(env, parts) { const key = env.ATTOM_API_KEY; if (!key) return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM_API_KEY not configured' }; const street = (parts && parts.street || '').trim(); const city = (parts && parts.city || '').trim(); const state = (parts && parts.state || '').trim(); const zip = (parts && parts.zip || '').trim(); if (!street || !zip) return { ok:false, reason:'need street + zip for comps' }; try { const url = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/property/v2/salescomparables/address' + '/' + encodeURIComponent(street) + '/' + encodeURIComponent(city) + '/' + encodeURIComponent(parts.county || '-') + '/' + encodeURIComponent(state) + '/' + encodeURIComponent(zip) + '?searchType=Radius&minComps=3&maxComps=5&miles=1&saleDateRange=12'; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'apikey': key, 'accept': 'application/json' } }); if (!r.ok) { const t = await r.text(); return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM comps ' + r.status + ': ' + String(t).slice(0,120) }; } const d = await r.json(); var comps = _extractComps(d, parts).slice(0,5); return { ok:true, comps: comps, foundCount: comps.length }; } catch (e) { return { ok:false, reason:'ATTOM comps error: ' + (e && e.message || 'network') }; } } // Recursively walk ATTOM's (deeply-nested MISMO) comps response and pull anything that looks like // a comparable sale — resilient to the exact nesting so we don't have to map it level by level. function _extractComps(root, parts){ var out = []; var subjectStreet = String((parts && parts.street) || '').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g,''); function num(v){ if(v==null) return null; var n = parseFloat(String(v).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')); return (isFinite(n)&&n>1000) ? n : null; } function scanFields(node){ // Look for amount/date/address across this node's own keys AND its immediate @-attribute children. var amount=null, date=null, address=null; function consider(k, v){ if (typeof v==='object') return; if (amount==null && /(LastSaleAmount|SalesPriceAmount|SaleAmount|SalesAmount|saleAmt|PropertySalesAmount|_Amount)/i.test(k)) { var n=num(v); if(n) amount=n; } if (date==null && /(LastSaleDate|SalesDate|SaleDate|saleDate|RecordingDate|_Date)/i.test(k) && typeof v==='string' && /\d/.test(v)) date=v; if (address==null && /(StreetAddress|_StreetAddress|address|line1|SitusStreet|PropertyStreet)/i.test(k) && typeof v==='string' && v.length>4 && /\d/.test(v)) address=v; } for (var k in node){ consider(k, node[k]); var c=node[k]; if(c&&typeof c==='object'&&!Array.isArray(c)){ for(var k2 in c){ consider(k2, c[k2]); } } } return { amount:amount, date:date, address:address }; } function walk(node, depth){ if (!node || typeof node!=='object' || depth>14 || out.length>60) return; if (Array.isArray(node)) { for (var i=0;i<node.length;i++) walk(node[i], depth+1); return; } var f = scanFields(node); if (f.amount && (f.address || f.date)) { var norm = String(f.address||'').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g,''); // skip the subject property itself if (!(subjectStreet && norm.indexOf(subjectStreet)===0)) out.push({ address:f.address||null, price:f.amount, date:f.date||null }); } for (var k in node) walk(node[k], depth+1); } walk(root, 0); var seen={}; return out.filter(function(c){ var key=(c.address||'')+'|'+c.price; if(seen[key]) return false; seen[key]=1; return true; }); } // ── Full home report: value (always) + free equity (if purchase data) + comps (optional). ── async function getHomeReport(env, parts, opts) { opts = opts || {}; const val = await getHomeValue(env, parts); if (!val.ok) return val; // no value → no report const report = { ok: true, value: val.value, high: val.high, low: val.low, confidence: val.confidence, lowConfidence: val.lowConfidence, monthlyChangePct: val.monthlyChangePct, matchedAddress: val.matchedAddress, equity: null, comps: [] }; if (opts.loan && Number(opts.loan.loanAmount)) { report.equity = estimateEquity(val.value, opts.purchasePrice, opts.purchaseDate, opts.loan); } else if (opts.purchasePrice) { report.equity = estimateEquity(val.value, opts.purchasePrice, opts.purchaseDate); } if (opts.comps) { try { const c = await attomGetComps(env, parts); if (c.ok) report.comps = c.comps; report.compsError = c.ok ? null : c.reason; if (opts.debugComps && c.ok) { report.compsFoundCount = c.foundCount; report.compsStructure = c.structure; } } catch(e){ report.compsError = String(e&&e.message||e); } } return report; } // GET /api/v1/nesttracker-test?address1=...&address2=... (or ?street=&city=&state=&zip=) // Live AVM check on the configured ATTOM key. Validates Phase 1 end-to-end. async function apiNestTrackerTest(request, env, cors) { let parts = {}; try { const u = new URL(request.url); const a1 = u.searchParams.get('address1'); const a2 = u.searchParams.get('address2'); if (a1 || a2) { parts.street = (a1 || '').trim(); const m = (a2 || '').trim(); const zm = m.match(/(\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?)\s*$/); let rest = m; if (zm) { parts.zip = zm[1]; rest = m.slice(0, zm.index).trim().replace(/,\s*$/, ''); } const sm = rest.match(/[,\s]([A-Za-z]{2})\s*$/); if (sm) { parts.state = sm[1].toUpperCase(); rest = rest.slice(0, sm.index).trim().replace(/,\s*$/, ''); } parts.city = rest.replace(/,\s*$/, '').trim(); } else { parts.street = (u.searchParams.get('street') || '').trim(); parts.city = (u.searchParams.get('city') || '').trim(); parts.state = (u.searchParams.get('state') || '').trim(); parts.zip = (u.searchParams.get('zip') || '').trim(); } } catch (e) {} const _u2 = new URL(request.url); const _wantComps = _u2.searchParams.get('comps') === '1'; const _pp = _u2.searchParams.get('purchasePrice') || ''; const _pd = _u2.searchParams.get('purchaseDate') || ''; const res = await getHomeReport(env, parts, { comps: _wantComps, purchasePrice: _pp, purchaseDate: _pd, debugComps: _u2.searchParams.get('compsraw') === '1' }); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ queried: parts, result: res }, null, 2), { status: res.ok ? 200 : 400, headers: cors }); } // ═══════════════════ NESTTRACKER — enroll / manage (Phase 2) ═══════════════════ // Enroll a home: value it once (ATTOM), mint a per-home token, store the FULL report // snapshot so the public report page + teaser email serve stored data ($0 per view). // Cap: 50 active homes per agent. The quarterly cron refreshes stored values. const NESTTRACKER_CAP = 40; // active homes INCLUDED with the base subscription (always safe) // Paid expansion: each +100 block adds 100 to an agent's effective cap while its subscription is // active. Blocks live on the agent's user doc: ntBlocks (int) + ntBlocksStatus ('active'/'past_due'/…). // Max homes the quarterly sweep processes PER hourly run, PLATFORM-WIDE (not per agent). // Effective daily throughput = this × ~10 hourly runs. At 100 that's ~1,000 due-homes/day, enough // for ~90,000 enrolled homes before the queue can back up. Raise as the team scales — but keep a // ceiling: it's the backstop that turns a hypothetical runaway into "only N homes, caught it early." // ⚠ OPERATOR CHECKLIST ITEM: watch "homes due today" on the marketing dashboard; bump this when it // starts approaching the daily throughput. const NESTTRACKER_SWEEP_CAP = 100; function _ntQuarter(d){ d = d || new Date(); return d.getUTCFullYear() + '-Q' + (Math.floor(d.getUTCMonth()/3) + 1); } // Count an agent's ACTIVE enrolled homes (single-field query → no composite index needed). async function _ntActiveCount(token, agentId){ // Cap counts homes THIS person ENROLLED (enrolledBy), not homes they're branded on. Legacy homes // predate enrolledBy — back then the enroller WAS the agentId, so a home with no enrolledBy counts // for whoever its agentId is. Union both, dedupe by doc id. try { const seen = {}; let n = 0; const tally = function(rows, legacyOnly){ if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return; for (const r of rows){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const id = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); if (seen[id]) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (legacyOnly && (d.enrolledBy || '') !== '') continue; // has enrolledBy → counted (or will be) by the other query if (d.active !== false){ seen[id] = 1; n++; } } }; const byEnroller = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'enrolledBy' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: agentId } } }, limit: 300 }); tally(byEnroller, false); // Legacy: homes with no enrolledBy fall back to agentId ownership. const byAgent = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'agentId' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: agentId } } }, limit: 300 }); tally(byAgent, true); return n; } catch(e){ return 0; } } // How many PAID blocks currently count toward an agent's cap. Mirrors the platform billing gate // (_billTier): active/trialing count fully; past_due keeps counting through a grace window (Stripe // retries cards — don't lock a paying agent on a transient blip); only definitive non-payment // (canceled/unpaid/incomplete_expired, or past_due beyond grace) drops the blocks to 0. const NESTTRACKER_BLOCK_GRACE_DAYS = 14; // matches the platform gate's outer lockout window function _ntEffectiveBlocks(doc){ const qty = Number(doc.ntBlockQty) || Number(doc.ntBlocks) || 0; // ntBlocks = legacy fallback if (qty <= 0) return 0; const st = String(doc.ntBlocksStatus || '').toLowerCase(); if (st === 'active' || st === 'trialing') return qty; if (st === 'past_due'){ const due = doc.ntBlockRenewsAt ? Date.parse(doc.ntBlockRenewsAt) : 0; if (!due) return qty; // no anchor yet → give benefit of the doubt const days = (Date.now() - due) / 86400000; return days < NESTTRACKER_BLOCK_GRACE_DAYS ? qty : 0; // grace, then drop } // canceled / unpaid / incomplete / incomplete_expired / none → not paid return 0; } // An agent's EFFECTIVE cap = base 40 + (paid +100 blocks × 100), with grace handling above. // Owner bypass: a user doc flagged ntUnlimited:true has no ceiling (enroll/confirm never blocked, // homes never locked). Costs still apply per home — "unlimited" means no cap, not free data. async function _ntEffectiveCap(token, agentId){ const base = NESTTRACKER_CAP; try { const a = await _ntResolveAgent(token, agentId); const doc = (a && a._doc) || {}; if (doc.ntUnlimited === true) return 1000000; // effectively no limit for this account return base + _ntEffectiveBlocks(doc) * 100; } catch(e){} return base; } // Notify an agent that homes have paused due to a block-payment lapse (push + email, once per episode). async function _ntNotifyLapse(token, env, agentId, agentDoc, lockedCount, cap){ const url = 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?ntdash=1'; const title = 'NestTracker homes paused'; const line = lockedCount + ' of your NestTracker home' + (lockedCount===1?'':'s') + ' paused — update your +100 payment to reactivate. Your first ' + NESTTRACKER_CAP + ' stay active.'; // Push try { if (env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY) await pushToAgentId(token, env, agentId, title, line, url); } catch(e){} // Email try { if (env.RESEND_API_KEY){ const agentEmail = (agentDoc && agentDoc.email) || AGENT_EMAILS[agentId] || AGENT_EMAILS['tom'] || ''; if (agentEmail){ const html = '<div style="font-family:sans-serif;max-width:480px;margin:0 auto;padding:24px;">' + '<div style="background:#B91C1C;border-radius:10px;padding:20px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:18px;"><div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;">NestTracker homes paused</div></div>' + '<div style="background:#f9fafb;border-radius:8px;padding:16px;margin-bottom:16px;font-size:15px;color:#222;line-height:1.55;">' + '<b>' + lockedCount + '</b> of your NestTracker home' + (lockedCount===1?' has':'s have') + ' been paused because your +100 block payment didn\u2019t go through.' + '<div style="margin-top:10px;color:#444;font-size:14px;">Paused homes stop getting quarterly value updates and client emails until payment is resolved. Nothing is deleted, and they reactivate automatically the moment your payment clears. Your first ' + NESTTRACKER_CAP + ' homes always stay active.</div>' + '</div>' + '<a href="' + url + '" style="display:block;background:#0E3B2E;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;text-align:center;padding:12px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;">Update payment in NestTracker \u2192</a>' + '</div>'; await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails',{method:'POST',headers:{'Authorization':'Bearer '+env.RESEND_API_KEY,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({from:'Nest Track <notifications@mail.nesttrack.io>',to:[agentEmail],subject:'Action needed: '+lockedCount+' NestTracker home'+(lockedCount===1?'':'s')+' paused',html})}); } } } catch(e){} } // Reconcile which of an agent's homes are LOCKED (paused) vs live, based on the effective cap. // Deterministic: keep the OLDEST-enrolled homes (most established past clients) live up to the cap; // lock the rest. Called when block payment status changes (purchase, lapse, restore). Sets a // `locked` bool on each home — the sweep skips locked homes, and reports show a paused state. // // FAIL-OPEN by design (the lesson from the platform billing gate): if we can't read the agent's // payment state, we DO NOT lock anything. A lock only ever happens on a definitive, successfully-read // over-cap condition — never because a lookup hiccuped. async function _ntReconcileAgentLocks(token, agentId, env){ // 1) Resolve the agent's payment state ONCE. If this fails, bail without touching any locks. let effBlocks = 0, agentDoc = null, agentUid = ''; try { const a = await _ntResolveAgent(token, agentId); agentDoc = (a && a._doc) || null; agentUid = (a && a._uid) || ''; if (!agentDoc) return { ok:false, reason:'agent doc unresolved — no lock changes' }; // fail-open effBlocks = _ntEffectiveBlocks(agentDoc); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'resolve failed — no lock changes: '+e.message }; } // fail-open const cap = (agentDoc.ntUnlimited === true) ? 1000000 : (NESTTRACKER_CAP + effBlocks * 100); // 2) List the agent's homes. On query failure, bail without changes. let rows; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'agentId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue: agentId||'' } } }, limit: 700 }); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, error:e.message }; } // fail-open if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return { ok:false }; // fail-open const homes = []; for (const r of rows){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (d.active === false) continue; // removed homes ignored if (d.pending === true) continue; // pending previews ignored homes.push({ id: r.document.name.split('/').pop(), enrolledAt: d.enrolledAt || d.lastPulledAt || '', locked: d.locked === true }); } homes.sort(function(a,b){ return String(a.enrolledAt).localeCompare(String(b.enrolledAt)); }); // oldest first let changed = 0, locked = 0, newlyLocked = 0; for (let i=0; i<homes.length; i++){ const shouldLock = i >= cap; // beyond the cap → lock if (shouldLock) locked++; if (shouldLock !== homes[i].locked){ if (shouldLock && !homes[i].locked) newlyLocked++; // a home that just went dark this run try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+homes[i].id, { locked: fsBool(shouldLock) }, ['locked']); changed++; } catch(e){} } } // ── One-time lapse notification ── // Tell the agent (email + push) the moment homes actually pause — but only once per lapse episode. // A flag on the user doc (ntLapseNotified) guards against re-sending on every dashboard open / sweep, // and clears itself once they're current again so a future lapse notifies afresh. try { if (env && agentUid){ const alreadyNotified = agentDoc.ntLapseNotified === true; if (locked > 0 && newlyLocked > 0 && !alreadyNotified){ await _ntNotifyLapse(token, env, agentId, agentDoc, locked, cap); try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/'+agentUid, { ntLapseNotified: fsBool(true), ntLapseNotifiedAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['ntLapseNotified','ntLapseNotifiedAt']); } catch(e){} } else if (locked === 0 && alreadyNotified){ try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/'+agentUid, { ntLapseNotified: fsBool(false) }, ['ntLapseNotified']); } catch(e){} // recovered → re-arm } } } catch(e){} return { ok:true, cap:cap, effectiveBlocks:effBlocks, total:homes.length, locked:locked, changed:changed }; } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-enroll → value the home once, store the FULL report snapshot // + a per-home token. The public report page and teaser email serve THIS stored snapshot, // so opening/refreshing the report costs $0. The quarterly cron refreshes it. async function apiNestTrackerEnroll(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch(e){ body = {}; } const c = (body && body.contact) ? body.contact : body; const firstName = String(c.firstName || '').trim(); const lastName = String(c.lastName || '').trim(); const email = String(c.email || '').trim(); const contactId = String(c.uid || c.contactId || '').trim(); const lenderId = String(c.lenderId || '').trim(); // assigned lender from the client card → co-brand (blank = agent-only) const address = String(c.mailingAddress || c.address || c.listingAddress || c.propertyAddress || '').trim(); // Prefer split parts the app sends (Google autocomplete); else heuristic-parse the string. let street = String(c.street || '').trim(); let city = String(c.city || '').trim(); let state = String(c.state || '').trim(); let zip = String(c.zip || '').trim(); if (!street && !zip && address) { const US_STATES = {'alabama':'AL','alaska':'AK','arizona':'AZ','arkansas':'AR','california':'CA','colorado':'CO','connecticut':'CT','delaware':'DE','florida':'FL','georgia':'GA','hawaii':'HI','idaho':'ID','illinois':'IL','indiana':'IN','iowa':'IA','kansas':'KS','kentucky':'KY','louisiana':'LA','maine':'ME','maryland':'MD','massachusetts':'MA','michigan':'MI','minnesota':'MN','mississippi':'MS','missouri':'MO','montana':'MT','nebraska':'NE','nevada':'NV','new hampshire':'NH','new jersey':'NJ','new mexico':'NM','new york':'NY','north carolina':'NC','north dakota':'ND','ohio':'OH','oklahoma':'OK','oregon':'OR','pennsylvania':'PA','rhode island':'RI','south carolina':'SC','south dakota':'SD','tennessee':'TN','texas':'TX','utah':'UT','vermont':'VT','virginia':'VA','washington':'WA','west virginia':'WV','wisconsin':'WI','wyoming':'WY'}; const STREET_TYPES = /\b(st|street|ave|avenue|rd|road|dr|drive|ln|lane|blvd|boulevard|ct|court|cir|circle|way|pl|place|ter|terrace|pkwy|parkway|hwy|highway|trl|trail|loop|run|pass|path|sq|square)\.?\b/i; const aclean = (x) => (x||'').replace(/^[.,\s]+|[.,\s]+$/g,'').trim(); let rest = address.replace(/,\s*(USA|United States(?: of America)?|US)\s*$/i, '').trim().replace(/,\s*$/, ''); const zm = rest.match(/(\d{5}(?:-\d{4})?)\s*$/); if (zm) { zip = zm[1]; rest = rest.slice(0, zm.index).trim().replace(/,\s*$/, ''); } let matchedFull = false; for (const nm in US_STATES) { const re = new RegExp('[,\\s]'+nm.replace(/ /g,'\\s+')+'\\s*$','i'); if (re.test(rest)) { state = US_STATES[nm]; rest = rest.replace(re,'').trim().replace(/,\s*$/,''); matchedFull = true; break; } } if (!matchedFull) { const sm = rest.match(/[,\s]([A-Za-z]{2})\s*$/); if (sm) { state = sm[1].toUpperCase(); rest = rest.slice(0, sm.index).trim().replace(/,\s*$/, ''); } } if (rest.indexOf(',') !== -1) { const p = rest.split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean); if (p.length >= 2) { city = aclean(p[p.length-1]); street = aclean(p.slice(0, p.length-1).join(', ')); } else if (p.length === 1) { street = aclean(p[0]); } } else { const m = rest.match(STREET_TYPES); if (m) { let idx = m.index + m[0].length; if (rest[idx]==='.') idx++; street = aclean(rest.slice(0, idx)); city = aclean(rest.slice(idx)); } else { street = aclean(rest); } } } if (!street || !zip) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'Need a full property address (street + ZIP) to value this home.' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); // Re-pull of an already-enrolled home passes ntToken and skips the cap check. const existingToken = String(c.ntToken || '').trim(); // Two DIFFERENT people, two DIFFERENT jobs: // • brandAgentId — whose name is on the report/email. The contact's ASSIGNED agent from the // client card. Defaults to the enroller when the contact has no explicit agent. // • who.agentId — the ENROLLER (from the API key). This is whose CAP the home counts against, // no matter who's assigned. A lender enrolling 40 homes for various agents burns the LENDER's // cap, never the agents'. Cap ownership follows the click, branding follows the assignment. const brandAgentId = String(c.agentId || '').trim() || who.agentId; if (!existingToken) { // Anti-spam gate: NestTracker emails are marketing mail and legally need the agent's physical // mailing address in the footer. Block new enrollment until they've set one in their profile. // A P.O. Box or brokerage office address works — surfaced in the message so it's not a dead end. try { const _ag = await _ntResolveAgent(token, brandAgentId); if (!((_ag && _ag.mailingAddress) || '').trim()) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'Add your business mailing address in Profile before enrolling homes — anti-spam law requires it on the emails NestTracker sends for you. A P.O. Box or your brokerage office address works; you don\'t have to use your home address.', needAddress:true }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } } catch(e){} const activeCount = await _ntActiveCount(token, who.agentId); const effCap = await _ntEffectiveCap(token, who.agentId); if (activeCount >= effCap) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'You\'ve reached your '+effCap+'-home limit. Upgrade for more, or remove some to add another.', capReached:true, cap:effCap }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } } const parts = { street, city, state, zip }; const purchasePrice = String(c.purchasePrice || c.salePrice || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); const purchaseDate = String(c.purchaseDate || c.closingDate || '').trim(); // Loan details (agent/lender entered). Any subset is fine; loanAmount is the trigger for amortized equity. const loanAmount = String(c.loanAmount || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); const ownerRate = String(c.ownerRate || c.interestRate || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); const loanTerm = (String(c.loanTerm||'').trim() === '15') ? '15' : (c.loanTerm ? '30' : ''); const loanStart = String(c.loanStart || c.loanStartDate || '').trim() || purchaseDate; const loanObj = { loanAmount: loanAmount, ownerRate: ownerRate, loanTerm: loanTerm || '30', loanStart: loanStart, purchaseDate: purchaseDate }; // A directly-entered current balance (ownerOwed) wins over amortized estimate for the starting snapshot. const ownerOwedIn = String(c.ownerOwed || '').replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''); let report = await getHomeReport(env, parts, { comps: true, purchasePrice: purchasePrice, purchaseDate: purchaseDate, loan: loanObj }); // If ATTOM has no automated value (common for duplexes / 2-4 unit / new construction / unusual // properties), don't hard-fail — let the agent/lender enter the value manually. We create a pending // home with value 0 and flag needsManualValue; the preview collects the number and confirm's existing // overrideValue path finalizes it (valueSource:'agent', drifts by ZIP trend thereafter). let needsManualValue = false; if (!report.ok){ needsManualValue = true; // any AVM miss → let the agent enter the value; confirm handles the rest } // If the agent gave an explicit current balance, override the computed equity with it at enroll. if (report.ok && ownerOwedIn !== '' && report.value){ const oo = Number(ownerOwedIn); if (!isNaN(oo)){ report.equity = report.equity || {}; report.equity.estLoanBalance = oo; report.equity.equity = Math.max(0, report.value - oo); report.equity.equityPct = report.value ? Math.round(report.equity.equity/report.value*100) : null; report.equity.estimated = false; } } // Normalize a failed report into a zero-value shell so the rest of the enroll write works unchanged. // (needsManualValue is already set above; the agent will supply the value in the preview.) if (!report.ok){ report = { ok:true, value:0, high:0, low:0, confidence:0, lowConfidence:true, monthlyChangePct:null, matchedAddress:'', equity:null, comps:[] }; } const homeToken = existingToken || ('nt_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)); const nowIso = new Date().toISOString(); const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); // History: one entry per quarter (keeps the trend). On re-pull, read + preserve prior entries. let history = []; if (existingToken) { try { const prev = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + existingToken); if (prev && prev.historyJson) history = JSON.parse(prev.historyJson) || []; } catch(e){} } history = history.filter(function(h){ return h && h.q !== q; }); history.push({ q: q, value: report.value, ts: nowIso }); if (history.length > 24) history = history.slice(history.length - 24); const isNew = !existingToken; const fields = { agentId: fsString(brandAgentId), enrolledBy: fsString(who.agentId), // whose CAP this counts against (the enroller) — separate from branding contactUid: fsString(contactId), lenderId: fsString(lenderId), // assigned lender for co-branding; mirrors the client card (blank = agent-only) homeDash: fsBool(!!(c.setupHome != null ? c.setupHome : body.setupHome)), // explicit, never inferred emailsOff: fsBool(false), // (re-)enrolling is (re-)subscribing — clears any prior unsubscribe so pulls resume firstName: fsString(firstName), lastName: fsString(lastName), email: fsString(email), mailingAddress: fsString(address), street: fsString(street), city: fsString(city), state: fsString(state), zip: fsString(zip), purchasePrice: fsString(purchasePrice), purchaseDate: fsString(purchaseDate), // Loan details for quarterly amortization (paydown). Blank → equity tracks value only. loanAmount: fsString(loanAmount), ownerRate: fsString(ownerRate), loanTerm: fsString(loanTerm || (loanAmount ? '30' : '')), loanStart: fsString(loanStart), ownerOwed: fsString(ownerOwedIn), // NEW enrollments start PENDING (active:false) until the agent confirms in the preview. // Pending homes don't count toward the cap and are never sent — confirm flips them live. active: fsBool(!isNew), pending: fsBool(isNew), value: fsInt(report.value || 0), refAvm: fsInt(report.value || 0), high: fsInt(report.high || 0), low: fsInt(report.low || 0), confidence: fsInt(report.confidence || 0), lowConfidence: fsBool(!!report.lowConfidence), monthlyChangePct: fsString(report.monthlyChangePct == null ? '' : String(report.monthlyChangePct)), matchedAddress: fsString(report.matchedAddress || ''), equityJson: fsString(report.equity ? JSON.stringify(report.equity) : ''), compsJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(report.comps || [])), compsPulledAt: fsTimestamp(), historyJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(history)), lastPulledAt: fsTimestamp(), lastPulledQuarter: fsString(q) }; if (!existingToken) fields.enrolledAt = fsTimestamp(); try { await firestoreWrite(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + homeToken, fields); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'Could not save enrollment: ' + e.message }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, preview: isNew, // NEW enroll → preview (needs confirm); legacy re-pull → live needsManualValue: needsManualValue, // true when ATTOM had no AVM — preview must collect a value token: homeToken, value: report.value, high: report.high, low: report.low, confidence: report.confidence, lowConfidence: !!report.lowConfidence, matchedAddress: report.matchedAddress || '', equity: report.equity ? report.equity.equity : null, equityPct: report.equity ? report.equity.equityPct : null, compsCount: (report.comps || []).length, reportUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io/report.html?t=' + homeToken }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-confirm → commit a pending preview live (agent decided). Optional // overrideValue lets the agent correct a bad AVM. NO new ATTOM pull — reuses the preview's data. async function apiNestTrackerConfirm(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch(e){} const tokenId = String(body.token || body.t || '').trim(); if (!tokenId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'missing token' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + tokenId); if (!home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'pending home not found' }), { status:404, headers:cors }); // Either the enroller (enrolledBy) or the assigned agent (agentId) can confirm/manage this home. if (home.agentId && who.agentId && home.agentId !== who.agentId && (home.enrolledBy||'') !== who.agentId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'not your home' }), { status:403, headers:cors }); // Cap check at the moment of going live (pending homes didn't count). const activeCount = await _ntActiveCount(token, who.agentId); const effCap = await _ntEffectiveCap(token, who.agentId); if (home.active !== true && activeCount >= effCap) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'You\'ve reached your '+effCap+'-home limit. Upgrade for more, or remove some to add another.', capReached:true, cap:effCap }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } const upd = { active: fsBool(true), pending: fsBool(false), confirmedAt: fsTimestamp(), needsReview: fsBool(false), reviewReason: fsString('') }; const override = (body.overrideValue!=null) ? Number(String(body.overrideValue).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) : 0; let finalValue = Number(home.value) || 0; if (override && override > 0) { finalValue = Math.round(override); const baseVal = Number(home.value) || finalValue; const hiRatio = (Number(home.high) && baseVal) ? Number(home.high)/baseVal : 1.09; const loRatio = (Number(home.low) && baseVal) ? Number(home.low)/baseVal : 0.91; upd.value = fsInt(finalValue); upd.high = fsInt(Math.round(finalValue*hiRatio)); upd.low = fsInt(Math.round(finalValue*loRatio)); upd.overrideValue = fsInt(finalValue); upd.valueSource = fsString('agent'); // Recompute equity against the corrected value. let equity=null; try { if(home.equityJson) equity=JSON.parse(home.equityJson); } catch(e){} if (equity && equity.estLoanBalance!=null){ equity.equity = Math.max(0, finalValue - Number(equity.estLoanBalance)); equity.equityPct = finalValue ? Math.round(equity.equity/finalValue*100) : null; upd.equityJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(equity)); } else { // Manual-value home (no prior equity): build it now from stored loan data or an entered balance, // so amortized equity works for duplexes/multifamily the same as AVM homes. const _owed = (home.ownerOwed!=null && String(home.ownerOwed)!=='') ? Number(String(home.ownerOwed).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) : null; const _loanAmt = Number(String(home.loanAmount||'').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,''))||0; let bal = null; if (_owed != null) bal = _owed; else if (_loanAmt > 0 && String(home.loanTerm||'') !== ''){ const _term = (String(home.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; let _ratePct; if (home.ownerRate!=null && String(home.ownerRate)!=='') _ratePct = Number(String(home.ownerRate).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')); else { let _yr=null; const _st=home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||''; if(_st){ const _sd=/^\d{4}$/.test(String(_st).trim())?new Date(Number(_st),0,1):new Date(_st); if(_sd&&!isNaN(_sd.getTime())) _yr=_sd.getFullYear(); } _ratePct=(_rateForYear(_yr)||0.06)*100; } bal = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, _ratePct, _term, _monthsSince(home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||'')); } if (bal != null){ const eqObj = { estimated:false, estLoanBalance: bal, equity: Math.max(0, finalValue-bal), equityPct: finalValue?Math.round(Math.max(0,finalValue-bal)/finalValue*100):null }; upd.equityJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(eqObj)); } else { // No balance and no loan amount, but we have what they paid — fall back to the same // 20%-down / 30-yr / rate-by-year estimate the AVM path uses. Without this a manual-value // home showed no equity at all, even with a purchase price on file, and the client had // nothing to correct from in their report. try { const _est = estimateEquity(finalValue, home.purchasePrice || '', home.purchaseDate || '', null); if (_est) upd.equityJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(_est)); } catch(e){} } } // Reflect override in the current quarter's history entry. let history=[]; try { history = home.historyJson? JSON.parse(home.historyJson):[]; } catch(e){} if (history.length){ history[history.length-1].value = finalValue; upd.historyJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(history)); } } else { upd.valueSource = fsString('avm'); } try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, upd, Object.keys(upd)); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'confirm write failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } // Point the client's own user doc at their live home. The in-app home dashboard reads this token // from their profile and fetches the (public, token-authed) report with it. Best-effort: if the // client has no portal account this is a harmless no-op and they just keep getting the email. if (home.contactUid){ try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/'+home.contactUid, { ntHomeToken: fsString(tokenId), ntHomeAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['ntHomeToken','ntHomeAt']); } catch(e){} // Seed the maintenance schedule off the agent's explicit choice at enrollment. Inferring it // from crmStatus meant enrolling before closing the deal silently produced no schedule, and // nothing anywhere said so. if (home.homeDash === true && !home.tasksJson){ try { const _ms = await _ntMaintSettings(token, home.agentId); await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, { tasksJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(ntSeedTasks(_ms, home.purchaseDate))) }, ['tasksJson']); } catch(e){} } // Seed the home file from anything the agent captured at the walkthrough. It was staged on the // contact before this home existed. Apply it ONLY if the address matches — a dead deal's data // must never land on a different house the client later buys. Filter sizes flow into reminders. if (!home.itemsJson){ try { const cDoc = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/'+home.contactUid); let intake = null; try { intake = cDoc && cDoc.homeIntake ? JSON.parse(cDoc.homeIntake) : null; } catch(e){} const norm = function(a){ return String(a||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,''); }; const homeAddr = norm(home.mailingAddress || home.matchedAddress || ''); const intakeAddr = norm(intake && intake.address); // Match if either address contains the other's street-and-number core (handles "139 Lakewood // Dr" vs "139 Lakewood Dr, Avon Lake, OH 44012"). Empty intake address → don't guess, skip. const matches = intake && intake.items && intake.items.length && intakeAddr && (homeAddr.indexOf(intakeAddr) === 0 || intakeAddr.indexOf(homeAddr) === 0 || homeAddr === intakeAddr); if (matches){ // Push captured filter sizes into the seeded schedule. let tasks = []; try { tasks = JSON.parse((await fsGetDoc(token,'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId)).tasksJson || '[]'); } catch(e){} intake.items.forEach(function(it){ if (it.taskKey && it.spec){ var t = tasks.filter(function(x){return x.key===it.taskKey;})[0]; if(t) t.spec = it.spec; } }); const patch = { itemsJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(intake.items)) }, mask = ['itemsJson']; if (tasks.length){ patch.tasksJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(tasks)); mask.push('tasksJson'); } await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, patch, mask); // Consume it, so it can't seed a second home later. await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/'+home.contactUid, { homeIntake: fsString('') }, ['homeIntake']); } } catch(e){} } } // Send the FIRST home-value report immediately on enroll (welcome), then quarterly after. // Skips if the home has no recipient email or the client already opted out. Best-effort — a send // failure never blocks the enroll from succeeding. let firstSent = false, firstSendReason = ''; try { // Merge the just-written fields onto the home so the email reflects the confirmed value/equity. const merged = Object.assign({}, home, { value: (upd.value && upd.value.integerValue!=null) ? Number(upd.value.integerValue) : (finalValue||Number(home.value)||0), high: (upd.high && upd.high.integerValue!=null) ? Number(upd.high.integerValue) : Number(home.high)||0, low: (upd.low && upd.low.integerValue!=null) ? Number(upd.low.integerValue) : Number(home.low)||0, equityJson: (upd.equityJson && upd.equityJson.stringValue!=null) ? upd.equityJson.stringValue : (home.equityJson||''), historyJson: (upd.historyJson && upd.historyJson.stringValue!=null) ? upd.historyJson.stringValue : (home.historyJson||'') }); if (home.emailsOff === true){ firstSendReason = 'client unsubscribed'; } else if (!(merged.email && String(merged.email).trim())){ firstSendReason = 'no recipient email'; } else { const sres = await _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, merged, tokenId, '', { isFirst: true }); firstSent = !!(sres && sres.ok); firstSendReason = (sres && sres.reason) || ''; // Stamp lastPulledAt so the very next quarterly sweep doesn't immediately re-send. if (firstSent){ try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, { lastPulledAt: fsTimestamp(), firstReportSentAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['lastPulledAt','firstReportSentAt']); } catch(e){} } } } catch(e){ firstSendReason = 'send error: '+(e&&e.message||'network'); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, token: tokenId, value: finalValue, firstSent: firstSent, firstSendReason: firstSendReason, reportUrl:'https://app.nesttrack.io/report.html?t='+tokenId }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST /api/v1/deals-stamp-members → write memberIds onto every deal from its client card. // Deal visibility is enforced by rules, and rules cannot look up another document on a query // (10-get limit), so the ids have to live ON the deal. The app stamps new deals; this repairs // every existing one in a single pass. Runs with an admin token, so it reaches every folder. async function apiDealsStampMembers(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const teamId = (who && (who.teamId || who.agentId)) || ''; if (!teamId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no team' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'deals', allDescendants:true }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'teamId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:teamId} } }, limit: 2000 }); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'query failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } const clientCache = {}; let scanned = 0, stamped = 0; for (const r of (rows||[])){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; scanned++; const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); const path = r.document.name.split('/documents/')[1]; const owner = (function(){ try { return path.split('/')[1]; } catch(e){ return ''; } })(); let c = null; if (d.clientUid){ if (clientCache[d.clientUid] === undefined){ try { clientCache[d.clientUid] = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/'+d.clientUid); } catch(e){ clientCache[d.clientUid] = null; } } c = clientCache[d.clientUid]; } const ids = []; [owner, d.agentId, d.coAgentId, d.lenderId, c && c.agentId, c && c.coAgentId, c && c.lenderId].forEach(function(v){ if (v && ids.indexOf(v) === -1) ids.push(v); }); if (!ids.length) continue; const before = (d.memberIds || []).slice().sort().join(','); if (before === ids.slice().sort().join(',')) continue; try { await fsPatchFields(token, path, { memberIds: { arrayValue: { values: ids.map(function(v){ return { stringValue: v }; }) } } }, ['memberIds']); stamped++; } catch(e){} } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, scanned:scanned, stamped:stamped }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // The branded listing email. Cadence is entirely the MLS's \u2014 the agent sets Daily or ASAP // in their saved search, we send when we receive. No batching, no scheduling, nothing to // configure twice. // // Rating links carry a token and need no login. Requiring one gets close to zero ratings, // and the whole point of this feature is learning what the buyer actually thinks. function _mlsListingEmailHtml(agent, client, listings, feedToken) { const RATE = 'https://app.tom-b52.workers.dev/r'; const first = (client.firstName || '').trim(); function card(l) { const rate = function (v) { return RATE + '?l=' + encodeURIComponent(l.mlsNumber || l.recordId) + '&c=' + encodeURIComponent(feedToken) + '&v=' + v; }; const specs = [l.beds ? l.beds + ' bd' : '', l.baths ? l.baths + ' ba' : '', l.sqft ? Number(l.sqft).toLocaleString() + ' sqft' : ''] .filter(Boolean).join(' · '); const drop = /decreas/i.test(l.status || ''); const pill = l.status ? '<div style="display:inline-block;background:' + (drop ? '#FAEEDA' : '#DDF3E7') + ';color:' + (drop ? '#8A5A08' : '#0F7A52') + ';font-size:11px;font-weight:600;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:10px;margin-bottom:8px;">' + l.status + '</div>' : ''; return '<table width="100%" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" style="border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:16px;background:#fff;">' + (l.photoUrl ? '<tr><td><a href="' + APP_WEB_URL + '/#listing=' + encodeURIComponent(l.mlsNumber || l.recordId) + '&c=' + encodeURIComponent(feedToken) + '"><img src="' + l.photoUrl + '" width="100%" alt="" style="display:block;width:100%;max-width:100%;border:0;"></a></td></tr>' : '') + '<tr><td style="padding:16px;">' + pill + '<div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:600;color:#1a1a1a;">' + (l.price || '') + '</div>' + '<div style="font-size:15px;color:#1a1a1a;margin-top:4px;">' + (l.street || '') + '</div>' + '<div style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">' + (l.cityState || '') + '</div>' + (specs ? '<div style="font-size:13px;color:#666;margin-top:6px;">' + specs + '</div>' : '') + '<div style="font-size:12px;color:#888;margin:14px 0 7px;">What do you think?</div>' + '<table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" border="0" width="100%"><tr>' + '<td width="33%" style="padding-right:4px;"><a href="' + rate('pass') + '" style="display:block;text-align:center;padding:10px 4px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#666;background:#f5f5f5;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;">Not for me</a></td>' + '<td width="33%" style="padding:0 2px;"><a href="' + rate('maybe') + '" style="display:block;text-align:center;padding:10px 4px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#666;background:#f5f5f5;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;">Maybe</a></td>' + '<td width="33%" style="padding-left:4px;"><a href="' + rate('love') + '" style="display:block;text-align:center;padding:10px 4px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#085041;background:#DDF3E7;border:1px solid #9FE1CB;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;">❤️ Love it</a></td>' + '</tr></table>' + '<a href="' + APP_WEB_URL + '/#listing=' + encodeURIComponent(l.mlsNumber || l.recordId) + '&c=' + encodeURIComponent(feedToken) + '" style="display:block;text-align:center;margin-top:9px;padding:12px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#fff;background:#1D9E75;border-radius:8px;text-decoration:none;">See all photos on the MLS</a>' + (l.mlsNumber ? '<div style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;text-align:center;margin-top:8px;">MLS #' + l.mlsNumber + '</div>' : '') + '</td></tr></table>'; } const n = listings.length; // A bare <div> has no declared character set, so mail clients guess \u2014 usually Latin-1 \u2014 // and every em dash and curly quote arrives as mojibake. return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"/>' + '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"/></head>' + '<body style="margin:0;padding:0;background:#f5f5f5;">' + '<div style="font-family:-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;max-width:560px;margin:0 auto;padding:20px 16px;">' + '<div style="font-size:15px;color:#1a1a1a;margin-bottom:4px;">Hi' + (first ? ' ' + first : '') + ',</div>' + '<div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:600;color:#1a1a1a;margin-bottom:16px;">' + (n === 1 ? 'A new home for you' : n + ' new homes for you') + '</div>' + listings.map(card).join('') + '<div style="border-top:1px solid #e5e7eb;margin-top:8px;padding-top:16px;font-size:13px;color:#666;line-height:1.6;">' + 'Listing data provided by the MLS.<br><br>' + 'Tap <strong>Love it</strong> or <strong>Not for me</strong> on any of these and I will see it right away \u2014 ' + 'it helps me send you better matches.<br><br>' + (agent.name || 'Your agent') + (agent.phone ? '<br>' + agent.phone : '') + '</div>' + '<div style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa;text-align:center;margin-top:20px;">' + 'Sent through NestTrack. Reply to this email to reach ' + (agent.firstName || 'your agent') + ' directly.' + '</div>' + '</div></body></html>'; } async function _mlsSendListingEmail(env, token, client, listings) { if (!env.RESEND_API_KEY) return { ok: false, reason: 'no RESEND_API_KEY' }; const to = (client.email || '').trim(); if (!to) return { ok: false, reason: 'client has no email' }; if (client.mlsEmailsOff === true) return { ok: false, reason: 'client opted out' }; if (!listings.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'nothing to send' }; const agent = await _ntResolveAgent(token, client.agentId || ''); const n = listings.length; const subject = n === 1 ? ('New listing: ' + (listings[0].street || 'a home for you')) : (n + ' new listings for you'); const payload = { from: _agentFromField(agent, LISTINGS_DOMAIN), to: [to], subject: subject, html: _mlsListingEmailHtml(agent, client, listings, client.mlsFeedToken || '') }; // Replies go to the agent's real inbox, not a no-reply nobody reads. if (agent && agent.email) payload.reply_to = agent.email; try { const r = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + env.RESEND_API_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }); if (!r.ok) return { ok: false, reason: 'resend ' + r.status }; try { await _logSentEmail(token, client.agentId || '', to, subject, 'mls-listings', _ntHomeFirst(client) || '', client.uid); } catch (e) {} return { ok: true }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: 'send error: ' + (e && e.message) }; } } // Push when genuinely new listings land. An update to one they already have is not news, // so this only fires on res.added \u2014 a price change already shows on the card. async function _mlsPushListings(env, token, client, listings, added) { if (!added) return 'no new listings'; if (client.mlsEmailsOff === true) return 'client opted out'; const first = listings[0] || {}; const heading = added === 1 ? 'New listing for you' : (added + ' new listings for you'); const body = added === 1 ? [first.price, first.street, (first.cityState || '').split(',')[0]].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ') : 'Tap to see them'; // Deep-links straight to the stack. Universal links ship in 1.6, so on a phone with the app // this opens the app rather than the browser; without it, the token view renders on the web. const url = 'https://app.nesttrack.io/#listing=' + encodeURIComponent(first.mlsNumber || first.recordId || '') + '&c=' + encodeURIComponent(client.mlsFeedToken || ''); const ok = await _pushClient(env, client.uid, heading, body, { token: token, url: url }); return ok ? 'pushed' : 'push failed'; } // Age listings out so the stack stays useful. Without this it becomes a junk drawer within // a month \u2014 an active buyer on a daily search collects a couple of hundred cards. // // Rules differ by what the client said, because the rating is the whole signal: // // unrated 10 days \u2014 they never engaged; a fortnight-old listing they ignored is noise // passed 30 days \u2014 hidden from them immediately, but kept so the agent's "passing on // Brecksville" read still has something to work from // maybe 60 days \u2014 a real maybe deserves longer than a shrug // loved kept \u2014 that is their shortlist; only they remove it // // Sold and closed cards follow the unrated rule: informational, and stale quickly. var MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS = { unrated: 10, pass: 30, maybe: 60, love: 0 }; // 0 = never function _mlsExpiryFor(l) { const r = String(l.rating || '').toLowerCase(); if (r === 'love') return 0; if (r === 'maybe') return MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS.maybe; if (r === 'pass') return MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS.pass; return MLS_EXPIRY_DAYS.unrated; } // A listing's clock runs from the last thing that happened to it, not from when it arrived \u2014 // a card they opened last week should not vanish because the email was three weeks ago. function _mlsLastTouch(l) { const stamps = [l.ratedAt, l.lastViewedAt, l.showingRequestedAt, l.lastSeenAt, l.firstSeenAt]; let newest = 0; for (const t of stamps) { if (!t) continue; const ms = (t.seconds ? t.seconds * 1000 : Date.parse(t)) || 0; if (ms > newest) newest = ms; } return newest; } // dryRun reports what would go without touching anything. The cron runs it for real; the // admin route defaults to dry so it can be inspected first. async function _mlsExpireListings(env, token, dryRun) { let scanned = 0, expired = 0, kept = 0; const sample = []; let clients = []; try { clients = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'mlsFeedActive' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { booleanValue: true } } }, limit: 2000 }); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: 'client query: ' + e.message }; } const now = Date.now(); for (const c of (clients || [])) { if (!c.document || !c.document.fields) continue; const uid = c.document.name.split('/').pop(); let docs = []; try { const url = 'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/users/' + uid + '/listings?pageSize=300'; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); const j = await r.json(); if (!r.ok) continue; docs = j.documents || []; } catch (e) { continue; } for (const doc of docs) { if (!doc.fields) continue; scanned++; const l = digDoc(doc.fields); const days = _mlsExpiryFor(l); if (!days) { kept++; continue; } // loved const touched = _mlsLastTouch(l); if (!touched) { kept++; continue; } // no timestamp \u2014 leave it alone const ageDays = Math.floor((now - touched) / 86400000); if (ageDays < days) { kept++; continue; } expired++; if (sample.length < 12) { sample.push({ uid: uid, mls: doc.name.split('/').pop(), rating: l.rating || 'unrated', ageDays: ageDays, address: l.address || l.street || '' }); } if (!dryRun) { try { await fetch('https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/' + doc.name, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); } catch (e) {} } } } return { ok: true, dryRun: !!dryRun, scanned, expired, kept, sample }; } // GET /api/v1/mls-view?c=<feedToken> — a client's listings, no login. // POST /api/v1/mls-view — { c, mls, rating } to record a rating. // // Runs through the worker rather than letting the app read Firestore directly, because a // signed-out lookup by token would need a list rule on /users that rules cannot constrain // to the token — it would have to pass for every user document. This keeps the rules shut. async function apiMlsView(request, env, cors) { const u = new URL(request.url); const tok = String(u.searchParams.get('c') || '').toLowerCase(); const json = function (o, st) { return new Response(JSON.stringify(o), { status: st || 200, headers: cors }); }; if (!tok) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'no token' }, 400); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const client = await _mlsResolveClient(token, 'mls-' + tok + '@x'); if (!client) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'not found' }, 404); if (request.method === 'POST') { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const mls = String(body.mls || ''); const rating = String(body.rating || '').toLowerCase(); if (!mls) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'no listing' }, 400); if (['love', 'maybe', 'pass', ''].indexOf(rating) === -1) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'bad rating' }, 400); try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + mls, { rating: fsString(rating), ratedAt: fsTimestamp(), ratedFrom: fsString('link'), lastActivityAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['rating', 'ratedAt', 'ratedFrom', 'lastActivityAt']); return json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { return json({ ok: false, reason: e.message }, 500); } } // Only what the listings view needs — no email, phone, notes or anything else off the // client record. The token is a link in an inbox, so it gets the minimum. // Straight list of the subcollection. A collectionGroup query on 'listings' filtered by // agentId needed a collection-group index that does not exist, so it came back empty and // the client saw "All caught up" with listings sitting right there in Firestore. let listings = []; try { const url = 'https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/' + FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID + '/databases/(default)/documents/users/' + client.uid + '/listings?pageSize=300'; const r = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } }); const j = await r.json(); if (!r.ok) return json({ ok: false, reason: 'list failed: ' + (j && j.error && j.error.message || r.status) }, 500); for (const doc of (j.documents || [])) { if (!doc.fields) continue; const d = digDoc(doc.fields); d.id = doc.name.split('/').pop(); listings.push(d); } } catch (e) { return json({ ok: false, reason: 'list error: ' + e.message }, 500); } return json({ ok: true, client: { uid: client.uid, firstName: client.firstName || '' }, listings: listings }); } // GET /r?l=<mls>&c=<feedToken>&v=love|maybe|pass \u2014 one-click rating from the email. // No login on purpose: requiring one gets close to zero ratings, and knowing what the // buyer thinks is the entire point of this feature. async function apiMlsRate(request, env, cors) { const u = new URL(request.url); const mls = u.searchParams.get('l') || ''; const tok = (u.searchParams.get('c') || '').toLowerCase(); const val = (u.searchParams.get('v') || '').toLowerCase(); const okVal = ['love', 'maybe', 'pass'].indexOf(val) !== -1; const page = function (title, body) { return new Response( '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"/>' + '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"/><title>' + title + '' + '' + '
' + body + '
' + '', { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8' } }); }; if (!mls || !tok || !okVal) { return page('Link problem', '
That link looks incomplete.
' + '
Open the app and rate it there instead.
'); } try { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const client = await _mlsResolveClient(token, 'mls-' + tok + '@x'); if (!client) return page('Not found', '
We could not match that link.
'); await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + mls, { rating: fsString(val), ratedAt: fsTimestamp(), ratedFrom: fsString('email'), lastActivityAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['rating', 'ratedAt', 'ratedFrom', 'lastActivityAt']); let l = null; try { l = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + mls); } catch (e) {} const said = val === 'love' ? 'Love it' : (val === 'maybe' ? 'Maybe' : 'Not for me'); const tint = val === 'love' ? '#0F7A52' : '#666'; return page('Thanks', '

Your agent will see this straight away.

We could not save that just now.
' + '
Try again from the app.
'); } } // POST /api/v1/mls-paste — run the parser against a raw email pasted by the agent. // Gmail rewrites HTML when you forward (strips comments and class attributes, which is // exactly what the parser reads), so forwarding is a useless test. "Show original" gives // the untouched message. Doubles as a recovery path if a real alert ever fails to parse. async function apiMlsPaste(request, env, cors, who) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const raw = String(body.raw || ''); const clientUid = String(body.clientUid || ''); if (!raw) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no raw email' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const html = _mimeHtmlPart(raw); const parsed = parseMlsAlert(html); // Dry run by default — report what was found without writing anything. if (!clientUid) { return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, dryRun: true, isWelcome: parsed.isWelcome, contactId: parsed.contactId, setId: parsed.setId, found: parsed.listings.length, bodyChars: html.length, listings: parsed.listings.map(function (l) { return { mls: l.mlsNumber, price: l.price, address: l.address, beds: l.beds, baths: l.baths, sqft: l.sqft, status: l.status, hasPhoto: !!l.photoUrl, hasLink: !!l.listingUrl }; }) }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const client = await fsGetDoc(token, 'users/' + clientUid); if (!client) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'client not found' }), { status:404, headers:cors }); client.uid = clientUid; const res = await _mlsStoreListings(token, client, parsed); try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + clientUid, { mlsFeedActive: fsBool(true), mlsFeedLastAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['mlsFeedActive', 'mlsFeedLastAt']); } catch (e) {} return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, added:res.added, updated:res.updated, found:parsed.listings.length }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-reanchor → apply the agent's CURRENT maintenance schedule to homes // they have ALREADY enrolled. Without this, changing the anchor month only ever affected future // enrollments, so an agent who set October watched existing clients keep firing in April and had // no way to correct it. Completed history and per-task overrides are untouched; only nextDue moves. async function apiNestTrackerReanchor(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const agentId = (who && who.agentId) || ''; if (!agentId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'no agent' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const settings = await _ntMaintSettings(token, agentId); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'agentId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue:agentId} } }, limit: 500 }); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'query failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } const base = Date.now(); let homes = 0, moved = 0, turnedOff = 0; for (const r of (rows||[])){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const h = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (h.active === false || h.pending === true || !h.tasksJson) continue; const tok = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); let tasks = []; try { tasks = JSON.parse(h.tasksJson); } catch(e){ continue; } let changed = false; tasks = tasks.filter(function(t){ // Only the agent-configurable big-ticket tasks follow the schedule. DIY consumables // (filter, batteries) and the anniversary tasks are timed by other rules entirely. if (!MAINT_EMAIL_KEYS[t.key] || t.anniversary) return true; const s = settings[t.key]; if (!s) return true; if (s.off === true){ turnedOff++; changed = true; return false; } // agent switched it off const every = (Number(s.freqDays) > 0) ? Number(s.freqDays) : (Number(t.everyDays)||182); const month = (s.month != null && s.month >= 0 && s.month <= 11) ? Number(s.month) : null; const next = (month != null) ? _nextAnchoredMonth(base, month, every) : _nextSeasonal(base); if (next !== t.nextDue){ t.nextDue = next; t.everyDays = every; moved++; changed = true; } return true; }); if (changed){ try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tok, { tasksJson: fsString(JSON.stringify(tasks)) }, ['tasksJson']); homes++; } catch(e){} } } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:true, homes:homes, moved:moved, turnedOff:turnedOff }), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-remove → deactivate a home (frees a cap slot; doc kept for history). async function apiNestTrackerRemove(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); } // GET /api/v1/nesttracker-dashboard → agent-scoped monitoring for the marketing tile. // Returns real, tracked metrics only (no fabricated numbers): counts, cap usage, the due/processed // queue readout, engagement (check-ins), and a per-home list. Reads only — no ATTOM calls. async function apiNestTrackerDashboard(request, env, cors, who){ const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let rows = []; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'agentId'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{stringValue: who.agentId||'' } } }, limit: 500 }); } catch(e){ return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'query failed: '+e.message }), { status:500, headers:cors }); } const now = Date.now(); const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); const dueMs = 89*24*60*60*1000; let active=0, pending=0, dueToday=0, processedToday=0, checkins=0, overridden=0, warm=0, lockedCount=0; const homes = []; if (Array.isArray(rows)) for (const r of rows){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; const h = digDoc(r.document.fields); const tokenId = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); const isActive = h.active === true; const isPending = h.active === false && h.pending === true; if (h.active === false && !isPending) continue; // skip removed if (isActive) active++; if (isPending) pending++; // Pending homes are normally mid-enroll (previewed, not confirmed) and hidden. BUT a home // flagged needsReview (auto-enrolled with no/low value) MUST be listed so the agent can act // on it — otherwise it's invisible with no way to review. Show those; hide plain pending. if (isPending && h.needsReview !== true) continue; const isLocked = h.locked === true; if (isLocked) lockedCount++; const lastMs = h.lastPulledAt ? Date.parse(h.lastPulledAt) : 0; if (isActive && !isLocked && (!lastMs || (now-lastMs) >= dueMs)) dueToday++; if (h.lastPulledAt && String(h.lastPulledAt).slice(0,10) === todayStr) processedToday++; if (h.lastCheckinAt) checkins++; if (h.valueSource === 'agent' || (h.overrideValue!=null && String(h.overrideValue)!=='')) overridden++; homes.push({ token: tokenId, name: ((h.firstName||'')+' '+(h.lastName||'')).trim() || (h.email||'Client'), email: h.email||'', uid: h.contactUid||'', address: h.mailingAddress || h.matchedAddress || '', value: Number(h.value)||0, status: isPending ? 'pending' : (isLocked ? 'locked' : 'active'), locked: isLocked, overridden: (h.valueSource==='agent' || (h.overrideValue!=null && String(h.overrideValue)!=='')), homeDash: (h.homeDash !== false), needsReview: (h.needsReview === true), reviewReason: h.reviewReason || '', lowConfidence: (h.lowConfidence === true), confidence: Number(h.confidence)||0, low: Number(h.low)||0, high: Number(h.high)||0, lastPulledAt: h.lastPulledAt||'', lastCheckinAt: h.lastCheckinAt||'', lastIntent: h.lastIntent||'', logCount: (function(){ try { return (JSON.parse(h.logJson||'[]')||[]).length; } catch(e){ return 0; } })(), valuePaused: (h.emailsOff === true), lastLogAt: (function(){ try { var L=JSON.parse(h.logJson||'[]')||[]; return (L[0]&&L[0].ts)||''; } catch(e){ return ''; } })(), lastViewedAt: h.lastViewedAt||'', viewCount: Number(h.viewCount)||0, lastSentAt: h.lastPulledAt || '', nextDueAt: (function(){ try { var lp = h.lastPulledAt; var ms = lp && lp.seconds ? lp.seconds*1000 : (lp ? Returns nulls // gracefully if the key is missing so the report never breaks. async function getLiveRates(env, token){ const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10); try { const cached = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_meta/rates'); if (cached && cached.day === todayStr && cached.rate30) { return { rate30: Number(cached.rate30)||null, rate15: Number(cached.rate15)||null, asOf: cached.asOf||todayStr }; } } catch(e){} const key = (env.FRED_API_KEY||'').trim(); if (!key) return { rate30:null, rate15:null, asOf:null }; async function series(id){ try { const u = 'https://api.stlouisfed.org/fred/series/observations?series_id='+id+'&api_key='+key+'&file_type=json&sort_order=desc&limit=1'; const r = await fetch(u); if(!r.ok) return null; const j = await r.json(); const o = j && j.observations && j.observations[0]; if (o && o.value && o.value!=='.') return { v:Number(o.value), d:o.date }; } catch(e){} return null; } const s30 = await series('MORTGAGE30US'); const s15 = await series('MORTGAGE15US'); const out = { rate30: s30? s30.v : null, rate15: s15? s15.v : null, asOf: (s30&&s30.d)||(s15&&s15.d)||todayStr }; try { await firestoreWrite(token, 'nesttracker_meta/rates', { day: fsString(todayStr), rate30: fsString(out.rate30!=null?String(out.rate30):''), rate15: fsString(out.rate15!=null?String(out.rate15):''), asOf: fsString(out.asOf||''), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} return out; } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-checkin?t=TOKEN → PUBLIC. Handles Gmail/Yahoo one-click POST too. // GET /api/v1/ics → PUBLIC. Serves a single-event calendar invite over https with // text/calendar headers — the only delivery that triggers iOS's native "Add to Calendar" // sheet (a blob download from the app can't). The app's Add-to-Calendar chooser builds // these URLs. No Firestore, no auth: the event data arrives in the query string and the // response contains nothing but what the caller sent. function apiCalendarIcs(request){ const u = new URL(request.url); const p = k => u.searchParams.get(k) || ''; const escI = s => String(s).replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/;/g,'\\;').replace(/,/g,'\\,').replace(/\n/g,'\\n'); const allDay = p('allday') === '1'; const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g,'').split('.')[0] + 'Z'; const lines = [ 'BEGIN:VCALENDAR','VERSION:2.0','PRODID:-//NestTrack//EN', 'BEGIN:VEVENT', 'UID:nt-' + Date.now() + '@nesttrack', 'DTSTAMP:' + stamp, allDay ? 'DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:' + p('start') : 'DTSTART:' + p('start'), allDay ? 'DTEND;VALUE=DATE:' + p('end') : 'DTEND:' + p('end'), 'SUMMARY:' + escI(p('title')), p('loc') ? 'LOCATION:' + escI(p('loc')) : '', 'END:VEVENT','END:VCALENDAR' ].filter(Boolean).join('\r\n'); return new Response(lines, { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/calendar; charset=utf-8', 'Content-Disposition': 'inline; filename="event.ics"', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': ALLOWED_ORIGIN } }); } async function apiOnboardingUnsub(request, env, cors){ let tok=''; try { tok=(new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('t')||'').trim(); } catch(e){} const isPost = request.method === 'POST'; function page(title, msg){ return ''+title+'' + '' + '

The app logs its own sends to the same collection. Writes // are fire-and-forget: a failed log must never break or delay a send. async function _logSentEmail(token, agentId, to, subject, type, clientName, clientUid){ try { if (!token || !subject) return; const _to = Array.isArray(to) ? to.filter(Boolean).join(', ') : String(to||''); if (!_to) return; const _id = 'e_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8); const _sub = String(subject).slice(0,200); // The agent's own log — one list across every client, powers Email → Sent. if (agentId){ await firestoreWrite(token, 'agents/'+agentId+'/sentEmails/'+_id, { to: fsString(_to), subject: fsString(_sub), type: fsString(type||'general'), clientName: fsString(String(clientName||'')), clientUid: fsString(String(clientUid||'')), source: fsString('worker'), sentAt: fsTimestamp() }); } // And the client's own card, so opening a contact shows what they have received. // Same collection the app writes to, same field names. if (clientUid){ await firestoreWrite(token, 'users/'+clientUid+'/emailLog/'+_id, { subject: fsString(_sub), type: fsString(type||'general'), agentId: fsString(String(agentId||'')), source: fsString('worker'), sentAt: fsTimestamp() }); } } catch(e){ /* logging is never allowed to matter */ } } async function _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, home, tokenId, toOverride, opts){ opts = opts || {}; if(!home) return { ok:false, reason:'no home' }; if(!env.RESEND_API_KEY) return { ok:false, reason:'no RESEND_API_KEY' }; const to = String(toOverride || home.email || '').trim(); if(!to) return { ok:false, reason:'no recipient email on this home' }; let equity=null, history=[]; try { if(home.equityJson) equity=JSON.parse(home.equityJson); } catch(e){} try { history = home.historyJson ? Number(equity.estLoanBalance) : null; let equityAmt = (equity && equity.equity!=null) ? Number(equity.equity) : (owed!=null?Math.max(0,value-owed):null); let equityPct = (equity && equity.equityPct!=null) ? Number(equity.equityPct) : (value&&equityAmt!=null?Math.round(equityAmt/value*100):null); if(home.ownerOwed!=null && String(home.ownerOwed)!==''){ const oo=Number(String(home.ownerOwed).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')); if(!isNaN(oo)){ owed=oo; equityAmt=Math.max(0,value-oo); equityPct=value?Math.round(equityAmt/value*100):null; } } let qtrDelta=0; if(Array.isArray(history)&&history.length>=2){ const a=Number(history[history.length-2].value)||0,b=Number(history[history.length-1].value)||0; if(a>0) qtrDelta=((b-a)/a)*100; } else if(home.monthlyChangePct) qtrDelta=Number(home.monthlyChangePct)||0; const agent = await _ntResolveAgent(token, home.agentId); const lender = await _ntResolveLender(token, home.lenderId); // co-brand the finance moments when a lender is assigned // The growth split and the hook. A failure here must not cost the client their report. let _rates = { rate30: null }; try { _rates = await getLiveRates(env, token); } catch(e){ _rates = { rate30: null }; } let insight = null; // equityJson is written as '' whenever the enroll couldn't value the home. Rebuild from the loan // inputs rather than going silent — they're all still on the doc. let eqObj = equity; if (!eqObj && value){ const _cl = function(v){ return Number(String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) || 0; }; const _la = _cl(home.loanAmount), _pp = _cl(home.purchasePrice); if (_la || _pp){ const _loan = _la ? { loanAmount:_la, ownerRate:_cl(home.ownerRate), loanTerm:(String(home.loanTerm)==='15'?'15':'30'), purchaseDate: home.purchaseDate || '' } : null; try { eqObj = estimateEquity(value, _pp, home.purchaseDate || '', _loan); } catch(e){ eqObj = null; } } } if (eqObj){ // Read the fallbacks off eqObj, not `equity`. When equityJson is empty, `equity` stays null // and eqObj is rebuilt from the loan inputs just above — referencing `equity` here threw // "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'estLoanBalance')" and killed the whole send. const eqEff = Object.assign({}, eqObj, { estLoanBalance: (owed != null ? owed : eqObj.estLoanBalance), equity: (equityAmt != null ? equityAmt : eqObj.equity), equityPct: (equityPct != null ? equityPct : eqObj.equityPct) }); try { insight = equityInsight(eqEff, home, history, value, _rates.rate30); } catch(e){ insight = null; } } // Exactly one review per ownership year, on the first quarterly at or after the anniversary. Math.floor(insight.monthsOwned / 12) : 0; const _isPreview = !!toOverride; const _force = String(opts.force || ''); // 'review' | 'pmi' — preview only, never stamps const reviewDue = (_force === 'review') || (!opts.isFirst && !_isPreview && _ownYear >= 1 && Number(home.reviewSentFor||0) < _ownYear); // Crossing 20% equity is real money on the table every month it goes unnoticed.
