((lender.calendly || '') || (lender.email ? 'mailto:'+lender.email+'?subject='+encodeURIComponent('My mortgage & refinance options') : (lender.phone ? 'tel:'+String(lender.phone).replace(/[^0-9+]/g,'') : APP_WEB_URL))) : (((agent._doc && agent._doc.calendly) || '') || (agent.email ? 'mailto:'+agent.email+'?subject='+encodeURIComponent('My equity review') : APP_WEB_URL)); const built = _ntTeaserHtml({ address: home.mailingAddress || home.matchedAddress || '', value: value, qtrDelta: qtrDelta, equity: equityAmt, equityPct: equityPct, clientFirst: _ntHomeFirst(home), reportUrl: 'https://app.nesttrack.io/report.html?t=' + tokenId, unsubUrl: 'https://app.tom-b52.workers.dev/api/v1/nesttracker-unsub?t=' + tokenId, agentAddress: agent.mailingAddress || '', isFirst: !!opts.isFirst, insight: insight, reviewDue: reviewDue, pmiAlert: pmiAlert, bookUrl: bookUrl, agent: agent, lender: lender, signatureHtml: _ntAgentSignature(agent._doc || {}) }); const fromName = _agentFromField(agent, NEWS_DOMAIN); const unsubUrl = 'https://app.tom-b52.workers.dev/api/v1/nesttracker-unsub?t=' + tokenId; const payload = { from: fromName, to:[to], subject: built.subject, html: built.html, headers: { 'List-Unsubscribe': '<'+unsubUrl+'>', 'List-Unsubscribe-Post': 'List-Unsubscribe=One-Click' } }; if (agent.email) payload.reply_to = agent.email; try { const r = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', { method:'POST', headers:{'Authorization':'Bearer '+env.RESEND_API_KEY,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }); if (r.ok){ // Only after it left the building. A failed send has to be allowed to try again next quarter. if ((reviewDue || pmiAlert) && !_force && !_isPreview){ var _st = {}, _mask = []; if (reviewDue){ _st.reviewSentFor = { integerValue: String(_ownYear) }; _mask.push('reviewSentFor'); } if (pmiAlert){ _st.pmiAlerted = fsBool(true); _mask.push('pmiAlerted'); } try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, _st, _mask); } catch(e){} } _logSentEmail(token, home.agentId||'', to, built.subject, (opts.isFirst ? 'home-value-welcome' : 'home-value'), _ntHomeLabel(home), home.contactUid||''); return { ok:true, sentTo: to, reviewSent: reviewDue, pmiAlerted: pmiAlert }; } const t = await r.text(); return { ok:false, reason:'resend '+r.status+': '+String(t).slice(0,140) }; } catch(e){ return { ok:false, reason:'send error: '+(e&&e.message||'network') }; } } // POST /api/v1/nesttracker-send-teaser → agent-authed. Sends a home's teaser now (for testing). // body: { t|token: homeToken, to?: override recipient for a safe preview }. async function apiNestTrackerSendTeaser(request, env, cors, who){ let body={}; try { body=await request.json(); } catch(e){} const tokenId = String(body.t || body.token || '').trim(); if(!tokenId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'missing home token'}),{status:400,headers:cors}); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const home = await fsGetDoc(token,'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId); if(!home) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'home not found'}),{status:404,headers:cors}); if(who && home.agentId && who.agentId && home.agentId !== who.agentId && (home.enrolledBy||'') !== who.agentId) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ok:false,reason:'not your home'}),{status:403,headers:cors}); const res = await _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, home, tokenId, (body.to||'').trim(), { force: (body.force||'') }); return new Response(JSON.stringify(res),{status: res.ok?200:400, headers:cors}); } // ── PROBE: validate the ZIP→geoIdV4→Sales Trend V4 chain (Model B) before wiring it live. ── // Old geoids (ZI44140) DON'T work with V4 — it needs the 32-char geoIdV4 hash. This: (1) converts // ZI{zip} to geoIdV4 via legacyLookup, (2) calls Sales Trend V4, (3) shows the median-price series // + the quarterly drift % we'd apply. GET ?zip=44140 async function apiNestTrackerMarketTest(request, env, cors){ const key = env.ATTOM_API_KEY; if (!key) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'ATTOM_API_KEY not set' }), { status:500, headers:cors }); const u = new URL(request.url); const zip = (u.searchParams.get('zip')||'').trim(); if (!/^\d{5}$/.test(zip)) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'pass ?zip=44140' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const H = { headers:{ 'apikey':key, 'accept':'application/json' } }; const out = { zip, oldGeoId:'ZI'+zip, geoIdV4:null, lookup:null, trend:null }; // 1) Resolve ZIP → geoIdV4 hash via the authorized /v4/location/lookup. try { const lurl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/location/lookup?geographyTypeAbbreviation=ZI&name=' + encodeURIComponent(zip); const lr = await fetch(lurl, H); const lt = await lr.text(); out.lookup = { status: lr.status, bodySnippet: lt.slice(0,300) }; let lj=null; try { lj=JSON.parse(lt); } catch(e){} if (lj && Array.isArray(lj.geographies) && lj.geographies.length){ let g = lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI' && String(x.geographyName)===zip; }) || lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI'; }) || lj.geographies[0]; out.geoIdV4 = (g && g.geoIdV4) || null; } } catch(e){ out.lookup = { error: e.message }; } if (!out.geoIdV4) { out.ok=false; out.reason='could not resolve geoIdV4 for this ZIP'; return new Response(JSON.stringify(out,null,2),{status:200,headers:cors}); } // 2) Sales Trend V4 with the hash — quarterly, last 3 years. const nowY = new Date().getUTCFullYear(); try { const turl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/transaction/salestrend?geoIdV4=' + encodeURIComponent(out.geoIdV4) + '&interval=quarterly&startyear=' + (nowY-3) + '&endyear=' + nowY; const tr = await fetch(turl, H); const tt = await tr.text(); let tj=null; try { tj=JSON.parse(tt); } catch(e){} const series = _ntParseTrendSeries(tj); out.trend = { status: tr.status, points: series.length, series: series.slice(-10), drift: _ntDriftFromSeries(series) }; if (!series.length) out.trend.bodySnippet = tt.slice(0,400); } catch(e){ out.trend = { error: e.message }; } out.ok = !!(out.trend && out.trend.points); return new Response(JSON.stringify(out, null, 2), { status:200, headers:cors }); } // Parse ATTOM salesTrends JSON → [{q, med, count}] sorted oldest→newest. function _ntParseTrendSeries(tj){ const series = []; if (tj && Array.isArray(tj.salesTrends)){ for (const s of tj.salesTrends){ const st = s.salesTrend || {}; const dr = s.dateRange || {}; const med = Number(st.medSalePrice)||0; if (dr.start && med) series.push({ q: dr.start, med: med, count: Number(st.homeSaleCount)||0 }); } } // sort by year then quarter (start like "Q1-2024") series.sort(function(a,b){ const pa=_ntQKey(a.q), pb=_ntQKey(b.q); return pa-pb; }); return series; } function _ntQKey(qstr){ const m=String(qstr).match(/Q(\d)-(\d{4})/); if(!m) return 0; return parseInt(m[2],10)*4 + parseInt(m[1],10); } // From a quarterly median-price series, compute a SMOOTHED quarterly drift %. // Small ZIPs are noisy quarter-to-quarter (mix of which homes sold), so we compare the trailing // 4-quarter average against the prior 4-quarter average — a year-over-year on averaged data — // then convert to a per-quarter geometric rate. Falls back to a simple span if <8 quarters. function _ntDriftFromSeries(series){ const s = (Array.isArray(series) ? series : []).filter(function(p){ return p && p.med>0; }); if (s.length < 2) return null; const avg = function(arr){ return arr.reduce(function(t,p){ return t+p.med; },0) / arr.length; }; if (s.length < 8){ const a=s[0], b=s[s.length-1], span=s.length-1; if (!a.med) return null; const tot=(b.med-a.med)/a.med, pq=Math.pow(1+tot,1/span)-1; return { method:'span', latestQ:b.q, priorQ:a.q, spanQuarters:span, perQuarterPct:Math.round(pq*10000)/100, annualizedPct:Math.round((Math.pow(1+pq,4)-1)*10000)/100, perQuarterFraction:pq }; } const recent = s.slice(-4), prior = s.slice(-8,-4); const rAvg = avg(recent), pAvg = avg(prior); if (!pAvg) return null; const yoy = (rAvg - pAvg) / pAvg; const perQuarter = Math.pow(1 + yoy, 1/4) - 1; return { method:'4v4', latestQ:s[s.length-1].q, recentAvg:Math.round(rAvg), priorAvg:Math.round(pAvg), yoyPct:Math.round(yoy*10000)/100, perQuarterPct:Math.round(perQuarter*10000)/100, annualizedPct:Math.round((Math.pow(1+perQuarter,4)-1)*10000)/100, perQuarterFraction:perQuarter }; } // ═══════════════════ NESTTRACKER — quarterly value drift (Step 5) ═══════════════════ // Each quarter every enrolled home's value drifts by the real market change since the last // pull, then the teaser email fires automatically (the agent pre-approved at enroll). The // SOURCE of the change % is swappable via env.NESTTRACKER_RATE_SOURCE: // 'avm' (default, in use now) — re-pull ATTOM's AVM per home for a fresh % (~$0.50/home/qtr) // 'trend' (future) — one ZIP-level appreciation % per area, reused across homes // (needs ATTOM's Sales-Trend package; not on the plan today) // Swapping later is a one-function change — the drift math below never changes. async function _ntAvmRate(env, home){ const parts = { street: home.street||'', city: home.city||'', state: home.state||'', zip: home.zip||'' }; const v = await getHomeValue(env, parts); if (!v.ok) return { ok:false, reason: v.reason || 'AVM re-pull failed' }; const refAvm = Number(home.refAvm) || Number(home.value) || v.value; const pct = refAvm ? (Number(v.value) - refAvm) / refAvm : 0; return { ok:true, newAvm: Number(v.value), pct: pct, confidence: v.confidence, monthlyChgPct: v.monthlyChangePct, high: v.high, low: v.low }; } async function _ntTrendRate(env, home){ // MODEL B: drift by the home's ZIP-level market appreciation (ATTOM Sales Trend V4). // One trend call per ZIP per quarter is CACHED and shared by every home in that ZIP. const zip = String(home.zip||'').trim(); if (!/^\d{5}$/.test(zip)) return { ok:false, reason:'no ZIP on home' }; const fsToken = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const pct = await _ntZipTrendPct(env, fsToken, zip); if (pct == null) return { ok:false, reason:'no trend for ZIP '+zip }; // Trend gives a % only; the "newAvm" for the ref chain stays the prior refAvm drifted by pct // so the baseline carry-forward still works without a per-home AVM pull. const refAvm = Number(home.refAvm) || Number(home.value) || 0; const newAvm = Math.round(refAvm * (1 + pct)); return { ok:true, newAvm: newAvm, pct: pct, confidence: Number(home.confidence)||null, monthlyChgPct: null, source:'trend' }; } // Cached ZIP → geoIdV4 hash (one-time lookup per ZIP, stored forever in nesttracker_geoids/{zip}). async function _ntZipGeoId(env, fsToken, zip){ try { const c = await fsGetDoc(fsToken, 'nesttracker_geoids/'+zip); if (c && c.geoIdV4) return c.geoIdV4; } catch(e){} try { const lurl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/location/lookup?geographyTypeAbbreviation=ZI&name=' + encodeURIComponent(zip); const lr = await fetch(lurl, { headers:{ 'apikey':env.ATTOM_API_KEY, 'accept':'application/json' } }); const lj = await lr.json(); let hash = null; if (lj && Array.isArray(lj.geographies) && lj.geographies.length){ const g = lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI' && String(x.geographyName)===zip; }) || lj.geographies.find(function(x){ return x.geographyTypeAbbreviation==='ZI'; }) || lj.geographies[0]; hash = (g && g.geoIdV4) || null; } if (hash){ try { await firestoreWrite(fsToken, 'nesttracker_geoids/'+zip, { geoIdV4: fsString(hash), zip: fsString(zip), resolvedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} } return hash; } catch(e){ return null; } } // Cached per-ZIP quarterly drift fraction (one Sales Trend V4 call per ZIP per quarter, shared // by all homes in that ZIP). Stored in nesttracker_trends/{zip}_{quarter}. async function _ntZipTrendPct(env, fsToken, zip){ const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); const cacheId = 'nesttracker_trends/' + zip + '_' + q; // If ANY doc exists for this zip+quarter, trust it and never re-pull — even a "no data" marker. let cached = null; try { cached = await fsGetDoc(fsToken, cacheId); } catch(e){} if (cached) return (cached.pct != null && cached.pct !== '') ? Number(cached.pct) : null; const noData = function(reason){ try { firestoreWrite(fsToken, cacheId, { noData: fsBool(true), zip: fsString(zip), quarter: fsString(q), reason: fsString(reason), computedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} return null; }; const geo = await _ntZipGeoId(env, fsToken, zip); if (!geo) return noData('no geoIdV4 for ZIP'); const nowY = new Date().getUTCFullYear(); try { const turl = 'https://api.gateway.attomdata.com/v4/transaction/salestrend?geoIdV4=' + encodeURIComponent(geo) + '&interval=quarterly&startyear=' + (nowY-3) + '&endyear=' + nowY; const tr = await fetch(turl, { headers:{ 'apikey':env.ATTOM_API_KEY, 'accept':'application/json' } }); const tj = await tr.json(); const series = _ntParseTrendSeries(tj); const drift = _ntDriftFromSeries(series); if (!drift || drift.perQuarterFraction == null) return noData('no usable trend series'); // Cap a single quarter's ZIP drift at +/-6% to guard against a thin/noisy quarter. let pct = drift.perQuarterFraction; if (pct > 0.06) pct = 0.06; if (pct < -0.06) pct = -0.06; try { await firestoreWrite(fsToken, cacheId, { pct: fsDouble(pct), annualizedPct: fsDouble(drift.annualizedPct||0), zip: fsString(zip), quarter: fsString(q), latestQ: fsString(drift.latestQ||''), computedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch(e){} return pct; } catch(e){ return noData('fetch error'); } } // Read-only "your area" stat for the report — reads the cached ZIP trend for the CURRENT quarter // only (never triggers a pull, so report views stay $0). Returns null if not cached yet. async function _ntZipTrendStat(fsToken, zip){ if (!/^\d{5}$/.test(String(zip||''))) return null; const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); try { const c = await fsGetDoc(fsToken, 'nesttracker_trends/' + zip + '_' + q); if (c && c.annualizedPct != null) return { annualizedPct: Number(c.annualizedPct), latestQ: c.latestQ || '', zip: zip }; } catch(e){} return null; } async function _ntGetQuarterlyRate(env, home){ const force = String(env.NESTTRACKER_RATE_SOURCE || '').toLowerCase(); // '', 'avm', 'trend' const isOverridden = (home.valueSource === 'agent') || (home.overrideValue != null && String(home.overrideValue) !== ''); // Forced modes (testing / manual override of the default behavior). if (force === 'avm') return await _ntAvmRate(env, home); if (force === 'trend'){ const t = await _ntTrendRate(env, home); return t.ok ? t : await _ntAvmRate(env, home); } // DEFAULT (hybrid): // • Overridden homes → drift the agent's corrected value by the ZIP trend. Re-pulling ATTOM // would just return the same value the agent already rejected, so we never pull for these. // If the ZIP has no usable trend, hold the value (skip this quarter) rather than guess. // • Normal homes → re-pull the AVM for a fresh, per-property number (most accurate). if (isOverridden){ const t = await _ntTrendRate(env, home); if (t.ok) return t; return { ok:false, reason:'overridden home, no ZIP trend this quarter — holding value' }; } return await _ntAvmRate(env, home); } // Apply the quarter's drift to ONE home, update the stored snapshot + history, then auto-send the teaser. async function _ntQuarterlyUpdateHome(env, token, home, tokenId, opts){ opts = opts || {}; if (home.active === false) return { ok:false, skipped:'inactive' }; if (home.locked === true) return { ok:false, skipped:'locked (over cap / unpaid)' }; // paused until payment clears // Value-unsubscribed → stop pulling. No email goes out, so a fresh ATTOM valuation would be paid // for and seen by no one. The card shows "value paused — unsubscribed" (via emailsOff) so the // stale number never silently misleads the agent. A forced/preview send (toOverride) still runs. if (home.emailsOff === true && !opts.toOverride){ return { ok:false, skipped:'value paused (client unsubscribed)' }; } const rate = await _ntGetQuarterlyRate(env, home); if (!rate.ok) return { ok:false, skipped:'rate unavailable: ' + (rate.reason||'') }; // don't corrupt value; retry next quarter const baseline = Number(home.value) || rate.newAvm || 0; if (!baseline) return { ok:false, skipped:'no baseline value' }; let newValue = Math.round(baseline * (1 + (rate.pct||0))); if (!(newValue > 0)) newValue = baseline; // Preserve the original high/low ratio around the drifted value. const hiRatio = (Number(home.high) && baseline) ? Number(home.high)/baseline : 1.09; const loRatio = (Number(home.low) && baseline) ? Number(home.low)/baseline : 0.91; const newHigh = Math.round(newValue * hiRatio); const newLow = Math.round(newValue * loRatio); // Recompute equity from the new value + amortized loan paydown. let equity=null; try { if(home.equityJson) equity=JSON.parse(home.equityJson); } catch(e){} let owed = null; // Loan-data path: re-amortize the balance to TODAY so equity grows from paydown too (not just value). // Anchor: a manually-entered ownerOwed (homeowner/agent edit) resets the balance + its date; from that // point we keep paying down. Otherwise amortize from the original loanStart. const _loanAmt = Number(String(home.loanAmount||'').replace(/[^0-9.]/g,''))||0; const _hasLoan = _loanAmt > 0 && String(home.loanTerm||'') !== ''; const _manualOwed = (home.ownerOwed!=null && String(home.ownerOwed)!=='') ? Number(String(home.ownerOwed).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')) : null; if (_hasLoan){ const term = (String(home.loanTerm)==='15') ? 180 : 360; let ratePct; if (home.ownerRate!=null && String(home.ownerRate)!=='') ratePct = Number(String(home.ownerRate).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,'')); else { let yr=null; const st=home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||''; if(st){ const sd=/^\d{4}$/.test(String(st).trim())?new Date(Number(st),0,1):new Date(st); if(sd&&!isNaN(sd.getTime())) yr=sd.getFullYear(); } ratePct=(_rateForYear(yr)||0.06)*100; } if (_manualOwed != null && home.ownerOwedAt){ // Balance was hand-set on ownerOwedAt → amortize the remaining balance forward from that date. const monthsSinceAnchor = _monthsSince(home.ownerOwedAt); // Derive months already paid at the anchor from the anchor balance, then add elapsed months. // Simpler + robust: treat the anchor balance as a fresh loan of (remaining term) and pay it down. const monthsPaidTotal = _monthsSince(home.loanStart||home.purchaseDate||''); owed = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, ratePct, term, monthsPaidTotal); // If the manual balance differs from the pure-schedule balance, honor the manual anchor by // shifting: keep the manual value and subtract this quarter's principal delta. const prevSched = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, ratePct, term, Math.max(0, monthsPaidTotal-3)); const principalThisQ = Math.max(0, prevSched - owed); owed = Math.max(0, Math.round(_manualOwed - principalThisQ)); } else { const monthsPaid = _monthsSince(home.loanStart || home.purchaseDate || ''); owed = _amortBalance(_loanAmt, ratePct, term, monthsPaid); } } else if (_manualOwed != null) { owed = _manualOwed; // flat balance, no loan schedule } else if (equity && equity.estLoanBalance!=null) { owed = Number(equity.estLoanBalance); // legacy estimate, carried flat } let newEquityJson = home.equityJson || ''; if (!equity && owed!=null) equity = {}; if (equity){ if (owed!=null){ equity.estLoanBalance = owed; equity.equity = Math.max(0, newValue - owed); equity.equityPct = newValue ? Math.round(equity.equity/newValue*100) : null; if(_hasLoan) equity.estimated=false; } newEquityJson = JSON.stringify(equity); } const q = _ntQuarter(new Date()); let history=[]; try { history = home.historyJson ? JSON.parse(home.historyJson) : []; } catch(e){} history = history.filter(function(h){ return h && h.q !== q; }); history.push({ q:q, value:newValue, ts:new Date().toISOString() }); if (history.length>24) history = history.slice(history.length-24); const historyStr = JSON.stringify(history); const upd = { value: fsInt(newValue), high: fsInt(newHigh), low: fsInt(newLow), refAvm: fsInt(rate.newAvm!=null ? rate.newAvm : (Number(home.refAvm)||newValue)), confidence: fsInt(rate.confidence!=null ? rate.confidence : (Number(home.confidence)||0)), monthlyChangePct: fsString(rate.monthlyChgPct==null ? '' : String(rate.monthlyChgPct)), equityJson: fsString(newEquityJson), historyJson: fsString(historyStr), lastPulledAt: fsTimestamp(), lastPulledQuarter: fsString(q), lastDriftPct: fsString(String(Math.round((rate.pct||0)*10000)/100)) }; // ── Conditional comps refresh ($0.25) — only when it's worth spending: ── // (1) the report was OPENED in the last 120 days (captures the open from last cycle — no point // refreshing comps for a report nobody looks at; they never saw the old ones either), AND // (2) comps are older than ~6 months (don't over-refresh). // Both gates must pass, so unopened reports never incur the comps cost. try { const now = Date.now(); const viewedMs = home.lastViewedAt ? Date.parse(home.lastViewedAt) : 0; const compsMs = home.compsPulledAt ? Date.parse(home.compsPulledAt) : 0; const openedRecently = viewedMs && (now - viewedMs) <= 120*24*60*60*1000; const compsStale = !compsMs || (now - compsMs) >= 182*24*60*60*1000; // ~6 months if (openedRecently && compsStale){ const parts = { street: home.street||'', city: home.city||'', state: home.state||'', zip: home.zip||'' }; const c = await attomGetComps(env, parts); if (c && c.ok && Array.isArray(c.comps)){ upd.compsJson = fsString(JSON.stringify(c.comps)); upd.compsPulledAt = fsTimestamp(); } } } catch(e){ /* comps refresh is best-effort; never block the value update */ } try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'nesttracker_homes/'+tokenId, upd, Object.keys(upd)); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, skipped:'write failed: '+e.message }; } // Fully-auto teaser (agent pre-approved at enroll). Send off the freshly-merged snapshot. // Client-unsubscribed homes still refresh their value above (keeps the agent's dashboard live), // but no email goes out — the relationship/contact stays intact, only the emails stop. let sent=false, sendReason=''; if (home.emailsOff === true){ sendReason='client unsubscribed'; } else if (!opts.noSend){ const merged = Object.assign({}, home, { value:newValue, high:newHigh, low:newLow, equityJson:newEquityJson, historyJson:historyStr, monthlyChangePct:(rate.monthlyChgPct==null?'':String(rate.monthlyChgPct)) }); const sres = await _ntSendTeaserForHome(env, token, merged, tokenId, opts.toOverride||''); sent = !!(sres && sres.ok); sendReason = (sres && sres.reason) || ''; } return { ok:true, newValue:newValue, pct: rate.pct, sent:sent, sendReason:sendReason }; } // Sweep due homes (anniversary-based: ~90 days since last pull). Self-gates — once processed, // lastPulledAt updates so a home won't be due again for a quarter. Capped per run to pace ATTOM/Resend. async function nesttrackerQuarterlySweep(token, env, opts){ opts = opts || {}; const cap = opts.cap || NESTTRACKER_SWEEP_CAP; const dueMs = 89*24*60*60*1000; let rows; try { rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from:[{ collectionId:'nesttracker_homes', allDescendants:false }], where:{ fieldFilter:{ field:{fieldPath:'active'}, op:'EQUAL', value:{booleanValue:true} } }, limit: 400 }); } catch(e){ return { ok:false, error:'query failed: '+e.message }; } if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return { ok:false, error:'query returned non-array' }; const now = Date.now(); const out = { ok:true, scanned:0, due:0, processed:0, sent:0, results:[] }; const seenAgents = {}; // refresh each agent's block/payment status at most once per run for (const r of rows){ if (!r.document || !r.document.fields) continue; out.scanned++; const home = digDoc(r.document.fields); if (home.active === false) continue; const tokenId = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); const lastMs = home.lastPulledAt ? Date.parse(home.lastPulledAt) : (home.enrolledAt ? Date.parse(home.enrolledAt) : 0); const isDue = opts.force || !lastMs || (now - lastMs) >= dueMs; if (!isDue) continue; out.due++; // First time we see this agent this run, sync their block/payment status from Stripe and reconcile // locks. This closes the cost-leak where an agent stops paying AND stops logging in — their // over-cap homes get locked here (skipped below) instead of quietly pulling ATTOM forever. const _aid = home.agentId || ''; if (_aid && !seenAgents[_aid]){ seenAgents[_aid] = 1; // Only hit Stripe for agents who have EVER held a block (they're the only ones who can lapse or // be over the base 40). Reads Firestore only — // ZERO AVM pulls, so opening/refreshing the report costs nothing. async function apiNestTrackerReport(request, env, cors){ let tok = ''; try { tok = (new URL(request.url).searchParams.get('t') || '').trim(); } catch(e){} if (!tok) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'missing token' }), { status:400, headers:cors }); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); const home = await fsGetDoc(token, 'nesttracker_homes/' + tok); if (!home || (home.active === false && home.pending !== true)) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok:false, reason:'report not found' }), { status:404, headers:cors }); // Stamp the view (engagement signal + gates the comps refresh). (Number(String(home.purchasePrice).replace(/[^0-9.]/g,''))||null) : null; const rates = await getLiveRates(env, token); const areaTrend = await _ntZipTrendStat(token, home.zip); // cache-only; null if not computed yet // Growth split + the one thing worth talking about. Mirrors inbound-lead field handling. async function apiCreateContact(request, env, cors, who) { const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const _lc = {}; for (const _k in body) { if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, _k)) _lc[String(_k).trim().toLowerCase()] = body[_k]; } const _pick = (...keys) => { for (const k of keys) { const v = _lc[k.toLowerCase()]; if (v != null && String(v).trim() !== '') return String(v).trim(); } return ''; }; let firstName = _pick('firstName', 'first', 'fname', 'first_name'); let lastName = _pick('lastName', 'last', 'lname', 'last_name'); const _full = _pick('name', 'fullName', 'full_name', 'contactName'); if (!firstName && _full) { const _p = _full.split(/\s+/); firstName = _p.shift() || ''; if (!lastName) lastName = _p.join(' '); } const email = _pick('email', 'emailAddress', 'email_address'); const phone = _pick('phone', 'phoneNumber', 'phone_number', 'mobile', 'tel'); const notes = _pick('notes', 'message', 'comments', 'comment', 'description'); const source = _pick('source', 'leadSource', 'lead_source') || 'Zapier'; const address = _pick('address', 'mailingAddress', 'propertyAddress'); const leadType = _pick('leadType', 'lead_type', 'type'); const leadStatus = _pick('leadStatus', 'status'); const leadSegment = _pick('leadSegment', 'segment'); const smartPlanId = _pick('smartPlanId'); const smartPlanName = _pick('smartPlanName'); if (!firstName && !email && !phone) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'contact needs a name, email, or phone' }), { status: 400, headers: cors }); const contactId = 'lead_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7); const fields = { firstName: fsString(firstName), lastName: fsString(lastName), email: fsString(email), phone: fsString(phone), agentId: fsString(who.agentId), role: fsString((leadType === 'seller' || leadType === 'both') ? 'seller' : 'buyer'), journeyType: fsString(leadType === 'both' ? 'both' : ((leadType === 'seller') ? 'seller' : 'buyer')), crmStatus: fsString(['pipeline', 'active', 'nurture', 'cold', 'contact'].indexOf(leadStatus) >= 0 ? leadStatus : 'pipeline'), segment: fsString(leadSegment || ''), currentStep: fsInt(0), currentSellerStep: fsInt(0), isContact: fsBool(true), portalAccess: fsBool(false), source: fsString(source), notes: fsString(notes || ''), mailingAddress: fsString(address || ''), createdAt: fsTimestamp(), lastActive: fsTimestamp() }; if (smartPlanId) { const enrollDay = (typeof spTodayET === 'function') ? spTodayET() : new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); fields.smartPlanId = fsString(smartPlanId); fields.smartPlanName = fsString(smartPlanName || 'Plan'); fields.smartPlanOwner = fsString(who.agentId); fields.smartPlanStatus = fsString('active'); fields.smartPlanStep = fsInt(0); fields.smartPlanNextDue = fsString(enrollDay); fields.smartPlanEnrolledAt = fsString(enrollDay); } const fsResp = await firestoreWrite(token, 'users/' + contactId, fields); if (!fsResp.ok) { const t = await fsResp.text(); return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'write failed', detail: t }), { status: 500, headers: cors }); } // notify the agent (same as inbound-lead) if (env.ONESIGNAL_REST_KEY) { const nm = ((firstName || '') + ' ' + (lastName || '')).trim() || email || 'New Contact'; await pushToAgentId(token, env, who.agentId, ' New Contact (Zapier)', nm + ' added via Zapier', 'https://app.nesttrack.io/?lead=' + contactId); } return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true, id: contactId, agentId: who.agentId }), { status: 200, headers: cors }); } // ─── Per-tenant email cap: count + gate a tenant's bulk sends (daily + monthly) ─── async function consumeEmailQuota(token, agentId, requested) { requested = Math.max(0, parseInt(requested, 10) || 0); if (!agentId) return { allowed: requested, uncapped: true, dailyCap: EMAIL_DAILY_CAP, monthlyCap: EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP }; // Owner bypass: a user doc flagged emailUnlimited:true is never capped. (Still draws on the shared // Resend plan — "unlimited" means the app won't throttle you, not that the sends are free.) try { const a = await _ntResolveAgent(token, agentId); const doc = (a && a._doc) || {}; if (doc.emailUnlimited === true) return { allowed: requested, uncapped: true, dailyCap: EMAIL_DAILY_CAP, monthlyCap: EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP }; } catch(e){} const today = spTodayET(); // YYYY-MM-DD (America/New_York) const month = today.slice(0, 7); // YYYY-MM const path = 'email_quota/' + agentId; let dayCount = 0, monthCount = 0; try { const cur = await fsGetDoc(token, path); if (cur) { if (cur.day === today) dayCount = parseInt(cur.dayCount, 10) || 0; if (cur.month === month) monthCount = parseInt(cur.monthCount, 10) || 0; } } catch (e) {} const dailyRemaining = Math.max(0, EMAIL_DAILY_CAP - dayCount); const monthlyRemaining = Math.max(0, EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP - monthCount); const allowed = Math.max(0, Math.min(requested, dailyRemaining, monthlyRemaining)); try { await firestoreWrite(token, path, { day: fsString(today), dayCount: fsInt(dayCount + allowed), month: fsString(month), monthCount: fsInt(monthCount + allowed), updatedAt: fsTimestamp() }); } catch (e) {} return { allowed, requested, dailyRemaining, monthlyRemaining, dailyCap: EMAIL_DAILY_CAP, monthlyCap: EMAIL_MONTHLY_CAP }; } // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // MLS ALERT EMAIL PIPELINE // An agent points a Matrix saved search at c-@in.nesttrack.io instead of // the client. Everything below turns those emails into listing cards the client // rates in the app, so the agent finally sees what their buyer actually thinks. // No MLS licence, no per-agent paperwork, works in any market on day one. // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Bodies arrive quoted-printable. Undo soft line breaks first, then =XX. function _qpDecode(s) { return String(s || '') .replace(/=\r?\n/g, '') .replace(/=([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})/g, function (_, h) { return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)); }); } function _htmlUnescape(s) { return String(s || '') .replace(/ /g, ' ').replace(/•/g, '\u2022') .replace(/&/g, '&').replace(/</g, '<').replace(/>/g, '>') .replace(/"/g, '"').replace(/'/g, "'") .replace(/&#(\d+);/g, function (_, d) { return String.fromCharCode(+d); }); } function _stripTags(s) { return _htmlUnescape(String(s || '').replace(/<[^>]*>/g, ' ')).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); } // The tag name is captured and back-referenced so a

holding nested s // reads through to its own

. Matching the first closing tag of any kind // truncated "4 bd \u2022 3 ba \u2022 2,636 sqft" down to just "4 bd". function _mlsClassRe(cls, flags) { return new RegExp("<(\\w+)[^>]*\\bclass=['\"][^'\"]*\\b" + cls + "\\b[^'\"]*['\"][^>]*>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/\\1>", flags); } function _byClass(block, cls) { var m = block.match(_mlsClassRe(cls, 'i')); return m ? _stripTags(m[2]) : ''; } function _allByClass(block, cls) { var re = _mlsClassRe(cls, 'gi'), out = [], m; while ((m = re.exec(block))) out.push(_stripTags(m[2])); return out; } // OneHome tokens are plain base64 JSON: contactid, setid, agentid, listingid. function _decodeOneHomeToken(tok) { try { var s2 = String(tok || '').replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'); s2 += '='.repeat((4 - (s2.length % 4)) % 4); return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(s2)))); } catch (e) { return null; } } // Status is an image; the filename is the reliable signal. Collected from real // samples, with the rest pre-mapped so they work the first time one arrives. var MLS_STATUS_MAP = { new_listing:'New Listing', price_decreased:'Price Decreased', price_increased:'Price Increased', back_on_market:'Back on Market', open_house:'Open House', under_contract:'Under Contract', pending:'Pending', sold:'Sold', closed:'Sold' }; function _mlsStatus(block) { var m = block.match(/assets\/email\/([a-z_]+)\.png/i); if (m && MLS_STATUS_MAP[m[1].toLowerCase()]) { return { status: MLS_STATUS_MAP[m[1].toLowerCase()], statusKey: m[1].toLowerCase() }; } // alt sometimes runs straight into the next attribute: alt="New Listing"height="22" var a = block.match(/assets\/email\/[a-z_]+\.png"[^>]*?alt="([^"]+)"/i) || block.match(/alt="([^"]+)"\s*height="22"/i); if (a) return { status: a[1].trim(), statusKey: a[1].toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_') }; return { status: '', statusKey: '' }; } function _mlsSpecs(line) { var out = { beds: null, baths: null, sqft: null, dom: null }; // Days on market, when the alert carries it. Sold emails sometimes do and new-listing ones // never do, so this stays optional rather than something the report depends on. var d = line.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:days?\s*on\s*market|DOM\b)/i); if (d) out.dom = parseInt(d[1], 10); var b = line.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*bd/i); if (b) out.beds = parseFloat(b[1]); var ba = line.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*ba/i); if (ba) out.baths = parseFloat(ba[1]); var sf = line.match(/([\d,]+)\s*sqft/i); if (sf) out.sqft = parseInt(sf[1].replace(/,/g, ''), 10); return out; } // Walk the MIME parts and return the decoded text/html body. Handles base64 and // quoted-printable, and recurses into nested multiparts — a forwarded email wraps the // original inside another multipart, which is exactly how the first real test failed. function _mimeHtmlPart(raw, depth) { depth = depth || 0; if (depth > 4) return raw; const bMatch = raw.match(/boundary="?([^"\r\n;]+)"?/i); if (!bMatch) return _decodePart(raw, raw); const parts = raw.split('--' + bMatch[1]); let best = ''; for (const part of parts) { if (!/Content-Type:/i.test(part)) continue; if (/Content-Type:\s*multipart\//i.test(part)) { const inner = _mimeHtmlPart(part, depth + 1); if (inner && inner.length > best.length) best = inner; continue; } if (!/Content-Type:\s*text\/html/i.test(part)) continue; const decoded = _decodePart(part, ''); if (decoded.length > best.length) best = decoded; } return best || raw; } function _decodePart(part, fallback) { const split = part.split(/\r?\n\r?\n/); // headers end at the first blank line const headers = split[0] || ''; const bodyText = split.slice(1).join('\n\n'); if (!bodyText) return fallback || ''; if (/Content-Transfer-Encoding:\s*base64/i.test(headers)) { try { const bin = atob(bodyText.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/=]/g, '')); // atob yields bytes \u2014 reinterpret as UTF-8 so em dashes and accents survive. const bytes = Uint8Array.from(bin, function (c) { return c.charCodeAt(0); }); return new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(bytes); } catch (e) { return bodyText; } } if (/Content-Transfer-Encoding:\s*quoted-printable/i.test(headers)) { return _qpDecode(bodyText); } return bodyText; } function parseMlsAlert(rawBody) { var html = /=\r?\n|=3D/.test(rawBody) ? _qpDecode(rawBody) : rawBody; var ctx = null; var t0 = html.match(/portal\.onehome\.com\/[^"']*[?&]token=([A-Za-z0-9+/=_-]+)/); if (t0) ctx = _decodeOneHomeToken(t0[1]); var parts = html.split(//); var listings = []; for (var i = 1; i < parts.length; i += 2) { var recordId = parts[i], block = parts[i + 1] || ''; if (listings.some(function (l) { return l.recordId === recordId; })) continue; // fence is doubled var specs = _allByClass(block, 'highlight-specs'); var specLine = specs.find(function (x) { return /bd|ba|sqft/i.test(x); }) || ''; var mlsLine = specs.find(function (x) { return /MLS\s*#/i.test(x); }) || ''; var priceTxt = _byClass(block, 'highlight-price'); var row = Object.assign({ recordId: recordId, mlsNumber: (mlsLine.match(/MLS\s*#\s*(\S+)/i) || [])[1] || '', price: priceTxt, priceNum: parseInt(String(priceTxt).replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''), 10) || null, propType: _byClass(block, 'highlight-title'), street: _byClass(block, 'highlight-description'), cityState: _byClass(block, 'highlight-address'), photoUrl: _htmlUnescape((block.match(/src="(https:\/\/[^"]*GetMedia\.ashx[^"]*)"/) || [])[1] || ''), listingUrl: _htmlUnescape((block.match(/href="(https:\/\/portal\.onehome\.com\/[^"]*listing[^"]*)"/) || [])[1] || '') }, _mlsSpecs(specLine), _mlsStatus(block)); row.address = (row.street + (row.cityState ? ', ' + row.cityState : '')).trim(); if (row.mlsNumber || row.street) listings.push(row); } // The welcome email carries no listings and a welcome.jpg banner. It is ALSO the // opt-in: Matrix leaves a saved search inactive until someone clicks through to // OneHome once. There is no link labelled "confirm" \u2014 "View Properties" is it. // Verified: a plain GET on that URL flips the search from pending to sending. var isWelcome = listings.length === 0 && /assets\/email\/welcome\.jpg/i.test(html); var activateUrl = ''; if (isWelcome) { var a2 = html.match(/href="(https:\/\/portal\.onehome\.com\/[^"]*properties\?token=[^"]*)"/); if (a2) activateUrl = _htmlUnescape(a2[1]); } return { contactId: ctx ? ctx.contactid : null, setId: ctx ? ctx.setid : null, agentId: ctx ? ctx.agentid : null, sentTo: ctx ? ctx.email : '', isWelcome: isWelcome, activateUrl: activateUrl, listings: listings }; } // Follow the opt-in link so the search starts delivering. Best-effort, but logged: // a silent failure means the agent set up a search that never sends anything. // Activating a saved search needs a REAL browser. OneHome is a JavaScript app: the code that // registers the visit runs client-side, so a plain fetch returns 200 while Matrix still shows // the search as inactive. That false success cost us most of a morning. // // Cloudflare Browser Rendering runs actual headless Chrome. Using the REST API rather than // @cloudflare/puppeteer on purpose — the npm package needs a bundler, and this worker is // deployed by pasting one file into Quick Edit. // // Requires: Workers Paid, CF_ACCOUNT_ID, and CF_BROWSER_TOKEN (an API token with // Browser Rendering edit permission). Without those it degrades to the in-app Activate // button rather than failing silently. async function _mlsActivate(env, url) { if (!url) return { ok: false, reason: 'no activation url' }; // Trimmed on purpose: a stray space pasted after the account ID turned the URL into // ".../accounts/%20/browser-rendering/content", Cloudflare answered 404, and every // search silently fell back to the manual Activate button. const cfAccount = String(env.CF_ACCOUNT_ID || '').trim(); const cfToken = String(env.CF_BROWSER_TOKEN || '').trim(); if (!cfAccount || !cfToken) { return { ok: false, reason: 'browser rendering not configured' }; } try { const r = await fetch( 'https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/accounts/' + encodeURIComponent(cfAccount) + '/browser-rendering/content', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + cfToken, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ url: url, // Wait for the network to settle so the registering script has actually run. gotoOptions: { waitUntil: 'networkidle0', timeout: 30000 } }) } ); const j = await r.json().catch(function () { return null; }); if (!r.ok || !j || j.success !== true) { return { ok: false, reason: 'render ' + r.status + ': ' + (j && j.errors ? JSON.stringify(j.errors).slice(0, 160) : 'no body') }; } // The page rendered. Not absolute proof Matrix recorded it — only a listing alert is — // but this is the same thing a browser does, which is what worked by hand. return { ok: true, bytes: (j.result || '').length }; } catch (e) { return { ok: false, reason: 'render error: ' + (e && e.message || 'network') }; } } // Resolve c-@in.nesttrack.io back to the client. Per-client address rather // than one shared inbox: the To: header only carries the client's real email when // the search still goes to them, and once NestTrack is the sole recipient that // disappears. The token is unambiguous. // Builds the monthly numbers from what has accumulated. The range rather than an average, // because in a mixed street an average is a number nobody can act on - a $250k house and a // $1.2m house average to something neither owner recognises. The range shows both. function _soldStats(rows) { const prices = rows.map(function (r) { return Number(r.priceNum) || 0; }) .filter(function (n) { return n > 0; }) .sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }); const sqfts = rows.map(function (r) { return Number(r.sqft) || 0; }) .filter(function (n) { return n > 0; }) .sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }); const doms = rows.map(function (r) { return Number(r.dom) || 0; }) .filter(function (n) { return n > 0; }) .sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }); const mid = function (arr) { if (!arr.length) return null; const m = Math.floor(arr.length / 2); return arr.length % 2 ? arr[m] : Math.round((arr[m - 1] + arr[m]) / 2); }; return { count: rows.length, low: prices.length ? prices[0] : null, high: prices.length ? prices[prices.length - 1] : null, median: mid(prices), medianSqft: mid(sqfts), medianDom: mid(doms) }; } function _money(n) { if (n == null) return ''; if (n >= 1000000) return '$' + (n / 1000000).toFixed(n % 1000000 === 0 ? 0 : 1) + 'm'; if (n >= 1000) return '$' + Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k'; return '$' + n; } // The email. Built to be read on a phone in ten seconds: a headline that says what happened, // three numbers, a few actual sales, and one line pointing at their home value. function _soldReportHtml(opts) { const st = opts.stats, prev = opts.prev, rows = opts.rows; const agent = opts.agent || {}; const area = opts.area || 'your area'; // The comparison is what makes this feel alive rather than a data dump. It can only appear // from the second month, so it is written to read naturally when absent. let lead = ''; if (prev && prev.count) { const diff = st.count - prev.count; if (diff > 0) lead = diff + ' more than last month.'; else if (diff < 0) lead = Math.abs(diff) + ' fewer than last month.'; else lead = 'The same as last month.'; } if (st.medianDom) lead += (lead ? ' ' : '') + 'Typically ' + st.medianDom + ' days to sell.'; const cells = []; if (st.median != null) cells.push([_money(st.median), 'typical price']); if (st.low != null && st.high != null) cells.push([_money(st.low) + '–' + _money(st.high), 'from / to']); if (st.medianSqft) cells.push([st.medianSqft.toLocaleString(), 'typical size']); const statRow = cells.map(function (c, i) { return '' + '
' + c[0] + '
' + '
' + c[1] + '
' + ''; }).join(''); // Cards rather than table rows, with the photo the MLS already gave us. A sale reads as a // house that way, not as a line of data. const listRows = rows.slice(0, 5).map(function (r) { const specs = [ r.beds ? (r.beds + ' bd') : '', r.baths ? (r.baths + ' ba') : '', r.sqft ? (Number(r.sqft).toLocaleString() + ' sq ft') : '' ].filter(Boolean).join(' · '); const photo = r.photoUrl ? '' + '' : ''; return '' + '
' + photo + '' + '
' + '
' + _esc(r.street || r.address || '') + '
' + (specs ? '
' + specs + '
' : '') + '		' + '' + _esc(r.price || _money(r.priceNum)) + '' + '
'; }).join(''); // Only promises a date when there is one. An agent who never enrolled this client should not // have their email referencing a report that will never arrive. const valueBlock = opts.onNestTracker ? '
Wondering where yours stands?
' + '
Your home value report is on the way — or just reply and I’ll pull it early.
' : '
Curious what yours would sell for?
' + '
Reply and I’ll put together a full picture of your home’s value.
'; const initials = String(agent.name || '?').trim().split(/\s+/).map(function (w) { return w[0]; }).join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase(); return '
' + '
' // Header. The month and area sit small above a headline that is the whole point of the email. + '
' + '
' + _esc(opts.monthLabel) + ' · ' + _esc(area) + '
' + '
' + st.count + ' home' + (st.count === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' sold
' + '
near you this month
' + (lead ? '
' + lead + '
' : '') + '
' + (statRow ? ('' + statRow + '
') : '') + '
' + '
Recent sales
' + listRows + (rows.length > 5 ? '
and ' + (rows.length - 5) + ' more
' : '') + '
' + '
' + valueBlock + '
' + '
' + '' + '' + '' + '
' + '
' + _esc(initials) + '
' + '		' + '
' + _esc(agent.name || '') + '
' + (agent.phone ? ('
' + _esc(agent.phone) + '
') : '') + '
' + '
' + '
'; } function _esc(x) { return String(x == null ? '' : x) .replace(/&/g, '&').replace(//g, '>').replace(/"/g, '"'); } // A second address, for a search of SOLD listings. Deliberately separate from the listing // feed rather than routing by status, because status cannot tell the two apart: an active // buyer's search often includes sold comps on purpose, and those belong in their feed as // context. The difference is intent, not data, so it has to be the address. async function _mlsResolveSoldClient(token, inboundAddr) { const tok = String(inboundAddr || '').split('@')[0].replace(/^sold-/i, '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!tok) return null; try { const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'soldFeedToken' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: tok } } }, limit: 1 }); for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (r.document && r.document.fields) { const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); d.uid = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); return d; } } } catch (e) { console.log('sold resolve:', e && e.message); } return null; } // Sold listings accumulate rather than being reported on arrival. The MLS decides when it // sends; the report goes out on a schedule the agent controls. Accumulating is also what makes // "six more than last month" possible - a single monthly batch has no history to compare with. async function _soldStoreListings(token, client, parsed) { const rows = (parsed && parsed.listings) || []; if (!rows.length) return { stored: 0 }; let stored = 0; const month = new Date(Date.now() - 5 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 7); for (const l of rows) { const mls = String(l.mlsNumber || l.recordId || '').trim(); if (!mls) continue; try { // Keyed on MLS number so the same sale arriving twice overwrites rather than duplicating. await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + client.uid + '/soldListings/' + mls, { mlsNumber: fsString(mls), street: fsString(l.street || ''), cityState: fsString(l.cityState || ''), address: fsString(l.address || ''), price: fsString(l.price || ''), priceNum: l.priceNum ? fsInt(l.priceNum) : fsInt(0), beds: l.beds != null ? fsInt(l.beds) : fsInt(0), baths: l.baths != null ? fsString(String(l.baths)) : fsString(''), sqft: l.sqft != null ? fsInt(l.sqft) : fsInt(0), dom: l.dom != null ? fsInt(l.dom) : fsInt(0), photoUrl: fsString(l.photoUrl || ''), listingUrl: fsString(l.listingUrl || ''), agentId: fsString(client.agentId || ''), // The month it landed, which is what the report groups by. Stored rather than derived // so a report run late still buckets a sale to the month it arrived. month: fsString(month), seenAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['mlsNumber','street','cityState','address','price','priceNum','beds','baths','sqft','dom','photoUrl','listingUrl','agentId','month','seenAt']); stored++; } catch (e) { console.log('sold store:', e && e.message); } } // Stamp a running count so the client card can show the feed is alive. A saved search that // quietly stops sending is otherwise invisible until a report comes out empty. if (stored) { try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + client.uid, { soldCount: fsInt((Number(client.soldCount) || 0) + stored), soldLastAt: fsTimestamp() }, ['soldCount','soldLastAt']); } catch (e) { console.log('sold count stamp:', e && e.message); } } return { stored: stored }; } async function _mlsResolveClient(token, inboundAddr) { // Strip whatever prefix the address carries. The app generates mls-@; the c- // form is from an earlier draft. Accept either so old addresses keep working, and // lowercase it because some mail servers rewrite the local part's case in transit. const tok = String(inboundAddr || '').split('@')[0].replace(/^(mls|c)-/i, '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!tok) return null; try { const rows = await digRunQuery(token, { from: [{ collectionId: 'users', allDescendants: false }], where: { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: 'mlsFeedToken' }, op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: tok } } }, limit: 1 }); for (const r of (rows || [])) { if (r.document && r.document.fields) { const d = digDoc(r.document.fields); d.uid = r.document.name.split('/').pop(); return d; } } } catch (e) { console.log('mls resolve:', e && e.message); } return null; } // One doc per listing per client, keyed on the MLS number so the same house // arriving next week updates rather than piling up. Ratings and firstSeenAt // survive an update \u2014 a price change must not wipe what the client already said. async function _mlsStoreListings(token, client, parsed) { let added = 0, updated = 0; for (const l of parsed.listings) { const key = l.mlsNumber || l.recordId; if (!key) continue; const path = 'users/' + client.uid + '/listings/' + key; let prior = null; try { prior = await fsGetDoc(token, path); } catch (e) {} const fields = { mlsNumber: fsString(l.mlsNumber), recordId: fsString(l.recordId), price: fsString(l.price), priceNum: fsInt(l.priceNum || 0), propType: fsString(l.propType), street: fsString(l.street), cityState: fsString(l.cityState), address: fsString(l.address), beds: fsInt(l.beds || 0), baths: fsInt(l.baths || 0), sqft: fsInt(l.sqft || 0), status: fsString(l.status), statusKey: fsString(l.statusKey), photoUrl: fsString(l.photoUrl), listingUrl: fsString(l.listingUrl), lastActivityAt: fsTimestamp(), agentId: fsString(client.agentId || parsed.agentId || ''), setId: fsString(parsed.setId || ''), lastSeenAt: fsTimestamp() }; if (!prior) { fields.firstSeenAt = fsTimestamp(); added++; } else { updated++; // Price moved on a listing they already reacted to \u2014 worth surfacing to the agent. if (prior.priceNum && l.priceNum && prior.priceNum !== l.priceNum) { fields.priorPriceNum = fsInt(prior.priceNum); fields.priceChangedAt = fsTimestamp(); } } try { await fsPatchFields(token, path, fields, Object.keys(fields)); } catch (e) { console.log('mls store:', e && e.message); } } return { added, updated }; } export default { // Inbound MLS alerts. Cloudflare Email Routing hands the message straight here — // free, and it never touches the Resend quota, which is for outbound only. async email(message, env, ctx) { try { const to = String(message.to || ''); const raw = await new Response(message.raw).text(); // Only accept mail that really came from the MLS. Anyone can guess an address. if (!/northeastmatrixmail\.com|mlsmatrix\.com/i.test(raw)) { console.log('mls email: rejected, not from the MLS \u2014', to); return; } // Pull out the text/html part and decode it properly. Splitting on the header line and // assuming quoted-printable was too naive: Gmail re-encodes forwarded mail as base64, // which produced 33k of gibberish and zero listings. Servers do the same in transit. const body = _mimeHtmlPart(raw); const parsed = parseMlsAlert(body); const token = await getFirestoreToken(env.FIREBASE_PRIVATE_KEY); // sold-@ is the neighbourhood report feed. Everything arriving there accumulates // for the monthly report and never reaches the swipe feed, whatever its status. if (/^sold-/i.test(to)) { const soldClient = await _mlsResolveSoldClient(token, to); if (!soldClient) { console.log('sold email: no client for', to); return; } // A sold search needs the same opt-in click as a listing search: Matrix leaves it // pending until someone follows the welcome link once. if (parsed.isWelcome) { const sact = await _mlsActivate(env, parsed.activateUrl); console.log('sold email: welcome for', soldClient.uid, sact.ok ? ('\u2014 activated in headless Chrome (' + sact.bytes + ' bytes)') : ('\u2014 auto-activation unavailable (' + sact.reason + '), Activate button shown instead')); try { await fsPatchFields(token, 'users/' + soldClient.uid, { soldFeedActivateUrl: fsString(sact.ok ?
